 else select cx.* into c from ins_policy_cancellations cx join ins_policy_cash_items k on k.source_id=cx.policy_id join fin_allocations a on a.open_item_id=k.id where a.settlement_id=sid and cx.status='posted' limit 1; end if;
 if c.id is null then if tg_op='DELETE' then return old; else return new; end if; end if;
 perform ins_policy_cash_require_finance(); perform id from ins_policies where id=c.policy_id for update;
 select * into s from fin_settlements where id=sid for update;
 if q.id is not null then
 if auth.uid() in(q.maker_id,q.checker_id,s.created_by) then raise exception 'INS_CXL_CASH_THIRD_PERSON: a third distinct Finance reconciler is required' using errcode='42501'; end if;
 if tg_op<>'DELETE' then
 select * into b from fin_bank_accounts where id=s.bank_account_id; select * into l from bank_statement_lines where id=new.bank_statement_line_id;
 if s.status<>'posted' or q.status<>'posted' or new.matched_by is distinct from auth.uid() or new.tenant_id<>s.tenant_id or new.variance is distinct from 0 or l.tenant_id<>s.tenant_id or l.bank_account<>b.name or l.currency<>s.currency or l.amount<>(case s.direction when 'in' then s.amount else -s.amount end) then raise exception 'INS_CXL_CASH_MATCH: exact bank, currency, amount and acting reconciler required'; end if;
 end if;
 end if;
 if tg_op='DELETE' and(s.direction='in' or q.kind='commission_return') and exists(select 1 from ins_cancellation_cash_requests where cancellation_id=c.id and status='posted' and kind in('refund','remittance','commission_withdrawal')) then raise exception 'INS_CXL_CASH_FUNDS_IN_USE: reverse downstream cancellation payments before removing funding evidence'; end if;
 if q.id is not null then insert into ins_cancellation_cash_events(tenant_id,request_id,event,actor_id,detail) values(c.tenant_id,q.id,case tg_op when 'DELETE' then 'unmatched' else 'matched' end,auth.uid(),case tg_op when 'DELETE' then to_jsonb(old) else to_jsonb(new) end); end if;
 if tg_op='DELETE' then return old; else return new; end if;
end $function$
-- FN ins_cancellation_complete_guard()
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.ins_cancellation_complete_guard()
 RETURNS trigger
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
declare c ins_policy_cancellations%rowtype; difference numeric;
begin
 select * into c from ins_policy_cancellations where id=new.id;
 if c.status='posting' then raise exception 'INS_CANCELLATION_INCOMPLETE: interrupted transaction'; end if;
 if c.status='posted' then
  if not exists(select 1 from ins_policies where id=c.policy_id and status='cancelled') or exists(select 1 from ins_cancellation_credit_links l left join fin_credit_notes n on n.id=l.credit_note_id where l.cancellation_id=c.id and (n.id is null or n.journal_entry_id is null or n.status<>'posted' or n.amount<>l.amount)) then
   raise exception 'INS_CANCELLATION_INCOMPLETE: missing policy state or credit document'; end if;
  select coalesce(sum(l.debit-l.credit),0) into difference from ins_cancellation_journals j join journal_entry_lines l on l.journal_entry_id=j.journal_entry_id where j.cancellation_id=c.id and l.gl_account_id=c.clearing_gl_account_id;
  if difference<>0 then raise exception 'INS_CANCELLATION_INCOMPLETE: clearing account does not reconcile'; end if;
  if (select count(*) from ins_cancellation_insurer_adjustments where cancellation_id=c.id)<>jsonb_array_length(c.calculation->'insurers')
   or coalesce((select sum(l.amount) from ins_cancellation_credit_links l join fin_open_items i on i.id=l.open_item_id where l.cancellation_id=c.id and i.source_type='ins_policy_premium'),0)<>(c.calculation->>'receivable_credit')::numeric then
   raise exception 'INS_CANCELLATION_INCOMPLETE: insurer rows or receivable credits missing'; end if;
  if ((c.calculation->>'client_refund_due')::numeric>0 and not exists(select 1 from fin_open_items i where i.id=c.refund_item_id and i.tenant_id=c.tenant_id and i.source_type='ins_cancellation_client_refund' and i.source_id=c.id and i.control_role='ap_control' and i.amount=(c.calculation->>'client_refund_due')::numeric))
    or ((c.calculation->>'client_refund_due')::numeric=0 and c.refund_item_id is not null) then raise exception 'INS_CANCELLATION_INCOMPLETE: refund liability mismatch'; end if;
  if exists(select 1 from ins_cancellation_insurer_adjustments a where a.cancellation_id=c.id and
   (a.payable_credit<>coalesce((select sum(l.amount) from ins_cancellation_credit_links l where l.cancellation_id=c.id and l.open_item_id=a.original_item_id),0)
    or (a.recovery_due>0 and not exists(select 1 from fin_open_items i where i.id=a.recovery_item_id and i.tenant_id=c.tenant_id and i.source_type='ins_cancellation_insurer_recovery' and i.source_id=a.id and i.amount=a.recovery_due)))) then
   raise exception 'INS_CANCELLATION_INCOMPLETE: insurer credits/recovery items missing'; end if;
  if exists(select 1 from ins_cancellation_journals j join journal_entries e on e.id=j.journal_entry_id where j.cancellation_id=c.id and (e.tenant_id<>c.tenant_id or e.status<>'posted' or e.entry_date<>c.posting_date)) then raise exception 'INS_CANCELLATION_INCOMPLETE: journal state/date mismatch'; end if;
 end if;
 return new;
end $function$
-- FN ins_cancellation_evidence_guard()
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.ins_cancellation_evidence_guard()
 RETURNS trigger
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
declare cid uuid; c ins_policy_cancellations%rowtype;
begin
 if tg_table_name='ins_policy_cancellations' then
  if tg_op='DELETE' then raise exception 'INS_CANCELLATION_IMMUTABLE: retain cancellation audit'; end if;
  if tg_op='INSERT' then return new; end if;
  if old.status in('posted','rejected','withdrawn') then raise exception 'INS_CANCELLATION_IMMUTABLE: terminal cases cannot change'; end if;
  if (to_jsonb(new)-array['status','prepared_by','prepared_at','clearing_gl_account_id','calculation','checked_by','checked_at','review_note','manual_exception_accepted','cancellation_no','posting_date','journal_entry_id','refund_item_id','cash_at_posting','accounting_mappings','operation_txid'])
    is distinct from (to_jsonb(old)-array['status','prepared_by','prepared_at','clearing_gl_account_id','calculation','checked_by','checked_at','review_note','manual_exception_accepted','cancellation_no','posting_date','journal_entry_id','refund_item_id','cash_at_posting','accounting_mappings','operation_txid']) then
   raise exception 'INS_CANCELLATION_IMMUTABLE: proposal inputs cannot be rewritten; withdraw and resubmit'; end if;
  return new;
 end if;
 if tg_table_name='ins_cancellation_events' then
  if tg_op<>'INSERT' then raise exception 'INS_CANCELLATION_IMMUTABLE: append-only audit'; end if;
  return new;
 end if;
 cid:=case when tg_op='DELETE' then old.cancellation_id else new.cancellation_id end;
 select * into c from ins_policy_cancellations where id=cid;
 if tg_op='DELETE' or c.status<>'posting' or c.operation_txid<>txid_current() then raise exception 'INS_CANCELLATION_IMMUTABLE: financial evidence is transaction-bound and immutable'; end if;
 if tg_op='UPDATE' and (tg_table_name<>'ins_cancellation_insurer_adjustments' or (to_jsonb(new)-'recovery_item_id') is distinct from (to_jsonb(old)-'recovery_item_id')) then
  raise exception 'INS_CANCELLATION_IMMUTABLE: adjustment history cannot change'; end if;
 return new;
end $function$
-- FN ins_cancellation_finance_lock_guard()
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.ins_cancellation_finance_lock_guard()
 RETURNS trigger
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
begin
 if tg_table_name='fin_allocations' then if platform_ins_cancellation_cash_authorized(new.open_item_id,new.settlement_id,new.amount) then return new; end if;
 elsif tg_table_name='fin_settlements' then if platform_ins_cancellation_cash_void_authorized(old.id) then return new; end if; end if;
 if tg_table_name='fin_credit_notes' then
  if new.status is distinct from old.status and exists(select 1 from ins_cancellation_credit_links where credit_note_id=old.id) then raise exception 'INS_CANCELLATION_LOCKED: linked credit cannot be voided independently'; end if;
 elsif tg_table_name='journal_entries' then
  if new.status is distinct from old.status then
   perform p.id from ins_policies p where exists(select 1 from fin_settlements s join fin_allocations a on a.settlement_id=s.id join ins_policy_cash_items k on k.id=a.open_item_id
    where k.source_id=p.id and old.id in(s.journal_entry_id,s.void_journal_entry_id)) or exists(select 1 from ins_commission_events e join ins_commission_entries ce on ce.id=e.entry_id where ce.policy_id=p.id and e.journal_entry_id=old.id) order by p.id for update;
   if exists(select 1 from ins_cancellation_journals where journal_entry_id=old.id)
    or exists(select 1 from fin_settlements s join fin_allocations a on a.settlement_id=s.id join ins_policy_cash_items k on k.id=a.open_item_id
      join ins_policy_cancellations c on c.policy_id=k.source_id and c.status='posted' where old.id in(s.journal_entry_id,s.void_journal_entry_id))
    or exists(select 1 from ins_cancellation_insurer_adjustments a join ins_commission_events e on e.entry_id=a.original_commission_entry_id where e.journal_entry_id=old.id)
    or exists(select 1 from ins_cancellation_insurer_adjustments a join ins_commission_transfers t on t.id=(a.original_commission_state->>'transfer_id')::uuid where old.id in(t.journal_entry_id,t.void_journal_entry_id)) then
    raise exception 'INS_CANCELLATION_LOCKED: linked or original cash/commission journal cannot be voided independently'; end if;
  end if;
 elsif tg_table_name='fin_settlements' then
  if new.status is distinct from old.status then
   perform p.id from ins_policies p join ins_policy_cash_items k on k.source_id=p.id join fin_allocations a on a.open_item_id=k.id where a.settlement_id=old.id order by p.id for update of p;
   if exists(select 1 from fin_allocations a join ins_policy_cash_items k on k.id=a.open_item_id join ins_policy_cancellations c on c.policy_id=k.source_id and c.status='posted' where a.settlement_id=old.id) then raise exception 'INS_CANCELLATION_LOCKED: historical cash is preserved after cancellation'; end if;
  end if;
 elsif tg_table_name='ins_commission_transfers' then
  if new.status is distinct from old.status and exists(select 1 from ins_cancellation_insurer_adjustments a join ins_policy_cancellations c on c.id=a.cancellation_id and c.status='posted' where (a.original_commission_state->>'transfer_id')::uuid=old.id) then raise exception 'INS_CANCELLATION_LOCKED: historical commission transfer is preserved; recover the clawback separately'; end if;
 elsif tg_table_name='fin_allocations' then
  if exists(select 1 from fin_open_items where id=new.open_item_id and source_type in('ins_cancellation_client_refund','ins_cancellation_insurer_recovery')) then raise exception 'INS_CANCELLATION_CASH_REQUIRED: separately approved, funded cash workflow required'; end if;
 elsif tg_table_name='fin_credit_applications' then
  if exists(select 1 from fin_open_items where id=new.open_item_id and source_type in('ins_cancellation_client_refund','ins_cancellation_insurer_recovery')) then raise exception 'INS_CANCELLATION_LOCKED: cancellation balances cannot be generically credited'; end if;
 end if;
 return new;
end $function$
-- FN ins_cancelled_policy_guard()
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.ins_cancelled_policy_guard()
 RETURNS trigger
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
begin
 if old.status='cancelled' and new.status is distinct from old.status then raise exception 'INS_CANCELLATION_LOCKED: cancelled policies cannot reactivate'; end if;
 if new.status='cancelled' and old.status<>'cancelled' and not exists(select 1 from ins_policy_cancellations c where c.policy_id=new.id and c.tenant_id=new.tenant_id and c.status='posted' and c.checked_by=auth.uid() and c.posting_date=current_date) then
  raise exception 'INS_CANCELLATION_CONTEXT: use the independently approved cancellation workflow'; end if;
 return new;
end $function$
-- FN ins_claim_apply_payment_totals(uuid,text)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.ins_claim_apply_payment_totals(p_claim_id uuid, p_note text)
 RETURNS ins_claims
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
declare
  v_claim ins_claims%rowtype;
  v_paid  numeric;
  v_from  text;
begin
  select * into v_claim from ins_claims where id = p_claim_id for update;
  if not found then
    raise exception 'INS_NOT_FOUND: claim';
  end if;
  select coalesce(sum(amount), 0) into v_paid
    from ins_claim_payments
   where claim_id = p_claim_id and status = 'posted' and kind in ('insurer_direct', 'client_payout');
  perform set_config('ins.via_rpc', 'on', true);
  v_from := v_claim.status;
  if v_claim.status = 'approved' and v_paid >= coalesce(v_claim.approved_amount, 0) then
    update ins_claims set paid_amount = v_paid, status = 'settled', settled_at = now()
     where id = v_claim.id returning * into v_claim;
    insert into ins_claim_events (tenant_id, claim_id, from_status, to_status, note)
    values (v_claim.tenant_id, v_claim.id, v_from, 'settled', p_note);
  elsif v_claim.status = 'settled' and v_paid < coalesce(v_claim.approved_amount, 0) then
    update ins_claims set paid_amount = v_paid, status = 'approved', settled_at = null
     where id = v_claim.id returning * into v_claim;
    insert into ins_claim_events (tenant_id, claim_id, from_status, to_status, note)
    values (v_claim.tenant_id, v_claim.id, v_from, 'approved', p_note);
  else
    update ins_claims set paid_amount = v_paid
     where id = v_claim.id returning * into v_claim;
  end if;
  return v_claim;
end;
$function$
-- FN ins_claim_reserve_history_guard()
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.ins_claim_reserve_history_guard()
 RETURNS trigger
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
begin
  if not public.ins_via_rpc() then
    raise exception 'INS_RPC_ONLY: revise a claim reserve with ins_revise_claim_reserve()' using errcode = '42501';
  end if;
  if tg_op in ('UPDATE', 'DELETE') then
    raise exception 'INS_RESERVE_HISTORY_IMMUTABLE: reserve history is never edited or deleted';
  end if;
  return new;
end $function$
-- FN ins_claim_settlement_void_sync()
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.ins_claim_settlement_void_sync()
 RETURNS trigger
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
declare
  v_pay        ins_claim_payments%rowtype;
  v_held_after numeric;
  v_status     text;
begin
  select * into v_pay from ins_claim_payments where settlement_id = new.id and status = 'posted' for update;
  if not found then
    return new;
  end if;
  select status into v_status from ins_claims where id = v_pay.claim_id;
  if v_status = 'closed' then
    raise exception 'INS_CLAIM_CLOSED: payments on a closed claim cannot be voided';
  end if;
  if v_pay.kind = 'insurer_receipt' then
    select coalesce(sum(amount) filter (where kind = 'insurer_receipt'), 0)
           - coalesce(sum(amount) filter (where kind = 'client_payout'), 0)
      into v_held_after
      from ins_claim_payments
     where claim_id = v_pay.claim_id and status = 'posted' and id <> v_pay.id;
    if v_held_after < 0 then
      raise exception 'INS_CLAIM_FUNDS_PAID_OUT: this receipt has already been paid out to the client; void the payout first';
    end if;
  end if;
  perform set_config('ins.via_rpc', 'on', true);
  update ins_claim_payments
     set status = 'void', voided_at = now(), voided_by = auth.uid(),
         void_reason = coalesce(new.void_reason, 'Settlement voided')
   where id = v_pay.id;
  perform ins_claim_apply_payment_totals(v_pay.claim_id, 'Payment voided: ' || coalesce(new.void_reason, 'settlement voided'));
  return new;
end;
$function$
-- FN ins_claims_guard()
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.ins_claims_guard()
 RETURNS trigger
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
begin
  if tg_op = 'INSERT' then
    if not public.ins_via_rpc() then
      raise exception 'INS_RPC_ONLY: create claims with ins_create_claim()' using errcode = '42501';
    end if;
    return new;
  end if;
  if new.claim_no is distinct from old.claim_no or new.policy_id is distinct from old.policy_id then
    raise exception 'INS_IMMUTABLE: a claim number and its policy cannot be changed';
  end if;
  if not public.ins_via_rpc() and (
    new.status is distinct from old.status
    or new.approved_amount is distinct from old.approved_amount
    or new.paid_amount is distinct from old.paid_amount
    or new.settled_at is distinct from old.settled_at
    or new.payment_mode is distinct from old.payment_mode
    or new.reserve_amount is distinct from old.reserve_amount
  ) then
    raise exception 'INS_CLAIM_RPC_ONLY: status, amounts, reserve and payment mode change through the claim functions' using errcode = '42501';
  end if;
  if new.loss_date is distinct from old.loss_date and not exists (
    select 1 from ins_policies p
     where p.id = new.policy_id and p.tenant_id = new.tenant_id
       and new.loss_date between p.inception_date and p.expiry_date
  ) then
    raise exception 'INS_LOSS_OUTSIDE_POLICY: loss date % is outside the policy period', new.loss_date;
  end if;
  return new;
end;
$function$
-- FN ins_claims_initial_reserve()
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.ins_claims_initial_reserve()
 RETURNS trigger
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
begin
  perform set_config('ins.via_rpc', 'on', true);
  insert into ins_claim_reserve_history (tenant_id, claim_id, kind, previous_amount, new_amount, reason, revised_by)
  values (new.tenant_id, new.id, 'initial', null, new.reserve_amount, 'Initial reserve when the claim was registered', new.created_by);
  return new;
end $function$
-- FN ins_clear_renewal_outcome(uuid)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.ins_clear_renewal_outcome(p_policy_id uuid)
 RETURNS void
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
declare
  v_tenant uuid := get_my_tenant_id();
begin
  if not can_manage_insurance() then
    raise exception 'INS_FORBIDDEN: only insurance admins and managers can clear a renewal outcome' using errcode = '42501';
  end if;
  perform set_config('ins.via_rpc', 'on', true);
  delete from ins_renewal_outcomes where tenant_id = v_tenant and policy_id = p_policy_id;
  if not found then
    raise exception 'INS_NOT_FOUND: no renewal outcome is recorded for this policy';
  end if;
end $function$
-- FN ins_clients_guard()
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.ins_clients_guard()
 RETURNS trigger
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
begin
  if tg_op = 'UPDATE' and new.client_id is distinct from old.client_id then
    raise exception 'INS_IMMUTABLE: the BD client link cannot be changed';
  end if;
  if tg_op = 'UPDATE' and new.party_account_id is distinct from old.party_account_id and not public.ins_via_rpc() then
    raise exception 'INS_PARTY_ACCOUNT_RPC_ONLY: the party account is managed by binding a policy' using errcode = '42501';
  end if;
  if not public.ins_via_rpc() and not public.can_manage_insurance() then
    if tg_op = 'INSERT' and new.kyc_status is distinct from 'pending' then
      raise exception 'INS_KYC_MANAGER_ONLY: new clients start as pending; a manager verifies KYC' using errcode = '42501';
    end if;
    if tg_op = 'UPDATE' and new.kyc_status is distinct from old.kyc_status then
      raise exception 'INS_KYC_MANAGER_ONLY: only insurance admins and managers can change KYC status' using errcode = '42501';
    end if;
  end if;
  if not exists (select 1 from bd_clients c where c.id = new.client_id and c.tenant_id = new.tenant_id) then
    raise exception 'INS_CROSS_TENANT: the BD client belongs to another tenant';
  end if;
  if new.party_account_id is not null and not exists (
    select 1 from accounts a where a.id = new.party_account_id and a.tenant_id = new.tenant_id
  ) then
    raise exception 'INS_CROSS_TENANT: party account belongs to another tenant';
  end if;
  return new;
end;
$function$
-- FN ins_commission_bank_accounts()
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.ins_commission_bank_accounts()
 RETURNS TABLE(id uuid, name text, kind text, currency text, bank_name text)
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 STABLE SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
begin
  if not can_manage_insurance() then
    raise exception 'INS_FORBIDDEN: only insurance admins and managers can list transfer bank accounts' using errcode = '42501';
  end if;
  return query
    select b.id, b.name, b.kind, b.currency, b.bank_name
      from fin_bank_accounts b
     where b.tenant_id = get_my_tenant_id()
       and b.is_active
       and b.gl_account_id is not null
       and b.currency = fin_ledger_currency(b.tenant_id)
     order by b.kind, b.name;
end;
$function$
-- FN ins_commission_on_settlement_posted()
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.ins_commission_on_settlement_posted()
 RETURNS trigger
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
declare
  r          record;
  v_unearned uuid;
  v_income   uuid;
  v_ev       uuid;
  v_je       journal_entries;
  v_line     jsonb;
  v_line2    jsonb;
begin
 if exists(select 1 from ins_cancellation_cash_requests where new.id in(settlement_id,to_settlement_id)) then return new; end if;
  if new.direction <> 'out' or new.status <> 'posted' or new.journal_entry_id is null then
    return new;
  end if;
  for r in
    select e.id as entry_id, e.amount, p.policy_no, i.currency as item_currency,
           i.fx_rate as item_rate, i.journal_entry_id as booking_entry
    from fin_allocations al
    join fin_open_items i  on i.id = al.open_item_id and i.source_type in ('ins_policy_insurer','ins_policy_share_insurer','ins_policy_endorsement_insurer')
    join fin_open_item_balances ob on ob.id = i.id
    join ins_commission_entries e on (
          -- An endorsement entry is earned only by its own insurer item.
          (e.source_open_item_id = i.id)
          -- The policy's bind entries earn off the original items, as before;
          -- provenance-tagged entries are excluded so a later endorsement item
          -- cannot earn them early.
       or (e.insurer_share_id = i.source_id and i.source_type = 'ins_policy_share_insurer'
           and e.source_open_item_id is null)
       or (e.policy_id = i.source_id and e.insurer_share_id is null
           and i.source_type = 'ins_policy_insurer' and e.source_open_item_id is null)
        ) and e.tenant_id = i.tenant_id
    join ins_policies p on p.id = e.policy_id
    where al.settlement_id = new.id
      and e.status = 'deferred'
      -- An item is done when its settlements plus its credit notes cover it:
      -- endorsement returns credit the original items, and a returned premium
      -- must not leave its commission deferred forever.
      and ins_item_paid_amount(i.id) + ob.credited_amount >= i.amount
    order by p.policy_no
    for update of e
  loop
    v_unearned := get_posting_account(new.tenant_id, 'unearned_commission');
    v_income   := get_posting_account(new.tenant_id, 'commission_income');
    if v_unearned is null or v_income is null then
      raise exception 'INS_POSTING_RULE_MISSING: paying the insurer for % earns commission, which needs the unearned_commission and commission_income posting rules', r.policy_no;
    end if;
    v_line  := jsonb_build_object('gl_account_id', v_unearned, 'debit',  r.amount, 'description', 'Commission earned on ' || r.policy_no);
    v_line2 := jsonb_build_object('gl_account_id', v_income,   'credit', r.amount, 'description', 'Commission earned on ' || r.policy_no);
    if r.item_currency is distinct from fin_ledger_currency(new.tenant_id) then
      -- The commission was deferred at the rate the policy was bound at; recognising it
      -- at the same rate keeps the unearned account at zero in base when it empties.
      if r.booking_entry is null or r.item_rate is null or r.item_rate <= 0 then
        raise exception 'INS_COMMISSION_RATE: commission on % has no booked rate; the insurer item must name its booking entry', r.policy_no;
      end if;
      v_line  := v_line  || jsonb_build_object('currency', r.item_currency, 'rate_from_entry', r.booking_entry, 'fx_rate', r.item_rate);
      v_line2 := v_line2 || jsonb_build_object('currency', r.item_currency, 'rate_from_entry', r.booking_entry, 'fx_rate', r.item_rate);
    end if;
    v_ev := gen_random_uuid();
    v_je := post_journal_entry(new.tenant_id, 'ins_commission_recognition', v_ev, new.settlement_date,
      'Commission earned on ' || r.policy_no, jsonb_build_array(v_line, v_line2));
    insert into ins_commission_events (id, tenant_id, entry_id, event, amount, journal_entry_id, settlement_id)
    values (v_ev, new.tenant_id, r.entry_id, 'recognised', r.amount, v_je.id, new.id);
    update ins_commission_entries
       set status = 'earned', earned_on = new.settlement_date, updated_at = now()
     where id = r.entry_id;
  end loop;
  return new;
end;
$function$
-- FN ins_commission_on_settlement_voided()
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.ins_commission_on_settlement_voided()
 RETURNS trigger
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
declare
  r          record;
  v_unearned uuid;
  v_income   uuid;
  v_ev       uuid;
  v_je       journal_entries;
  v_date     date;
  v_rec_je   uuid;
  v_lines    jsonb;
begin
 if exists(select 1 from ins_cancellation_cash_requests where new.id in(settlement_id,to_settlement_id)) then return new; end if;
  if new.direction <> 'out' or old.status <> 'posted' or new.status <> 'void' then
    return new;
  end if;
  select entry_date into v_date from journal_entries where id = new.void_journal_entry_id;
  v_date := coalesce(v_date, current_date);
  for r in
    select e.id as entry_id, e.amount, e.status as entry_status, p.policy_no, i.currency as item_currency
    from fin_allocations al
    join fin_open_items i  on i.id = al.open_item_id and i.source_type in ('ins_policy_insurer','ins_policy_share_insurer')
    join ins_commission_entries e on ((e.insurer_share_id=i.source_id and i.source_type='ins_policy_share_insurer') or (e.policy_id=i.source_id and e.insurer_share_id is null and i.source_type='ins_policy_insurer')) and e.tenant_id=i.tenant_id
    join ins_policies p on p.id = e.policy_id
    where al.settlement_id = new.id
      and e.status in ('earned', 'transferred')
      and ins_item_paid_amount(i.id) < i.amount
    order by p.policy_no
    for update of e
  loop
    if r.entry_status = 'transferred' then
      raise exception 'INS_COMMISSION_TRANSFERRED: commission on % has already been transferred to the operating account; void that commission transfer first', r.policy_no;
    end if;
    v_unearned := get_posting_account(new.tenant_id, 'unearned_commission');
    v_income   := get_posting_account(new.tenant_id, 'commission_income');
    if v_unearned is null or v_income is null then
      raise exception 'INS_POSTING_RULE_MISSING: reversing commission on % needs the unearned_commission and commission_income posting rules', r.policy_no;
    end if;
    v_ev := gen_random_uuid();
    if r.item_currency is distinct from fin_ledger_currency(new.tenant_id) then
      -- Reverse the recognition journal exactly: its lines carry the currency and the
      -- booked rate, and reversal mode keeps them, so the recognition negates to zero.
      select journal_entry_id into v_rec_je
        from ins_commission_events
       where entry_id = r.entry_id and event = 'recognised' and journal_entry_id is not null
       order by created_at desc
       limit 1;
      if v_rec_je is null then
        raise exception 'INS_COMMISSION_RATE: reversing commission on % needs its recognition journal', r.policy_no;
      end if;
      select jsonb_agg(jsonb_build_object(
               'gl_account_id', l.gl_account_id, 'debit', l.credit, 'credit', l.debit,
               'currency', l.currency, 'fx_rate', l.fx_rate, 'is_rounding', l.is_rounding,
               'description', 'Reversal of commission earned on ' || r.policy_no))
        into v_lines
        from journal_entry_lines l
       where l.journal_entry_id = v_rec_je;
      perform set_config('fin.reversal_of', v_rec_je::text, true);
      v_je := post_journal_entry(new.tenant_id, 'ins_commission_recognition_reversal', v_ev, v_date,
        'Commission no longer earned on ' || r.policy_no || ' (settlement voided)', v_lines);
      perform set_config('fin.reversal_of', '', true);
    else
      v_je := post_journal_entry(new.tenant_id, 'ins_commission_recognition_reversal', v_ev, v_date,
        'Commission no longer earned on ' || r.policy_no || ' (settlement voided)',
        jsonb_build_array(
          jsonb_build_object('gl_account_id', v_income,   'debit',  r.amount, 'description', 'Reversal of commission earned on ' || r.policy_no),
          jsonb_build_object('gl_account_id', v_unearned, 'credit', r.amount, 'description', 'Reversal of commission earned on ' || r.policy_no)));
    end if;
    insert into ins_commission_events (id, tenant_id, entry_id, event, amount, journal_entry_id, settlement_id)
    values (v_ev, new.tenant_id, r.entry_id, 'recognition_reversed', r.amount, v_je.id, new.id);
    update ins_commission_entries
       set status = 'deferred', earned_on = null, updated_at = now()
     where id = r.entry_id;
  end loop;
  return new;
end;
$function$
-- FN ins_complex_policy_credit_guard()
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.ins_complex_policy_credit_guard()
 RETURNS trigger
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
declare linked ins_cancellation_credit_links%rowtype; c ins_policy_cancellations%rowtype;
begin
 select * into linked from ins_cancellation_credit_links where credit_note_id=new.credit_note_id and tenant_id=new.tenant_id;
 if found then
  c:=platform_ins_cancellation_context(linked.cancellation_id);
  if linked.open_item_id<>new.open_item_id or linked.amount<>new.amount then raise exception 'INS_CANCELLATION_CONTEXT: credit outside exact authorization'; end if;
  return new;
 end if;
 if exists(select 1 from ins_policy_cash_items m join ins_policy_financial_snapshots sn on sn.policy_id=m.source_id and sn.tenant_id=m.tenant_id and sn.extended
   where m.id=new.open_item_id and m.tenant_id=new.tenant_id and m.source_type in('ins_policy_premium','ins_policy_insurer'))
  or exists(select 1 from ins_policy_cash_items m join ins_policy_cancellations c2 on c2.policy_id=m.source_id and c2.tenant_id=m.tenant_id and c2.status in('posting','posted') where m.id=new.open_item_id) then
  raise exception 'INS_CANCELLATION_REQUIRED: policy credits require the cancellation workflow'; end if;
 return new;
end $function$
-- FN ins_create_claim(uuid,date,date,text,text,numeric)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.ins_create_claim(p_policy_id uuid, p_loss_date date, p_notified_date date, p_loss_description text, p_insurer_claim_ref text DEFAULT NULL::text, p_reserve_amount numeric DEFAULT 0)
 RETURNS ins_claims
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
declare
  v_tenant uuid := get_my_tenant_id();
  v_pol    ins_policies%rowtype;
  v_row    ins_claims%rowtype;
begin
  if not can_access_insurance() then
    raise exception 'INS_FORBIDDEN: insurance access required' using errcode = '42501';
  end if;
  select * into v_pol from ins_policies where id = p_policy_id and tenant_id = v_tenant;
  if not found then
    raise exception 'INS_NOT_FOUND: policy';
  end if;
  if v_pol.status = 'draft' then
    raise exception 'INS_CLAIM_STATE: a claim needs a bound policy';
  end if;
  if p_loss_date < v_pol.inception_date or p_loss_date > v_pol.expiry_date then
    raise exception 'INS_LOSS_OUTSIDE_POLICY: loss date % is outside the policy period % to %',
      p_loss_date, v_pol.inception_date, v_pol.expiry_date;
  end if;
  if nullif(btrim(p_loss_description), '') is null then
    raise exception 'INS_REQUIRED: describe the loss';
  end if;
  if coalesce(p_reserve_amount, 0) < 0 then
    raise exception 'INS_AMOUNT: reserve cannot be negative';
  end if;
  perform set_config('ins.via_rpc', 'on', true);
  insert into ins_claims (
    tenant_id, claim_no, policy_id, status, loss_date, notified_date,
    loss_description, insurer_claim_ref, reserve_amount
  ) values (
    v_tenant, next_doc_number(v_tenant, 'INS_CLAIM', 'CLM', 5), v_pol.id, 'notified', p_loss_date,
    coalesce(p_notified_date, current_date), btrim(p_loss_description),
    nullif(btrim(p_insurer_claim_ref), ''), coalesce(p_reserve_amount, 0)
  )
  returning * into v_row;
  insert into ins_claim_events (tenant_id, claim_id, from_status, to_status, note)
  values (v_tenant, v_row.id, null, 'notified', 'Claim notified');
  return v_row;
end;
$function$
-- FN ins_create_policy(uuid,uuid,uuid,date,date,numeric,text,numeric,numeric,text,text)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.ins_create_policy(p_client_id uuid, p_insurer_id uuid, p_product_line_id uuid, p_inception_date date, p_expiry_date date, p_sum_insured numeric, p_currency text, p_gross_premium numeric, p_commission_rate_pct numeric, p_risk_description text DEFAULT NULL::text, p_notes text DEFAULT NULL::text)
 RETURNS ins_policies
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
begin
  return public.ins_create_policy(
    p_client_id, p_insurer_id, p_product_line_id, p_inception_date, p_expiry_date,
    p_sum_insured, p_currency, p_gross_premium, p_commission_rate_pct,
    null::uuid, null::uuid, p_risk_description, p_notes);
end;
$function$
-- FN ins_create_policy(uuid,uuid,uuid,date,date,numeric,text,numeric,numeric,uuid,uuid,text,text)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.ins_create_policy(p_client_id uuid, p_insurer_id uuid, p_product_line_id uuid, p_inception_date date, p_expiry_date date, p_sum_insured numeric, p_currency text, p_gross_premium numeric, p_commission_rate_pct numeric, p_branch_id uuid, p_agent_id uuid, p_risk_description text DEFAULT NULL::text, p_notes text DEFAULT NULL::text)
 RETURNS ins_policies
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
declare
  v_tenant uuid := get_my_tenant_id();
  v_row    ins_policies%rowtype;
begin
  if not can_access_insurance() then
    raise exception 'INS_FORBIDDEN: insurance access required' using errcode = '42501';
  end if;
  if p_expiry_date is null or p_inception_date is null or p_expiry_date <= p_inception_date then
    raise exception 'INS_DATES: expiry must be after inception';
  end if;
  if coalesce(p_sum_insured, -1) < 0 or coalesce(p_gross_premium, -1) < 0 then
    raise exception 'INS_AMOUNT: sum insured and premium cannot be negative';
  end if;
  if p_commission_rate_pct is null or p_commission_rate_pct < 0 or p_commission_rate_pct >= 100 then
    raise exception 'INS_COMMISSION: commission rate must be at least 0 and below 100';
  end if;
  if not exists (select 1 from ins_clients where id = p_client_id and tenant_id = v_tenant) then
    raise exception 'INS_NOT_FOUND: client';
  end if;
  if not exists (select 1 from ins_insurers where id = p_insurer_id and tenant_id = v_tenant and is_active) then
    raise exception 'INS_NOT_FOUND: active insurer';
  end if;
  if not exists (select 1 from ins_product_lines where id = p_product_line_id and tenant_id = v_tenant and is_active) then
    raise exception 'INS_NOT_FOUND: active product line';
  end if;
  -- A new policy is produced by an agent who is active today. A policy keeps an agent who is
  -- suspended later; the guard only requires activity when the reference changes.
  if p_agent_id is not null and not exists (
    select 1 from ins_intermediaries a
    where a.id = p_agent_id and a.tenant_id = v_tenant and a.status = 'active'
  ) then
    raise exception 'INS_NOT_FOUND: active intermediary (agent)';
  end if;
  if p_branch_id is not null and not exists (
    select 1 from organizations o
    where o.id = p_branch_id and o.tenant_id = v_tenant and o.is_active
  ) then
    raise exception 'INS_NOT_FOUND: active organisation (branch)';
  end if;
  perform set_config('ins.via_rpc', 'on', true);
  insert into ins_policies (
    tenant_id, policy_no, client_id, insurer_id, product_line_id, status,
    inception_date, expiry_date, sum_insured, currency, gross_premium,
    commission_rate_pct, risk_description, notes, branch_id, agent_id, transaction_type
  ) values (
    v_tenant, next_doc_number(v_tenant, 'INS_POLICY', 'POL', 5), p_client_id, p_insurer_id, p_product_line_id, 'draft',
    p_inception_date, p_expiry_date, coalesce(p_sum_insured, 0), coalesce(nullif(p_currency, ''), 'UGX'), coalesce(p_gross_premium, 0),
    p_commission_rate_pct, nullif(btrim(p_risk_description), ''), nullif(btrim(p_notes), ''),
    p_branch_id, p_agent_id, 'new_business'
  )
  returning * into v_row;
  return v_row;
end;
$function$
-- FN ins_create_quote(uuid,uuid,uuid,numeric,text,numeric,numeric,date,text)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.ins_create_quote(p_client_id uuid, p_insurer_id uuid, p_product_line_id uuid, p_sum_insured numeric, p_currency text, p_gross_premium numeric, p_commission_rate_pct numeric, p_valid_until date DEFAULT NULL::date, p_notes text DEFAULT NULL::text)
 RETURNS ins_quotes
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
declare
  v_tenant uuid := public.get_my_tenant_id();
  v_quote public.ins_quotes;
begin
  if v_tenant is null or not public.can_access_insurance() then
    raise exception 'INS_ACCESS_DENIED' using errcode = '42501';
  end if;
  -- Referenced rows must exist in the caller's own tenant.
  if not exists (select 1 from public.ins_clients c where c.tenant_id = v_tenant and c.id = p_client_id) then
    raise exception 'INS_CLIENT_NOT_FOUND';
  end if;
  if not exists (select 1 from public.ins_insurers i where i.tenant_id = v_tenant and i.id = p_insurer_id and i.is_active) then
    raise exception 'INS_INSURER_NOT_FOUND_OR_INACTIVE';
  end if;
  if not exists (select 1 from public.ins_product_lines pl where pl.tenant_id = v_tenant and pl.id = p_product_line_id and pl.is_active) then
    raise exception 'INS_PRODUCT_LINE_NOT_FOUND_OR_INACTIVE';
  end if;
  set local ins.via_rpc = 'on';
  insert into public.ins_quotes (
    tenant_id, client_id, insurer_id, product_line_id, status,
    sum_insured, currency, gross_premium, commission_rate_pct,
    valid_until, notes, created_by
  ) values (
    v_tenant, p_client_id, p_insurer_id, p_product_line_id, 'draft',
    p_sum_insured, upper(p_currency), p_gross_premium, p_commission_rate_pct,
    p_valid_until, p_notes, auth.uid()
  )
  returning * into v_quote;
  reset ins.via_rpc;
  return v_quote;
end;
$function$
-- FN ins_create_renewal_draft(uuid)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.ins_create_renewal_draft(p_policy_id uuid)
 RETURNS ins_policies
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
begin
  if not can_access_insurance() then
    raise exception 'INS_FORBIDDEN: insurance access required' using errcode = '42501';
  end if;
  if exists (select 1 from ins_renewal_outcomes where tenant_id = get_my_tenant_id() and policy_id = p_policy_id) then
    raise exception 'INS_RENEWAL_CLOSED: a renewal outcome is recorded for this policy; clear it before starting a renewal';
  end if;
  return platform_ins_create_renewal_pre_outcome_impl(p_policy_id);
end $function$
-- FN ins_create_renewal_drafts(uuid[])
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.ins_create_renewal_drafts(p_policy_ids uuid[])
 RETURNS jsonb
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
declare
  v_tenant uuid := get_my_tenant_id();
  v_ids    uuid[];
  v_id     uuid;
  v_no     text;
  v_draft  ins_policies%rowtype;
  v_out    jsonb := '[]'::jsonb;
begin
  if not can_access_insurance() then
    raise exception 'INS_FORBIDDEN: insurance access required' using errcode = '42501';
  end if;
  select coalesce(array_agg(distinct x), '{}'::uuid[]) into v_ids from unnest(p_policy_ids) as x where x is not null;
  if cardinality(v_ids) = 0 then
    raise exception 'INS_BULK_EMPTY: choose at least one policy';
  end if;
  if cardinality(v_ids) > 100 then
    raise exception 'INS_BULK_LIMIT: renew at most 100 policies at a time';
  end if;
  foreach v_id in array v_ids loop
    select policy_no into v_no from ins_policies where id = v_id and tenant_id = v_tenant;
    begin
      v_draft := public.ins_create_renewal_draft(v_id);
      v_out := v_out || jsonb_build_array(jsonb_build_object(
        'policy_id', v_id, 'policy_no', v_no, 'ok', true, 'draft_id', v_draft.id));
    exception when others then
      v_out := v_out || jsonb_build_array(jsonb_build_object(
        'policy_id', v_id, 'policy_no', v_no, 'ok', false, 'error', sqlerrm));
    end;
  end loop;
  return v_out;
end $function$
-- FN ins_decide_cancellation_cash(uuid,text,text)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.ins_decide_cancellation_cash(p_request_id uuid, p_decision text, p_reason text)
 RETURNS ins_cancellation_cash_requests
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
declare q ins_cancellation_cash_requests%rowtype; target ins_cancellation_cash_requests%rowtype; c ins_policy_cancellations%rowtype; s fin_settlements%rowtype; item uuid; v jsonb;
begin
 perform ins_policy_cash_require_finance();
 if p_decision is null or p_decision not in('approve','reject','cancel') or p_reason is null or length(btrim(p_reason)) not between 5 and 2000 then raise exception 'INS_CXL_CASH_INPUT: decision and detailed review note required'; end if;
 select cx.* into c from ins_policy_cancellations cx join ins_cancellation_cash_requests r on r.cancellation_id=cx.id where r.id=p_request_id and r.tenant_id=get_my_tenant_id();
 if not found then raise exception 'INS_NOT_FOUND: cash request'; end if;
 -- All actors lock policy, banks in UUID order, request, then items/settlements.
 perform id from ins_policies where id=c.policy_id for update;
 perform id from fin_bank_accounts where tenant_id=c.tenant_id order by id for update;
 perform pg_advisory_xact_lock(hashtextextended('ins-cxl-cash-book:'||c.tenant_id::text,0));
 select * into q from ins_cancellation_cash_requests where id=p_request_id for update;
 if(p_decision='cancel' and q.maker_id<>auth.uid()) or(p_decision<>'cancel' and q.maker_id=auth.uid()) then raise exception 'INS_CXL_CASH_SELF_APPROVAL: maker cannot act as checker'; end if;
 if p_decision='approve' and q.status in('posted','voided') and q.checker_id=auth.uid() then return q; end if;
 if q.status<>'pending' then raise exception 'INS_CXL_CASH_STATE: pending request required'; end if;
 if p_decision in('reject','cancel') then
 update ins_cancellation_cash_requests set status=case p_decision when 'reject' then 'rejected' else 'cancelled' end,checker_id=case p_decision when 'reject' then auth.uid() end,checked_at=now(),decision_reason=btrim(p_reason) where id=q.id returning * into q;
 else
 if q.cash_date<>current_date or exists(select 1 from accounting_periods where tenant_id=c.tenant_id and status='closed' and current_date between period_start and period_end) then raise exception 'INS_CXL_CASH_PERIOD: current open posting date required; cancel and recreate stale-date requests'; end if;
 if q.kind<>'void' then item:=platform_ins_cancellation_cash_validate(c.id,q.kind,q.adjustment_id,q.bank_account_id,q.to_bank_account_id,q.amount,q.cash_date); end if;
 update ins_cancellation_cash_requests set status='posting',checker_id=auth.uid(),checked_at=now(),decision_reason=btrim(p_reason),operation_txid=txid_current() where id=q.id returning * into q;
 if q.kind='void' then
 select * into target from ins_cancellation_cash_requests where id=q.voids_request_id and cancellation_id=c.id and status='posted' for update;
 if not found then raise exception 'INS_CXL_CASH_VOID: target is not posted'; end if;
 if exists(select 1 from bank_reconciliations where settlement_id in(target.settlement_id,target.to_settlement_id)) then raise exception 'INS_CXL_CASH_MATCHED: unmatch both legs before requesting a void'; end if;
 if target.kind in('recovery','premium_receipt','commission_return') and exists(select 1 from ins_cancellation_cash_requests where cancellation_id=c.id and status='posted' and kind in('refund','remittance','commission_withdrawal')) then raise exception 'INS_CXL_CASH_FUNDS_IN_USE: reverse downstream cancellation payments first'; end if;
 if target.kind='remittance' and exists(select 1 from ins_cancellation_cash_requests where adjustment_id=target.adjustment_id and kind='commission_withdrawal' and status='posted') then raise exception 'INS_CXL_CASH_FUNDS_IN_USE: return the retained commission withdrawal first'; end if;
 if target.kind in('recovery','premium_receipt','commission_return') then
 v:=platform_ins_cancellation_cash_position(c.id,case target.kind when 'commission_return' then target.to_bank_account_id else target.bank_account_id end);
 if least((v->>'bank_raw_cash')::numeric,(v->>'bank_book_cash')::numeric)<target.amount then raise exception 'INS_CXL_CASH_FUNDS_IN_USE: receipt cash is no longer available at its bank'; end if;
 elsif target.kind='commission_withdrawal' then
 v:=platform_ins_cancellation_cash_position(c.id,target.to_bank_account_id);
 if(v->>'bank_book_cash')::numeric<target.amount then raise exception 'INS_CXL_CASH_FUNDS_IN_USE: operating cash is no longer available'; end if;
 end if;
 s:=platform_fin_void_settlement_impl(c.tenant_id,target.settlement_id,q.notes,current_date);
 insert into ins_cancellation_cash_journals values(c.tenant_id,q.id,s.void_journal_entry_id,'cash_void');
 if target.to_settlement_id is not null then
 s:=platform_fin_void_settlement_impl(c.tenant_id,target.to_settlement_id,q.notes,current_date);
 insert into ins_cancellation_cash_journals values(c.tenant_id,q.id,s.void_journal_entry_id,'destination_void'); end if;
 update ins_cancellation_cash_requests set status='voided',voided_by_request_id=q.id where id=target.id;
 if target.kind='remittance' then perform platform_ins_cancellation_cash_recognize(q.id,target.adjustment_id); end if;
 else
 perform platform_ins_cancellation_cash_emit(q.id,item);
 if q.kind in('commission_return','commission_withdrawal') then perform platform_ins_cancellation_cash_emit(q.id,null,true); end if;
 if q.kind='remittance' then perform platform_ins_cancellation_cash_recognize(q.id,q.adjustment_id); end if;
 end if;
 update ins_cancellation_cash_requests set status='posted',operation_txid=null where id=q.id returning * into q;
 end if;
 insert into ins_cancellation_cash_events(tenant_id,request_id,event,actor_id,detail) values(c.tenant_id,q.id,q.status,auth.uid(),jsonb_build_object('reason',p_reason));
 return q;
end $function$
-- FN ins_decide_insurer_statement(uuid,text,text)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.ins_decide_insurer_statement(p_statement_id uuid, p_status text, p_notes text DEFAULT NULL::text)
 RETURNS ins_insurer_statements
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
declare
  v_tenant uuid := public.get_my_tenant_id();
  v_stmt public.ins_insurer_statements;
begin
  if v_tenant is null or not public.can_manage_insurance() then
    raise exception 'INS_ACCESS_DENIED' using errcode = '42501';
  end if;
  if p_status not in ('reconciled', 'disputed') then
    raise exception 'INS_STATEMENT_BAD_STATUS';
  end if;
  select * into v_stmt from public.ins_insurer_statements
  where tenant_id = v_tenant and id = p_statement_id;
  if v_stmt.id is null then raise exception 'INS_STATEMENT_NOT_FOUND'; end if;
  set local ins.via_rpc = 'on';
  update public.ins_insurer_statements
  set status = p_status, notes = coalesce(p_notes, notes)
  where tenant_id = v_tenant and id = p_statement_id
  returning * into v_stmt;
  reset ins.via_rpc;
  return v_stmt;
end;
$function$
-- FN ins_decide_policy_cancellation(uuid,text,text,boolean)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.ins_decide_policy_cancellation(p_cancellation_id uuid, p_decision text, p_review_note text, p_accept_manual_exception boolean DEFAULT false)
 RETURNS ins_policy_cancellations
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
declare c ins_policy_cancellations%rowtype; p ins_policies%rowtype; pid uuid; fresh jsonb; mappings jsonb; cash jsonb;
 client_item fin_open_items%rowtype; item fin_open_items%rowtype; created_item fin_open_items%rowtype; ce ins_commission_entries%rowtype;
 line jsonb; charge jsonb; outstanding numeric; amount numeric; applied numeric; recovered numeric; remaining numeric; claw numeric;
 target_status text; adjustment_id uuid; insurer uuid; share_id uuid; lines jsonb:='[]'; debit_total numeric:=0; recognition_lines jsonb:='[]'; recognized numeric:=0;
 je journal_entries%rowtype; transfer_due numeric; client_credit numeric; refund numeric;
 v_functional text; v_foreign boolean; v_items jsonb:='[]'::jsonb; v_todo jsonb; v_bound_rate numeric;
begin
 perform ins_policy_cash_require_finance();
 if p_decision not in('approve','reject') or p_decision is null or p_review_note is null or length(btrim(p_review_note)) not between 5 and 2000 then
  raise exception 'INS_CANCELLATION_INPUT: decision and detailed Finance review note required'; end if;
 select policy_id into pid from ins_policy_cancellations where id=p_cancellation_id and tenant_id=get_my_tenant_id();
 if not found then raise exception 'INS_NOT_FOUND: cancellation'; end if;
 select * into p from ins_policies where id=pid and tenant_id=get_my_tenant_id() for update;
 select * into c from ins_policy_cancellations where id=p_cancellation_id for update;
 if c.prepared_by=auth.uid() or c.proposed_by=auth.uid() then raise exception 'INS_CANCELLATION_SELF_APPROVAL: an independent Finance checker is required'; end if;
 if c.status='posted' and p_decision='approve' and c.checked_by=auth.uid() then
  if c.review_note is distinct from btrim(p_review_note) or c.manual_exception_accepted is distinct from coalesce(p_accept_manual_exception,false) then raise exception 'INS_CANCELLATION_RETRY_CONFLICT: different approval payload'; end if;
  return c;
 end if;
 if p_decision='reject' then
  if c.status not in('proposed','prepared') then raise exception 'INS_CANCELLATION_STATE: open proposal required'; end if;
  update ins_policy_cancellations set status='rejected',checked_by=auth.uid(),checked_at=now(),review_note=btrim(p_review_note) where id=c.id returning * into c;
  insert into ins_cancellation_events(tenant_id,cancellation_id,event,actor_id,detail) values(c.tenant_id,c.id,'rejected',auth.uid(),jsonb_build_object('note',p_review_note));
  return c;
 end if;
 if c.status<>'prepared' then raise exception 'INS_CANCELLATION_STATE: Finance preparation required before approval'; end if;
 -- Serialize with cash approvals, claims, credits and commission transfers.
 perform m.id from fin_open_items m join ins_policy_cash_items k on k.id=m.id where k.source_id=p.id and k.tenant_id=p.tenant_id order by m.id for update of m;
 perform e.id from ins_commission_entries e where e.policy_id=p.id order by e.id for update;
 fresh:=ins_preview_policy_cancellation(p.id,c.effective_date,c.insurer_terms,c.charge_refunds)||platform_ins_cancellation_review_position(p.id);
 if exists(select 1 from accounting_periods where tenant_id=p.tenant_id and status='closed' and current_date between period_start and period_end) then raise exception 'INS_CANCELLATION_PERIOD: current accounting period closed'; end if;
 if (fresh-'generated_at') is distinct from (c.calculation-'generated_at') then raise exception 'INS_CANCELLATION_STALE: amounts or collection position changed; Finance maker must refresh preparation'; end if;
 if (fresh->>'requires_finance_exception')::boolean and not coalesce(p_accept_manual_exception,false) then raise exception 'INS_CANCELLATION_EXCEPTION: explicit independent Finance acceptance of the documented manual exception required'; end if;
 mappings:=platform_ins_cancellation_mappings(p.id,c.clearing_gl_account_id);
 if mappings is distinct from c.accounting_mappings then raise exception 'INS_CANCELLATION_STALE: accounting mappings changed'; end if;
 cash:=ins_policy_cash_totals(p.id);
 v_functional:=fin_ledger_currency(p.tenant_id);
 v_foreign:=p.currency is distinct from v_functional;
 -- Existing credits to any insurer payable would otherwise double-adjust its
 -- original bound obligations. Treat them as an exception, not as cash paid.
 if exists(select 1 from fin_credit_applications a join fin_credit_notes n on n.id=a.credit_note_id and n.status='posted'
   join ins_policy_cash_items k on k.id=a.open_item_id where k.source_id=p.id and k.source_type='ins_policy_insurer') then
  raise exception 'INS_CANCELLATION_REVIEW_REQUIRED: original insurer credits need separate Finance review'; end if;
 update ins_policy_cancellations set status='posting',checked_by=auth.uid(),checked_at=now(),review_note=btrim(p_review_note),manual_exception_accepted=coalesce(p_accept_manual_exception,false),
  posting_date=current_date,cancellation_no=next_doc_number(c.tenant_id,'ins_policy_cancellation','CXL'),cash_at_posting=cash,operation_txid=txid_current() where id=c.id returning * into c;
 select * into client_item from fin_open_items where tenant_id=p.tenant_id and source_type='ins_policy_premium' and source_id=p.id;
 client_credit:=(fresh->>'receivable_credit')::numeric; refund:=(fresh->>'client_refund_due')::numeric;
 if client_credit>0 then perform platform_ins_cancellation_credit(c.id,client_item.id,client_credit); end if;
 if refund>0 then
  -- The refund item is created after the journal posts, so it can name its booking entry.
  v_items:=v_items||jsonb_build_object('kind','refund','party',client_item.party_account_id,'amount',refund);
  lines:=lines||jsonb_build_array(jsonb_build_object('gl_account_id',c.clearing_gl_account_id,'debit',refund),jsonb_build_object('gl_account_id',(mappings->>'refund_payable')::uuid,'credit',refund));
 end if;
 for line in select value from jsonb_array_elements(fresh->'insurers') loop
  insurer:=(line->>'insurer_id')::uuid; amount:=(line->>'total_insurer_credit')::numeric; claw:=(line->>'commission_clawback')::numeric;
  select id into share_id from ins_policy_insurer_shares where policy_id=p.id and insurer_id=insurer;
  select i.* into item from fin_open_items i join ins_policy_cash_items k on k.id=i.id
   where k.source_id=p.id and k.source_type='ins_policy_insurer' and k.tenant_id=p.tenant_id
    and (k.insurer_share_id=share_id or (k.insurer_share_id is null and insurer=p.insurer_id));
  if not found then raise exception 'INS_CANCELLATION_HISTORY: original insurer item missing'; end if;
  select outstanding_amount into outstanding from fin_open_item_balances where id=item.id;
  applied:=least(outstanding,amount); recovered:=amount-applied;
  select * into ce from ins_commission_entries where policy_id=p.id and insurer_share_id is not distinct from
   (case when exists(select 1 from ins_policy_financial_snapshots where policy_id=p.id and extended) then share_id else null end);
  if (line->>'original_commission')::numeric>0 and (mappings->>'commission_treatment')='deferred' and ce.id is null then raise exception 'INS_CANCELLATION_HISTORY: original commission entry missing'; end if;
  if ce.id is not null and ce.amount<>(line->>'original_commission')::numeric then raise exception 'INS_CANCELLATION_HISTORY: original commission amount differs'; end if;
  remaining:=(line->>'original_commission')::numeric-claw;
  target_status:=case when ce.id is null then 'legacy' when ce.status='transferred' then 'transferred' when ce.status='earned' then 'earned'
   when outstanding-applied=0 and remaining>0 then 'earned' else 'deferred' end;
  transfer_due:=case when ce.status='transferred' then claw else 0 end;
  adjustment_id:=gen_random_uuid();
  insert into ins_cancellation_insurer_adjustments(id,tenant_id,cancellation_id,insurer_id,insurer_share_id,original_item_id,original_commission_entry_id,original_commission_state,
   return_premium,commission_clawback,pass_through_refund,total_insurer_credit,payable_credit,recovery_due,retained_commission,retained_commission_status,transferred_clawback_due)
  values(adjustment_id,c.tenant_id,c.id,insurer,share_id,item.id,ce.id,case when ce.id is not null then to_jsonb(ce) else null end,
   (line->>'return_premium')::numeric,claw,(line->>'pass_through_refund')::numeric,amount,applied,recovered,remaining,target_status,transfer_due);
  if applied>0 then perform platform_ins_cancellation_credit(c.id,item.id,applied); end if;
  if recovered>0 then
   v_items:=v_items||jsonb_build_object('kind','recovery','party',item.party_account_id,'adjustment',adjustment_id,'amount',recovered);
   lines:=lines||jsonb_build_array(jsonb_build_object('gl_account_id',(mappings->>'ar')::uuid,'debit',recovered),jsonb_build_object('gl_account_id',c.clearing_gl_account_id,'credit',recovered));
  end if;
  if claw>0 then
   lines:=lines||jsonb_build_object('gl_account_id',(mappings->>case when ce.id is not null and ce.status='deferred' then 'unearned' else 'income' end)::uuid,'debit',claw);
   debit_total:=debit_total+claw;
  end if;
  if ce.status='deferred' and target_status='earned' and remaining>0 then
   if v_foreign then
    -- Recognition releases the unearned commission at the rate the policy was bound at,
    -- the same convention as the settlement-triggered recognition.
    if v_bound_rate is null then
     if p.journal_entry_id is null then raise exception 'INS_CANCELLATION_RATE: the policy has no bind journal, so its commission rate is unknown'; end if;
     select l.fx_rate into v_bound_rate from journal_entry_lines l
      where l.journal_entry_id=p.journal_entry_id and l.tenant_id=p.tenant_id and l.currency=p.currency
        and l.gl_account_id=(mappings->>'unearned')::uuid
      order by l.id limit 1;
     if v_bound_rate is null then raise exception 'INS_CANCELLATION_RATE: the bind journal has no % line on unearned commission, so the booked rate is unknown', p.currency; end if;
    end if;
    recognition_lines:=recognition_lines||jsonb_build_array(
     jsonb_build_object('gl_account_id',(mappings->>'unearned')::uuid,'debit',remaining)||jsonb_build_object('currency',p.currency,'rate_from_entry',p.journal_entry_id,'fx_rate',v_bound_rate),
     jsonb_build_object('gl_account_id',(mappings->>'income')::uuid,'credit',remaining)||jsonb_build_object('currency',p.currency,'rate_from_entry',p.journal_entry_id,'fx_rate',v_bound_rate));
   else
    recognition_lines:=recognition_lines||jsonb_build_array(jsonb_build_object('gl_account_id',(mappings->>'unearned')::uuid,'debit',remaining),jsonb_build_object('gl_account_id',(mappings->>'income')::uuid,'credit',remaining));
   end if;
   recognized:=recognized+remaining;
  end if;
 end loop;
 for charge in select value from jsonb_array_elements(fresh->'charges') where value->>'kind'='broker_fee' loop
  if (charge->>'refund_base')::numeric>0 then
   lines:=lines||jsonb_build_object('gl_account_id',(charge->>'income_gl_account_id')::uuid,'debit',(charge->>'refund_base')::numeric);
   debit_total:=debit_total+(charge->>'refund_base')::numeric;
  end if;
  if (charge->>'refund_vat')::numeric>0 then
   lines:=lines||jsonb_build_object('gl_account_id',(mappings->>'vat')::uuid,'debit',(charge->>'refund_vat')::numeric);
   debit_total:=debit_total+(charge->>'refund_vat')::numeric;
  end if;
 end loop;
 if debit_total>0 then lines:=lines||jsonb_build_object('gl_account_id',c.clearing_gl_account_id,'credit',debit_total); end if;
 if jsonb_array_length(lines)>0 then
  if v_foreign then
   -- Every adjustment leg posts in the policy's currency at the cancellation-date rate,
   -- so the clearing account nets to zero in the currency and in base.
   lines:=(select jsonb_agg(value || jsonb_build_object('currency',p.currency)) from jsonb_array_elements(lines));
  end if;
  je:=post_journal_entry(c.tenant_id,'ins_policy_cancellation',c.id,current_date,'Cancellation '||c.cancellation_no||': '||c.reason,lines);
  insert into ins_cancellation_journals values(c.tenant_id,c.id,je.id,'cancellation_adjustments');
  for v_todo in select * from jsonb_array_elements(v_items) loop
   if v_todo->>'kind'='refund' then
    created_item:=fin_create_open_item_fx(c.tenant_id,(v_todo->>'party')::uuid,'ap_control','ins_cancellation_client_refund',c.id,c.cancellation_no,current_date,current_date,(v_todo->>'amount')::numeric,'Client refund due: '||p.policy_no,p.currency,je.id);
    update ins_policy_cancellations set refund_item_id=created_item.id where id=c.id;
   else
    created_item:=fin_create_open_item_fx(c.tenant_id,(v_todo->>'party')::uuid,'ar_control','ins_cancellation_insurer_recovery',(v_todo->>'adjustment')::uuid,c.cancellation_no,current_date,current_date,(v_todo->>'amount')::numeric,'Insurer recovery: '||p.policy_no,p.currency,je.id);
    update ins_cancellation_insurer_adjustments set recovery_item_id=created_item.id where id=(v_todo->>'adjustment')::uuid;
   end if;
  end loop;
 end if;
 if recognized>0 then
  declare recognition journal_entries%rowtype; begin
   recognition:=post_journal_entry(c.tenant_id,'ins_policy_cancellation',gen_random_uuid(),current_date,'Retained commission earned: '||c.cancellation_no,recognition_lines);
   insert into ins_cancellation_journals values(c.tenant_id,c.id,recognition.id,'retained_commission_recognition');
  end;
 end if;
 update ins_policy_cancellations set status='posted',journal_entry_id=je.id,operation_txid=null where id=c.id returning * into c;
 perform set_config('ins.via_rpc','on',true);
 update ins_policies set status='cancelled' where id=p.id;
 update ins_policy_cash_requests set status='cancelled',checker_id=null,checked_at=now(),decision_reason='Policy cancellation posted: '||c.cancellation_no
  where policy_id=p.id and status='pending';
 insert into ins_cancellation_events(tenant_id,cancellation_id,event,actor_id,detail) values(c.tenant_id,c.id,'posted',auth.uid(),jsonb_build_object('review_note',p_review_note,'manual_exception_accepted',p_accept_manual_exception,'cash_authorized',false));
 return c;
end $function$
-- FN ins_decide_policy_cash_request(uuid,text,text)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.ins_decide_policy_cash_request(p_request_id uuid, p_decision text, p_reason text DEFAULT NULL::text)
 RETURNS ins_policy_cash_requests
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
declare q ins_policy_cash_requests%rowtype; i fin_open_items%rowtype; s fin_settlements%rowtype; pid uuid;
begin
  perform ins_policy_cash_require_finance();
  if p_decision is null or p_decision not in ('approve','reject','cancel') then raise exception 'INS_CASH_INPUT: invalid decision'; end if;
  select policy_id into pid from ins_policy_cash_requests where id=p_request_id and tenant_id=get_my_tenant_id();
  if not found then raise exception 'INS_NOT_FOUND: cash request'; end if;
  perform id from ins_policies where id=pid and tenant_id=get_my_tenant_id() for update;
  select * into q from ins_policy_cash_requests where id=p_request_id and tenant_id=get_my_tenant_id() for update;
  if p_decision='cancel' then
    if q.maker_id<>auth.uid() then raise exception 'INS_CASH_MAKER: only the maker can cancel a pending request'; end if;
  elsif q.maker_id=auth.uid() then raise exception 'INS_CASH_SELF_APPROVAL: another Finance user must check the request';
  end if;
  -- A lost response may be retried by the same checker, without reposting.
  if q.status='posted' and p_decision='approve' and q.checker_id=auth.uid() then return q; end if;
  if q.status<>'pending' then raise exception 'INS_CASH_DECIDED: request is no longer pending'; end if;
  if p_decision in ('reject','cancel') then
    if p_reason is null or length(btrim(p_reason))<5 then raise exception 'INS_CASH_REASON: at least five characters are required'; end if;
    update ins_policy_cash_requests set status=case p_decision when 'reject' then 'rejected' else 'cancelled' end,
      checker_id=case when p_decision='reject' then auth.uid() else null end,checked_at=now(),decision_reason=btrim(p_reason)
      where id=q.id returning * into q;
    return q;
  end if;
  i := ins_policy_cash_validate(q.policy_id,q.kind,q.bank_account_id,q.amount,q.cash_date,q.insurer_share_id);
  update ins_policy_cash_requests set status='posting',checker_id=auth.uid(),checked_at=now(),operation_txid=txid_current()
    where id=q.id;
  s := platform_fin_record_settlement_impl(q.tenant_id,q.bank_account_id,
    case q.kind when 'receipt' then 'in' else 'out' end,i.party_account_id,q.amount,q.cash_date,
    jsonb_build_array(jsonb_build_object('open_item_id',i.id,'amount',q.amount)),q.reference,q.notes);
  -- Allocation trigger binds the exact settlement to this approval context.
  update ins_policy_cash_requests set status='posted',operation_txid=null
    where id=q.id and settlement_id=s.id returning * into q;
  if not found then raise exception 'INS_CASH_CONTEXT: approved allocation was not recorded'; end if;
  return q;
end $function$
-- FN ins_decide_policy_endorsement(uuid,text,text,boolean)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.ins_decide_policy_endorsement(p_endorsement_id uuid, p_decision text, p_review_note text, p_accept_manual_exception boolean DEFAULT false)
 RETURNS ins_policy_endorsements
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
declare
  c ins_policy_endorsements%rowtype;
  p ins_policies%rowtype;
  fresh jsonb; mappings jsonb; share jsonb;
  insurer uuid; v_share_id uuid;
  delta numeric; v_claw numeric; net numeric;
  v_ar uuid; v_payable uuid; v_unearned uuid; v_income uuid; v_clearing uuid;
  v_client_acc uuid; v_ins_acc uuid;
  item fin_open_items%rowtype; created_item fin_open_items%rowtype; note fin_credit_notes%rowtype;
  ce record;
  v_claw_left numeric; v_take numeric; v_claw_lines jsonb := '[]'::jsonb; v_debit_total numeric := 0;
  v_add_lines jsonb := '[]'::jsonb; v_add_items jsonb := '[]'::jsonb;
  v_recos jsonb := '[]'::jsonb; v_todo jsonb;
  v_credit_notes jsonb := '[]'::jsonb; v_new_entries jsonb := '[]'::jsonb;
  v_outstanding numeric; v_applied numeric; v_recovered numeric;
  note_id uuid;
  je journal_entries%rowtype; je_adj uuid := null; je_add uuid := null;
  v_first_premium_item uuid := null;
  v_desc text;
begin
  perform ins_policy_cash_require_finance();
  if p_decision not in ('approve','reject') or p_decision is null
     or p_review_note is null or length(btrim(p_review_note)) not between 5 and 2000 then
    raise exception 'INS_ENDORSEMENT_INPUT: decision and detailed Finance review note required';
  end if;
  select policy_id into p.id from ins_policy_endorsements
   where id = p_endorsement_id and tenant_id = get_my_tenant_id();
  if not found then raise exception 'INS_NOT_FOUND: endorsement'; end if;
  select * into p from ins_policies where id = p.id and tenant_id = get_my_tenant_id() for update;
  select * into c from ins_policy_endorsements where id = p_endorsement_id for update;
  if p.status not in ('active','renewed') then
    raise exception 'INS_ENDORSEMENT_STATE: the policy is %; only a bound policy endorses', p.status;
  end if;
  if c.prepared_by = auth.uid() or c.proposed_by = auth.uid() then
    raise exception 'INS_ENDORSEMENT_SELF_APPROVAL: an independent Finance checker is required';
  end if;
  -- An approve retry on a posted case is idempotent only with the same payload.
  if c.status = 'posted' and p_decision = 'approve' and c.checked_by = auth.uid() then
    if c.review_note is distinct from btrim(p_review_note)
       or c.manual_exception_accepted is distinct from coalesce(p_accept_manual_exception, false) then
      raise exception 'INS_ENDORSEMENT_RETRY_CONFLICT: different approval payload';
    end if;
    return c;
  end if;
  if p_decision = 'reject' then
    if c.status not in ('proposed','prepared') then
      raise exception 'INS_ENDORSEMENT_STATE: open proposal required';
    end if;
    update ins_policy_endorsements
       set status = 'rejected', checked_by = auth.uid(), checked_at = now(),
           review_note = btrim(p_review_note)
     where id = c.id
     returning * into c;
    insert into ins_policy_endorsement_events(tenant_id, endorsement_id, event, actor_id, detail)
    values (c.tenant_id, c.id, 'rejected', auth.uid(), jsonb_build_object('note', p_review_note));
    return c;
  end if;
  if c.status <> 'prepared' then
    raise exception 'INS_ENDORSEMENT_STATE: Finance preparation required before approval';
  end if;
  -- Serialize with cash approvals, claims, credits and commission transfers.
  perform m.id from fin_open_items m join ins_policy_cash_items k on k.id = m.id
   where k.source_id = p.id and k.tenant_id = p.tenant_id order by m.id for update of m;
  perform e.id from ins_commission_entries e where e.policy_id = p.id order by e.id for update;
  fresh := ins_preview_policy_endorsement(p.id, c.effective_date, c.insurer_terms, c.changes)
           || ins_endorsement_review_position(p.id);
  if exists(select 1 from accounting_periods
             where tenant_id = p.tenant_id and status = 'closed'
               and current_date between period_start and period_end) then
    raise exception 'INS_ENDORSEMENT_PERIOD: current accounting period closed';
  end if;
  -- live_endorsements carries the case's own lifecycle state (proposed ->
  -- prepared between maker and checker), not amounts; the one-live rule means
  -- it cannot hide a competitor. Everything else in the recompute must match.
  if (fresh - 'generated_at' - 'live_endorsements') is distinct from (c.calculation - 'generated_at' - 'live_endorsements') then
    raise exception 'INS_ENDORSEMENT_STALE: amounts or collection position changed; Finance maker must refresh preparation';
  end if;
  -- requires_review lives per share (a share delta bigger than that share's
  -- whole premium); any share flagging demands the checker's explicit nod.
  if exists (select 1 from jsonb_array_elements(fresh -> 'shares') sh
              where (sh ->> 'requires_review')::boolean)
     and not coalesce(p_accept_manual_exception, false) then
    raise exception 'INS_ENDORSEMENT_EXCEPTION: explicit independent Finance acceptance of the reviewed calculation required';
  end if;
  mappings := ins_endorsement_mappings(p.id, (c.accounting_mappings ->> 'clearing')::uuid);
  if mappings is distinct from c.accounting_mappings then
    raise exception 'INS_ENDORSEMENT_STALE: accounting mappings changed';
  end if;
  update ins_policy_endorsements
     set status = 'posting', checked_by = auth.uid(), checked_at = now(),
         review_note = btrim(p_review_note),
         manual_exception_accepted = coalesce(p_accept_manual_exception, false),
         posting_date = current_date,
         endorsement_no = next_doc_number(c.tenant_id, 'ins_policy_endorsement', 'END'),
         operation_txid = txid_current()
   where id = c.id
   returning * into c;
  v_ar       := (mappings ->> 'ar')::uuid;
  v_payable  := (mappings ->> 'payable')::uuid;
  v_unearned := (mappings ->> 'unearned')::uuid;
  v_income   := (mappings ->> 'income')::uuid;
  v_clearing := (mappings ->> 'clearing')::uuid;
  v_client_acc := ins_ensure_client_account(p.client_id);
  v_desc := 'Endorsement ' || c.endorsement_no || ' on ' || p.policy_no;
  for share in select value from jsonb_array_elements(fresh -> 'shares') loop
    insurer := (share ->> 'insurer_id')::uuid;
    delta   := (share ->> 'premium_delta')::numeric;
    continue when delta = 0;
    v_claw  := (share ->> 'commission_delta')::numeric;
    select id into v_share_id from ins_policy_insurer_shares
     where policy_id = p.id and tenant_id = p.tenant_id and insurer_id = insurer
     order by id limit 1;
    v_ins_acc := ins_ensure_insurer_account(insurer);
    if delta > 0 then
      ---------------------------------------------------------------
      -- Additional premium: AR item + insurer-share item + a new
      -- deferred commission entry tied to the endorsement's own item.
      ---------------------------------------------------------------
      net := delta - v_claw;
      if net <= 0 then
        raise exception 'INS_ENDORSEMENT_COMMISSION: commission would leave nothing to remit to the insurer';
      end if;
      v_add_lines := v_add_lines || jsonb_build_array(
        jsonb_build_object('gl_account_id', v_ar, 'debit', delta, 'currency', p.currency, 'description', v_desc),
        jsonb_build_object('gl_account_id', v_payable, 'credit', net, 'currency', p.currency, 'description', v_desc)
      );
      if v_claw > 0 then
        v_add_lines := v_add_lines || jsonb_build_object(
          'gl_account_id', v_unearned, 'credit', v_claw, 'currency', p.currency,
          'description', v_desc || ' - unearned commission');
      end if;
      v_add_items := v_add_items || jsonb_build_object(
        'insurer_id', insurer, 'share_id', v_share_id, 'premium', delta,
        'net', net, 'commission', v_claw);
    else
      ---------------------------------------------------------------
      -- Return premium: credit notes at the booked rates, capped at
      -- each original item's outstanding; honest clawback of the
      -- commission (deferred pool first, then what is earned).
      ---------------------------------------------------------------
      v_claw := -v_claw;  -- the claw is a positive amount on a return
      -- Client side: the original premium item is policy-level.
      select * into item from fin_open_items
       where tenant_id = p.tenant_id and source_type = 'ins_policy_premium' and source_id = p.id;
      if not found then raise exception 'INS_ENDORSEMENT_HISTORY: original premium item missing'; end if;
      select outstanding_amount into v_outstanding from fin_open_item_balances where id = item.id;
      v_applied := least(v_outstanding, -delta);
      if v_applied > 0 then
        -- The 3d-1 note machinery: the note takes the item's currency; in a
        -- foreign currency the control line rides the item's booked rate, the
        -- clearing offset prices at the credit-date rate, and the difference
        -- books as realised result. A functional note is unchanged (rate 1).
        note := platform_fin_raise_credit_note_impl(c.tenant_id, item.control_role, item.party_account_id,
          v_applied, current_date, v_clearing,
          jsonb_build_array(jsonb_build_object('open_item_id', item.id, 'amount', v_applied)),
          c.reason, c.endorsement_no);
        note_id := note.id;
        v_credit_notes := v_credit_notes || jsonb_build_object(
          'note_id', note_id, 'open_item_id', item.id, 'amount', v_applied, 'side', item.side);
      end if;
      v_recovered := (-delta) - v_applied;
      if v_recovered > 0 then
        -- The return exceeds the billed premium still outstanding: the client
        -- owes the difference back (the cancellation engine's recovery item).
        v_recos := v_recos || jsonb_build_object(
          'party', item.party_account_id, 'role', 'ar_control', 'source_type',
          'ins_policy_endorsement_recovery', 'source_id', c.id, 'amount', v_recovered);
      end if;
      -- Insurer side: mirror the share's net reduction against its item.
      net := (-delta) - v_claw;
      -- The ORIGINAL bind items: pinned by their booking journal (the policy's
      -- bind entry), so a prior additional endorsement's items never match.
      if v_share_id is not null then
        select i.* into item from fin_open_items i join ins_policy_cash_items k on k.id = i.id
         where k.source_id = p.id and k.source_type = 'ins_policy_insurer'
           and k.insurer_share_id = v_share_id and k.tenant_id = p.tenant_id
           and i.journal_entry_id = p.journal_entry_id;
      else
        select i.* into item from fin_open_items i join ins_policy_cash_items k on k.id = i.id
         where k.source_id = p.id and k.source_type = 'ins_policy_insurer'
           and k.insurer_share_id is null and i.party_account_id = v_ins_acc
           and i.journal_entry_id = p.journal_entry_id and k.tenant_id = p.tenant_id;
      end if;
      if not found then raise exception 'INS_ENDORSEMENT_HISTORY: original insurer item missing'; end if;
      select outstanding_amount into v_outstanding from fin_open_item_balances where id = item.id;
      v_applied := least(v_outstanding, net);
      if v_applied > 0 then
        note := platform_fin_raise_credit_note_impl(c.tenant_id, item.control_role, item.party_account_id,
          v_applied, current_date, v_clearing,
          jsonb_build_array(jsonb_build_object('open_item_id', item.id, 'amount', v_applied)),
          c.reason, c.endorsement_no);
        note_id := note.id;
        v_credit_notes := v_credit_notes || jsonb_build_object(
          'note_id', note_id, 'open_item_id', item.id, 'amount', v_applied, 'side', item.side);
      end if;
      v_recovered := net - v_applied;
      if v_recovered > 0 then
        v_recos := v_recos || jsonb_build_object(
          'party', item.party_account_id, 'role', 'ar_control', 'source_type',
          'ins_policy_endorsement_recovery_insurer', 'source_id', c.id, 'amount', v_recovered);
      end if;
      -- Commission clawback: the deferred pool first, then earned, then
      -- transferred; the rows are never mutated, the ledger records the take.
      v_claw_left := v_claw;
      for ce in
        select e.id, e.amount, e.status,
               (e.amount - coalesce((select sum(x.amount) from ins_endorsement_clawbacks x
                                      where x.commission_entry_id = e.id and x.tenant_id = e.tenant_id), 0)) as available
          from ins_commission_entries e
         where e.policy_id = p.id and e.tenant_id = p.tenant_id
           and case when v_share_id is not null
                    then e.insurer_share_id = v_share_id
                    else e.insurer_share_id is null end
         order by case e.status when 'deferred' then 0 when 'earned' then 1 else 2 end,
                  e.created_at, e.id
      loop
        exit when v_claw_left <= 0;
        v_take := least(v_claw_left, greatest(ce.available, 0));
        continue when v_take <= 0;
        v_claw_lines := v_claw_lines || jsonb_build_object(
          'gl_account_id', case when ce.status = 'deferred' then v_unearned else v_income end,
          'debit', v_take, 'currency', p.currency,
          'description', v_desc || ' - commission clawback');
        insert into ins_endorsement_clawbacks(id, tenant_id, endorsement_id, commission_entry_id, amount)
        values (gen_random_uuid(), c.tenant_id, c.id, ce.id, v_take);
        v_claw_left := v_claw_left - v_take;
      end loop;
      if v_claw_left > 0 then
        raise exception 'INS_ENDORSEMENT_HISTORY: the policy''s commission cannot cover this clawback';
      end if;
    end if;
  end loop;
  -- The return-side adjustments journal: clawback debits, clearing balance.
  if jsonb_array_length(v_claw_lines) > 0 then
    v_debit_total := (select coalesce(sum((l ->> 'debit')::numeric), 0) from jsonb_array_elements(v_claw_lines) l);
    -- The balancing clearing line joins the array FIRST so the E3 mapping
    -- below stamps it too.
    v_claw_lines := v_claw_lines || jsonb_build_object('gl_account_id', v_clearing, 'credit', v_debit_total);
    if p.currency is distinct from fin_ledger_currency(c.tenant_id) then
      -- E3/3d-3 convention: every adjustment leg rides the policy's currency at
      -- the posting-date rate, so the clearing account nets to zero in the
      -- currency and in base (the balancing line must not fall back to the
      -- ledger currency).
