      v_claw_lines := (select jsonb_agg(value || jsonb_build_object('currency', p.currency))
                         from jsonb_array_elements(v_claw_lines) value);
    end if;
    je := post_journal_entry(c.tenant_id, 'ins_policy_endorsement', c.id, current_date,
      v_desc || ': commission clawback', v_claw_lines);
    je_adj := je.id;
  end if;
  -- Recovery items name the adjustments journal (created after posting, the
  -- cancellation pattern).
  for v_todo in select * from jsonb_array_elements(v_recos) loop
    created_item := fin_create_open_item_fx(c.tenant_id, (v_todo ->> 'party')::uuid,
      (v_todo ->> 'role'), (v_todo ->> 'source_type'), (v_todo ->> 'source_id')::uuid,
      c.endorsement_no, current_date, current_date, (v_todo ->> 'amount')::numeric,
      'Endorsement recovery: ' || p.policy_no, p.currency, je_adj);
  end loop;
  -- The additional-side journal, then one client item for the whole
  -- endorsement, and per-insurer payable items. Each insurer item is keyed by
  -- its deferred commission entry (created first, naming the item once it
  -- exists): when that item is fully paid, the recognition trigger earns
  -- exactly that entry.
  if jsonb_array_length(v_add_lines) > 0 then
    je := post_journal_entry(c.tenant_id, 'ins_policy_endorsement', c.id, current_date, v_desc, v_add_lines);
    je_add := je.id;
    created_item := fin_create_open_item_fx(c.tenant_id, v_client_acc, 'ar_control',
      'ins_policy_endorsement', c.id, c.endorsement_no, current_date, current_date,
      (select sum((v ->> 'premium')::numeric) from jsonb_array_elements(v_add_items) v),
      'Endorsement premium due: ' || p.policy_no, p.currency, je_add);
    v_first_premium_item := created_item.id;
  end if;
  for v_todo in select * from jsonb_array_elements(v_add_items) loop
    -- The commission entry is born first (open items are immutable, so the
    -- item must carry its final source at birth); the item then carries the
    -- entry's id as its source, and the entry names the item whose full
    -- payment earns it.
    if (v_todo ->> 'commission')::numeric > 0 then
      insert into ins_commission_entries(id, tenant_id, policy_id, amount, status, insurer_share_id)
      values (gen_random_uuid(), c.tenant_id, p.id, (v_todo ->> 'commission')::numeric, 'deferred',
              (v_todo ->> 'share_id')::uuid)
      returning id into note_id;
      v_new_entries := v_new_entries || to_jsonb(note_id);
    else
      note_id := null;
    end if;
    created_item := fin_create_open_item_fx(c.tenant_id,
      ins_ensure_insurer_account((v_todo ->> 'insurer_id')::uuid), 'insurer_payable',
      'ins_policy_endorsement_insurer', note_id, c.endorsement_no, current_date, current_date,
      (v_todo ->> 'net')::numeric, 'Net premium payable to insurer: ' || p.policy_no, p.currency, je_add);
    if note_id is not null then
      update ins_commission_entries set source_open_item_id = created_item.id where id = note_id;
    end if;
  end loop;
  update ins_policy_endorsements
     set status = 'posted',
         journal_entry_id = coalesce(je_adj, je_add),
         premium_item_id = v_first_premium_item,
         credit_note_ids = v_credit_notes,
         commission_entry_ids = v_new_entries,
         operation_txid = null
   where id = c.id
   returning * into c;
  insert into ins_policy_endorsement_events(tenant_id, endorsement_id, event, actor_id, detail)
  values (c.tenant_id, c.id, 'posted', auth.uid(), jsonb_build_object(
    'review_note', p_review_note,
    'manual_exception_accepted', coalesce(p_accept_manual_exception, false),
    'adjustment_journal', je_adj, 'additional_journal', je_add,
    'credit_notes', v_credit_notes));
  return c;
end $function$
-- FN ins_decide_policy(uuid,text,text)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.ins_decide_policy(p_policy_id uuid, p_decision text, p_note text DEFAULT NULL::text)
 RETURNS ins_policies
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
declare
  v_tenant uuid := public.get_my_tenant_id();
  v_pol public.ins_policies;
begin
  if v_tenant is null or not public.can_manage_insurance() then
    raise exception 'INS_ACCESS_DENIED' using errcode = '42501';
  end if;
  if p_decision not in ('approved', 'rejected') then
    raise exception 'INS_BAD_DECISION';
  end if;
  select * into v_pol from public.ins_policies
  where tenant_id = v_tenant and id = p_policy_id for update;
  if not found then raise exception 'INS_NOT_FOUND: policy'; end if;
  if v_pol.status <> 'pending_approval' then
    raise exception 'INS_NOT_PENDING: policy % is %', v_pol.policy_no, v_pol.status;
  end if;
  -- segregation of duties: the submitter cannot approve their own policy
  if exists (
    select 1 from public.ins_policy_approvals a
    where a.tenant_id = v_tenant and a.policy_id = p_policy_id
      and a.action = 'submitted' and a.actor_id = auth.uid()
  ) then
    raise exception 'INS_SELF_APPROVAL: the submitter cannot decide their own policy';
  end if;
  set local ins.via_rpc = 'on';
  update public.ins_policies
  set status = case when p_decision = 'approved' then 'approved' else 'draft' end,
      updated_at = now()
  where tenant_id = v_tenant and id = p_policy_id
  returning * into v_pol;
  insert into public.ins_policy_approvals (tenant_id, policy_id, action, actor_id, note)
  values (v_tenant, p_policy_id, p_decision, auth.uid(), p_note);
  reset ins.via_rpc;
  return v_pol;
end;
$function$
-- FN ins_decide_quote(uuid,text,text)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.ins_decide_quote(p_quote_id uuid, p_decision text, p_insurer_ref text DEFAULT NULL::text)
 RETURNS ins_quotes
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
declare
  v_tenant uuid := public.get_my_tenant_id();
  v_quote public.ins_quotes;
begin
  if v_tenant is null or not public.can_manage_insurance() then
    raise exception 'INS_ACCESS_DENIED' using errcode = '42501';
  end if;
  if p_decision not in ('accepted', 'declined') then
    raise exception 'INS_QUOTE_BAD_DECISION';
  end if;
  select * into v_quote from public.ins_quotes
  where tenant_id = v_tenant and id = p_quote_id;
  if v_quote.id is null then
    raise exception 'INS_QUOTE_NOT_FOUND';
  end if;
  if v_quote.status <> 'submitted' then
    raise exception 'INS_QUOTE_NOT_SUBMITTED';
  end if;
  set local ins.via_rpc = 'on';
  update public.ins_quotes
  set status = p_decision,
      insurer_ref = coalesce(p_insurer_ref, insurer_ref),
      decided_at = now(),
      decided_by = auth.uid(),
      updated_at = now()
  where tenant_id = v_tenant and id = p_quote_id
  returning * into v_quote;
  reset ins.via_rpc;
  return v_quote;
end;
$function$
-- FN ins_end_insurer_product_rate(uuid,date)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.ins_end_insurer_product_rate(p_id uuid, p_effective_to date)
 RETURNS ins_insurer_product_rates
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
declare
  v_row ins_insurer_product_rates%rowtype;
begin
  select * into v_row from ins_insurer_product_rates
  where id = p_id and tenant_id = get_my_tenant_id()
  for update;
  if not found then
    raise exception 'INS_RATE_NOT_FOUND: rate not found';
  end if;
  if not can_manage_insurance() then
    raise exception 'INS_FORBIDDEN: only insurance admins and managers end a commission rate' using errcode = '42501';
  end if;
  if v_row.status <> 'approved' then
    raise exception 'INS_RATE_NOT_APPROVED: only an approved rate can be end-dated (this one is %)', v_row.status;
  end if;
  if p_effective_to is null or p_effective_to < v_row.effective_from then
    raise exception 'INS_RATE_WINDOW: the new end date cannot be empty or before the rate starts (%)', v_row.effective_from;
  end if;
  perform set_config('ins.via_rpc', 'on', true);
  update ins_insurer_product_rates
     set effective_to = p_effective_to
   where id = p_id
   returning * into v_row;
  perform set_config('ins.via_rpc', 'off', true);
  return v_row;
end;
$function$
-- FN ins_endorsement_cross_guard()
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.ins_endorsement_cross_guard()
 RETURNS trigger
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
begin
  if tg_table_name = 'ins_policy_cancellations' then
    if new.status in ('proposed','prepared','posting') and exists (
      select 1 from ins_policy_endorsements
       where policy_id = new.policy_id and status in ('proposed','prepared','posting')
    ) then
      raise exception 'INS_ENDORSEMENT_LIVE: finish or withdraw the live endorsement before cancelling this policy';
    end if;
    if new.status in ('proposed','prepared','posting') and exists (
      select 1 from ins_policy_endorsements
       where policy_id = new.policy_id and status = 'posted'
    ) then
      raise exception 'INS_ENDORSEMENT_POSTED: this policy has posted endorsements; cancelling it needs the endorsement-items extension (E4)';
    end if;
  else
    if new.status in ('proposed','prepared','posting') and exists (
      select 1 from ins_policy_cancellations
       where policy_id = new.policy_id and status in ('proposed','prepared','posting','posted')
    ) then
      raise exception 'INS_CANCELLATION_LIVE: this policy has a cancellation in flight; endorse it another day';
    end if;
  end if;
  return new;
end $function$
-- FN ins_endorsement_mappings(uuid,uuid)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.ins_endorsement_mappings(p_policy_id uuid, p_clearing_gl_account_id uuid)
 RETURNS jsonb
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
declare
  v_tenant uuid;
  v_ar uuid; v_payable uuid; v_unearned uuid; v_income uuid;
begin
  select tenant_id into v_tenant from ins_policies where id = p_policy_id;
  if not found then raise exception 'INS_NOT_FOUND: policy'; end if;
  v_ar       := get_posting_account(v_tenant, 'ar_control');
  v_payable  := get_posting_account(v_tenant, 'insurer_payable');
  v_unearned := get_posting_account(v_tenant, 'unearned_commission');
  v_income   := get_posting_account(v_tenant, 'commission_income');
  if v_ar is null or v_payable is null or v_unearned is null or v_income is null then
    raise exception 'INS_ENDORSEMENT_POSTING_RULE_MISSING: the chart of accounts needs ar_control, insurer_payable, unearned_commission and commission_income';
  end if;
  if p_clearing_gl_account_id is null or not exists (
    select 1 from gl_accounts
     where id = p_clearing_gl_account_id and tenant_id = v_tenant
  ) then
    raise exception 'INS_ENDORSEMENT_CLEARING: choose a clearing account from this tenant''s chart of accounts';
  end if;
  return jsonb_build_object(
    'ar', v_ar, 'payable', v_payable, 'unearned', v_unearned,
    'income', v_income, 'clearing', p_clearing_gl_account_id);
end $function$
-- FN ins_endorsement_review_position(uuid)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.ins_endorsement_review_position(p_policy_id uuid)
 RETURNS jsonb
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 STABLE SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
declare
  p ins_policies%rowtype;
  v_gross numeric;
  v_commission numeric;
  v_sum_insured numeric;
  v_live jsonb;
  v_posted integer;
  v_cxl_live boolean;
  r record;
  c record;
begin
  if auth.uid() is null or not (can_access_insurance() or is_finance_team_member('finance')) then
    raise exception 'INS_FORBIDDEN' using errcode = '42501';
  end if;
  select * into p from ins_policies where id = p_policy_id and tenant_id = get_my_tenant_id();
  if not found then
    raise exception 'INS_NOT_FOUND: policy';
  end if;
  v_gross := p.gross_premium;
  v_commission := p.commission_amount;
  v_sum_insured := p.sum_insured;
  v_posted := 0;
  for r in
    select calculation
      from ins_policy_endorsements
     where policy_id = p.id and tenant_id = p.tenant_id and status = 'posted'
     order by posting_date nulls last, proposed_at
  loop
    v_posted := v_posted + 1;
    v_gross := v_gross + coalesce((r.calculation ->> 'premium_delta')::numeric, 0);
    v_commission := v_commission + coalesce((r.calculation ->> 'commission_delta')::numeric, 0);
    -- The latest posted change to sum_insured wins.
    if r.calculation -> 'changes' <> '[]'::jsonb then
      for c in select * from jsonb_array_elements(r.calculation -> 'changes') where value ->> 'field' = 'sum_insured' loop
        v_sum_insured := (c.value ->> 'after')::numeric;
      end loop;
    end if;
  end loop;
  select jsonb_agg(to_jsonb(x)) into v_live
    from ins_policy_endorsements x
   where x.policy_id = p.id and x.tenant_id = p.tenant_id and x.status in ('proposed','prepared');
  select exists (
    select 1 from ins_policy_cancellations
     where policy_id = p.id and tenant_id = p.tenant_id and status in ('proposed','prepared','posting','posted')
  ) into v_cxl_live;
  return jsonb_build_object(
    'policy_id', p.id, 'policy_no', p.policy_no, 'currency', p.currency,
    'bound_gross_premium', p.gross_premium,
    'bound_commission_amount', p.commission_amount,
    'current_gross_premium', v_gross,
    'current_commission_amount', v_commission,
    'current_sum_insured', v_sum_insured,
    'posted_endorsements', v_posted,
    'live_endorsements', coalesce(v_live, '[]'::jsonb),
    'cancellation_in_flight', v_cxl_live);
end $function$
-- FN ins_ensure_client_account(uuid)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.ins_ensure_client_account(p_ins_client_id uuid)
 RETURNS uuid
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
declare
  v_client ins_clients%rowtype;
  v_name   text;
  v_acc    uuid;
begin
  select * into v_client from ins_clients where id = p_ins_client_id for update;
  if v_client.party_account_id is not null then
    return v_client.party_account_id;
  end if;
  select name into v_name from bd_clients where id = v_client.client_id;
  insert into accounts (tenant_id, account_code, name, account_type, is_active)
  values (
    v_client.tenant_id,
    next_doc_number(v_client.tenant_id, 'INS_CLIENT_ACCOUNT', 'CLI', 5),
    v_name, 'client', true
  )
  returning id into v_acc;
  perform set_config('ins.via_rpc', 'on', true);
  update ins_clients set party_account_id = v_acc where id = v_client.id;
  return v_acc;
end;
$function$
-- FN ins_ensure_insurer_account(uuid)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.ins_ensure_insurer_account(p_insurer_id uuid)
 RETURNS uuid
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
declare
  v_ins ins_insurers%rowtype;
  v_acc uuid;
begin
  select * into v_ins from ins_insurers where id = p_insurer_id for update;
  if v_ins.party_account_id is not null then
    return v_ins.party_account_id;
  end if;
  insert into accounts (tenant_id, account_code, name, account_type, is_active)
  values (
    v_ins.tenant_id,
    next_doc_number(v_ins.tenant_id, 'INS_INSURER_ACCOUNT', 'INSA', 4),
    v_ins.name, 'vendor', true
  )
  returning id into v_acc;
  perform set_config('ins.via_rpc', 'on', true);
  update ins_insurers set party_account_id = v_acc where id = v_ins.id;
  return v_acc;
end;
$function$
-- FN ins_finance_pending_approvals()
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.ins_finance_pending_approvals()
 RETURNS TABLE(item_type text, item_id uuid, policy_id uuid, policy_no text, client_name text, insurer_name text, currency text, kind text, amount numeric, submitted_at timestamp with time zone, submitted_by_name text, is_mine boolean)
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
begin
  perform ins_policy_cash_require_finance();
  return query
  with pending as (
    select 'premium_cash'::text as p_type, r.id as p_id, r.policy_id as p_policy, r.kind as p_kind, r.amount as p_amount,
           r.created_at as p_at, r.maker_id as p_by, (r.maker_id = auth.uid()) as p_mine
      from ins_policy_cash_requests r
     where r.tenant_id = get_my_tenant_id() and r.status = 'pending'
    union all
    select 'cancellation_cash'::text, c.id, pc.policy_id, c.kind, c.amount,
           c.created_at, c.maker_id, (c.maker_id = auth.uid())
      from ins_cancellation_cash_requests c
      join ins_policy_cancellations pc on pc.id = c.cancellation_id and pc.tenant_id = c.tenant_id
     where c.tenant_id = get_my_tenant_id() and c.status = 'pending'
    union all
    select 'cancellation'::text, x.id, x.policy_id, x.status, null::numeric,
           x.proposed_at, x.proposed_by, (x.proposed_by = auth.uid() or coalesce(x.prepared_by = auth.uid(), false))
      from ins_policy_cancellations x
     where x.tenant_id = get_my_tenant_id() and x.status in ('proposed', 'prepared')
  )
  select pe.p_type, pe.p_id, pe.p_policy, po.policy_no, bc.name, ii.name, po.currency,
         pe.p_kind, pe.p_amount, pe.p_at, coalesce(au.name, 'Unknown'), pe.p_mine
    from pending pe
    join ins_policies po on po.id = pe.p_policy and po.tenant_id = get_my_tenant_id()
    join ins_clients ic on ic.id = po.client_id and ic.tenant_id = po.tenant_id
    join bd_clients bc on bc.id = ic.client_id and bc.tenant_id = ic.tenant_id
    join ins_insurers ii on ii.id = po.insurer_id and ii.tenant_id = po.tenant_id
    left join app_users au on au.id = pe.p_by and au.tenant_id = get_my_tenant_id()
   order by pe.p_at, pe.p_id
   limit 200;
end $function$
-- FN ins_financial_configuration_guard()
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.ins_financial_configuration_guard()
 RETURNS trigger
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
declare pid uuid;
begin
 if tg_table_name='ins_policy_financial_snapshots' then
   if tg_op<>'INSERT' then raise exception 'INS_FINANCIAL_IMMUTABLE: bind snapshot cannot be changed or deleted'; end if;
   return new;
 end if;
 pid:=case when tg_op='DELETE' then old.policy_id else new.policy_id end;
 perform id from ins_policies where id=pid for update;
 if exists(
   select 1 from ins_policies
   where id=pid and status<>'draft'
     -- the bind step of THIS policy may fill in computed amounts while approved
     and not (status='approved' and tg_op='UPDATE'
              and coalesce(current_setting('ins.binding_policy', true), '') = pid::text)
     -- the import commit may write the share rows of a policy it is creating
     and not (tg_op in ('INSERT','UPDATE')
              and coalesce(current_setting('ins.import_commit', true), '') <> ''
              and import_batch_id::text = current_setting('ins.import_commit', true))
 ) then
   raise exception 'INS_FINANCIAL_IMMUTABLE: insurer shares and charge lines are locked after bind';
 end if;
 if tg_op='UPDATE' and (new.policy_id is distinct from old.policy_id or new.tenant_id is distinct from old.tenant_id) then
   raise exception 'INS_FINANCIAL_IMMUTABLE: configuration cannot move between policies';
 end if;
 if tg_op='DELETE' then return old; else return new; end if;
end $function$
-- FN ins_generate_renewal_notices()
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.ins_generate_renewal_notices()
 RETURNS TABLE(notice_id uuid, policy_id uuid)
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
declare
  v_tenant uuid := public.get_my_tenant_id();
  p record;
begin
  if v_tenant is null or not public.can_access_insurance() then
    raise exception 'INS_ACCESS_DENIED' using errcode = '42501';
  end if;
  for p in
    select ip.id as pid
      from public.ins_policies ip
     where ip.tenant_id = v_tenant
       and ip.status in ('active', 'renewed')
       and ip.expiry_date <= current_date + 120
       and not exists (
         select 1 from public.ins_renewal_notices n
         where n.tenant_id = v_tenant and n.policy_id = ip.id
       )
  loop
    set local ins.via_rpc = 'on';
    insert into public.ins_renewal_notices (tenant_id, policy_id, created_by)
    values (v_tenant, p.pid, auth.uid())
    returning id into notice_id;
    reset ins.via_rpc;
    policy_id := p.pid;
    return next;
  end loop;
end;
$function$
-- FN ins_import_commit_policies(uuid)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.ins_import_commit_policies(p_batch_id uuid)
 RETURNS jsonb
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'pg_catalog', 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
declare
  v_tenant   uuid := public.get_my_tenant_id();
  v_batch    public.ins_import_batches;
  r          record;
  v_res      jsonb;
  v_no       text;
  v_policy   uuid;
  v_shares   jsonb;
  v_n_shares integer;
  v_policies integer := 0;
  v_share_rows integer := 0;
  v_totals   jsonb;
begin
  if v_tenant is null or not public.can_manage_insurance() then
    raise exception 'INS_ACCESS_DENIED' using errcode = '42501';
  end if;
  select * into v_batch from public.ins_import_batches
   where id = p_batch_id and tenant_id = v_tenant
   for update;
  if not found then raise exception 'INS_IMPORT_BATCH_NOT_FOUND'; end if;
  if v_batch.status = 'committed' then
    raise exception 'INS_IMPORT_ALREADY_COMMITTED: this batch was committed on %', v_batch.committed_at;
  end if;
  if v_batch.status <> 'validated' then
    raise exception 'INS_IMPORT_NOT_VALIDATED: a % batch cannot be committed; validate it with no errors first', v_batch.status;
  end if;
  if exists (select 1 from public.ins_import_rows where batch_id = p_batch_id and status <> 'valid')
     or not exists (select 1 from public.ins_import_rows where batch_id = p_batch_id and sheet = 'policies') then
    raise exception 'INS_IMPORT_NOT_VALIDATED: every staged row must be valid and the batch needs at least one policy';
  end if;
  perform set_config('ins.via_rpc', 'on', true);
  perform set_config('ins.import_commit', p_batch_id::text, true);
  perform set_config('acct.import_batch', p_batch_id::text, true);
  for r in select * from public.ins_import_rows
            where batch_id = p_batch_id and sheet = 'policies' order by line loop
    v_res := r.resolved;
    v_no  := v_res ->> 'policy_no';
    insert into public.ins_policies (
      tenant_id, policy_no, client_id, insurer_id, product_line_id, status,
      inception_date, expiry_date, sum_insured, currency, gross_premium,
      commission_rate_pct, commission_amount, net_premium_to_insurer,
      bound_at, bound_by, risk_description, notes, import_batch_id, migrated_at)
    values (
      v_tenant, v_no, (v_res ->> 'client_id')::uuid, (v_res ->> 'insurer_id')::uuid,
      (v_res ->> 'product_line_id')::uuid, 'active',
      (v_res ->> 'inception_date')::date, (v_res ->> 'expiry_date')::date,
      (v_res ->> 'sum_insured')::numeric, v_res ->> 'currency', (v_res ->> 'gross_premium')::numeric,
      (v_res ->> 'commission_rate_pct')::numeric, (v_res ->> 'commission_amount')::numeric,
      (v_res ->> 'net_premium')::numeric,
      (v_res ->> 'inception_date')::date, auth.uid(),
      nullif(btrim(r.raw ->> 'risk_description'), ''), nullif(btrim(r.raw ->> 'notes'), ''),
      p_batch_id, now())
    returning id into v_policy;
    v_policies := v_policies + 1;
    -- co-insurance: share rows only when the batch lists shares for this policy
    select jsonb_agg(x.resolved order by x.line), count(*)
      into v_shares, v_n_shares
      from public.ins_import_rows x
     where x.batch_id = p_batch_id and x.sheet = 'shares'
       and btrim(x.raw ->> 'legacy_policy_no') = v_no;
    if v_n_shares > 0 then
      if (select count(distinct e ->> 'insurer_id') from jsonb_array_elements(v_shares) e) <> v_n_shares then
        raise exception 'INS_IMPORT_SHARE_DUPLICATE_INSURER: policy % lists the same insurer more than once', v_no;
      end if;
      if not exists (select 1 from jsonb_array_elements(v_shares) e where (e ->> 'insurer_id')::uuid = (v_res ->> 'insurer_id')::uuid) then
        raise exception 'INS_IMPORT_SHARE_LEAD_MISSING: policy % has an insurer that is not one of its shares; the policy insurer is the lead', v_no;
      end if;
      insert into public.ins_policy_insurer_shares (
        tenant_id, policy_id, insurer_id, is_lead, share_pct, commission_rate_pct,
        premium_amount, pass_through_amount, commission_amount, payable_amount, reviewed)
      select v_tenant, v_policy, s.insurer_id,
             s.insurer_id = (v_res ->> 'insurer_id')::uuid,
             s.pct, (v_res ->> 'commission_rate_pct')::numeric,
             prem.amount, 0, comm.amount, prem.amount - comm.amount, true
        from (select (e ->> 'insurer_id')::uuid as insurer_id, (e ->> 'share_pct')::numeric as pct
                from jsonb_array_elements(v_shares) e) s
        join public.platform_ins_import_allocate((v_res ->> 'gross_premium')::numeric, v_shares) prem on prem.insurer_id = s.insurer_id
        join public.platform_ins_import_allocate((v_res ->> 'commission_amount')::numeric, v_shares) comm on comm.insurer_id = s.insurer_id;
      get diagnostics v_n_shares = row_count;
      v_share_rows := v_share_rows + v_n_shares;
      if exists (select 1 from public.ins_policy_insurer_shares
                  where policy_id = v_policy and (premium_amount <= 0 or payable_amount <= 0)) then
        raise exception 'INS_IMPORT_SHARE_TOO_SMALL: every insurer share of policy % needs a positive premium and payable', v_no;
      end if;
    end if;
  end loop;
  update public.ins_import_batches
     set status = 'committed', committed_at = now(), committed_by = auth.uid(), updated_at = now()
   where id = p_batch_id;
  select coalesce(jsonb_agg(jsonb_build_object(
           'currency', t.currency, 'policies', t.policies, 'gross_premium', t.gross,
           'commission', t.comm, 'net_premium', t.net) order by t.currency), '[]'::jsonb)
    into v_totals
    from (select currency, count(*) as policies, sum(gross_premium) as gross,
                 sum(commission_amount) as comm, sum(net_premium_to_insurer) as net
            from public.ins_policies where import_batch_id = p_batch_id group by currency) t;
  perform set_config('ins.import_commit', '', true);
  perform set_config('acct.import_batch', '', true);
  perform set_config('ins.via_rpc', 'off', true);
  return jsonb_build_object(
    'batch_id', p_batch_id, 'policies_created', v_policies, 'share_rows_created', v_share_rows,
    'accounting_policy_version', 1, 'totals_by_currency', v_totals,
    'posted', false,
    'note', 'Policies only. Nothing is posted; opening journals and open items are a separate step.');
end $function$
-- FN ins_import_create_batch(text,date)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.ins_import_create_batch(p_label text, p_cut_over_date date)
 RETURNS ins_import_batches
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'pg_catalog', 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
declare
  v_tenant uuid := public.get_my_tenant_id();
  v_row    public.ins_import_batches;
begin
  if v_tenant is null or not public.can_manage_insurance() then
    raise exception 'INS_ACCESS_DENIED' using errcode = '42501';
  end if;
  if p_label is null or btrim(p_label) = '' then
    raise exception 'INS_IMPORT_LABEL_REQUIRED';
  end if;
  if p_cut_over_date is null or p_cut_over_date > current_date then
    raise exception 'INS_IMPORT_CUTOVER_INVALID: the cut-over date is required and cannot be in the future';
  end if;
  insert into public.ins_import_batches (tenant_id, label, cut_over_date, created_by)
  values (v_tenant, btrim(p_label), p_cut_over_date, auth.uid())
  returning * into v_row;
  return v_row;
end $function$
-- FN ins_import_post_opening_position(uuid)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.ins_import_post_opening_position(p_batch_id uuid)
 RETURNS jsonb
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'pg_catalog', 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
declare
  v_plug numeric := 0;
  v_tenant uuid := public.get_my_tenant_id();
  b public.ins_import_batches;
  pol record;
  v_je journal_entries;
  v_lines jsonb := '[]'::jsonb;
  v_desc text;
  v_ar_gl uuid; v_pay_gl uuid; v_unearned_gl uuid; v_clearing_gl uuid;
  v_ar_acc uuid; v_ins_acc uuid;
  v_comm numeric; v_net numeric;
  v_total_ar numeric := 0; v_total_pay numeric := 0; v_total_unearned numeric := 0;
  v_posted boolean := false;
begin
  if v_tenant is null or not public.can_manage_insurance() then
    raise exception 'INS_ACCESS_DENIED' using errcode = '42501';
  end if;
  select * into b from public.ins_import_batches
  where id = p_batch_id and tenant_id = v_tenant;
  if not found then raise exception 'INS_IMPORT_BATCH_NOT_FOUND'; end if;
  if b.status <> 'committed' then
    raise exception 'INS_IMPORT_NOT_COMMITTED: commit policies before posting the opening position';
  end if;
  if exists (select 1 from public.ins_import_receipts where batch_id = p_batch_id)
     or exists (select 1 from public.journal_entries
                 where tenant_id = v_tenant and source_type = 'ins_import_opening'
                   and source_id = p_batch_id) then
    return jsonb_build_object('already_posted', true, 'batch_id', p_batch_id);
  end if;
  v_ar_gl       := public.get_posting_account(v_tenant, 'ar_control');
  v_pay_gl      := public.get_posting_account(v_tenant, 'insurer_payable');
  v_unearned_gl := public.get_posting_account(v_tenant, 'unearned_commission');
  v_clearing_gl := public.get_posting_account(v_tenant, 'opening_balance_clearing');
  if v_ar_gl is null or v_pay_gl is null or v_unearned_gl is null or v_clearing_gl is null then
    raise exception 'INS_POSTING_RULE_MISSING: chart needs ar_control, insurer_payable, unearned_commission, opening_balance_clearing';
  end if;
  set local ins.via_rpc = 'on';
  for pol in
    select p.id, p.policy_no, p.inception_date, p.gross_premium, p.currency,
           p.commission_amount, p.client_id, p.insurer_id
    from public.ins_policies p
    where p.tenant_id = v_tenant and p.import_batch_id = p_batch_id
    order by p.policy_no
  loop
    v_comm := coalesce(pol.commission_amount, 0);
    v_net  := pol.gross_premium - v_comm;
    v_ar_acc  := public.ins_ensure_client_account(pol.client_id);
    v_ins_acc := public.ins_ensure_insurer_account(pol.insurer_id);
    v_desc := 'Opening position (import ' || left(p_batch_id::text, 8) || '): ' || pol.policy_no;
    v_lines := v_lines || jsonb_build_array(
      jsonb_build_object('gl_account_id', v_ar_gl, 'debit', pol.gross_premium, 'currency', pol.currency, 'description', v_desc));
    v_total_ar := v_total_ar + pol.gross_premium;
    perform public.fin_create_open_item_fx(
      v_tenant, v_ar_acc, 'ar_control', 'ins_policy_premium', pol.id,
      pol.policy_no, pol.inception_date, b.cut_over_date, pol.gross_premium,
      'Opening receivable: ' || pol.policy_no, pol.currency, null);
    v_lines := v_lines || jsonb_build_array(
      jsonb_build_object('gl_account_id', v_pay_gl, 'credit', v_net, 'currency', pol.currency, 'description', v_desc));
    v_total_pay := v_total_pay + v_net;
    perform public.fin_create_open_item_fx(
      v_tenant, v_ins_acc, 'insurer_payable', 'ins_policy_insurer', pol.id,
      pol.policy_no, pol.inception_date, b.cut_over_date, v_net,
      'Opening payable: ' || pol.policy_no, pol.currency, null);
    if v_comm > 0 then
      v_lines := v_lines || jsonb_build_array(
        jsonb_build_object('gl_account_id', v_unearned_gl, 'credit', v_comm, 'currency', pol.currency,
                           'description', v_desc || ' - unearned commission'));
      v_total_unearned := v_total_unearned + v_comm;
      insert into public.ins_commission_entries (tenant_id, policy_id, amount, status)
      values (v_tenant, pol.id, v_comm, 'deferred');
    end if;
  end loop;
  -- balancing line to opening_balance_clearing. By construction receivable =
  -- payable + unearned commission, so the plug is normally zero; a journal
  -- line must have exactly one non-zero side, so the line is added only when
  -- there is a difference to clear.
  v_plug := v_total_pay + v_total_unearned - v_total_ar;   -- > 0: debit clearing
  if v_plug <> 0 then
    v_lines := v_lines || jsonb_build_array(
      jsonb_build_object('gl_account_id', v_clearing_gl,
        case when v_plug > 0 then 'debit' else 'credit' end, abs(v_plug),
        'currency', (select distinct currency from public.ins_policies where tenant_id = v_tenant and import_batch_id = p_batch_id),
        'description', 'Opening balance clearing (import ' || left(p_batch_id::text, 8) || ')'));
  end if;
  v_je := public.post_journal_entry(v_tenant, 'ins_import_opening', p_batch_id, b.cut_over_date,
    'Opening position for import batch ' || left(p_batch_id::text, 8), v_lines);
  v_posted := true;
  reset ins.via_rpc;
  return jsonb_build_object(
    'already_posted', false, 'batch_id', p_batch_id, 'journal_entry_id', v_je.id,
    'ar_total', v_total_ar, 'payable_total', v_total_pay, 'unearned_total', v_total_unearned);
end $function$
-- FN ins_import_reconciliation(uuid)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.ins_import_reconciliation(p_batch_id uuid)
 RETURNS TABLE(currency text, measure text, imported_amount numeric, control_amount numeric, match boolean)
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 STABLE SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'pg_catalog', 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
declare
  v_tenant uuid := public.get_my_tenant_id();
begin
  if v_tenant is null or not public.can_access_insurance() then
    raise exception 'INS_ACCESS_DENIED' using errcode = '42501';
  end if;
  if not exists (select 1 from public.ins_import_batches where id = p_batch_id and tenant_id = v_tenant) then
    raise exception 'INS_IMPORT_BATCH_NOT_FOUND';
  end if;
  return query
  with imp as (
    select p.currency,
           sum(p.gross_premium) as gross,
           sum(coalesce(p.commission_amount, 0)) as comm,
           coalesce((select sum(r.amount) from public.ins_import_receipts r
                      where r.batch_id = p_batch_id and r.tenant_id = v_tenant), 0) as receipts,
           coalesce((select sum(r.amount) from public.ins_import_remittances r
                      where r.batch_id = p_batch_id and r.tenant_id = v_tenant), 0) as remits
    from public.ins_policies p
    where p.tenant_id = v_tenant and p.import_batch_id = p_batch_id
    group by p.currency
  ),
  ctl as (
    select c->>'currency' as currency,
           (c->>'gross_premium')::numeric as gross,
           (c->>'commission')::numeric as comm,
           (c->>'receipts')::numeric as receipts,
           (c->>'remittances')::numeric as remits
    from public.ins_import_batches b,
         jsonb_array_elements(coalesce(b.control_totals, '[]'::jsonb)) c
    where b.id = p_batch_id and b.tenant_id = v_tenant
  )
  select i.currency, m.measure, m.amt, coalesce(c."value", 0), (m.amt = coalesce(c."value", 0))
  from imp i
  cross join lateral (values
    ('gross_premium', i.gross), ('commission', i.comm),
    ('receipts_to_date', i.receipts), ('remittances_to_date', i.remits)) m(measure, amt)
  left join lateral (select case m.measure
      when 'gross_premium' then c.gross when 'commission' then c.comm
      when 'receipts_to_date' then c.receipts when 'remittances_to_date' then c.remits end as "value"
    from ctl c where c.currency = i.currency) c on true;
end $function$
-- FN ins_import_record_history(uuid,jsonb,jsonb)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.ins_import_record_history(p_batch_id uuid, p_receipts jsonb, p_remittances jsonb)
 RETURNS jsonb
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'pg_catalog', 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
declare
  v_tenant uuid := public.get_my_tenant_id();
  r jsonb; n integer := 0;
begin
  if v_tenant is null or not public.can_manage_insurance() then
    raise exception 'INS_ACCESS_DENIED' using errcode = '42501';
  end if;
  if not exists (select 1 from public.ins_import_batches where id = p_batch_id and tenant_id = v_tenant) then
    raise exception 'INS_IMPORT_BATCH_NOT_FOUND';
  end if;
  set local ins.via_rpc = 'on';
  for r in select * from jsonb_array_elements(coalesce(p_receipts, '[]'::jsonb)) loop
    insert into public.ins_import_receipts (tenant_id, batch_id, policy_id, receipt_date, reference, amount)
    values (v_tenant, p_batch_id, (r->>'policy_id')::uuid, (r->>'receipt_date')::date,
            r->>'reference', (r->>'amount')::numeric);
    n := n + 1;
  end loop;
  for r in select * from jsonb_array_elements(coalesce(p_remittances, '[]'::jsonb)) loop
    insert into public.ins_import_remittances (tenant_id, batch_id, policy_id, remit_date, reference, amount)
    values (v_tenant, p_batch_id, (r->>'policy_id')::uuid, (r->>'remit_date')::date,
            r->>'reference', (r->>'amount')::numeric);
    n := n + 1;
  end loop;
  reset ins.via_rpc;
  return jsonb_build_object('recorded', n);
end $function$
-- FN ins_import_save_profile(text,jsonb)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.ins_import_save_profile(p_name text, p_mapping jsonb)
 RETURNS ins_import_profiles
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'pg_catalog', 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
declare
  v_tenant uuid := public.get_my_tenant_id();
  v_row    public.ins_import_profiles;
begin
  if v_tenant is null or not public.can_manage_insurance() then
    raise exception 'INS_ACCESS_DENIED' using errcode = '42501';
  end if;
  if p_name is null or btrim(p_name) = '' then raise exception 'INS_IMPORT_PROFILE_NAME_REQUIRED'; end if;
  if p_mapping is null or jsonb_typeof(p_mapping) <> 'object' then
    raise exception 'INS_IMPORT_PROFILE_INVALID: mapping must be a JSON object';
  end if;
  insert into public.ins_import_profiles (tenant_id, name, mapping, created_by)
  values (v_tenant, btrim(p_name), p_mapping, auth.uid())
  on conflict (tenant_id, name)
  do update set mapping = excluded.mapping, updated_at = now()
  returning * into v_row;
  return v_row;
end $function$
-- FN ins_import_stage_rows(uuid,text,jsonb,boolean)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.ins_import_stage_rows(p_batch_id uuid, p_sheet text, p_rows jsonb, p_replace boolean DEFAULT false)
 RETURNS integer
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'pg_catalog', 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
declare
  v_tenant uuid := public.get_my_tenant_id();
  v_batch  public.ins_import_batches;
  v_base   integer;
  v_n      integer;
begin
  if v_tenant is null or not public.can_manage_insurance() then
    raise exception 'INS_ACCESS_DENIED' using errcode = '42501';
  end if;
  if p_sheet is null or p_sheet not in ('policies', 'receipts', 'remittances', 'shares', 'control_totals') then
    raise exception 'INS_IMPORT_SHEET_INVALID';
  end if;
  if p_rows is null or jsonb_typeof(p_rows) <> 'array' then
    raise exception 'INS_IMPORT_ROWS_INVALID: rows must be a JSON array';
  end if;
  if jsonb_array_length(p_rows) > 2000 then
    raise exception 'INS_IMPORT_ROWS_TOO_MANY: at most 2000 rows per call';
  end if;
  if exists (select 1 from jsonb_array_elements(p_rows) e where jsonb_typeof(e) <> 'object') then
    raise exception 'INS_IMPORT_ROWS_INVALID: every row must be a JSON object';
  end if;
  select * into v_batch from public.ins_import_batches
   where id = p_batch_id and tenant_id = v_tenant
   for update;
  if not found then raise exception 'INS_IMPORT_BATCH_NOT_FOUND'; end if;
  if v_batch.status not in ('staged', 'validated') then
    raise exception 'INS_IMPORT_BATCH_LOCKED: a % batch cannot be changed', v_batch.status;
  end if;
  if p_replace then
    delete from public.ins_import_rows where batch_id = p_batch_id and sheet = p_sheet;
  end if;
  select coalesce(max(line), 0) into v_base
    from public.ins_import_rows where batch_id = p_batch_id and sheet = p_sheet;
  insert into public.ins_import_rows (tenant_id, batch_id, sheet, line, raw)
  select v_tenant, p_batch_id, p_sheet, v_base + t.ord::integer, t.val
    from jsonb_array_elements(p_rows) with ordinality as t(val, ord);
  get diagnostics v_n = row_count;
  update public.ins_import_batches
     set status = 'staged', validated_at = null, updated_at = now()
   where id = p_batch_id;
  return v_n;
end $function$
-- FN ins_import_validate_batch(uuid)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.ins_import_validate_batch(p_batch_id uuid)
 RETURNS jsonb
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'pg_catalog', 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
declare
  v_tenant   uuid := public.get_my_tenant_id();
  v_batch    public.ins_import_batches;
  v_ledger   text;
  r          record;
  v_err      jsonb;
  v_no       text;
  v_name     text;
  v_tax      text;
  v_cur      text;
  v_incep    date;
  v_exp      date;
  v_gross    numeric;
  v_comm     numeric;
  v_rate     numeric;
  v_sum      numeric;
  v_client   uuid;
  v_insurer  uuid;
  v_line     uuid;
  v_n        integer;
  v_amount   numeric;
  v_date     date;
  v_res      jsonb;
  v_errors   integer;
  v_sheets   jsonb;
  v_totals   jsonb;
begin
  if v_tenant is null or not public.can_manage_insurance() then
    raise exception 'INS_ACCESS_DENIED' using errcode = '42501';
  end if;
  select * into v_batch from public.ins_import_batches
   where id = p_batch_id and tenant_id = v_tenant
   for update;
  if not found then raise exception 'INS_IMPORT_BATCH_NOT_FOUND'; end if;
  if v_batch.status not in ('staged', 'validated') then
    raise exception 'INS_IMPORT_BATCH_LOCKED: a % batch cannot be validated', v_batch.status;
  end if;
  v_ledger := public.fin_ledger_currency(v_tenant);
  update public.ins_import_rows
     set status = 'pending', errors = '[]'::jsonb, resolved = '{}'::jsonb
   where batch_id = p_batch_id;
  -- ---------------------------------------------------------------
  -- policies
  -- ---------------------------------------------------------------
  for r in select * from public.ins_import_rows
            where batch_id = p_batch_id and sheet = 'policies' order by line loop
    v_err := '[]'::jsonb;
    v_no := nullif(btrim(r.raw ->> 'legacy_policy_no'), '');
    v_name := nullif(btrim(r.raw ->> 'client_name'), '');
    v_tax := nullif(btrim(r.raw ->> 'client_tax_id'), '');
    v_cur := upper(nullif(btrim(r.raw ->> 'currency'), ''));
    v_incep := public.platform_ins_import_date(r.raw ->> 'inception_date');
    v_exp := public.platform_ins_import_date(r.raw ->> 'expiry_date');
    v_gross := public.platform_ins_import_num(r.raw ->> 'gross_premium');
    v_comm := public.platform_ins_import_num(r.raw ->> 'commission_amount');
    v_rate := public.platform_ins_import_num(r.raw ->> 'commission_rate_pct');
    v_sum := coalesce(public.platform_ins_import_num(r.raw ->> 'sum_insured'), 0);
    v_client := null; v_insurer := null; v_line := null;
    if v_no is null then v_err := v_err || public.platform_ins_import_err('MISSING_FIELD', 'legacy_policy_no is required'); end if;
    if v_name is null and v_tax is null then
      v_err := v_err || public.platform_ins_import_err('MISSING_FIELD', 'client_name or client_tax_id is required');
    end if;
    if v_no is not null then
      select count(*) into v_n from public.ins_import_rows x
       where x.batch_id = p_batch_id and x.sheet = 'policies' and btrim(x.raw ->> 'legacy_policy_no') = v_no;
      if v_n > 1 then v_err := v_err || public.platform_ins_import_err('DUPLICATE_IN_FILE', 'policy number appears ' || v_n || ' times in this batch'); end if;
      if exists (select 1 from public.ins_policies p where p.tenant_id = v_tenant and p.policy_no = v_no) then
        v_err := v_err || public.platform_ins_import_err('POLICY_EXISTS', 'a policy with this number already exists');
      end if;
    end if;
    -- client: tax id first (when it finds exactly one), then exact name
    if v_tax is not null then
      select count(*), min(c.id::text)::uuid into v_n, v_client
        from public.ins_clients c where c.tenant_id = v_tenant and c.tax_id = v_tax;
      if v_n <> 1 then v_client := null; end if;
    end if;
    if v_client is null and v_name is not null then
      select count(*), min(c.id::text)::uuid into v_n, v_client
        from public.ins_clients c
        join public.bd_clients b on b.id = c.client_id and b.tenant_id = c.tenant_id
       where c.tenant_id = v_tenant and lower(btrim(b.name)) = lower(v_name);
      if v_n = 0 then
        v_err := v_err || public.platform_ins_import_err('CLIENT_NOT_FOUND', 'no client matches ' || coalesce(v_name, v_tax));
        v_client := null;
      elsif v_n > 1 then
        v_err := v_err || public.platform_ins_import_err('CLIENT_AMBIGUOUS', 'more than one client is named ' || v_name);
        v_client := null;
      end if;
    elsif v_client is null then
      v_err := v_err || public.platform_ins_import_err('CLIENT_NOT_FOUND', 'no client matches tax id ' || v_tax);
    end if;
    select i.id into v_insurer from public.ins_insurers i
     where i.tenant_id = v_tenant
       and (i.code = upper(nullif(btrim(r.raw ->> 'insurer'), '')) or lower(i.name) = lower(nullif(btrim(r.raw ->> 'insurer'), '')))
     order by i.code limit 1;
    if v_insurer is null then v_err := v_err || public.platform_ins_import_err('INSURER_NOT_FOUND', 'unknown insurer ' || coalesce(r.raw ->> 'insurer', '(blank)')); end if;
    select l.id into v_line from public.ins_product_lines l
     where l.tenant_id = v_tenant
       and (l.code = upper(nullif(btrim(r.raw ->> 'product_line'), '')) or lower(l.name) = lower(nullif(btrim(r.raw ->> 'product_line'), '')))
     order by l.code limit 1;
    if v_line is null then v_err := v_err || public.platform_ins_import_err('PRODUCT_LINE_NOT_FOUND', 'unknown product line ' || coalesce(r.raw ->> 'product_line', '(blank)')); end if;
    if v_incep is null then v_err := v_err || public.platform_ins_import_err('BAD_DATE', 'inception_date must be YYYY-MM-DD'); end if;
    if v_exp is null then v_err := v_err || public.platform_ins_import_err('BAD_DATE', 'expiry_date must be YYYY-MM-DD'); end if;
    if v_incep is not null and v_exp is not null and v_exp <= v_incep then
      v_err := v_err || public.platform_ins_import_err('DATE_ORDER', 'expiry_date must be after inception_date');
    end if;
    if v_cur is null or v_cur !~ '^[A-Z]{3}$' then
      v_err := v_err || public.platform_ins_import_err('BAD_CURRENCY', 'currency must be a 3-letter code');
    elsif v_cur <> v_ledger and not exists (
      select 1 from public.fx_rates f
       where f.tenant_id = v_tenant and f.currency = v_cur and f.status = 'approved' and f.rate_date <= v_batch.cut_over_date) then
      v_err := v_err || public.platform_ins_import_err('FX_RATE_MISSING', 'no approved ' || v_cur || ' rate on or before the cut-over date');
    end if;
    if v_gross is null or v_gross <= 0 then v_err := v_err || public.platform_ins_import_err('BAD_AMOUNT', 'gross_premium must be a positive number'); end if;
    if v_sum < 0 then v_err := v_err || public.platform_ins_import_err('BAD_AMOUNT', 'sum_insured cannot be negative'); end if;
    if v_gross is not null and v_gross > 0 then
      if v_comm is null and v_rate is null then
        v_err := v_err || public.platform_ins_import_err('COMMISSION_MISSING', 'commission_amount or commission_rate_pct is required');
      elsif v_comm is null then
        if v_rate < 0 or v_rate >= 100 then
          v_err := v_err || public.platform_ins_import_err('COMMISSION_RANGE', 'commission_rate_pct must be at least 0 and below 100');
        else
          v_comm := round(v_gross * v_rate / 100, 2);
        end if;
      else
        if v_comm < 0 or v_comm >= v_gross then
          v_err := v_err || public.platform_ins_import_err('COMMISSION_RANGE', 'commission_amount must be at least 0 and below the gross premium');
        else
          if v_rate is not null and abs(round(v_gross * v_rate / 100, 2) - v_comm) > 0.01 then
            v_err := v_err || public.platform_ins_import_err('COMMISSION_MISMATCH', 'commission_amount and commission_rate_pct disagree');
          end if;
          v_rate := round(v_comm / v_gross * 100, 2);
        end if;
      end if;
    end if;
    v_res := '{}'::jsonb;
    if jsonb_array_length(v_err) = 0 then
      v_res := jsonb_build_object(
        'policy_no', v_no, 'client_id', v_client, 'insurer_id', v_insurer, 'product_line_id', v_line,
        'currency', v_cur, 'inception_date', v_incep, 'expiry_date', v_exp, 'sum_insured', v_sum,
        'gross_premium', v_gross, 'commission_amount', v_comm, 'commission_rate_pct', v_rate,
        'net_premium', v_gross - v_comm);
    end if;
    update public.ins_import_rows
       set errors = v_err, resolved = v_res,
           status = case when jsonb_array_length(v_err) = 0 then 'valid' else 'error' end
     where id = r.id;
  end loop;
  -- ---------------------------------------------------------------
  -- receipts and remittances (history only)
  -- ---------------------------------------------------------------
  for r in select * from public.ins_import_rows
            where batch_id = p_batch_id and sheet in ('receipts', 'remittances') order by sheet, line loop
    v_err := '[]'::jsonb;
    v_no := nullif(btrim(r.raw ->> 'legacy_policy_no'), '');
    v_date := public.platform_ins_import_date(r.raw ->> case when r.sheet = 'receipts' then 'receipt_date' else 'remit_date' end);
    v_amount := public.platform_ins_import_num(r.raw ->> 'amount');
    if v_no is null or not exists (
      select 1 from public.ins_import_rows p
       where p.batch_id = p_batch_id and p.sheet = 'policies' and btrim(p.raw ->> 'legacy_policy_no') = v_no) then
      v_err := v_err || public.platform_ins_import_err('POLICY_NOT_IN_BATCH', 'no policy in this batch has number ' || coalesce(v_no, '(blank)'));
    end if;
    if v_date is null then
      v_err := v_err || public.platform_ins_import_err('BAD_DATE', 'date must be YYYY-MM-DD');
    elsif v_date > v_batch.cut_over_date then
      v_err := v_err || public.platform_ins_import_err('AFTER_CUTOVER', 'history after the cut-over date belongs in the live system');
    end if;
    if v_amount is null or v_amount <= 0 then
      v_err := v_err || public.platform_ins_import_err('BAD_AMOUNT', 'amount must be a positive number');
    end if;
    update public.ins_import_rows
       set errors = v_err, status = case when jsonb_array_length(v_err) = 0 then 'valid' else 'error' end
     where id = r.id;
  end loop;
  -- ---------------------------------------------------------------
  -- co-insurance shares
  -- ---------------------------------------------------------------
  for r in select * from public.ins_import_rows
            where batch_id = p_batch_id and sheet = 'shares' order by line loop
    v_err := '[]'::jsonb;
    v_no := nullif(btrim(r.raw ->> 'legacy_policy_no'), '');
    v_amount := public.platform_ins_import_num(r.raw ->> 'share_pct');
    v_insurer := null;
    if v_no is null or not exists (
      select 1 from public.ins_import_rows p
       where p.batch_id = p_batch_id and p.sheet = 'policies' and btrim(p.raw ->> 'legacy_policy_no') = v_no) then
      v_err := v_err || public.platform_ins_import_err('POLICY_NOT_IN_BATCH', 'no policy in this batch has number ' || coalesce(v_no, '(blank)'));
    end if;
    select i.id into v_insurer from public.ins_insurers i
     where i.tenant_id = v_tenant
       and (i.code = upper(nullif(btrim(r.raw ->> 'insurer'), '')) or lower(i.name) = lower(nullif(btrim(r.raw ->> 'insurer'), '')))
     order by i.code limit 1;
    if v_insurer is null then v_err := v_err || public.platform_ins_import_err('INSURER_NOT_FOUND', 'unknown insurer ' || coalesce(r.raw ->> 'insurer', '(blank)')); end if;
    if v_amount is null or v_amount <= 0 or v_amount > 100 then
      v_err := v_err || public.platform_ins_import_err('BAD_AMOUNT', 'share_pct must be above 0 and at most 100');
    end if;
    update public.ins_import_rows
       set errors = v_err,
           resolved = case when jsonb_array_length(v_err) = 0 then jsonb_build_object('insurer_id', v_insurer, 'share_pct', v_amount) else '{}'::jsonb end,
           status = case when jsonb_array_length(v_err) = 0 then 'valid' else 'error' end
     where id = r.id;
  end loop;
  -- ---------------------------------------------------------------
  -- control totals (format only; compared at reconciliation)
  -- ---------------------------------------------------------------
  for r in select * from public.ins_import_rows
            where batch_id = p_batch_id and sheet = 'control_totals' order by line loop
    v_err := '[]'::jsonb;
    v_cur := upper(nullif(btrim(r.raw ->> 'currency'), ''));
    if v_cur is null or v_cur !~ '^[A-Z]{3}$' then
      v_err := v_err || public.platform_ins_import_err('BAD_CURRENCY', 'currency must be a 3-letter code');
    elsif (select count(*) from public.ins_import_rows x
            where x.batch_id = p_batch_id and x.sheet = 'control_totals' and upper(btrim(x.raw ->> 'currency')) = v_cur) > 1 then
      v_err := v_err || public.platform_ins_import_err('DUPLICATE_IN_FILE', 'one control-totals row per currency');
    end if;
    if exists (
      select 1 from jsonb_each_text(r.raw) kv
       where kv.key <> 'currency' and nullif(btrim(kv.value), '') is not null
         and public.platform_ins_import_num(kv.value) is null) then
      v_err := v_err || public.platform_ins_import_err('BAD_AMOUNT', 'every control total other than currency must be a number');
    end if;
    update public.ins_import_rows
       set errors = v_err, status = case when jsonb_array_length(v_err) = 0 then 'valid' else 'error' end
     where id = r.id;
  end loop;
  -- ---------------------------------------------------------------
  -- policy-level aggregate checks
  -- ---------------------------------------------------------------
  update public.ins_import_rows p
     set errors = p.errors || public.platform_ins_import_err('RECEIPTS_EXCEED_PREMIUM', 'receipts to date exceed the gross premium'),
         status = 'error'
    from (
      select btrim(x.raw ->> 'legacy_policy_no') as policy_no,
             sum(coalesce(public.platform_ins_import_num(x.raw ->> 'amount'), 0)) as total
        from public.ins_import_rows x
       where x.batch_id = p_batch_id and x.sheet = 'receipts' and x.status = 'valid'
       group by 1) s
   where p.batch_id = p_batch_id and p.sheet = 'policies' and p.resolved ? 'policy_no'
     and btrim(p.raw ->> 'legacy_policy_no') = s.policy_no
     and s.total > (p.resolved ->> 'gross_premium')::numeric;
  update public.ins_import_rows p
     set errors = p.errors || public.platform_ins_import_err('REMITTANCES_EXCEED_NET', 'remittances to date exceed the net premium'),
         status = 'error'
    from (
      select btrim(x.raw ->> 'legacy_policy_no') as policy_no,
             sum(coalesce(public.platform_ins_import_num(x.raw ->> 'amount'), 0)) as total
        from public.ins_import_rows x
       where x.batch_id = p_batch_id and x.sheet = 'remittances' and x.status = 'valid'
       group by 1) s
   where p.batch_id = p_batch_id and p.sheet = 'policies' and p.resolved ? 'policy_no'
     and btrim(p.raw ->> 'legacy_policy_no') = s.policy_no
     and s.total > (p.resolved ->> 'net_premium')::numeric;
  update public.ins_import_rows p
     set errors = p.errors || public.platform_ins_import_err('SHARES_NOT_100', 'co-insurance shares must add up to 100'),
         status = 'error'
    from (
      select btrim(x.raw ->> 'legacy_policy_no') as policy_no,
             sum((x.resolved ->> 'share_pct')::numeric) as total
        from public.ins_import_rows x
       where x.batch_id = p_batch_id and x.sheet = 'shares' and x.status = 'valid'
       group by 1) s
   where p.batch_id = p_batch_id and p.sheet = 'policies' and p.resolved ? 'policy_no'
     and btrim(p.raw ->> 'legacy_policy_no') = s.policy_no
     and abs(s.total - 100) > 0.01;
  -- ---------------------------------------------------------------
  -- report
  -- ---------------------------------------------------------------
  select count(*) into v_errors from public.ins_import_rows where batch_id = p_batch_id and status = 'error';
  select coalesce(jsonb_object_agg(sheet, jsonb_build_object('total', total, 'valid', valid, 'error', bad)), '{}'::jsonb)
    into v_sheets
    from (select sheet, count(*) as total,
                 count(*) filter (where status = 'valid') as valid,
                 count(*) filter (where status = 'error') as bad
            from public.ins_import_rows where batch_id = p_batch_id group by sheet) s;
  select coalesce(jsonb_agg(jsonb_build_object(
           'currency', t.currency, 'policies', t.policies, 'gross_premium', t.gross,
           'commission', t.comm, 'net_premium', t.net, 'receipts', t.receipts, 'remittances', t.remits)
           order by t.currency), '[]'::jsonb)
    into v_totals
    from (
      select p.resolved ->> 'currency' as currency,
             count(*) as policies,
             sum((p.resolved ->> 'gross_premium')::numeric) as gross,
             sum((p.resolved ->> 'commission_amount')::numeric) as comm,
             sum((p.resolved ->> 'net_premium')::numeric) as net,
             coalesce(sum((select sum(public.platform_ins_import_num(x.raw ->> 'amount'))
                             from public.ins_import_rows x
                            where x.batch_id = p_batch_id and x.sheet = 'receipts' and x.status = 'valid'
                              and btrim(x.raw ->> 'legacy_policy_no') = p.resolved ->> 'policy_no')), 0) as receipts,
             coalesce(sum((select sum(public.platform_ins_import_num(x.raw ->> 'amount'))
                             from public.ins_import_rows x
                            where x.batch_id = p_batch_id and x.sheet = 'remittances' and x.status = 'valid'
                              and btrim(x.raw ->> 'legacy_policy_no') = p.resolved ->> 'policy_no')), 0) as remits
        from public.ins_import_rows p
       where p.batch_id = p_batch_id and p.sheet = 'policies' and p.status = 'valid'
       group by 1) t;
  update public.ins_import_batches
     set error_count = v_errors,
         status = case when v_errors = 0 and exists (
                    select 1 from public.ins_import_rows where batch_id = p_batch_id and sheet = 'policies')
                  then 'validated' else 'staged' end,
         validated_at = now(), updated_at = now()
   where id = p_batch_id;
  return jsonb_build_object(
    'batch_id', p_batch_id, 'cut_over_date', v_batch.cut_over_date,
    'accounting_policy_version', 1,
    'error_count', v_errors, 'sheets', v_sheets, 'totals_by_currency', v_totals);
end $function$
-- FN ins_insurer_commission_statement_data(uuid)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.ins_insurer_commission_statement_data(p_insurer_id uuid)
 RETURNS jsonb
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
declare policies jsonb; ledger jsonb;
begin
 if not can_access_insurance() then raise exception 'INS_FORBIDDEN: Insurance access required' using errcode='42501'; end if;
 if not exists(select 1 from ins_insurers where id=p_insurer_id and tenant_id=get_my_tenant_id()) then raise exception 'INS_NOT_FOUND: insurer'; end if;
 select coalesce(jsonb_agg(to_jsonb(p)||jsonb_build_object('gross_premium',coalesce(s.premium_amount,p.gross_premium)-coalesce(adj.return_premium,0),
  'commission_rate_pct',coalesce(s.commission_rate_pct,p.commission_rate_pct),'commission_amount',coalesce(s.commission_amount,p.commission_amount)-coalesce(adj.commission_clawback,0),
  'net_premium_to_insurer',coalesce(s.payable_amount,p.net_premium_to_insurer)-coalesce(adj.total_insurer_credit,0),'insurer_id',p_insurer_id,
  'ins_clients',jsonb_build_object('bd_clients',jsonb_build_object('name',b.name)))),'[]'::jsonb) into policies
 from ins_policies p left join ins_policy_insurer_shares s on s.policy_id=p.id and s.insurer_id=p_insurer_id
  and exists(select 1 from ins_policy_financial_snapshots sn where sn.policy_id=p.id and sn.extended)
 left join ins_cancellation_insurer_adjustments adj on adj.insurer_id=p_insurer_id and adj.cancellation_id in(select cx.id from ins_policy_cancellations cx where cx.policy_id=p.id and cx.status='posted')
 join ins_clients c on c.id=p.client_id join bd_clients b on b.id=c.client_id
 where p.tenant_id=get_my_tenant_id() and p.status<>'draft' and (s.id is not null or
  (p.insurer_id=p_insurer_id and not exists(select 1 from ins_policy_financial_snapshots sn where sn.policy_id=p.id and sn.extended)));
 select coalesce(jsonb_agg(to_jsonb(l)),'[]'::jsonb) into ledger from ins_commission_ledger l where tenant_id=get_my_tenant_id() and insurer_id=p_insurer_id;
 return jsonb_build_object('policies',policies,'ledger',ledger);
end $function$
-- FN ins_insurer_product_rates_guard()
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.ins_insurer_product_rates_guard()
 RETURNS trigger
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
begin
  if not public.ins_via_rpc() then
    raise exception 'INS_RPC_ONLY: manage commission rates with ins_set_insurer_product_rate()' using errcode = '42501';
  end if;
  if tg_op = 'INSERT' then
    if new.status <> 'draft' then
      raise exception 'INS_RATE_STATUS: a new rate starts as a draft; approval is a separate step by a different person';
    end if;
    if new.approved_by is not null or new.approved_at is not null or new.withdrawn_reason is not null then
      raise exception 'INS_RATE_STATUS: approval and withdrawal fields cannot be set on a new draft';
    end if;
  else
    if new.tenant_id is distinct from old.tenant_id
       or new.insurer_id is distinct from old.insurer_id
       or new.product_line_id is distinct from old.product_line_id then
      raise exception 'INS_RATE_KEY_IMMUTABLE: a rate cannot move to another company, insurer or product line';
    end if;
    -- Immutable: company, insurer, product line, rate, window start, source and
    -- entrant. What RPCs may change on top: status and its stamps (approve,
    -- withdraw) and, for an approved rate, the window end (end-date).
    if (to_jsonb(new) - array['status', 'approved_by', 'approved_at', 'withdrawn_reason', 'effective_to', 'updated_at'])
       is distinct from (to_jsonb(old) - array['status', 'approved_by', 'approved_at', 'withdrawn_reason', 'effective_to', 'updated_at']) then
      raise exception 'INS_RATE_IMMUTABLE: a rate''s insurer, product line, rate, start date, source and entrant cannot be changed; enter a correcting rate';
    end if;
    if new.status is distinct from old.status and not public.ins_via_rpc() then
      raise exception 'INS_RATE_STATUS_VIA_RPC: change status with ins_approve_insurer_product_rate or ins_withdraw_insurer_product_rate';
    end if;
  end if;
  new.updated_at := now();
  return new;
end $function$
-- FN ins_insurer_remittance_position(date)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.ins_insurer_remittance_position(p_as_of date DEFAULT CURRENT_DATE)
 RETURNS TABLE(insurer_id uuid, insurer_name text, currency text, unremitted_amount numeric, earliest_receipt date, earliest_deadline date, status text)
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 STABLE SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
declare
  v_tenant uuid := public.get_my_tenant_id();
  r record;
begin
  if not public.has_module_role('insurance', array['admin', 'manager', 'member']) then
    raise exception 'INS_FORBIDDEN: insurance access is required' using errcode = '42501';
  end if;
  for r in
    select ip.insurer_id,
           coalesce(ins.name, '?') as insurer_name,
           ip.currency,
           sum(r2.unremitted_amount) as unremitted_amount,
           min(r2.receipt_date) as earliest_receipt,
           min(r2.deadline_date) as earliest_deadline
      from public.ins_policies ip
      join public.ins_insurers ins on ins.tenant_id = ip.tenant_id and ins.id = ip.insurer_id
      cross join lateral public.ins_unremitted_premium_report(p_as_of) r2
     where ip.tenant_id = v_tenant
       and r2.policy_id = ip.id
     group by ip.insurer_id, ins.name, ip.currency
  loop
    insurer_id := r.insurer_id;
    insurer_name := r.insurer_name;
    currency := r.currency;
    unremitted_amount := r.unremitted_amount;
    earliest_receipt := r.earliest_receipt;
    earliest_deadline := r.earliest_deadline;
    status := case
      when r.earliest_receipt is null then 'receipt_date_missing'
      when r.earliest_deadline is null then 'deadline_not_set'
      when r.earliest_deadline < p_as_of then 'overdue'
      when r.earliest_deadline = p_as_of then 'due_today'
      else 'within_deadline'
    end;
    return next;
  end loop;
end;
$function$
-- FN ins_insurer_statement_reconciliation(uuid)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.ins_insurer_statement_reconciliation(p_statement_id uuid)
 RETURNS TABLE(line_id uuid, policy_id uuid, policy_no text, field text, insurer_amount numeric, our_amount numeric, match boolean)
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 STABLE SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
declare
  v_tenant uuid := public.get_my_tenant_id();
  l record;
begin
  if v_tenant is null or not public.can_access_insurance() then
    raise exception 'INS_ACCESS_DENIED' using errcode = '42501';
  end if;
  if not exists (
    select 1 from public.ins_insurer_statements s
    where s.tenant_id = v_tenant and s.id = p_statement_id
  ) then
    raise exception 'INS_STATEMENT_NOT_FOUND';
  end if;
  for l in
    select sl.id as lid, sl.policy_id as pid, p.policy_no,
           sl.premium, sl.commission, sl.claims_paid,
           p.gross_premium,
           coalesce((select sum(e.amount) from public.ins_commission_entries e
                     where e.tenant_id = v_tenant and e.policy_id = sl.policy_id), 0) as our_commission,
           coalesce((select sum(c.paid_amount) from public.ins_claims c
                     where c.tenant_id = v_tenant and c.policy_id = sl.policy_id), 0) as our_claims
      from public.ins_insurer_statement_lines sl
      join public.ins_policies p on p.tenant_id = sl.tenant_id and p.id = sl.policy_id
     where sl.tenant_id = v_tenant and sl.statement_id = p_statement_id
  loop
    line_id := l.lid; policy_id := l.pid; policy_no := l.policy_no;
    field := 'premium';
    insurer_amount := l.premium; our_amount := l.gross_premium;
    match := (l.premium = l.gross_premium); return next;
    field := 'commission';
    insurer_amount := l.commission; our_amount := l.our_commission;
    match := (l.commission = l.our_commission); return next;
    field := 'claims_paid';
    insurer_amount := l.claims_paid; our_amount := l.our_claims;
    match := (l.claims_paid = l.our_claims); return next;
  end loop;
end;
$function$
-- FN ins_insurers_guard()
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.ins_insurers_guard()
 RETURNS trigger
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
begin
  if tg_op = 'UPDATE' and new.party_account_id is distinct from old.party_account_id and not public.ins_via_rpc() then
    raise exception 'INS_PARTY_ACCOUNT_RPC_ONLY: the party account is managed by binding a policy' using errcode = '42501';
  end if;
  if new.party_account_id is not null and not exists (
    select 1 from accounts a where a.id = new.party_account_id and a.tenant_id = new.tenant_id
  ) then
    raise exception 'INS_CROSS_TENANT: party account belongs to another tenant';
  end if;
  return new;
end;
$function$
-- FN ins_intermediary_chain_has(uuid,uuid,uuid)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.ins_intermediary_chain_has(p_tenant uuid, p_start uuid, p_target uuid)
 RETURNS boolean
 LANGUAGE sql
 STABLE SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
  with recursive chain(id, parent_id, depth) as (
    select i.id, i.parent_id, 0 from ins_intermediaries i where i.tenant_id = p_tenant and i.id = p_start
    union all
    select p.id, p.parent_id, c.depth + 1
      from chain c join ins_intermediaries p on p.tenant_id = p_tenant and p.id = c.parent_id
     where c.depth < 100
  )
  select exists (select 1 from chain where id = p_target);
$function$
-- FN ins_issue_certificate(text,uuid,text)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.ins_issue_certificate(p_serial_no text, p_risk_id uuid, p_issued_to text)
 RETURNS uuid
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'pg_catalog', 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
declare t uuid := get_my_tenant_id(); c ins_certificate_stock%rowtype;
begin
  if not can_manage_insurance() then raise exception 'INS_FORBIDDEN: Insurance manager required' using errcode = '42501'; end if;
  select * into c from ins_certificate_stock where tenant_id = t and serial_no = btrim(p_serial_no) for update;
  if not found then raise exception 'INS_NOT_FOUND: certificate'; end if;
  if c.status <> 'in_stock' then raise exception 'INS_CERTIFICATE_STATE: certificate % is %, not in stock', c.serial_no, c.status; end if;
  if not exists (select 1 from ins_risks where id = p_risk_id and tenant_id = t and status = 'active' and risk_type = 'vehicle') then
    raise exception 'INS_CERTIFICATE_RISK: certificates are issued to an active vehicle risk';
  end if;
  update ins_certificate_stock
     set status = 'issued', risk_id = p_risk_id, issued_to = nullif(btrim(coalesce(p_issued_to, '')), ''), issued_at = now()
   where id = c.id;
  return c.id;
end $function$
-- FN ins_item_paid_amount(uuid)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.ins_item_paid_amount(p_open_item_id uuid)
 RETURNS numeric
 LANGUAGE sql
 STABLE SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
  select coalesce(sum(al.amount), 0)
  from fin_allocations al
  join fin_settlements s on s.id = al.settlement_id
  where al.open_item_id = p_open_item_id and s.status = 'posted';
$function$
-- FN ins_link_policy_risk(uuid,uuid)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.ins_link_policy_risk(p_policy_id uuid, p_risk_id uuid)
 RETURNS void
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'pg_catalog', 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
declare t uuid := get_my_tenant_id(); p ins_policies%rowtype;
begin
  if not can_manage_insurance() then raise exception 'INS_FORBIDDEN: Insurance manager required' using errcode = '42501'; end if;
  select * into p from ins_policies where id = p_policy_id and tenant_id = t for update;
  if not found then raise exception 'INS_NOT_FOUND: policy'; end if;
  if p.status <> 'draft' then raise exception 'INS_NOT_DRAFT: risks can only be linked to a draft policy'; end if;
  if not exists (select 1 from ins_risks r where r.id = p_risk_id and r.tenant_id = t and r.client_id = p.client_id and r.status = 'active') then
    raise exception 'INS_RISK_CLIENT: an active risk of this policy''s client is required';
  end if;
  insert into ins_policy_risks (tenant_id, policy_id, risk_id) values (t, p_policy_id, p_risk_id)
  on conflict (policy_id, risk_id) do nothing;
end $function$
-- FN ins_load_certificate_range(text,bigint,bigint,integer)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.ins_load_certificate_range(p_prefix text, p_from bigint, p_to bigint, p_width integer DEFAULT 8)
 RETURNS integer
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'pg_catalog', 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
declare t uuid := get_my_tenant_id(); n integer;
begin
  if not can_manage_insurance() then raise exception 'INS_FORBIDDEN: Insurance manager required' using errcode = '42501'; end if;
  if p_from is null or p_to is null or p_from < 0 or p_to < p_from then raise exception 'INS_INPUT: the range must start at 0 or more and end at or after its start'; end if;
  if p_to - p_from >= 5000 then raise exception 'INS_INPUT: load at most 5000 certificates at a time'; end if;
  if p_width is null or p_width < 1 or p_width > 20 then raise exception 'INS_INPUT: serial width must be 1 to 20'; end if;
  begin
    with gen as (
      select coalesce(btrim(p_prefix), '') || lpad(g::text, p_width, '0') as serial
      from generate_series(p_from, p_to) g
    )
    insert into ins_certificate_stock (tenant_id, serial_no)
    select t, serial from gen;
  exception when unique_violation then
    raise exception 'INS_DUPLICATE: some serials in this range are already in stock';
  end;
  get diagnostics n = row_count;
  return n;
end $function$
-- FN ins_log_claim_correspondence(uuid,text,text,text,text,text)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.ins_log_claim_correspondence(p_claim_id uuid, p_direction text, p_subject text, p_body text DEFAULT NULL::text, p_channel text DEFAULT 'email'::text, p_reference text DEFAULT NULL::text)
 RETURNS ins_claim_correspondence
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
declare
  v_tenant uuid := public.get_my_tenant_id();
  v_row public.ins_claim_correspondence;
begin
  if v_tenant is null or not public.can_access_insurance() then
    raise exception 'INS_ACCESS_DENIED' using errcode = '42501';
  end if;
  if p_direction not in ('in','out') then raise exception 'INS_CORR_BAD_DIRECTION'; end if;
  if btrim(p_subject) = '' then raise exception 'INS_CORR_SUBJECT_REQUIRED'; end if;
  if not exists (select 1 from public.ins_claims c where c.tenant_id = v_tenant and c.id = p_claim_id) then
    raise exception 'INS_CLAIM_NOT_FOUND';
  end if;
  insert into public.ins_claim_correspondence
    (tenant_id, claim_id, direction, channel, subject, body, reference, created_by)
  values (v_tenant, p_claim_id, p_direction, p_channel, p_subject, p_body, p_reference, auth.uid())
  returning * into v_row;
  return v_row;
end $function$
-- FN ins_log_endorsement_note(uuid,text)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.ins_log_endorsement_note(p_endorsement_id uuid, p_note text)
 RETURNS ins_endorsement_notes
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
declare
  v_tenant uuid := public.get_my_tenant_id();
  v_row public.ins_endorsement_notes;
begin
  if v_tenant is null or not public.can_access_insurance() then
    raise exception 'INS_ACCESS_DENIED' using errcode = '42501';
  end if;
  if btrim(p_note) = '' then raise exception 'INS_NOTE_REQUIRED'; end if;

  insert into public.ins_endorsement_notes (tenant_id, endorsement_id, note, created_by)

end $function$
-- FN ins_mark_renewal_notice_sent(uuid,text)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.ins_mark_renewal_notice_sent(p_notice_id uuid, p_channel text DEFAULT 'email'::text)
 RETURNS ins_renewal_notices
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
declare
  v_tenant uuid := public.get_my_tenant_id();
  v_n public.ins_renewal_notices;
begin
  if v_tenant is null or not public.can_manage_insurance() then
    raise exception 'INS_ACCESS_DENIED' using errcode = '42501';
  end if;
  if p_channel not in ('email', 'portal', 'print') then
    raise exception 'INS_NOTICE_BAD_CHANNEL';
  end if;
  select * into v_n from public.ins_renewal_notices
  where tenant_id = v_tenant and id = p_notice_id;
  if v_n.id is null then raise exception 'INS_NOTICE_NOT_FOUND'; end if;
  if v_n.status <> 'draft' then raise exception 'INS_NOTICE_NOT_DRAFT'; end if;
  set local ins.via_rpc = 'on';
  update public.ins_renewal_notices
  set status = 'sent', sent_at = now(), sent_by = auth.uid(), channel = p_channel, updated_at = now()
  where tenant_id = v_tenant and id = p_notice_id
  returning * into v_n;
  reset ins.via_rpc;
  return v_n;
end;
$function$
-- FN ins_notify_approval_case()
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.ins_notify_approval_case()
 RETURNS trigger
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
declare
  v_actor     uuid := auth.uid();
  v_row       jsonb := to_jsonb(NEW);
  v_what      text;
  v_slug      text;
  v_no        text;
  v_policy_no text;
  v_link      text;
  v_people    uuid[];
begin
  if TG_TABLE_NAME = 'ins_policy_endorsements' then
    v_what := 'Endorsement'; v_slug := 'ins_endorsement';
    v_no := v_row ->> 'endorsement_no';
  else
    v_what := 'Cancellation'; v_slug := 'ins_cancellation';
    v_no := v_row ->> 'cancellation_no';
  end if;
  select p.policy_no into v_policy_no
  from ins_policies p
  where p.id = NEW.policy_id and p.tenant_id = NEW.tenant_id;
  v_policy_no := coalesce(v_policy_no, 'policy');
  v_link := '/insurance/policies/' || NEW.policy_id::text;
  v_people := array[NEW.proposed_by, NEW.prepared_by];
  if TG_OP = 'INSERT' then
    if NEW.status = 'proposed' then
      perform ins_notify(
        NEW.tenant_id, ins_notify_finance_users(NEW.tenant_id), array[v_actor, NEW.proposed_by],
        v_slug || '_proposed',
        v_what || ' proposed: ' || v_policy_no,
        format('A %s on policy %s, effective %s, is waiting for a Finance maker to prepare it.',
               lower(v_what), v_policy_no, NEW.effective_date),
        v_link);
    end if;
    return NEW;
  end if;
  if NEW.status is not distinct from OLD.status then
    return NEW;
  end if;
  if NEW.status = 'prepared' then
    perform ins_notify(
      NEW.tenant_id, ins_notify_finance_users(NEW.tenant_id),
      array[v_actor, NEW.proposed_by, NEW.prepared_by],
      v_slug || '_prepared',
      v_what || ' awaiting your check: ' || v_policy_no,
      format('A %s on policy %s was prepared and needs an independent Finance checker.',
             lower(v_what), v_policy_no),
      v_link);
  elsif NEW.status = 'posted' then
    perform ins_notify(
      NEW.tenant_id, v_people, array[v_actor],
      v_slug || '_posted',
      v_what || ' posted: ' || coalesce(v_no, v_policy_no),
      format('The %s on policy %s was approved and posted.', lower(v_what), v_policy_no),
      v_link);
  elsif NEW.status = 'rejected' then
    perform ins_notify(
      NEW.tenant_id, v_people, array[v_actor],
      v_slug || '_rejected',
      v_what || ' rejected: ' || v_policy_no,
      format('The %s on policy %s was rejected.%s', lower(v_what), v_policy_no,
             case when nullif(btrim(coalesce(NEW.review_note, '')), '') is not null
                  then ' Reviewer note: ' || left(btrim(NEW.review_note), 200) else '' end),
      v_link);
  end if;
  return NEW;
exception when others then
  raise warning 'ins_notify_approval_case failed: % (%)', sqlerrm, sqlstate;
  return NEW;
end;
$function$
-- FN ins_notify_cancellation_cash()
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.ins_notify_cancellation_cash()
 RETURNS trigger
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
declare
  v_actor     uuid := auth.uid();
  v_policy_id uuid;
  v_policy_no text;
  v_currency  text;
  v_link      text;
  v_summary   text;
begin
  select p.id, p.policy_no, p.currency into v_policy_id, v_policy_no, v_currency
  from ins_policy_cancellations c
  join ins_policies p on p.id = c.policy_id and p.tenant_id = c.tenant_id
  where c.id = NEW.cancellation_id and c.tenant_id = NEW.tenant_id;
  v_policy_no := coalesce(v_policy_no, 'policy');
  v_link := case when v_policy_id is null then null else '/insurance/policies/' || v_policy_id::text end;
  v_summary := format('%s of %s %s on policy %s',
                      replace(NEW.kind, '_', ' '), coalesce(v_currency, ''),
                      to_char(NEW.amount, 'FM999,999,999,990.00'), v_policy_no);
  if TG_OP = 'INSERT' then
    if NEW.status = 'pending' then
      perform ins_notify(
        NEW.tenant_id, ins_notify_finance_users(NEW.tenant_id), array[v_actor, NEW.maker_id],
        'ins_cancellation_cash_pending',
        'Cash request awaiting your check: ' || v_policy_no,
        'A cancellation cash request needs an independent Finance checker: ' || v_summary || '.',
        v_link);
    end if;
    return NEW;
  end if;
  if NEW.status is not distinct from OLD.status then
    return NEW;
  end if;
  if NEW.status in ('posted', 'rejected') then
    perform ins_notify(
      NEW.tenant_id, array[NEW.maker_id], array[v_actor],
      'ins_cancellation_cash_' || NEW.status,
      'Cash request ' || NEW.status || ': ' || v_policy_no,
      format('Your cancellation cash request (%s) was %s.', v_summary, NEW.status),
      v_link);
  end if;
  return NEW;
exception when others then
  raise warning 'ins_notify_cancellation_cash failed: % (%)', sqlerrm, sqlstate;
  return NEW;
end;
$function$
-- FN ins_notify_claim_change()
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.ins_notify_claim_change()
 RETURNS trigger
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
declare
  v_actor     uuid := auth.uid();
  v_policy_no text;
  v_link      text := '/insurance/claims/' || NEW.id::text;
