begin
  select p.policy_no into v_policy_no
  from ins_policies p
  where p.id = NEW.policy_id and p.tenant_id = NEW.tenant_id;
  v_policy_no := coalesce(v_policy_no, 'policy');
  if TG_OP = 'INSERT' then
    perform ins_notify(
      NEW.tenant_id, ins_notify_insurance_users(NEW.tenant_id, array['admin', 'manager']),
      array[v_actor, NEW.assigned_to],
      'ins_claim_registered',
      'New claim ' || NEW.claim_no,
      format('Claim %s on policy %s (loss date %s) was registered.', NEW.claim_no, v_policy_no, NEW.loss_date),
      v_link);
    if NEW.assigned_to is not null then
      perform ins_notify(
        NEW.tenant_id, array[NEW.assigned_to], array[v_actor],
        'ins_claim_assigned',
        'Claim assigned to you: ' || NEW.claim_no,
        format('Claim %s on policy %s was assigned to you.', NEW.claim_no, v_policy_no),
        v_link);
    end if;
    return NEW;
  end if;
  if NEW.assigned_to is not null and NEW.assigned_to is distinct from OLD.assigned_to then
    perform ins_notify(
      NEW.tenant_id, array[NEW.assigned_to], array[v_actor],
      'ins_claim_assigned',
      'Claim assigned to you: ' || NEW.claim_no,
      format('Claim %s on policy %s was assigned to you.', NEW.claim_no, v_policy_no),
      v_link);
  end if;
  if NEW.status is distinct from OLD.status and NEW.status in ('approved', 'repudiated', 'settled') then
    perform ins_notify(
      NEW.tenant_id, array[NEW.assigned_to, NEW.created_by], array[v_actor],
      'ins_claim_' || NEW.status,
      'Claim ' || NEW.status || ': ' || NEW.claim_no,
      format('Claim %s on policy %s is now %s.', NEW.claim_no, v_policy_no, NEW.status),
      v_link);
  end if;
  return NEW;
exception when others then
  raise warning 'ins_notify_claim_change failed: % (%)', sqlerrm, sqlstate;
  return NEW;
end;
$function$
-- FN ins_notify_finance_users(uuid)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.ins_notify_finance_users(p_tenant uuid)
 RETURNS uuid[]
 LANGUAGE sql
 STABLE SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
  select coalesce(array_agg(distinct f.user_id), '{}'::uuid[])
  from finance_team_members f
  where f.tenant_id = p_tenant
    and f.role = 'finance';
$function$
-- FN ins_notify_insurance_users(uuid,text[])
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.ins_notify_insurance_users(p_tenant uuid, p_roles text[])
 RETURNS uuid[]
 LANGUAGE sql
 STABLE SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
  select coalesce(array_agg(distinct sr.user_id), '{}'::uuid[])
  from staff_roles sr
  where sr.tenant_id = p_tenant
    and sr.module = 'insurance'
    and sr.role = any (p_roles);
$function$
-- FN ins_notify(uuid,uuid[],uuid[],text,text,text,text)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.ins_notify(p_tenant uuid, p_recipients uuid[], p_exclude uuid[], p_type text, p_title text, p_body text, p_link text)
 RETURNS void
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
begin
  insert into notifications (tenant_id, recipient_id, type, title, body, link_path)
  select p_tenant, u.id, p_type, p_title, p_body, p_link
  from app_users u
  where u.tenant_id = p_tenant
    and u.id = any (array_remove(coalesce(p_recipients, '{}'::uuid[]), null))
    and u.id <> all (array_remove(coalesce(p_exclude, '{}'::uuid[]), null));
exception when others then
  raise warning 'ins_notify % failed: % (%)', p_type, sqlerrm, sqlstate;
end;
$function$
-- FN ins_place_quote(uuid)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.ins_place_quote(p_quote_id uuid)
 RETURNS ins_policies
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
declare
  v_tenant uuid := public.get_my_tenant_id();
  v_quote public.ins_quotes;
  v_policy public.ins_policies;
begin
  if v_tenant is null or not public.can_manage_insurance() then
    raise exception 'INS_ACCESS_DENIED' using errcode = '42501';
  end if;
  select * into v_quote from public.ins_quotes
  where tenant_id = v_tenant and id = p_quote_id;
  if v_quote.id is null then
    raise exception 'INS_QUOTE_NOT_FOUND';
  end if;
  if v_quote.status <> 'accepted' then
    raise exception 'INS_QUOTE_NOT_ACCEPTED';
  end if;
  set local ins.via_rpc = 'on';
  insert into public.ins_policies (
    tenant_id, policy_no, client_id, insurer_id, product_line_id, status,
    inception_date, expiry_date,
    sum_insured, currency, gross_premium, commission_rate_pct,
    notes, created_by
  ) values (
    v_tenant, next_doc_number(v_tenant, 'INS_POLICY', 'POL', 5),
    v_quote.client_id, v_quote.insurer_id, v_quote.product_line_id, 'draft',
    current_date, current_date + interval '1 year',
    v_quote.sum_insured, v_quote.currency, v_quote.gross_premium, v_quote.commission_rate_pct,
    v_quote.notes, auth.uid()
  )
  returning * into v_policy;
  update public.ins_quotes
  set status = 'placed', accepted_policy_id = v_policy.id, updated_at = now()
  where tenant_id = v_tenant and id = p_quote_id;
  reset ins.via_rpc;
  return v_policy;
end;
$function$
-- FN ins_policies_active_refs()
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.ins_policies_active_refs()
 RETURNS trigger
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'pg_catalog', 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
begin
  if new.insurer_id is distinct from old.insurer_id
     and not exists (select 1 from ins_insurers i
                     where i.id = new.insurer_id and i.tenant_id = new.tenant_id and i.is_active) then
    raise exception 'INS_NOT_FOUND: active insurer' using errcode = '23514';
  end if;
  if new.product_line_id is distinct from old.product_line_id
     and not exists (select 1 from ins_product_lines p
                     where p.id = new.product_line_id and p.tenant_id = new.tenant_id and p.is_active) then
    raise exception 'INS_NOT_FOUND: active product line' using errcode = '23514';
  end if;
  return new;
end $function$
-- FN ins_policies_guard()
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.ins_policies_guard()
 RETURNS trigger
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
declare
  v_ignore text[] := array['status', 'notes', 'updated_at'];
begin
  if tg_op = 'INSERT' then
    if not public.ins_via_rpc() then
      raise exception 'INS_RPC_ONLY: create policies with ins_create_policy()' using errcode = '42501';
    end if;
  else
    if new.status is distinct from old.status and not public.ins_via_rpc() then
      raise exception 'INS_STATUS_RPC_ONLY: policy status changes go through RPCs' using errcode = '42501';
    end if;
    -- The bind transition fills in the bind columns; nothing else.
    if old.status = 'approved' and new.status = 'active' and public.ins_via_rpc() then
      v_ignore := v_ignore || array[
        'commission_amount', 'net_premium_to_insurer',
        'bound_at', 'bound_by', 'journal_entry_id',
        'accounting_config_snapshot'
      ];
    end if;
    if old.status <> 'draft' and (
      to_jsonb(old) - v_ignore
    ) is distinct from (
      to_jsonb(new) - v_ignore
    ) then
      raise exception 'INS_POLICY_LOCKED: a % policy cannot be edited', old.status;
    end if;
    -- A pending policy returns to draft when rejected; nothing else may.
    if old.status not in ('draft', 'pending_approval') and new.status = 'draft' then
      raise exception 'INS_STATUS_INVALID: a % policy cannot return to draft', old.status;
    end if;
    -- Draft-only rules (a non-draft policy was handled by the lock above).
    if not public.ins_via_rpc() then
      if new.renewal_of_id is distinct from old.renewal_of_id then
        raise exception 'INS_RENEWAL_LINK_RPC_ONLY: the renewal link is set by ins_create_renewal_draft()' using errcode = '42501';
      end if;
      if new.commission_amount      is distinct from old.commission_amount
         or new.net_premium_to_insurer is distinct from old.net_premium_to_insurer
         or new.bound_at            is distinct from old.bound_at
         or new.bound_by            is distinct from old.bound_by
         or new.journal_entry_id    is distinct from old.journal_entry_id then
        raise exception 'INS_BIND_FIELDS_RPC_ONLY: commission, net premium and the bind stamps are set by ins_bind_policy()' using errcode = '42501';
      end if;
    end if;
  end if;
  if not exists (select 1 from ins_clients c where c.id = new.client_id and c.tenant_id = new.tenant_id) then
    raise exception 'INS_CROSS_TENANT: client belongs to another tenant';
  end if;
  if not exists (select 1 from ins_insurers i where i.id = new.insurer_id and i.tenant_id = new.tenant_id) then
    raise exception 'INS_CROSS_TENANT: insurer belongs to another tenant';
  end if;
  if not exists (select 1 from ins_product_lines p where p.id = new.product_line_id and p.tenant_id = new.tenant_id) then
    raise exception 'INS_CROSS_TENANT: product line belongs to another tenant';
  end if;
  if new.agent_id is not null and not exists (
    select 1 from ins_intermediaries a where a.id = new.agent_id and a.tenant_id = new.tenant_id
  ) then
    raise exception 'INS_CROSS_TENANT: agent belongs to another tenant';
  end if;
  if new.branch_id is not null and not exists (
    select 1 from organizations o where o.id = new.branch_id and o.tenant_id = new.tenant_id
  ) then
    raise exception 'INS_CROSS_TENANT: branch belongs to another tenant';
  end if;
  -- Activity only matters when the reference is being moved: a draft keeps an agent or branch
  -- that was suspended after it was chosen, the same rule as the insurer and product line.
  if tg_op = 'UPDATE' then
    if new.agent_id is distinct from old.agent_id and new.agent_id is not null and not exists (
      select 1 from ins_intermediaries a
      where a.id = new.agent_id and a.tenant_id = new.tenant_id and a.status = 'active'
    ) then
      raise exception 'INS_NOT_ACTIVE: agent must be an active intermediary';
    end if;
    if new.branch_id is distinct from old.branch_id and new.branch_id is not null and not exists (
      select 1 from organizations o
      where o.id = new.branch_id and o.tenant_id = new.tenant_id and o.is_active
    ) then
      raise exception 'INS_NOT_ACTIVE: branch must be an active organisation';
    end if;
  end if;
  return new;
end $function$
-- FN ins_policies_import_lock()
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.ins_policies_import_lock()
 RETURNS trigger
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
begin
  if tg_op = 'INSERT' then
    if new.import_batch_id is not null
       and coalesce(current_setting('ins.import_commit', true), '') <> new.import_batch_id::text then
      raise exception 'INS_IMPORT_RPC_ONLY: migrated policies are created by ins_import_commit_policies()' using errcode = '42501';
    end if;
  elsif new.import_batch_id is distinct from old.import_batch_id
        or new.migrated_at is distinct from old.migrated_at then
    raise exception 'INS_IMPORT_IMMUTABLE: the import flag of a policy cannot change' using errcode = '42501';
  end if;
  return new;
end $function$
-- FN ins_policies_link_lock()
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.ins_policies_link_lock()
 RETURNS trigger
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'pg_catalog', 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
begin
  if not exists (select 1 from ins_policy_risks r where r.tenant_id = new.tenant_id and r.policy_id = new.id) then
    return new;
  end if;
  if new.client_id is distinct from old.client_id then
    raise exception 'INS_POLICY_LINKED: risks are linked to this policy, so its client cannot change. Unlink the risks first.'
      using errcode = '23514';
  end if;
  if new.product_line_id is distinct from old.product_line_id then
    raise exception 'INS_POLICY_LINKED: risks are linked to this policy, so its product line cannot change. Unlink the risks first.'
      using errcode = '23514';
  end if;
  return new;
end $function$
-- FN ins_policy_bind_guard()
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.ins_policy_bind_guard()
 RETURNS trigger
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
begin
  if new.status is distinct from old.status
     and new.status = 'active'
     and old.status <> 'approved' then
    raise exception 'INS_NOT_APPROVED: policy % must be approved before binding (status is %)', old.policy_no, old.status;
  end if;
  return new;
end;
$function$
-- FN ins_policy_cancellation_context(uuid,integer)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.ins_policy_cancellation_context(p_policy_id uuid, p_offset integer DEFAULT 0)
 RETURNS jsonb
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
declare p ins_policies%rowtype; sn ins_policy_financial_snapshots%rowtype; shares jsonb; charge jsonb; charges jsonb:='[]'; parts jsonb; cases jsonb; accounts jsonb:='[]';
begin
 if auth.uid() is null or not(can_access_insurance() or is_finance_team_member('finance')) then raise exception 'INS_FORBIDDEN: Insurance or Finance required' using errcode='42501'; end if;
 if p_offset is null or p_offset<0 then raise exception 'INS_REPORT_PAGE: invalid offset'; end if;
 select * into p from ins_policies where id=p_policy_id and tenant_id=get_my_tenant_id();
 if not found then raise exception 'INS_NOT_FOUND: policy'; end if;
 select * into sn from ins_policy_financial_snapshots where policy_id=p.id;
 shares:=sn.snapshot->'shares';
 if shares is null or shares='[]'::jsonb then shares:=jsonb_build_array(jsonb_build_object('insurer_id',p.insurer_id,'premium_amount',p.gross_premium,'commission_amount',p.commission_amount,'commission_rate_pct',p.commission_rate_pct,'share_pct',100)); end if;
 select jsonb_agg(x||jsonb_build_object('premium_amount',x->>'premium_amount','commission_amount',x->>'commission_amount','commission_rate_pct',x->>'commission_rate_pct','insurer_name',i.name)) into shares
 from jsonb_array_elements(shares) x join ins_insurers i on i.id=(x->>'insurer_id')::uuid and i.tenant_id=p.tenant_id;
 for charge in select value from jsonb_array_elements(coalesce(sn.snapshot->'charges','[]')) loop
  parts:='[]';
  if charge->>'kind'='insurer_pass_through' then
   if charge->>'recipient_mode'='one_insurer' then parts:=jsonb_build_array(jsonb_build_object('insurer_id',charge->>'recipient_insurer_id','amount',charge->>'amount'));
   else
    with bases as(select x->>'insurer_id' as insurer_id,trunc((charge->>'amount')::numeric*(x->>'share_pct')::numeric/100,2) as base,
     (charge->>'amount')::numeric*(x->>'share_pct')::numeric/100-trunc((charge->>'amount')::numeric*(x->>'share_pct')::numeric/100,2) as fraction from jsonb_array_elements(shares) x),
    ranked as(select *,row_number() over(order by fraction desc,insurer_id collate "C") as rank,round(((charge->>'amount')::numeric-sum(base) over())*100) as cents from bases)
    select jsonb_agg(jsonb_build_object('insurer_id',insurer_id,'amount',(base+case when rank<=cents then .01 else 0 end)::text)) into parts from ranked;
   end if;
   select jsonb_agg(x||jsonb_build_object('insurer_name',i.name)) into parts from jsonb_array_elements(parts) x join ins_insurers i on i.id=(x->>'insurer_id')::uuid and i.tenant_id=p.tenant_id;
  end if;
  charges:=charges||jsonb_build_array(charge||jsonb_build_object('amount',charge->>'amount','vat_amount',coalesce(charge->>'vat_amount','0'),'original_allocations',parts));
 end loop;
 select coalesce(jsonb_agg(to_jsonb(q)||jsonb_build_object(
  'insurer_adjustments',coalesce((select jsonb_agg(to_jsonb(a)||jsonb_build_object('insurer_name',i.name,'payable_credit',a.payable_credit::text,'recovery_due',a.recovery_due::text,'retained_commission',a.retained_commission::text,'transferred_clawback_due',a.transferred_clawback_due::text)) from ins_cancellation_insurer_adjustments a join ins_insurers i on i.id=a.insurer_id where a.cancellation_id=q.id),'[]'::jsonb),
  'credits',coalesce((select jsonb_agg(to_jsonb(l)||jsonb_build_object('credit_note_no',n.credit_note_no,'amount',l.amount::text,'journal_entry_id',n.journal_entry_id)) from ins_cancellation_credit_links l join fin_credit_notes n on n.id=l.credit_note_id where l.cancellation_id=q.id),'[]'::jsonb),
  'journals',coalesce((select jsonb_agg(to_jsonb(j)) from ins_cancellation_journals j where j.cancellation_id=q.id),'[]'::jsonb)) order by q.proposed_at desc,q.id),'[]'::jsonb) into cases
 from(select * from ins_policy_cancellations where policy_id=p.id order by proposed_at desc,id limit 20 offset p_offset) q;
 if is_finance_team_member('finance') and effective_user_id()=auth.uid() then
  select coalesce(jsonb_agg(jsonb_build_object('id',g.id,'name',g.name,'account_code',g.account_code) order by g.account_code),'[]'::jsonb) into accounts from gl_accounts g
   where g.tenant_id=p.tenant_id and g.is_active and g.account_type='asset' and not g.is_control_account
    and not exists(select 1 from gl_posting_rules r where r.tenant_id=p.tenant_id and r.gl_account_id=g.id)
    and not exists(select 1 from fin_bank_accounts b where b.tenant_id=p.tenant_id and b.gl_account_id=g.id);
 end if;
 return jsonb_build_object('policy_id',p.id,'policy_no',p.policy_no,'policy_status',p.status,'currency',p.currency,'inception_date',p.inception_date,'expiry_date',p.expiry_date,
  'actor_id',auth.uid(),'can_propose',effective_user_id()=auth.uid() and (can_manage_insurance() or is_finance_team_member('finance')),
  'can_finance',effective_user_id()=auth.uid() and is_finance_team_member('finance'),'has_live_case',exists(select 1 from ins_policy_cancellations where policy_id=p.id and status in('proposed','prepared','posted')),'has_claims',exists(select 1 from ins_claims where policy_id=p.id),
  'insurers',shares,'charges',charges,'cases',cases,'offset',p_offset,'has_more',(select count(*)>p_offset+20 from ins_policy_cancellations where policy_id=p.id),'clearing_accounts',accounts);
end $function$
-- FN ins_policy_cash_allocation_guard()
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.ins_policy_cash_allocation_guard()
 RETURNS trigger
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
declare m record; s fin_settlements%rowtype; q ins_policy_cash_requests%rowtype;
begin
 if platform_ins_cancellation_cash_authorized(new.open_item_id,new.settlement_id,new.amount) then return new; end if;
 select * into m from ins_policy_cash_items where id=new.open_item_id and tenant_id=new.tenant_id;
 if m.source_type not in('ins_policy_premium','ins_policy_insurer') then return new; end if;
 perform ins_policy_cash_require_finance();
 select * into s from fin_settlements where id=new.settlement_id and tenant_id=new.tenant_id;
 select * into q from ins_policy_cash_requests where tenant_id=new.tenant_id and policy_id=m.source_id
  and insurer_share_id is not distinct from m.insurer_share_id
  and status='posting' and operation_txid=txid_current() and checker_id=auth.uid() and maker_id<>auth.uid()
  and bank_account_id=s.bank_account_id and amount=new.amount and amount=s.amount and cash_date=s.settlement_date
  and kind=case m.source_type when 'ins_policy_premium' then 'receipt' else 'remittance' end
  and(settlement_id is null or settlement_id=s.id) for update;
 if q.id is null or s.direction<>(case q.kind when 'receipt' then 'in' else 'out' end)
  or s.party_account_id<>m.party_account_id or exists(select 1 from fin_allocations where settlement_id=s.id) then
   raise exception 'INS_CASH_APPROVAL_REQUIRED: independent Finance approval required for this insurer payable';
 end if;
 perform ins_policy_cash_validate(q.policy_id,q.kind,q.bank_account_id,q.amount,q.cash_date,q.insurer_share_id);
 update ins_policy_cash_requests set settlement_id=s.id where id=q.id;
 return new;
end $function$
-- FN ins_policy_cash_bank_accounts()
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.ins_policy_cash_bank_accounts()
 RETURNS SETOF fin_bank_accounts
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
begin
  perform ins_policy_cash_require_finance();
  return query select b.* from fin_bank_accounts b where b.tenant_id=get_my_tenant_id()
    and b.kind='client_money' and b.is_active and b.gl_account_id is not null
    and b.currency=fin_ledger_currency(b.tenant_id) order by b.name;
end $function$
-- FN ins_policy_cash_commission_guard()
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.ins_policy_cash_commission_guard()
 RETURNS trigger
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
declare v jsonb;
begin
 if new.status='transferred' and old.status<>'transferred' then
  perform id from ins_policies where id=new.policy_id and tenant_id=new.tenant_id for update;
  v:=case when new.insurer_share_id is null then ins_policy_cash_totals(new.policy_id) else ins_share_cash_totals(new.insurer_share_id) end;
  if (v->>'available_allocated_funds')::numeric<new.amount then raise exception 'INS_CASH_COMMISSION_UNFUNDED: this insurer share has insufficient collected commission funds'; end if;
 end if;
 return new;
end $function$
-- FN ins_policy_cash_policies(text,integer)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.ins_policy_cash_policies(p_search text DEFAULT ''::text, p_offset integer DEFAULT 0)
 RETURNS TABLE(id uuid, policy_no text, status text, currency text, client_name text, insurer_name text)
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
begin
  perform ins_policy_cash_require_finance();
  if p_offset is null or p_offset<0 then raise exception 'INS_CASH_INPUT: invalid page'; end if;
  return query select p.id,p.policy_no,p.status,p.currency,c.name,i.name
    from ins_policies p join ins_clients ic on ic.id=p.client_id and ic.tenant_id=p.tenant_id
    join bd_clients c on c.id=ic.client_id and c.tenant_id=ic.tenant_id
    join ins_insurers i on i.id=p.insurer_id and i.tenant_id=p.tenant_id
    where p.tenant_id=get_my_tenant_id() and p.status<>'draft'
      and (p.policy_no ilike '%'||coalesce(p_search,'')||'%' or c.name ilike '%'||coalesce(p_search,'')||'%'
           or i.name ilike '%'||coalesce(p_search,'')||'%'
           or exists(select 1 from ins_policy_insurer_shares sh join ins_insurers co on co.id=sh.insurer_id and co.tenant_id=sh.tenant_id
              where sh.policy_id=p.id and co.name ilike '%'||coalesce(p_search,'')||'%'))
    order by p.created_at desc,p.id limit 50 offset p_offset;
end $function$
-- FN ins_policy_cash_position(uuid)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.ins_policy_cash_position(p_policy_id uuid)
 RETURNS jsonb
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
declare v jsonb;
begin
 v:=platform_ins_cash_position_before_cancellation(p_policy_id);
 if v->>'policy_status'='cancelled' then
  if jsonb_typeof(v->'shares')='array' then v:=jsonb_set(v,'{shares}',coalesce((select jsonb_agg(x||jsonb_build_object('maximum_remittance',0)) from jsonb_array_elements(v->'shares') x),'[]'::jsonb)); end if;
  v:=v||jsonb_build_object('can_prepare',false,'maximum_remittance',0,'cash_notice','Policy cancelled. Use the cancellation-specific recovery/refund workflow; cancellation approval did not authorize cash.'); end if;
 return v;
end $function$
-- FN ins_policy_cash_reconciliation_audit()
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.ins_policy_cash_reconciliation_audit()
 RETURNS trigger
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
declare sid uuid; snapshot jsonb;
begin
  sid := case when tg_op='DELETE' then old.settlement_id else new.settlement_id end;
  if sid is null then return null; end if;
  snapshot := case when tg_op='DELETE' then to_jsonb(old) else to_jsonb(new) end;
  insert into ins_policy_cash_reconciliation_events(tenant_id,policy_id,settlement_id,event,actor_id,reconciliation_snapshot)
    select distinct i.tenant_id,i.source_id,sid,case tg_op when 'DELETE' then 'unmatched' else 'matched' end,auth.uid(),snapshot
    from fin_allocations a join ins_policy_cash_items i on i.id=a.open_item_id and i.tenant_id=a.tenant_id
    where a.settlement_id=sid and i.source_type in ('ins_policy_premium','ins_policy_insurer');
  return null;
end $function$
-- FN ins_policy_cash_reconciliation_guard()
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.ins_policy_cash_reconciliation_guard()
 RETURNS trigger
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
declare sid uuid; s fin_settlements%rowtype; q ins_policy_cash_requests%rowtype;
begin
  if tg_op='UPDATE' and exists(select 1 from fin_allocations a join ins_policy_cash_items i on i.id=a.open_item_id
    where a.settlement_id in (old.settlement_id,new.settlement_id)
      and i.source_type in ('ins_policy_premium','ins_policy_insurer')) then
    raise exception 'INS_CASH_RECONCILIATION: policy matches are immutable; unmatch and rematch';
  end if;
  sid := case when tg_op='DELETE' then old.settlement_id else new.settlement_id end;
  if sid is null or not exists(select 1 from fin_allocations a join ins_policy_cash_items i on i.id=a.open_item_id
    where a.settlement_id=sid and i.source_type in ('ins_policy_premium','ins_policy_insurer')) then
    if tg_op='DELETE' then return old; else return new; end if;
  end if;
  perform ins_policy_cash_require_finance();
  select * into s from fin_settlements where id=sid and tenant_id=get_my_tenant_id() for update;
  if not found or s.status<>'posted' then raise exception 'INS_CASH_RECONCILIATION: posted settlement in this company required'; end if;
  select * into q from ins_policy_cash_requests where settlement_id=s.id and tenant_id=s.tenant_id;
  if auth.uid()=s.created_by or auth.uid()=q.maker_id or auth.uid()=q.checker_id then
    raise exception 'INS_CASH_THIRD_PERSON: reconciliation requires a third Finance user, not the maker, checker or poster' using errcode='42501';
  end if;
  if tg_op<>'DELETE' then
    if new.tenant_id<>s.tenant_id or new.matched_by is distinct from auth.uid() then
      raise exception 'INS_CASH_RECONCILIATION: the reconciler must be the acting Finance user';
    end if;
    return new;
  end if;
  return old;
end $function$
-- FN ins_policy_cash_require_finance()
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.ins_policy_cash_require_finance()
 RETURNS void
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
begin
  if auth.uid() is null or effective_user_id() is distinct from auth.uid()
     or not is_finance_team_member('finance') then
    raise exception 'INS_CASH_FORBIDDEN: an authorized Finance user, acting as themselves, is required' using errcode='42501';
  end if;
end $function$
-- FN ins_policy_cash_totals(uuid)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.ins_policy_cash_totals(p_policy_id uuid)
 RETURNS jsonb
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
declare sn ins_policy_financial_snapshots%rowtype; v jsonb; sh jsonb; shares jsonb:='[]'; c numeric; due numeric;
 remitted numeric:=0; withdrawn numeric:=0; available numeric:=0; liability numeric:=0; cap numeric:=0;
 endo_c numeric:=0; endo_client numeric:=0; endo_insurer numeric:=0;
begin
 -- Endorsement money rides endorsement-scoped items, not the policy's: the
 -- client's payments against an endorsement item fund remittances all the
 -- same, and an endorsement's receivables and payables are the policy's
 -- outstanding on both sides. (Endorsement payables are settled by generic
 -- settlements, so they display here but are not remittance-capped.)
 select coalesce(sum(a.amount),0) into endo_c
  from ins_policy_endorsements x
  join fin_open_items i on i.source_id=x.id and i.tenant_id=x.tenant_id and i.source_type='ins_policy_endorsement'
  join fin_allocations a on a.open_item_id=i.id and a.tenant_id=i.tenant_id
  join fin_settlements f on f.id=a.settlement_id and f.status='posted' and f.direction='in'
  where x.policy_id=p_policy_id;
 select coalesce(sum(b.outstanding_amount) filter (where b.side='receivable'),0),
        coalesce(sum(b.outstanding_amount) filter (where b.side='payable'),0)
  into endo_client, endo_insurer
  from ins_policy_endorsements x
  join fin_open_item_balances b on b.source_id=x.id and b.tenant_id=x.tenant_id
   and b.source_type in ('ins_policy_endorsement','ins_policy_endorsement_insurer')
  where x.policy_id=p_policy_id;
 select * into sn from ins_policy_financial_snapshots where policy_id=p_policy_id;
 if sn.policy_id is null or not sn.extended then
  v := platform_ins_policy_cash_legacy_totals(p_policy_id);
  v := jsonb_set(v, '{collected}', to_jsonb((v->>'collected')::numeric + endo_c));
  v := jsonb_set(v, '{available_allocated_funds}', to_jsonb(greatest((v->>'available_allocated_funds')::numeric + endo_c, 0)));
  v := jsonb_set(v, '{client_outstanding}', to_jsonb(greatest((v->>'client_outstanding')::numeric + endo_client, 0)));
  v := jsonb_set(v, '{insurer_outstanding}', to_jsonb(greatest((v->>'insurer_outstanding')::numeric + endo_insurer, 0)));
  return v;
 end if;
 select coalesce(sum(a.amount),0) into c from fin_open_items i join fin_allocations a on a.open_item_id=i.id
  join fin_settlements f on f.id=a.settlement_id and f.status='posted' and f.direction='in'
  where i.source_id=p_policy_id and i.source_type='ins_policy_premium';
 select outstanding_amount into due from fin_open_item_balances where source_id=p_policy_id and source_type='ins_policy_premium';
 for sh in select ins_share_cash_totals(s.id)||jsonb_build_object('insurer_name',i.name,'is_lead',s.is_lead) from ins_policy_insurer_shares s
  join ins_insurers i on i.id=s.insurer_id where s.policy_id=p_policy_id order by s.insurer_id
 loop
  shares:=shares||jsonb_build_array(sh); remitted:=remitted+(sh->>'remitted')::numeric;
  withdrawn:=withdrawn+(sh->>'commission_transferred')::numeric; available:=available+(sh->>'available_allocated_funds')::numeric;
  liability:=liability+(sh->>'outstanding')::numeric; cap:=cap+(sh->>'maximum_remittance')::numeric;
 end loop;
 return jsonb_build_object('collected',c+endo_c,'remitted',remitted,'commission_transferred',withdrawn,
  'available_allocated_funds',available+endo_c,
  'insurer_outstanding',liability+endo_insurer,'client_outstanding',coalesce(due,0)+endo_client,
  'maximum_remittance',cap,'shares',shares,
  'invoice_total',sn.invoice_total,'broker_fee_collected',trunc(sn.broker_fee_total*least((c+endo_c)/sn.invoice_total,1),2),
  'vat_collected',trunc(sn.vat_total*least((c+endo_c)/sn.invoice_total,1),2));
end $function$
-- FN ins_policy_cash_validate(uuid,text,uuid,numeric,date,uuid)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.ins_policy_cash_validate(p_policy_id uuid, p_kind text, p_bank_id uuid, p_amount numeric, p_date date, p_share_id uuid DEFAULT NULL::uuid)
 RETURNS fin_open_items
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
declare p ins_policies%rowtype; b fin_bank_accounts%rowtype; i fin_open_items%rowtype; v jsonb;
begin
 perform ins_policy_cash_require_finance();
 if p_kind is distinct from 'remittance' or not exists(select 1 from ins_policy_financial_snapshots where policy_id=p_policy_id and extended) then
   if p_share_id is not null then raise exception 'INS_SHARE_INPUT: share selection applies only to co-insurance/charge remittances'; end if;
   return platform_ins_policy_cash_legacy_validate(p_policy_id,p_kind,p_bank_id,p_amount,p_date);
 end if;
 if p_amount is null or p_amount<=0 or p_amount<>round(p_amount,2) or p_amount>9999999999999999.99 then raise exception 'INS_CASH_INPUT: invalid amount'; end if;
 if p_date is null or p_date>current_date then raise exception 'INS_CASH_DATE: invalid cash date'; end if;
 select * into p from ins_policies where id=p_policy_id and tenant_id=get_my_tenant_id() for update;
 if not found then raise exception 'INS_NOT_FOUND: policy'; end if;
 if p.status not in('active','renewed') then raise exception 'INS_CASH_STATE: bound, non-reversed policy required'; end if;
 perform f.id from fin_open_items f join ins_policy_cash_items m on m.id=f.id where m.source_id=p.id and f.tenant_id=p.tenant_id
  and m.source_type in('ins_policy_premium','ins_policy_insurer') order by f.id for update of f;
 select * into b from fin_bank_accounts where id=p_bank_id and tenant_id=p.tenant_id for share;
 if not found or b.kind<>'client_money' or not b.is_active or b.gl_account_id is null or b.currency<>p.currency then raise exception 'INS_CASH_BANK: active mapped client-money bank required'; end if;
 if not exists(select 1 from ins_policy_insurer_shares where id=p_share_id and policy_id=p.id and tenant_id=p.tenant_id) then raise exception 'INS_SHARE_INPUT: select a participating insurer'; end if;
 select * into i from fin_open_items where source_id=p_share_id and source_type='ins_policy_share_insurer' and tenant_id=p.tenant_id;
 if not found then raise exception 'INS_CASH_ITEM: insurer payable missing'; end if;
 v:=ins_share_cash_totals(p_share_id);
 if p_amount>(v->>'maximum_remittance')::numeric then raise exception 'INS_CASH_CAP: remittance exceeds this insurer share pro-rata funding or outstanding payable'; end if;
 return i;
end $function$
-- FN ins_policy_cash_void_guard()
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.ins_policy_cash_void_guard()
 RETURNS trigger
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
declare x record; v jsonb;
begin
 if platform_ins_cancellation_cash_void_authorized(old.id) then return new; end if;
  if old.status<>'posted' or new.status<>'void' then return new; end if;
  for x in select i.source_id as policy_id,sum(a.amount) as removed
    from fin_allocations a join ins_policy_cash_items i on i.id=a.open_item_id and i.tenant_id=a.tenant_id
    where a.settlement_id=old.id and i.source_type='ins_policy_premium'
    group by i.source_id order by i.source_id
  loop
    perform id from ins_policies where id=x.policy_id and tenant_id=old.tenant_id for update;
    v := ins_policy_cash_totals(x.policy_id);
    if (v->>'collected')::numeric-x.removed < (v->>'remitted')::numeric+(v->>'commission_transferred')::numeric then
      raise exception 'INS_CASH_FUNDS_IN_USE: reverse the funded remittances/commission transfers before voiding this receipt';
    end if;
    if exists(select 1 from ins_policy_insurer_shares sh join ins_policy_financial_snapshots sn on sn.policy_id=sh.policy_id and sn.extended
      where sh.policy_id=x.policy_id and trunc((sh.premium_amount+sh.pass_through_amount)*least(greatest((v->>'collected')::numeric-x.removed,0)/sn.invoice_total,1),2)
        < (ins_share_cash_totals(sh.id)->>'remitted')::numeric+(ins_share_cash_totals(sh.id)->>'commission_transferred')::numeric) then
      raise exception 'INS_CASH_FUNDS_IN_USE: receipt reduction would underfund an individual insurer share';
    end if;
  end loop;
  -- Outgoing voids also serialize with concurrent approvals and receipt voids.
  perform p.id from ins_policies p join ins_policy_cash_items i on i.source_id=p.id and i.tenant_id=p.tenant_id
    join fin_allocations a on a.open_item_id=i.id and a.tenant_id=i.tenant_id
    where a.settlement_id=old.id and i.source_type='ins_policy_insurer' order by p.id for update of p;
  return new;
end $function$
-- FN ins_policy_charge_income_accounts()
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.ins_policy_charge_income_accounts()
 RETURNS TABLE(id uuid, account_code text, name text)
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
begin
 if not can_manage_insurance() then raise exception 'INS_FORBIDDEN: Insurance manager required' using errcode='42501'; end if;
 return query select g.id,g.account_code,g.name from gl_accounts g where tenant_id=get_my_tenant_id() and account_type='revenue' and is_active order by g.account_code;
end $function$
-- FN ins_policy_child_guard()
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.ins_policy_child_guard()
 RETURNS trigger
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
declare p ins_policies%rowtype; target uuid;
begin
  target:=case when tg_table_name='ins_claims' then (to_jsonb(new)->>'policy_id')::uuid
               else (to_jsonb(new)->>'renewal_of_id')::uuid end;
  if target is null then return new; end if;
  select * into p from ins_policies where id=target and tenant_id=new.tenant_id for update;
  if not found or p.status not in ('active','renewed') then
    raise exception 'INS_POLICY_UNAVAILABLE: claims and renewals require an unreversed bound policy';
  end if;
  return new;
end $function$
-- FN ins_policy_endorsement_receipts(uuid)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.ins_policy_endorsement_receipts(p_policy_id uuid)
 RETURNS numeric
 LANGUAGE sql
 STABLE SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
  select coalesce(sum(a.amount), 0::numeric)
  from public.ins_policy_endorsements x
  join public.fin_open_items oi
    on oi.source_id = x.id
   and oi.tenant_id = x.tenant_id
   and oi.source_type = 'ins_policy_endorsement'
  join public.fin_allocations a
    on a.open_item_id = oi.id
   and a.tenant_id = oi.tenant_id
  join public.fin_settlements s
    on s.id = a.settlement_id
   and s.tenant_id = a.tenant_id
  where public.can_access_insurance()
    and x.policy_id = p_policy_id
    and x.tenant_id = public.get_my_tenant_id()
    and s.status = 'posted'
    and s.direction = 'in';
$function$
-- FN ins_policy_financial_details(uuid)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.ins_policy_financial_details(p_policy_id uuid)
 RETURNS jsonb
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
declare p ins_policies%rowtype; v jsonb;
begin
 if not(can_access_insurance() or is_finance_team_member('finance')) or auth.uid() is null then raise exception 'INS_FORBIDDEN: Insurance or Finance access required' using errcode='42501'; end if;
 select * into p from ins_policies where id=p_policy_id and tenant_id=get_my_tenant_id();
 if not found then raise exception 'INS_NOT_FOUND: policy'; end if;
 select jsonb_build_object('policy_id',p.id,'policy_no',p.policy_no,'status',p.status,'currency',p.currency,'gross_premium',p.gross_premium,
  'shares',coalesce((select jsonb_agg(to_jsonb(s)||jsonb_build_object('insurer_name',i.name) order by s.insurer_id) from ins_policy_insurer_shares s join ins_insurers i on i.id=s.insurer_id where s.policy_id=p.id),'[]'::jsonb),
  'charges',coalesce((select jsonb_agg(to_jsonb(c) order by c.id) from ins_policy_charges c where c.policy_id=p.id),'[]'::jsonb),
  'snapshot',(select to_jsonb(sn) from ins_policy_financial_snapshots sn where sn.policy_id=p.id),
  'can_configure',can_manage_insurance() and effective_user_id()=auth.uid(),
  'client_name',(select b.name from ins_clients c join bd_clients b on b.id=c.client_id where c.id=p.client_id)) into v;
 return v;
end $function$
-- FN ins_policy_reversal_context(uuid,boolean)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.ins_policy_reversal_context(p_policy_id uuid, p_in_progress boolean DEFAULT false)
 RETURNS jsonb
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
begin
 if not can_manage_insurance() or not exists(select 1 from ins_policies where id=p_policy_id and tenant_id=get_my_tenant_id()) then
   raise exception 'INS_FORBIDDEN: policy access required' using errcode='42501';
 end if;
 perform id from ins_policies where id=p_policy_id and tenant_id=get_my_tenant_id() for update;
 if (select count(*) from ins_policy_insurer_shares where policy_id=p_policy_id)>1 or exists(select 1 from ins_policy_charges where policy_id=p_policy_id) then
   raise exception 'INS_REVERSAL_COMPLEX: co-insurance or ANY charge line (including zero) requires the future cancellation workflow';
 end if;
 return platform_ins_policy_reversal_legacy_context(p_policy_id,p_in_progress);
end $function$
-- FN ins_policy_reversal_eligibility(uuid)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.ins_policy_reversal_eligibility(p_policy_id uuid)
 RETURNS jsonb
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
begin
  perform ins_policy_reversal_context(p_policy_id);
  return jsonb_build_object('eligible',true,'reason',null);
exception when others then
  if sqlerrm like 'INS_REVERSAL_%' then return jsonb_build_object('eligible',false,'reason',sqlerrm); end if;
  raise;
end $function$
-- FN ins_prepare_policy_cancellation(uuid,uuid)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.ins_prepare_policy_cancellation(p_cancellation_id uuid, p_clearing_gl_account_id uuid)
 RETURNS ins_policy_cancellations
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
declare c ins_policy_cancellations%rowtype; pid uuid; quote jsonb; mappings jsonb;
begin
 perform ins_policy_cash_require_finance();
 select policy_id into pid from ins_policy_cancellations where id=p_cancellation_id and tenant_id=get_my_tenant_id();
 if not found then raise exception 'INS_NOT_FOUND: cancellation'; end if;
 perform id from ins_policies where id=pid for update;
 select * into c from ins_policy_cancellations where id=p_cancellation_id for update;
 if c.status not in('proposed','prepared') or (c.status='prepared' and c.prepared_by<>auth.uid()) then raise exception 'INS_CANCELLATION_STATE: proposal or own prepared case required'; end if;
 quote:=ins_preview_policy_cancellation(c.policy_id,c.effective_date,c.insurer_terms,c.charge_refunds)||platform_ins_cancellation_review_position(c.policy_id);
 mappings:=platform_ins_cancellation_mappings(c.policy_id,p_clearing_gl_account_id);
 update ins_policy_cancellations set status='prepared',prepared_by=auth.uid(),prepared_at=now(),calculation=quote,clearing_gl_account_id=p_clearing_gl_account_id,accounting_mappings=mappings
 where id=c.id returning * into c;
 insert into ins_cancellation_events(tenant_id,cancellation_id,event,actor_id,detail) values(c.tenant_id,c.id,'finance_prepared',auth.uid(),jsonb_build_object('calculation',quote,'mappings',mappings));
 return c;
end $function$
-- FN ins_prepare_policy_endorsement(uuid,uuid)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.ins_prepare_policy_endorsement(p_endorsement_id uuid, p_clearing_gl_account_id uuid)
 RETURNS ins_policy_endorsements
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
declare
  c ins_policy_endorsements%rowtype;
  pid uuid;
  quote jsonb;
  mappings jsonb;
begin
  perform ins_policy_cash_require_finance();
  select policy_id into pid from ins_policy_endorsements
   where id = p_endorsement_id and tenant_id = get_my_tenant_id();
  if not found then raise exception 'INS_NOT_FOUND: endorsement'; end if;
  perform id from ins_policies where id = pid for update;
  select * into c from ins_policy_endorsements where id = p_endorsement_id for update;
  if c.status not in ('proposed','prepared')
     or (c.status = 'prepared' and c.prepared_by <> auth.uid()) then
    raise exception 'INS_ENDORSEMENT_STATE: proposal or own prepared case required';
  end if;
  quote := ins_preview_policy_endorsement(c.policy_id, c.effective_date, c.insurer_terms, c.changes)
           || ins_endorsement_review_position(c.policy_id);
  mappings := ins_endorsement_mappings(c.policy_id, p_clearing_gl_account_id);
  update ins_policy_endorsements
     set status = 'prepared', prepared_by = auth.uid(), prepared_at = now(),
         calculation = quote, accounting_mappings = mappings
   where id = c.id
   returning * into c;
  insert into ins_policy_endorsement_events(tenant_id, endorsement_id, event, actor_id, detail)
  values (c.tenant_id, c.id, 'prepared', auth.uid(),
          jsonb_build_object('calculation', quote, 'mappings', mappings));
  return c;
end $function$
-- FN ins_preview_policy_cancellation(uuid,date,jsonb,jsonb)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.ins_preview_policy_cancellation(p_policy_id uuid, p_effective_date date, p_insurer_terms jsonb, p_charge_refunds jsonb)
 RETURNS jsonb
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
declare p ins_policies%rowtype; snap ins_policy_financial_snapshots%rowtype; shares jsonb; charges jsonb;
 allocation jsonb; term jsonb; charge jsonb; rule jsonb; calc jsonb; insurers jsonb:='[]'; charge_results jsonb:='[]'; pass_rows jsonb:='[]';
 original_parts jsonb; parts jsonb; part jsonb; decision jsonb; recipient_refund numeric; recipient_total numeric; final_insurers jsonb:='[]';
 refund numeric; original_amount numeric; original_vat numeric; vat_refund numeric; premium_return numeric:=0; total_credit numeric:=0;
 invoice numeric; unpaid numeric; pass_refund numeric; requires_exception boolean:=false; item_count integer;
begin
 if auth.uid() is null or effective_user_id() is distinct from auth.uid()
  or not (can_manage_insurance() or is_finance_team_member('finance')) then
  raise exception 'INS_FORBIDDEN: Insurance manager or Finance acting as themselves required' using errcode='42501'; end if;
 select * into p from ins_policies where id=p_policy_id and tenant_id=get_my_tenant_id();
 if not found then raise exception 'INS_NOT_FOUND: policy'; end if;
 if p.status not in('active','renewed') then raise exception 'INS_CANCELLATION_STATE: bound policy required'; end if;
 if exists(select 1 from ins_claims where policy_id=p.id and tenant_id=p.tenant_id) then
  raise exception 'INS_CANCELLATION_CLAIM: policies with existing claims are not supported in v1'; end if;
 if p_effective_date is null or p_effective_date>current_date then raise exception 'INS_CANCELLATION_INPUT: a non-future effective cancellation date is required'; end if;
 if jsonb_typeof(p_insurer_terms) is distinct from 'array' or jsonb_typeof(p_charge_refunds) is distinct from 'array' then
  raise exception 'INS_CANCELLATION_INPUT: insurer terms and charge decisions must be arrays'; end if;
 if jsonb_array_length(p_insurer_terms)>30 or jsonb_array_length(p_charge_refunds)>100 then raise exception 'INS_CANCELLATION_INPUT: too many input lines'; end if;
 select * into snap from ins_policy_financial_snapshots where policy_id=p.id and tenant_id=p.tenant_id;
 -- Financial data comes only from immutable bind-time records, never from
 -- client-supplied premiums, shares, commission rates, recipients or VAT rates.
 shares:=snap.snapshot->'shares'; charges:=coalesce(snap.snapshot->'charges','[]'::jsonb);
 if shares is null or shares='[]'::jsonb then
  shares:=jsonb_build_array(jsonb_build_object('insurer_id',p.insurer_id,'premium_amount',p.gross_premium,
   'commission_amount',p.commission_amount,'commission_rate_pct',p.commission_rate_pct,'share_pct',100));
 end if;
 invoice:=coalesce(snap.invoice_total,p.gross_premium);
 if jsonb_array_length(p_insurer_terms)<>jsonb_array_length(shares)
  or (select count(*)<>count(distinct x->>'insurer_id') from jsonb_array_elements(p_insurer_terms) x) then
  raise exception 'INS_CANCELLATION_REVIEW_REQUIRED: provide one set of terms for every original insurer'; end if;
 for allocation in select value from jsonb_array_elements(shares) loop
  select x->'terms' into term from jsonb_array_elements(p_insurer_terms) x where x->>'insurer_id'=allocation->>'insurer_id';
  if term is null then raise exception 'INS_CANCELLATION_REVIEW_REQUIRED: missing original insurer terms'; end if;
  calc:=platform_ins_calculate_insurer_cancellation(allocation,term,p.inception_date,p.expiry_date,p_effective_date);
  insurers:=insurers||jsonb_build_array(calc);
  premium_return:=premium_return+(calc->>'return_premium')::numeric;
  requires_exception:=requires_exception or (calc->>'requires_finance_exception')::boolean;
 end loop;
 if jsonb_array_length(p_charge_refunds)<>jsonb_array_length(charges)
  or (select count(*)<>count(distinct x->>'charge_id') from jsonb_array_elements(p_charge_refunds) x) then
  raise exception 'INS_CANCELLATION_REVIEW_REQUIRED: decide every original charge, including zero-valued lines'; end if;
 total_credit:=premium_return;
 for charge in select value from jsonb_array_elements(charges) loop
  select x into rule from jsonb_array_elements(p_charge_refunds) x where x->>'charge_id'=charge->>'id';
  if rule is null or rule->'legal_basis_confirmed' is distinct from 'true'::jsonb or length(btrim(coalesce(rule->>'basis','')))<5 then
   raise exception 'INS_CANCELLATION_REVIEW_REQUIRED: confirm the contractual/legal basis for every refundable or retained charge'; end if;
  original_amount:=platform_ins_cancellation_money(charge->>'amount');
  refund:=platform_ins_cancellation_money(rule->>'refund_amount');
  if refund>original_amount then raise exception 'INS_CANCELLATION_INPUT: charge refund exceeds its original amount'; end if;
  original_vat:=coalesce((charge->>'vat_amount')::numeric,0); vat_refund:=0;
  if charge->>'kind'='broker_fee' then
   if original_amount=0 and original_vat<>0 then raise exception 'INS_CANCELLATION_HISTORY: VAT without broker-fee base'; end if;
   vat_refund:=case when original_amount=0 then 0 when refund=original_amount then original_vat else platform_ins_cancellation_round_ratio(original_vat*refund,original_amount) end;
  else
   if charge->>'recipient_mode'='one_insurer' then
    original_parts:=jsonb_build_array(jsonb_build_object('insurer_id',charge->>'recipient_insurer_id','amount',original_amount::text));
   else
    -- Reconstruct the exact original Hamilton allocation from immutable shares.
    with bases as(select x->>'insurer_id' as insurer_id,trunc(original_amount*(x->>'share_pct')::numeric/100,2) as base,
      original_amount*(x->>'share_pct')::numeric/100-trunc(original_amount*(x->>'share_pct')::numeric/100,2) as fraction from jsonb_array_elements(shares) x),
    ranked as(select *,row_number() over(order by fraction desc,insurer_id collate "C") as rank,round((original_amount-sum(base) over())*100) as cents from bases)
    select jsonb_agg(jsonb_build_object('insurer_id',insurer_id,'amount',(base+case when rank<=cents then .01 else 0 end)::text)) into original_parts from ranked;
   end if;
   if jsonb_typeof(rule->'insurer_refunds') is distinct from 'array' then
    raise exception 'INS_CANCELLATION_REVIEW_REQUIRED: each pass-through recipient needs its own explicit refund decision'; end if;
   if jsonb_array_length(rule->'insurer_refunds')<>jsonb_array_length(original_parts)
     or (select count(*)<>count(distinct x->>'insurer_id') from jsonb_array_elements(rule->'insurer_refunds') x) then
    raise exception 'INS_CANCELLATION_INPUT: exactly one charge decision per original recipient required'; end if;
   recipient_total:=0;
   for part in select value from jsonb_array_elements(original_parts) loop
    select x into decision from jsonb_array_elements(rule->'insurer_refunds') x where x->>'insurer_id'=part->>'insurer_id';
    if decision is null or length(btrim(coalesce(decision->>'basis','')))<5 then
     raise exception 'INS_CANCELLATION_REVIEW_REQUIRED: recipient-specific charge refund/retention basis required'; end if;
    recipient_refund:=platform_ins_cancellation_money(decision->>'refund_amount');
    if recipient_refund>(part->>'amount')::numeric then raise exception 'INS_CANCELLATION_INPUT: refund exceeds recipient original charge allocation'; end if;
    recipient_total:=recipient_total+recipient_refund;
    pass_rows:=pass_rows||jsonb_build_array(jsonb_build_object('insurer_id',part->>'insurer_id','charge_id',charge->>'id',
     'amount',recipient_refund::text,'original_amount',part->>'amount','retained_amount',((part->>'amount')::numeric-recipient_refund)::text,'basis',decision->>'basis'));
   end loop;
   if recipient_total<>refund then raise exception 'INS_CANCELLATION_INPUT: recipient refunds must equal the charge refund'; end if;
  end if;
  total_credit:=total_credit+refund+vat_refund;
  charge_results:=charge_results||jsonb_build_array(jsonb_build_object('charge_id',charge->>'id','kind',charge->>'kind',
   'refund_base',refund::text,'retained_base',(original_amount-refund)::text,'refund_vat',vat_refund::text,'retained_vat',(original_vat-vat_refund)::text,
   'basis',btrim(rule->>'basis'),'recipient_mode',charge->>'recipient_mode','recipient_insurer_id',charge->>'recipient_insurer_id',
   'vat_mode',charge->>'vat_mode','income_gl_account_id',charge->>'income_gl_account_id'));
 end loop;
 for calc in select value from jsonb_array_elements(insurers) loop
  select coalesce(sum((x->>'amount')::numeric),0) into pass_refund from jsonb_array_elements(pass_rows) x where x->>'insurer_id'=calc->>'insurer_id';
  final_insurers:=final_insurers||jsonb_build_array(calc||jsonb_build_object('pass_through_refund',pass_refund::text,
   'total_insurer_credit',((calc->>'net_insurer_credit')::numeric+pass_refund)::text));
 end loop;
 select count(*),sum(outstanding_amount) into item_count,unpaid from fin_open_item_balances where tenant_id=p.tenant_id and source_id=p.id and source_type='ins_policy_premium';
 if item_count<>1 or unpaid<0 or unpaid>invoice or total_credit>invoice then raise exception 'INS_CANCELLATION_HISTORY: policy receivable does not reconcile'; end if;
 if exists(select 1 from fin_credit_applications a join fin_credit_notes n on n.id=a.credit_note_id
   join fin_open_items i on i.id=a.open_item_id where i.tenant_id=p.tenant_id and i.source_type='ins_policy_premium' and i.source_id=p.id and n.status='posted') then
  raise exception 'INS_CANCELLATION_REVIEW_REQUIRED: existing policy credits require Finance review'; end if;
 return jsonb_build_object('stage','calculation_only','approval_status','not_approved','payment_authorized',false,
  'policy_id',p.id,'currency',p.currency,'effective_date',p_effective_date,'generated_at',now(),
  'cover_day_convention','inclusive cover dates; cancellation day is not earned cover',
  'insurers',final_insurers,'charges',charge_results,'pass_through_allocations',pass_rows,
  'invoice_credit',total_credit::text,'receivable_credit',least(total_credit,unpaid)::text,'client_refund_due',greatest(total_credit-unpaid,0)::text,
  'requires_finance_review',true,'requires_finance_exception',requires_exception,
  'funding_status','not_checked','notice','Calculation only. Cancellation approval and separately approved, funded cash payment are still required.');
end $function$
-- FN ins_preview_policy_endorsement(uuid,date,jsonb,jsonb)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.ins_preview_policy_endorsement(p_policy_id uuid, p_effective_date date, p_insurer_terms jsonb, p_changes jsonb)
 RETURNS jsonb
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
declare
  p ins_policies%rowtype;
  snap ins_policy_financial_snapshots%rowtype;
  shares jsonb;
  term jsonb;
  allocation jsonb;
  matched text := '';
  calc jsonb := '{}'::jsonb;
  rows_out jsonb := '[]'::jsonb;
  changes_out jsonb := '[]'::jsonb;
  field text;
  before_val jsonb;
  after_val jsonb;
  delta numeric;
  total_days integer;
  remaining_days integer;
  premium_delta numeric := 0;
  commission_delta numeric := 0;
  premium_zero boolean := true;
begin
  if auth.uid() is null or effective_user_id() is distinct from auth.uid()
     or not (can_manage_insurance() or is_finance_team_member('finance')) then
    raise exception 'INS_FORBIDDEN: Insurance manager or Finance acting as themselves required' using errcode = '42501';
  end if;
  select * into p from ins_policies where id = p_policy_id and tenant_id = get_my_tenant_id() for update;
  if not found then
    raise exception 'INS_NOT_FOUND: policy';
  end if;
  if p.status not in ('active','renewed') then
    raise exception 'INS_ENDORSEMENT_STATE: a bound policy is required (this one is %)', p.status;
  end if;
  if p_effective_date is null or p_effective_date > current_date then
    raise exception 'INS_ENDORSEMENT_INPUT: a non-future effective date is required';
  end if;
  if jsonb_typeof(p_insurer_terms) is distinct from 'array'
     or jsonb_array_length(p_insurer_terms) > 30 then
    raise exception 'INS_ENDORSEMENT_INPUT: insurer terms must be an array of at most 30 lines';
  end if;
  if p_changes is null or jsonb_typeof(p_changes) is distinct from 'object' then
    raise exception 'INS_ENDORSEMENT_INPUT: changes must be a JSON object';
  end if;
  -- The immutable bind-time shares, with the single-insurer fallback the
  -- cancellation preview uses.
  select * into snap from ins_policy_financial_snapshots where policy_id = p.id and tenant_id = p.tenant_id;
  shares := coalesce(snap.snapshot -> 'shares', '[]'::jsonb);
  if shares is null or shares = '[]'::jsonb then
    shares := jsonb_build_array(jsonb_build_object(
      'insurer_id', p.insurer_id, 'premium_amount', p.gross_premium,
      'commission_amount', p.commission_amount, 'commission_rate_pct', p.commission_rate_pct, 'share_pct', 100));
  end if;
  total_days := (p.expiry_date - p.inception_date);
  remaining_days := (p.expiry_date - p_effective_date);
  if remaining_days < 0 then
    raise exception 'INS_ENDORSEMENT_INPUT: the effective date is after the policy expires';
  end if;
  -- Per-insurer premium deltas. The insurer's agreed delta wins; pro-rata by
  -- the remaining days of the policy is the fallback. An insurer without a
  -- term simply takes no delta -- unlike a cancellation, an endorsement may
  -- concern one co-insurer only.
  for term in select value from jsonb_array_elements(p_insurer_terms) loop
    if term ->> 'insurer_id' is null then
      raise exception 'INS_ENDORSEMENT_INPUT: each term names its insurer';
    end if;
    for allocation in select value from jsonb_array_elements(shares) loop
      continue when allocation ->> 'insurer_id' is distinct from term ->> 'insurer_id';
      if matched like '%|' || (term ->> 'insurer_id') || '|%' then
        raise exception 'INS_ENDORSEMENT_INPUT: insurer % appears twice in the terms', term ->> 'insurer_id';
      end if;
      matched := matched || '|' || (term ->> 'insurer_id') || '|';
      if term ? 'agreed_delta' then
        delta := platform_ins_endorsement_money(term ->> 'agreed_delta');
      elsif term ->> 'method' = 'pro_rata' then
        delta := round(
          platform_ins_endorsement_money(term ->> 'annual_delta')
          * remaining_days / greatest(total_days, 1), 2);
      else
        raise exception 'INS_ENDORSEMENT_INPUT: a term is either an agreed_delta or pro_rata with annual_delta';
      end if;
      calc := jsonb_build_object(
        'insurer_id', term ->> 'insurer_id',
        'share_pct', allocation ->> 'share_pct',
        'share_premium', allocation ->> 'premium_amount',
        'basis', case when term ? 'agreed_delta' then 'agreed' else 'pro_rata' end,
        'input', case when term ? 'agreed_delta' then term ->> 'agreed_delta' else term ->> 'annual_delta' end,
        'premium_delta', delta,
        'commission_delta', round(delta * (p.commission_rate_pct / 100.0), 2),
        'rate_pct', p.commission_rate_pct,
        'requires_review', abs(delta) > (allocation ->> 'premium_amount')::numeric);
      rows_out := rows_out || calc;
      premium_delta := premium_delta + delta;
      commission_delta := commission_delta + (calc ->> 'commission_delta')::numeric;
    end loop;
    if not matched like '%|' || (term ->> 'insurer_id') || '|%' then
      raise exception 'INS_ENDORSEMENT_INPUT: insurer % is not on this policy', term ->> 'insurer_id';
    end if;
  end loop;
  premium_zero := premium_delta = 0;
  -- Descriptive changes ride along; only known fields, with before/after
  -- recorded (the policy row itself is never mutated).
  for field in select jsonb_object_keys(p_changes) loop
    if field not in ('sum_insured','risk_description','notes') then
      raise exception 'INS_ENDORSEMENT_INPUT: % is not an endorsable field', field;
    end if;
    after_val := p_changes -> field;
    if field = 'sum_insured' then
      if jsonb_typeof(after_val) is distinct from 'number'
         or (after_val)::numeric is null or (after_val)::numeric <= 0 then
        raise exception 'INS_ENDORSEMENT_INPUT: sum_insured must be a positive number';
      end if;
      before_val := to_jsonb(p.sum_insured);
    else
      if jsonb_typeof(after_val) is distinct from 'string'
         or length(btrim(after_val #>> '{}')) > 2000 then
        raise exception 'INS_ENDORSEMENT_INPUT: % must be text of at most 2000 characters', field;
      end if;
      before_val := to_jsonb(case when field = 'notes' then p.notes else p.risk_description end);
    end if;
    changes_out := changes_out || jsonb_build_object('field', field, 'before', before_val, 'after', after_val);
  end loop;
  if premium_zero and jsonb_array_length(changes_out) = 0 then
    raise exception 'INS_ENDORSEMENT_INPUT: an endorsement changes the premium or the policy record -- this one changes neither';
  end if;
  return jsonb_build_object(
    'policy_id', p.id, 'policy_no', p.policy_no, 'currency', p.currency,
    'inception_date', p.inception_date, 'expiry_date', p.expiry_date,
    'effective_date', p_effective_date,
    'total_days', total_days, 'remaining_days', remaining_days,
    'commission_rate_pct', p.commission_rate_pct,
    'shares', rows_out,
    'premium_delta', premium_delta,
    'commission_delta', commission_delta,
    'premium_zero', premium_zero,
    'changes', changes_out);
end $function$
-- FN ins_propose_policy_endorsement(uuid,date,jsonb,jsonb,text,uuid)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.ins_propose_policy_endorsement(p_policy_id uuid, p_effective_date date, p_insurer_terms jsonb, p_changes jsonb, p_reason text, p_request_key uuid)
 RETURNS ins_policy_endorsements
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
declare
  e ins_policy_endorsements%rowtype;
  quote jsonb;
begin
  if auth.uid() is null or effective_user_id() is distinct from auth.uid()
     or not (can_manage_insurance() or is_finance_team_member('finance')) then
    raise exception 'INS_FORBIDDEN: Insurance manager or Finance acting as themselves required' using errcode = '42501';
  end if;
  if p_request_key is null or p_reason is null or length(btrim(p_reason)) not between 5 and 2000 then
    raise exception 'INS_ENDORSEMENT_INPUT: request key and reason required';
  end if;
  -- Idempotent retry by key, with a payload-conflict refusal.
  select * into e from ins_policy_endorsements
   where tenant_id = get_my_tenant_id() and request_key = p_request_key;
  if found then
    if e.proposed_by <> auth.uid()
       or e.policy_id is distinct from p_policy_id
       or e.effective_date is distinct from p_effective_date
       or e.insurer_terms is distinct from p_insurer_terms
       or e.changes is distinct from coalesce(p_changes, '{}'::jsonb)
       or e.reason is distinct from btrim(p_reason) then
      raise exception 'INS_ENDORSEMENT_RETRY_CONFLICT: different payload';
    end if;
    return e;
  end if;
  perform id from ins_policies where id = p_policy_id and tenant_id = get_my_tenant_id() for update;
  if not found then
    raise exception 'INS_NOT_FOUND: policy';
  end if;
  if exists (select 1 from ins_policy_endorsements
              where policy_id = p_policy_id and status in ('proposed','prepared','posting')) then
    raise exception 'INS_ENDORSEMENT_EXISTS: a live endorsement already exists on this policy';
  end if;
  if exists (select 1 from ins_policy_cancellations
              where policy_id = p_policy_id and status in ('proposed','prepared','posting','posted')) then
    raise exception 'INS_ENDORSEMENT_CXL_LIVE: this policy has a cancellation in flight; endorse it another day';
  end if;
  quote := ins_preview_policy_endorsement(p_policy_id, p_effective_date, p_insurer_terms, p_changes);
  insert into ins_policy_endorsements
    (tenant_id, policy_id, request_key, reason, effective_date, insurer_terms, changes, proposed_by, calculation)
  values
    (get_my_tenant_id(), p_policy_id, p_request_key, btrim(p_reason), p_effective_date,
     p_insurer_terms, coalesce(p_changes, '{}'::jsonb), auth.uid(), quote)
  returning * into e;
  insert into ins_policy_endorsement_events(tenant_id, endorsement_id, event, actor_id)
    values (e.tenant_id, e.id, 'proposed', auth.uid());
  return e;
end $function$
-- FN ins_recognise_claim_receivable(uuid,text)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.ins_recognise_claim_receivable(p_claim_id uuid, p_insurer_confirmation text)
 RETURNS ins_claims
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
declare
  v_tenant uuid := get_my_tenant_id();
  v_claim  ins_claims%rowtype;
  v_insurer uuid;
  v_client  uuid;
  v_ccy     text;
  v_recv_gl uuid;
  v_pay_gl  uuid;
  v_ins_acc uuid;
  v_cli_acc uuid;
  v_desc    text;
  v_je      journal_entries%rowtype;
begin
  if not can_manage_insurance() then
    raise exception 'INS_FORBIDDEN: only insurance admins and managers recognise claim receivables' using errcode = '42501';
  end if;
  select * into v_claim from ins_claims where id = p_claim_id and tenant_id = v_tenant for update;
  if not found then
    raise exception 'INS_NOT_FOUND: claim';
  end if;
  if v_claim.status <> 'approved' then
    raise exception 'INS_CLAIM_STATE: only an approved claim can be recognised (this one is %)', v_claim.status;
  end if;
  if v_claim.payment_mode <> 'via_broker' then
    raise exception 'INS_CLAIM_MODE: only a claim paid through the brokerage is recognised here';
  end if;
  if v_claim.recognised_at is not null then
    raise exception 'INS_CLAIM_RECOGNISED: this claim is already recognised';
  end if;
  if nullif(btrim(p_insurer_confirmation), '') is null then
    raise exception 'INS_NOTE_REQUIRED: give the insurer''s written confirmation reference before recognising the receivable';
  end if;
  select p.insurer_id, p.client_id, p.currency into v_insurer, v_client, v_ccy from ins_policies p where p.id = v_claim.policy_id;
  v_recv_gl := get_posting_account(v_tenant, 'insurer_claim_receivable');
  v_pay_gl  := get_posting_account(v_tenant, 'client_claim_payable');
  if v_recv_gl is null or v_pay_gl is null then
    raise exception 'INS_POSTING_RULE_MISSING: this insurer pays claims through the brokerage, so the chart of accounts needs insurer_claim_receivable and client_claim_payable';
  end if;
  perform set_config('ins.via_rpc', 'on', true);
  v_ins_acc := ins_ensure_insurer_account(v_insurer);
  v_cli_acc := ins_ensure_client_account(v_client);
  v_desc := 'Claim ' || v_claim.claim_no || ' recognised: insurer confirmed payment through the brokerage';
  v_je := post_journal_entry(v_tenant, 'ins_claim_accrual', v_claim.id, current_date, v_desc, jsonb_build_array(
    jsonb_build_object('gl_account_id', v_recv_gl, 'debit',  v_claim.approved_amount, 'currency', v_ccy, 'description', v_desc),
    jsonb_build_object('gl_account_id', v_pay_gl,  'credit', v_claim.approved_amount, 'currency', v_ccy, 'description', v_desc)));
  -- The claim items hold the rate the accrual was booked at.
  perform fin_create_open_item_fx(v_tenant, v_ins_acc, 'insurer_claim_receivable', 'ins_claim_insurer', v_claim.id,
    v_claim.claim_no, current_date, current_date, v_claim.approved_amount, 'Claim funds due from the insurer', v_ccy, v_je.id);
  perform fin_create_open_item_fx(v_tenant, v_cli_acc, 'client_claim_payable', 'ins_claim_client', v_claim.id,
    v_claim.claim_no, current_date, current_date, v_claim.approved_amount, 'Claim proceeds due to the client', v_ccy, v_je.id);
  update ins_claims set recognised_at = now(), recognised_by = auth.uid()
   where id = v_claim.id returning * into v_claim;
  insert into ins_claim_events (tenant_id, claim_id, from_status, to_status, note)
  values (v_tenant, v_claim.id, 'approved', 'approved', 'Receivable recognised. Insurer confirmation: ' || btrim(p_insurer_confirmation));
  return v_claim;
end;
$function$
-- FN ins_record_claim_payment(uuid,numeric,date,text,text)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.ins_record_claim_payment(p_claim_id uuid, p_amount numeric, p_payment_date date, p_reference text, p_notes text DEFAULT NULL::text)
 RETURNS ins_claim_payments
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
declare
  v_tenant uuid := get_my_tenant_id();
  v_claim  ins_claims%rowtype;
  v_pay    ins_claim_payments%rowtype;
begin
  if not can_manage_insurance() then
    raise exception 'INS_FORBIDDEN: only insurance admins and managers record claim payments' using errcode = '42501';
  end if;
  select * into v_claim from ins_claims where id = p_claim_id and tenant_id = v_tenant for update;
  if not found then raise exception 'INS_NOT_FOUND: claim'; end if;
  if v_claim.status <> 'approved' then
    raise exception 'INS_CLAIM_STATE: payments are recorded on an approved claim (this one is %)', v_claim.status;
  end if;
  if v_claim.payment_mode <> 'insurer_direct' then
    raise exception 'INS_CLAIM_MODE: this claim is paid through the brokerage; record the insurer receipt and the client payout instead';
  end if;
  if p_amount is null or p_amount <= 0 then
    raise exception 'INS_AMOUNT: the payment amount must be greater than zero';
  end if;
  if p_amount > v_claim.approved_amount - v_claim.paid_amount then
    raise exception 'INS_AMOUNT: only % is still unpaid on this claim', v_claim.approved_amount - v_claim.paid_amount;
  end if;
  if p_payment_date is null or p_payment_date > current_date or p_payment_date < v_claim.notified_date then
    raise exception 'INS_DATE: the payment date must be between the notified date and today';
  end if;
  if nullif(btrim(p_reference), '') is null then
    raise exception 'INS_REFERENCE_REQUIRED: give the insurer payment reference, because nothing else in the system evidences this payment';
  end if;
  insert into ins_claim_payments (tenant_id, claim_id, kind, amount, payment_date, reference, notes)
  values (v_tenant, v_claim.id, 'insurer_direct', p_amount, p_payment_date, btrim(p_reference), nullif(btrim(p_notes), ''))
  returning * into v_pay;
  perform ins_claim_apply_payment_totals(v_claim.id, 'Paid in full by the insurer');
  return v_pay;
end;
$function$
-- FN ins_record_claim_payout(uuid,uuid,numeric,date,text,text)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.ins_record_claim_payout(p_claim_id uuid, p_bank_account_id uuid, p_amount numeric, p_payment_date date, p_reference text DEFAULT NULL::text, p_notes text DEFAULT NULL::text)
 RETURNS ins_claim_payments
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
declare
  v_tenant   uuid := get_my_tenant_id();
  v_claim    ins_claims%rowtype;
  v_kind     text;
  v_item     record;
  v_held     numeric;
  v_set      fin_settlements%rowtype;
  v_pay      ins_claim_payments%rowtype;
begin
  if not can_manage_insurance() then
    raise exception 'INS_FORBIDDEN: only insurance admins and managers pay out claims' using errcode = '42501';
  end if;
  select * into v_claim from ins_claims where id = p_claim_id and tenant_id = v_tenant for update;
  if not found then raise exception 'INS_NOT_FOUND: claim'; end if;
  if v_claim.status <> 'approved' then
    raise exception 'INS_CLAIM_STATE: payouts are made on an approved claim (this one is %)', v_claim.status;
  end if;
  if v_claim.payment_mode <> 'via_broker' then
    raise exception 'INS_CLAIM_MODE: this insurer pays the client directly; there is nothing for the brokerage to pay out';
  end if;
  if v_claim.recognised_at is null then
    raise exception 'INS_CLAIM_NOT_RECOGNISED: record the insurer''s confirmation first, so the payable is recognised';
  end if;
  if p_amount is null or p_amount <= 0 then
    raise exception 'INS_AMOUNT: the payout amount must be greater than zero';
  end if;
  select kind into v_kind from fin_bank_accounts where id = p_bank_account_id and tenant_id = v_tenant;
  if v_kind is distinct from 'client_money' then
    raise exception 'INS_BANK_KIND: claim money is paid out of a client-money bank account';
  end if;
  -- Held funds are counted per currency: only money that arrived in this
  -- account's currency satisfies a payout from it. The settlement engine
  -- refuses a cross-currency payout outright (SETTLEMENT_CURRENCY); this
  -- keeps the anti-fronting arithmetic honest when a claim ever carries
  -- payments in more than one currency.
  select coalesce(sum(p.amount) filter (where p.kind = 'insurer_receipt'), 0)
         - coalesce(sum(p.amount) filter (where p.kind = 'client_payout'), 0)
    into v_held
    from ins_claim_payments p
   where p.claim_id = v_claim.id
     and p.status = 'posted'
     and (select s.currency from fin_settlements s where s.id = p.settlement_id)
       = (select b.currency from fin_bank_accounts b where b.id = p_bank_account_id);
  if p_amount > v_held then
    raise exception 'INS_CLAIM_FUNDS_NOT_RECEIVED: only % has been received from the insurer in this account''s currency and not yet paid out; the brokerage does not pay a client ahead of the insurer', v_held;
  end if;
  select b.id, b.party_account_id, b.outstanding_amount into v_item
    from fin_open_item_balances b
   where b.tenant_id = v_tenant and b.source_type = 'ins_claim_client' and b.source_id = v_claim.id;
  if not found then raise exception 'INS_NOT_FOUND: the client item for this claim'; end if;
  if p_amount > v_item.outstanding_amount then
    raise exception 'INS_AMOUNT: only % is still owed to the client on this claim', v_item.outstanding_amount;
  end if;
  perform set_config('ins.via_rpc', 'on', true);
  v_set := platform_fin_record_settlement_impl(
    v_tenant, p_bank_account_id, 'out', v_item.party_account_id, p_amount, p_payment_date,
    jsonb_build_array(jsonb_build_object('open_item_id', v_item.id, 'amount', p_amount)),
    coalesce(nullif(btrim(p_reference), ''), v_claim.claim_no), nullif(btrim(p_notes), ''));
  insert into ins_claim_payments (tenant_id, claim_id, kind, amount, payment_date, reference, notes, settlement_id)
  values (v_tenant, v_claim.id, 'client_payout', p_amount, p_payment_date, nullif(btrim(p_reference), ''), nullif(btrim(p_notes), ''), v_set.id)
  returning * into v_pay;
  perform ins_claim_apply_payment_totals(v_claim.id, 'Paid in full to the client by the brokerage');
  return v_pay;
end;
$function$
-- FN ins_record_claim_receipt(uuid,uuid,numeric,date,text,text)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.ins_record_claim_receipt(p_claim_id uuid, p_bank_account_id uuid, p_amount numeric, p_receipt_date date, p_reference text DEFAULT NULL::text, p_notes text DEFAULT NULL::text)
 RETURNS ins_claim_payments
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
declare
  v_tenant uuid := get_my_tenant_id();
  v_claim  ins_claims%rowtype;
  v_kind   text;
  v_item   record;
  v_set    fin_settlements%rowtype;
  v_pay    ins_claim_payments%rowtype;
begin
  if not can_manage_insurance() then
    raise exception 'INS_FORBIDDEN: only insurance admins and managers record claim receipts' using errcode = '42501';
  end if;
  select * into v_claim from ins_claims where id = p_claim_id and tenant_id = v_tenant for update;
  if not found then raise exception 'INS_NOT_FOUND: claim'; end if;
  if v_claim.status <> 'approved' then
    raise exception 'INS_CLAIM_STATE: receipts are recorded on an approved claim (this one is %)', v_claim.status;
  end if;
  if v_claim.payment_mode <> 'via_broker' then
    raise exception 'INS_CLAIM_MODE: this insurer pays the client directly; record the insurer payment instead';
  end if;
  if v_claim.recognised_at is null then
    raise exception 'INS_CLAIM_NOT_RECOGNISED: record the insurer''s confirmation first, so the receivable is recognised';
  end if;
  if p_amount is null or p_amount <= 0 then
    raise exception 'INS_AMOUNT: the receipt amount must be greater than zero';
  end if;
  select kind into v_kind from fin_bank_accounts where id = p_bank_account_id and tenant_id = v_tenant;
  if v_kind is distinct from 'client_money' then
    raise exception 'INS_BANK_KIND: claim money is received into a client-money bank account';
  end if;
  select b.id, b.party_account_id, b.outstanding_amount into v_item
    from fin_open_item_balances b
   where b.tenant_id = v_tenant and b.source_type = 'ins_claim_insurer' and b.source_id = v_claim.id;
  if not found then raise exception 'INS_NOT_FOUND: the insurer item for this claim'; end if;
  if p_amount > v_item.outstanding_amount then
    raise exception 'INS_AMOUNT: the insurer still owes only % on this claim', v_item.outstanding_amount;
  end if;
  perform set_config('ins.via_rpc', 'on', true);
  v_set := platform_fin_record_settlement_impl(
    v_tenant, p_bank_account_id, 'in', v_item.party_account_id, p_amount, p_receipt_date,
    jsonb_build_array(jsonb_build_object('open_item_id', v_item.id, 'amount', p_amount)),
    coalesce(nullif(btrim(p_reference), ''), v_claim.claim_no), nullif(btrim(p_notes), ''));
  insert into ins_claim_payments (tenant_id, claim_id, kind, amount, payment_date, reference, notes, settlement_id)
  values (v_tenant, v_claim.id, 'insurer_receipt', p_amount, p_receipt_date, nullif(btrim(p_reference), ''), nullif(btrim(p_notes), ''), v_set.id)
  returning * into v_pay;
  return v_pay;
end;
$function$
-- FN ins_record_insurer_statement(uuid,text,date,date,jsonb,text)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.ins_record_insurer_statement(p_insurer_id uuid, p_statement_ref text, p_period_from date, p_period_to date, p_lines jsonb, p_notes text DEFAULT NULL::text)
 RETURNS ins_insurer_statements
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
declare
  v_tenant uuid := public.get_my_tenant_id();
  v_stmt public.ins_insurer_statements;
  l jsonb;
begin
  if v_tenant is null or not public.can_manage_insurance() then
    raise exception 'INS_ACCESS_DENIED' using errcode = '42501';
  end if;
  if not exists (
    select 1 from public.ins_insurers i
    where i.tenant_id = v_tenant and i.id = p_insurer_id
  ) then
    raise exception 'INS_INSURER_NOT_FOUND';
  end if;
  if coalesce(jsonb_array_length(p_lines), 0) = 0 then
    raise exception 'INS_STATEMENT_NO_LINES';
  end if;
  set local ins.via_rpc = 'on';
  insert into public.ins_insurer_statements
    (tenant_id, insurer_id, statement_ref, period_from, period_to, notes, created_by)
  values
    (v_tenant, p_insurer_id, p_statement_ref, p_period_from, p_period_to, p_notes, auth.uid())
  returning * into v_stmt;
  for l in select * from jsonb_array_elements(p_lines)
  loop
    -- every line's policy must belong to this tenant
    if not exists (
      select 1 from public.ins_policies p
      where p.tenant_id = v_tenant and p.id = (l->>'policy_id')::uuid
    ) then
      raise exception 'INS_STATEMENT_FOREIGN_POLICY';
    end if;
    insert into public.ins_insurer_statement_lines
      (tenant_id, statement_id, policy_id, premium, commission, claims_paid)
    values (
      v_tenant, v_stmt.id, (l->>'policy_id')::uuid,
      coalesce((l->>'premium')::numeric, 0),
      coalesce((l->>'commission')::numeric, 0),
      coalesce((l->>'claims_paid')::numeric, 0)
    );
  end loop;
  reset ins.via_rpc;
  return v_stmt;
end;
$function$
-- FN ins_record_renewal_outcome(uuid,text,text)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.ins_record_renewal_outcome(p_policy_id uuid, p_outcome text, p_reason text)
 RETURNS ins_renewal_outcomes
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
declare
  v_tenant uuid := get_my_tenant_id();
  v_pol    ins_policies%rowtype;
  v_row    ins_renewal_outcomes%rowtype;
begin
  if not can_access_insurance() then
    raise exception 'INS_FORBIDDEN: insurance access required' using errcode = '42501';
  end if;
  if p_outcome is null or p_outcome not in ('lost', 'declined', 'lapsed') then
    raise exception 'INS_OUTCOME_INVALID: choose lost, declined or lapsed';
  end if;
  if p_reason is null or char_length(btrim(p_reason)) < 5 then
    raise exception 'INS_OUTCOME_REASON: give a reason of at least 5 characters';
  end if;
  select * into v_pol from ins_policies where id = p_policy_id and tenant_id = v_tenant for update;
  if not found then
    raise exception 'INS_NOT_FOUND: policy';
  end if;
  if v_pol.status <> 'active' then
    raise exception 'INS_RENEWAL_STATE: only an active policy can have a renewal outcome (this one is %)', v_pol.status;
  end if;
