  if exists (select 1 from ins_policies where renewal_of_id = v_pol.id and status = 'draft') then
    raise exception 'INS_RENEWAL_EXISTS: a renewal draft is already open for %; finish or discard it first', v_pol.policy_no;
  end if;
  perform set_config('ins.via_rpc', 'on', true);
  delete from ins_renewal_outcomes where tenant_id = v_tenant and policy_id = v_pol.id;
  insert into ins_renewal_outcomes (tenant_id, policy_id, outcome, reason)
  values (v_tenant, v_pol.id, p_outcome, btrim(p_reason))
  returning * into v_row;
  return v_row;
end $function$
-- FN ins_register_client(text,text,text,text,text,text)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.ins_register_client(p_name text, p_client_type text DEFAULT 'corporate'::text, p_tax_id text DEFAULT NULL::text, p_email text DEFAULT NULL::text, p_phone text DEFAULT NULL::text, p_risk_rating text DEFAULT 'medium'::text)
 RETURNS ins_clients
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
declare
  v_tenant uuid := get_my_tenant_id();
  v_bd_id  uuid;
  v_row    ins_clients%rowtype;
begin
  if not can_access_insurance() then
    raise exception 'INS_FORBIDDEN: insurance access required' using errcode = '42501';
  end if;
  if nullif(btrim(p_name), '') is null then
    raise exception 'INS_REQUIRED: client name is required';
  end if;
  insert into bd_clients (tenant_id, name, email, phone, created_by)
  values (v_tenant, btrim(p_name), nullif(btrim(p_email), ''), nullif(btrim(p_phone), ''), auth.uid())
  returning id into v_bd_id;
  perform set_config('ins.via_rpc', 'on', true);
  insert into ins_clients (tenant_id, client_id, client_type, tax_id, risk_rating, relationship_owner_id)
  values (v_tenant, v_bd_id, p_client_type, nullif(btrim(p_tax_id), ''), p_risk_rating, auth.uid())
  returning * into v_row;
  return v_row;
end;
$function$
-- FN ins_register_risk(uuid,uuid,text,text,text,text,text,text,text,integer,numeric)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.ins_register_risk(p_client_id uuid, p_product_line_id uuid, p_risk_type text, p_description text, p_reg_no text DEFAULT NULL::text, p_chassis_no text DEFAULT NULL::text, p_engine_no text DEFAULT NULL::text, p_make text DEFAULT NULL::text, p_model text DEFAULT NULL::text, p_year_made integer DEFAULT NULL::integer, p_sum_insured numeric DEFAULT 0)
 RETURNS uuid
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'pg_catalog', 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
declare t uuid := get_my_tenant_id(); needs_cert boolean; new_id uuid;
begin
  if not can_manage_insurance() then raise exception 'INS_FORBIDDEN: Insurance manager required' using errcode = '42501'; end if;
  if not exists (select 1 from ins_clients where id = p_client_id and tenant_id = t) then raise exception 'INS_NOT_FOUND: client'; end if;
  perform 1 from ins_product_lines where id = p_product_line_id and tenant_id = t and is_active;
  if not found then raise exception 'INS_NOT_FOUND: active product line'; end if;
  needs_cert := public.platform_ins_line_needs_certificate(t, p_product_line_id);
  if needs_cert and p_risk_type <> 'vehicle' then
    raise exception 'INS_RISK_TYPE: a motor product line needs a vehicle risk';
  end if;
  if needs_cert and nullif(btrim(coalesce(p_chassis_no, '')), '') is null then
    raise exception 'INS_RISK_INPUT: a vehicle needs a chassis number';
  end if;
  begin
    insert into ins_risks (tenant_id, client_id, product_line_id, risk_type, description, reg_no, chassis_no, engine_no,
      make, model, year_made, sum_insured)
    values (t, p_client_id, p_product_line_id, p_risk_type, btrim(p_description),
      nullif(btrim(coalesce(p_reg_no, '')), ''), nullif(btrim(coalesce(p_chassis_no, '')), ''),
      nullif(btrim(coalesce(p_engine_no, '')), ''), nullif(btrim(coalesce(p_make, '')), ''),
      nullif(btrim(coalesce(p_model, '')), ''), p_year_made, coalesce(p_sum_insured, 0))
    returning id into new_id;
  exception when unique_violation then
    raise exception 'INS_DUPLICATE: a risk with this chassis number already exists';
  end;
  return new_id;
end $function$
-- FN ins_remittance_due_date(uuid,date)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.ins_remittance_due_date(p_tenant_id uuid, p_receipt_date date)
 RETURNS date
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 STABLE SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
declare
  v_days numeric;
  v_due  date;
begin
  if p_receipt_date is null then
    return null;
  end if;
  begin
    v_days := platform_reg_number(p_tenant_id, 'remittance.deadline_working_days', p_receipt_date);
  exception when others then
    if sqlerrm like 'REG_PARAM_MISSING%' then
      return null;   -- no approved value: no deadline, and no default is assumed
    end if;
    raise;
  end;
  if v_days is null or v_days < 0 or v_days <> trunc(v_days) then
    raise exception 'REG_VALUE_INVALID: remittance deadline must be a whole number of working days';
  end if;
  if v_days = 0 then
    return p_receipt_date;
  end if;
  select (p_receipt_date + g.n) into v_due
    from generate_series(1, 120) as g(n)
   where extract(isodow from p_receipt_date + g.n) < 6
   order by g.n
   offset (v_days::int - 1)
   limit 1;
  return v_due;
end;
$function$
-- FN ins_renewal_notification_sweep()
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.ins_renewal_notification_sweep()
 RETURNS integer
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
declare
  v_tenant  uuid := get_my_tenant_id();
  v_recips  uuid[];
  v_kinds   text[] := '{}';
  v_nos     text[] := '{}';
  v_ins     integer;
  v_total   integer := 0;
  r         record;
  k         text;
  v_list    text[];
  v_count   integer;
  v_shown   text;
begin
  if auth.uid() is null or v_tenant is null
     or not has_module_role('insurance', array['admin', 'manager', 'member']) then
    return 0;
  end if;
  -- Nobody to tell: leave the markers alone so the policies are picked up
  -- once a manager exists, rather than marking them as sent.
  v_recips := ins_notify_insurance_users(v_tenant, array['admin', 'manager']);
  if coalesce(array_length(v_recips, 1), 0) = 0 then
    return 0;
  end if;
  for r in
    select p.id, p.policy_no,
      case
        when p.expiry_date < current_date              then 'lapsed'
        when p.expiry_date - current_date <= 7         then 'renewal_7'
        when p.expiry_date - current_date <= 30        then 'renewal_30'
        else 'renewal_60'
      end as kind
    from ins_policies p
    where p.tenant_id = v_tenant
      and p.status = 'active'
      and p.expiry_date >= current_date - 30
      and p.expiry_date <= current_date + 60
      and not exists (select 1 from ins_policies d
                      where d.tenant_id = p.tenant_id and d.renewal_of_id = p.id and d.status = 'draft')
      and not exists (select 1 from ins_renewal_outcomes o
                      where o.tenant_id = p.tenant_id and o.policy_id = p.id)
      and not exists (select 1 from ins_policy_cancellations c
                      where c.tenant_id = p.tenant_id and c.policy_id = p.id
                        and c.status in ('proposed', 'prepared', 'posting', 'posted'))
    order by p.expiry_date, p.policy_no
  loop
    -- A policy first seen inside a tighter window skips the looser notices.
    if r.kind = 'renewal_7' then
      insert into ins_notification_marks (tenant_id, policy_id, kind)
      values (v_tenant, r.id, 'renewal_30'), (v_tenant, r.id, 'renewal_60')
      on conflict do nothing;
    elsif r.kind = 'renewal_30' then
      insert into ins_notification_marks (tenant_id, policy_id, kind)
      values (v_tenant, r.id, 'renewal_60')
      on conflict do nothing;
    end if;
    insert into ins_notification_marks (tenant_id, policy_id, kind)
    values (v_tenant, r.id, r.kind)
    on conflict do nothing;
    get diagnostics v_ins = row_count;
    if v_ins = 1 then
      v_kinds := v_kinds || r.kind;
      v_nos   := v_nos || r.policy_no;
    end if;
  end loop;
  foreach k in array array['renewal_7', 'renewal_30', 'renewal_60', 'lapsed'] loop
    select coalesce(array_agg(t.n order by t.ord), '{}') into v_list
    from unnest(v_kinds, v_nos) with ordinality as t(kind, n, ord)
    where t.kind = k;
    v_count := coalesce(array_length(v_list, 1), 0);
    continue when v_count = 0;
    v_total := v_total + v_count;
    v_shown := array_to_string(v_list[1:5], ', ')
               || case when v_count > 5 then format(' and %s more', v_count - 5) else '' end;
    perform ins_notify(
      v_tenant, v_recips, '{}'::uuid[],
      case when k = 'lapsed' then 'ins_policy_lapsed' else 'ins_renewal_due' end,
      case k
        when 'renewal_7'  then 'Renewals due within 7 days'
        when 'renewal_30' then 'Renewals due within 30 days'
        when 'renewal_60' then 'Renewals due within 60 days'
        else 'Policies lapsed without renewal'
      end,
      case when k = 'lapsed'
           then format('%s %s expired in the last 30 days with no renewal, outcome or cancellation: %s.',
                       v_count, case when v_count = 1 then 'policy' else 'policies' end, v_shown)
           else format('%s %s expire%s %s: %s.',
                       v_count, case when v_count = 1 then 'policy' else 'policies' end,
                       case when v_count = 1 then 's' else '' end,
                       case k when 'renewal_7' then 'within 7 days'
                              when 'renewal_30' then 'within 30 days'
                              else 'within 60 days' end,
                       v_shown)
      end,
      '/insurance/renewals');
  end loop;
  return v_total;
end;
$function$
-- FN ins_renewal_outcomes_guard()
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.ins_renewal_outcomes_guard()
 RETURNS trigger
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
begin
  if not public.ins_via_rpc() then
    raise exception 'INS_RPC_ONLY: record renewal outcomes with ins_record_renewal_outcome()' using errcode = '42501';
  end if;
  if tg_op = 'UPDATE' then
    raise exception 'INS_OUTCOME_IMMUTABLE: a renewal outcome is not edited; record a new one or clear it';
  end if;
  if tg_op = 'DELETE' then
    return old;
  end if;
  return new;
end $function$
-- FN ins_reversal_complete_guard()
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.ins_reversal_complete_guard()
 RETURNS trigger
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
declare r ins_policy_reversals%rowtype;
begin
  select * into r from ins_policy_reversals where id=new.id;
  if r.outcome<>'posted' or r.premium_credit_note_id is null or r.insurer_credit_note_id is null
    or r.premium_journal_entry_id is null or r.insurer_journal_entry_id is null
    or r.reissue_requested is distinct from (r.replacement_policy_id is not null)
    or not exists(select 1 from ins_policies where id=r.original_policy_id and status='reversed') then
    raise exception 'INS_REVERSAL_INCOMPLETE: reversal must be complete at commit';
  end if;
  return null;
end $function$
-- FN ins_reversal_immutable_guard()
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.ins_reversal_immutable_guard()
 RETURNS trigger
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
begin
  if old.outcome='posted' then raise exception 'INS_REVERSAL_IMMUTABLE: reversal audit cannot be changed'; end if;
  return new;
end $function$
-- FN ins_reversal_journal_void_guard()
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.ins_reversal_journal_void_guard()
 RETURNS trigger
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
begin
  if new.status is distinct from old.status and (
    old.source_type='ins_policy_bind' or exists(select 1 from ins_policy_reversals
      where old.id in (premium_journal_entry_id,insurer_journal_entry_id))) then
    raise exception 'INS_REVERSAL_JOURNAL_LOCKED: use the policy workflow, not independent journal voids';
  end if;
  return new;
end $function$
-- FN ins_reversal_note_void_guard()
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.ins_reversal_note_void_guard()
 RETURNS trigger
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
begin
  if new.status is distinct from old.status and exists(select 1 from ins_policy_reversals
    where old.id in (premium_credit_note_id,insurer_credit_note_id)) then
    raise exception 'INS_REVERSAL_NOTE_LOCKED: reversal credit notes cannot be voided independently';
  end if;
  return new;
end $function$
-- FN ins_reverse_policy(uuid,text,boolean,uuid)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.ins_reverse_policy(p_policy_id uuid, p_reason text, p_reissue boolean, p_request_key uuid)
 RETURNS ins_policy_reversals
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
declare
  p ins_policies%rowtype; r ins_policy_reversals%rowtype; c jsonb;
  cn fin_credit_notes%rowtype; draft ins_policies%rowtype;
  old_flag text := coalesce(current_setting('ins.via_rpc',true),'');
begin
  if not can_manage_insurance() or effective_user_id() is distinct from auth.uid() then
    raise exception 'INS_FORBIDDEN: insurance manager required; user-level impersonation cannot reverse policies' using errcode='42501';
  end if;
  if p_request_key is null or p_reissue is null or p_reason is null or length(btrim(p_reason))<5 then
    raise exception 'INS_REVERSAL_INPUT: request key, reissue choice and a reason of at least 5 characters are required';
  end if;
  select * into p from ins_policies where id=p_policy_id and tenant_id=get_my_tenant_id() for update;
  if not found then raise exception 'INS_NOT_FOUND: policy'; end if;
  select * into r from ins_policy_reversals where original_policy_id=p.id;
  if found then
    if r.request_key=p_request_key and r.reason=btrim(p_reason) and r.reissue_requested=p_reissue and r.outcome='posted' then
      return r;
    end if;
    raise exception 'INS_REVERSAL_DUPLICATE: policy already reversed with a different request';
  end if;
  if exists(select 1 from ins_policy_reversals where tenant_id=p.tenant_id and request_key=p_request_key) then
    raise exception 'INS_REVERSAL_DUPLICATE: request key already used for another policy';
  end if;
  c:=ins_policy_reversal_context(p.id);
  insert into ins_policy_reversals(tenant_id,original_policy_id,request_key,reason,reissue_requested,
    accounting_treatment,original_journal_entry_id)
  values(p.tenant_id,p.id,p_request_key,btrim(p_reason),p_reissue,c->>'treatment',p.journal_entry_id)
  returning * into r;
  cn:=platform_fin_raise_policy_credit_note_impl(p.tenant_id,'ar_control',(c->>'client_party_id')::uuid,
    p.gross_premium,current_date,(c->>'offset_id')::uuid,
    jsonb_build_array(jsonb_build_object('open_item_id',c->>'premium_item_id','amount',p.gross_premium)),
    btrim(p_reason),p.policy_no,p.id);
  update ins_policy_reversals set premium_credit_note_id=cn.id,premium_journal_entry_id=cn.journal_entry_id where id=r.id;
  cn:=platform_fin_raise_policy_credit_note_impl(p.tenant_id,'insurer_payable',(c->>'insurer_party_id')::uuid,
    p.net_premium_to_insurer,current_date,(c->>'offset_id')::uuid,
    jsonb_build_array(jsonb_build_object('open_item_id',c->>'insurer_item_id','amount',p.net_premium_to_insurer)),
    btrim(p_reason),p.policy_no,p.id);
  update ins_policy_reversals set insurer_credit_note_id=cn.id,insurer_journal_entry_id=cn.journal_entry_id where id=r.id;
  -- Compare the aggregate inverse by actual account, not just total balance.
  if exists(
    select gl_account_id from journal_entry_lines
    where journal_entry_id in (p.journal_entry_id,
      (select premium_journal_entry_id from ins_policy_reversals where id=r.id),cn.journal_entry_id)
    group by gl_account_id having sum(debit-credit)<>0
  ) then raise exception 'INS_REVERSAL_BALANCE: reversal does not negate the original bind'; end if;
  if p_reissue then
    draft:=ins_create_policy(p.client_id,p.insurer_id,p.product_line_id,p.inception_date,p.expiry_date,
      p.sum_insured,p.currency,p.gross_premium,p.commission_rate_pct,p.risk_description,p.notes);
    update ins_policy_reversals set replacement_policy_id=draft.id where id=r.id;
  end if;
  update ins_policy_reversals set outcome='posted' where id=r.id returning * into r;
  perform set_config('ins.via_rpc','on',true);
  update ins_policies set status='reversed' where id=p.id;
  perform set_config('ins.via_rpc',old_flag,true);
  perform log_platform_event('insurance.policy.reverse',p.tenant_id,'ins_policy',p.id::text,btrim(p_reason),
    jsonb_build_object('status','active','journal',p.journal_entry_id),to_jsonb(r));
  return r;
end $function$
-- FN ins_reversed_policy_guard()
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.ins_reversed_policy_guard()
 RETURNS trigger
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
begin
  if old.status='reversed' and new.status is distinct from old.status then
    raise exception 'INS_REVERSAL_LOCKED: a reversed policy cannot be reactivated';
  end if;
  if new.status='reversed' and old.status<>'reversed' and not exists(
    select 1 from ins_policy_reversals where original_policy_id=new.id and tenant_id=new.tenant_id and outcome='posted'
  ) then raise exception 'INS_REVERSAL_RPC_ONLY: use ins_reverse_policy'; end if;
  return new;
end $function$
-- FN ins_revise_claim_reserve(uuid,numeric,text)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.ins_revise_claim_reserve(p_claim_id uuid, p_new_amount numeric, p_reason text)
 RETURNS ins_claims
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
declare
  v_tenant uuid := get_my_tenant_id();
  v_claim  ins_claims%rowtype;
  v_sum    numeric;
  v_new    numeric(18,2);
begin
  if not can_access_insurance() then
    raise exception 'INS_FORBIDDEN: insurance access required' using errcode = '42501';
  end if;
  if p_new_amount is null or p_new_amount < 0 then
    raise exception 'INS_AMOUNT: the reserve cannot be negative';
  end if;
  if p_reason is null or char_length(btrim(p_reason)) < 5 then
    raise exception 'INS_RESERVE_REASON: give a reason of at least 5 characters';
  end if;
  v_new := round(p_new_amount, 2);
  select * into v_claim from ins_claims where id = p_claim_id and tenant_id = v_tenant for update;
  if not found then
    raise exception 'INS_NOT_FOUND: claim';
  end if;
  if v_claim.status not in ('notified', 'assessing', 'approved') then
    raise exception 'INS_CLAIM_STATE: the reserve of a % claim cannot be revised', v_claim.status;
  end if;
  if v_new = v_claim.reserve_amount then
    raise exception 'INS_RESERVE_UNCHANGED: the reserve is already %', v_claim.reserve_amount;
  end if;
  select sum_insured into v_sum from ins_policies where id = v_claim.policy_id and tenant_id = v_tenant;
  if coalesce(v_sum, 0) > 0 and v_new > v_sum then
    raise exception 'INS_AMOUNT: the reserve cannot exceed the policy sum insured';
  end if;
  perform set_config('ins.via_rpc', 'on', true);
  insert into ins_claim_reserve_history (tenant_id, claim_id, kind, previous_amount, new_amount, reason)
  values (v_tenant, v_claim.id, 'revision', v_claim.reserve_amount, v_new, btrim(p_reason));
  update ins_claims set reserve_amount = v_new where id = v_claim.id returning * into v_claim;
  return v_claim;
end $function$
-- FN ins_save_intermediary(uuid,text,text,text,text,text,text,uuid,text,text,date,date,text,text,text)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.ins_save_intermediary(p_id uuid DEFAULT NULL::uuid, p_code text DEFAULT NULL::text, p_kind text DEFAULT 'business'::text, p_name text DEFAULT NULL::text, p_category text DEFAULT NULL::text, p_contact_email text DEFAULT NULL::text, p_contact_phone text DEFAULT NULL::text, p_parent_id uuid DEFAULT NULL::uuid, p_licence_ref text DEFAULT NULL::text, p_licence_authority text DEFAULT NULL::text, p_licence_valid_from date DEFAULT NULL::date, p_licence_valid_to date DEFAULT NULL::date, p_appointment_ref text DEFAULT NULL::text, p_authorisation_basis text DEFAULT NULL::text, p_authorisation_reference text DEFAULT NULL::text)
 RETURNS ins_intermediaries
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
declare
  v_tenant  uuid := get_my_tenant_id();
  v_row     ins_intermediaries%rowtype;
  v_old     ins_intermediaries%rowtype;
  v_parent  ins_intermediaries%rowtype;
  v_code    text := nullif(btrim(p_code), '');
  v_name    text := nullif(btrim(p_name), '');
  v_ref     text := nullif(btrim(p_licence_ref), '');
  v_auth    text := nullif(btrim(p_licence_authority), '');
  v_appt    text := nullif(btrim(p_appointment_ref), '');
  v_basis   text := nullif(btrim(p_authorisation_basis), '');
  v_aref    text := nullif(btrim(p_authorisation_reference), '');
  v_email   text := nullif(btrim(p_contact_email), '');
  v_phone   text := nullif(btrim(p_contact_phone), '');
  v_changed boolean := false;
  v_status_before text;
begin
  if not can_manage_insurance() then
    raise exception 'INS_FORBIDDEN: only insurance admins and managers maintain intermediaries' using errcode = '42501';
  end if;
  if p_id is not null then
    select * into v_old from ins_intermediaries where id = p_id and tenant_id = v_tenant for update;
    if not found then
      raise exception 'INS_NOT_FOUND: intermediary';
    end if;
    if v_old.status = 'terminated' then
      raise exception 'INS_TERMINATED: a terminated intermediary cannot be changed';
    end if;
  end if;
  if v_code is null then raise exception 'INS_REQUIRED: give the intermediary a reference code'; end if;
  if v_name is null then raise exception 'INS_REQUIRED: give the intermediary a name'; end if;
  if p_kind not in ('individual', 'business') then raise exception 'INS_INVALID: kind must be individual or business'; end if;
  if p_category not in ('insurer_appointed_agent', 'independent_broker', 'sub_agent', 'referral_partner') then
    raise exception 'INS_INVALID: unknown category %', p_category;
  end if;
  -- Credentials. A referral partner needs none. Every other category needs a licence.
  if p_category <> 'referral_partner' then
    if v_ref is null or v_auth is null or p_licence_valid_to is null then
      raise exception 'INS_REQUIRED: a licence needs its reference, issuing authority and expiry date';
    end if;
    if p_licence_valid_to < current_date then
      raise exception 'INS_LICENCE_EXPIRED: the licence has already expired';
    end if;
    if p_licence_valid_from is not null and p_licence_valid_to < p_licence_valid_from then
      raise exception 'INS_INVALID: the licence expiry is before its start date';
    end if;
  end if;
  if p_category = 'insurer_appointed_agent' and v_appt is null then
    raise exception 'INS_REQUIRED: an insurer-appointed agent needs the insurer''s appointment reference';
  end if;
  -- Parent link.
  if p_category = 'sub_agent' then
    if p_parent_id is null then
      raise exception 'INS_PARENT_REQUIRED: a sub-agent must be placed under a parent intermediary';
    end if;
    if v_basis is null or v_aref is null then
      raise exception 'INS_AUTHORISATION_REQUIRED: record the basis on which the parent may appoint this sub-agent, and its reference';
    end if;
  end if;
  if p_parent_id is not null then
    select * into v_parent from ins_intermediaries where id = p_parent_id and tenant_id = v_tenant;
    if not found then
      raise exception 'INS_NOT_FOUND: parent intermediary';
    end if;
    if v_parent.status <> 'active' then
      raise exception 'INS_PARENT_NOT_ACTIVE: the parent must be verified and active before it can be linked (it is %)', v_parent.status;
    end if;
    if v_parent.category = 'referral_partner' then
      raise exception 'INS_PARENT_CATEGORY: a referral partner cannot appoint or be a parent';
    end if;
    if p_id is not null and ins_intermediary_chain_has(v_tenant, p_parent_id, p_id) then
      raise exception 'INS_CYCLE: that parent sits below this intermediary in the chain';
    end if;
  end if;
  if p_id is null then
    insert into ins_intermediaries (
      tenant_id, code, kind, name, category, contact_email, contact_phone, parent_id,
      licence_ref, licence_authority, licence_valid_from, licence_valid_to, appointment_ref,
      authorisation_basis, authorisation_reference, status, last_changed_by, last_changed_at)
    values (
      v_tenant, v_code, p_kind, v_name, p_category, v_email, v_phone, p_parent_id,
      v_ref, v_auth, p_licence_valid_from, p_licence_valid_to, v_appt,
      v_basis, v_aref, 'pending_verification', auth.uid(), now())
    returning * into v_row;
    insert into ins_intermediary_events (tenant_id, intermediary_id, action, to_status, note)
    values (v_tenant, v_row.id, 'created', 'pending_verification', null);
    return v_row;
  end if;
  -- Update. Any change to a credential, category or parent means it must be verified again.
  v_changed := (v_old.category, v_old.parent_id, v_old.licence_ref, v_old.licence_authority,
                v_old.licence_valid_from, v_old.licence_valid_to, v_old.appointment_ref,
                v_old.authorisation_basis, v_old.authorisation_reference)
               is distinct from
               (p_category, p_parent_id, v_ref, v_auth, p_licence_valid_from, p_licence_valid_to, v_appt,
                v_basis, v_aref);
  v_status_before := v_old.status;
  update ins_intermediaries set
    code = v_code, kind = p_kind, name = v_name, category = p_category,
    contact_email = v_email, contact_phone = v_phone, parent_id = p_parent_id,
    licence_ref = v_ref, licence_authority = v_auth,
    licence_valid_from = p_licence_valid_from, licence_valid_to = p_licence_valid_to,
    appointment_ref = v_appt, authorisation_basis = v_basis, authorisation_reference = v_aref,
    status = case when v_changed then 'pending_verification' else status end,
    verified_by = case when v_changed then null else verified_by end,
    verified_at = case when v_changed then null else verified_at end,
    last_changed_by = auth.uid(), last_changed_at = now(), updated_at = now()
  where id = p_id and tenant_id = v_tenant
  returning * into v_row;
  if v_changed then
    insert into ins_intermediary_events (tenant_id, intermediary_id, action, from_status, to_status, note)
    values (v_tenant, v_row.id, 'changed', v_status_before, 'pending_verification',
            'Credentials, category or parent changed: verification required again');
  else
    insert into ins_intermediary_events (tenant_id, intermediary_id, action, from_status, to_status, note)
    values (v_tenant, v_row.id, 'edited', v_status_before, v_status_before, 'Details edited');
  end if;
  return v_row;
end;
$function$
-- FN ins_set_claim_document(uuid,text,text,text)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.ins_set_claim_document(p_claim_id uuid, p_doc_type text, p_status text, p_note text DEFAULT NULL::text)
 RETURNS ins_claim_documents
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
declare
  v_tenant uuid := public.get_my_tenant_id();
  v_doc public.ins_claim_documents;
begin
  if v_tenant is null or not public.can_access_insurance() then
    raise exception 'INS_ACCESS_DENIED' using errcode = '42501';
  end if;
  if p_status not in ('missing', 'provided') then
    raise exception 'INS_CLAIM_DOC_BAD_STATUS';  -- 'verified' is manager-only, below
  end if;
  if btrim(p_doc_type) = '' then
    raise exception 'INS_CLAIM_DOC_TYPE_REQUIRED';
  end if;
  if not exists (
    select 1 from public.ins_claims c
    where c.tenant_id = v_tenant and c.id = p_claim_id
  ) then
    raise exception 'INS_CLAIM_NOT_FOUND';
  end if;
  set local ins.via_rpc = 'on';
  insert into public.ins_claim_documents (tenant_id, claim_id, doc_type, status, note, updated_by)
  values (v_tenant, p_claim_id, p_doc_type, p_status, p_note, auth.uid())
  on conflict (tenant_id, claim_id, doc_type) do update
    set status = excluded.status, note = excluded.note, updated_by = auth.uid(), updated_at = now()
  returning * into v_doc;
  reset ins.via_rpc;
  return v_doc;
end;
$function$
-- FN ins_set_insurer_product_rate(uuid,uuid,numeric,date,date,text)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.ins_set_insurer_product_rate(p_insurer_id uuid, p_product_line_id uuid, p_rate numeric, p_effective_from date, p_effective_to date DEFAULT NULL::date, p_source_ref text DEFAULT NULL::text)
 RETURNS ins_insurer_product_rates
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
declare
  v_tenant uuid := get_my_tenant_id();
  v_row    ins_insurer_product_rates%rowtype;
begin
  if not can_manage_insurance() then
    raise exception 'INS_FORBIDDEN: only insurance admins and managers maintain commission rates' using errcode = '42501';
  end if;
  if not exists (select 1 from ins_insurers where id = p_insurer_id and tenant_id = v_tenant and is_active) then
    raise exception 'INS_NOT_FOUND: active insurer';
  end if;
  if not exists (select 1 from ins_product_lines where id = p_product_line_id and tenant_id = v_tenant and is_active) then
    raise exception 'INS_NOT_FOUND: active product line';
  end if;
  if p_rate is null or p_rate < 0 or p_rate >= 100 then
    raise exception 'INS_RATE_VALUE: commission rate must be at least 0 and below 100';
  end if;
  if p_effective_from is null then
    raise exception 'INS_RATE_WINDOW: give the date the rate takes effect';
  end if;
  if p_effective_to is not null and p_effective_to < p_effective_from then
    raise exception 'INS_RATE_WINDOW: the end date cannot be before the start date';
  end if;
  if p_source_ref is null or btrim(p_source_ref) = '' then
    raise exception 'INS_RATE_SOURCE: record the agreement the rate comes from';
  end if;
  perform set_config('ins.via_rpc', 'on', true);
  insert into ins_insurer_product_rates (
    tenant_id, insurer_id, product_line_id, rate, effective_from, effective_to, source_ref
  ) values (
    v_tenant, p_insurer_id, p_product_line_id, p_rate, p_effective_from, p_effective_to, btrim(p_source_ref)
  )
  returning * into v_row;
  perform set_config('ins.via_rpc', 'off', true);
  return v_row;
end;
$function$
-- FN ins_set_intermediary_status(uuid,text,text)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.ins_set_intermediary_status(p_id uuid, p_to text, p_note text DEFAULT NULL::text)
 RETURNS ins_intermediaries
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
declare
  v_tenant uuid := get_my_tenant_id();
  v_row    ins_intermediaries%rowtype;
  v_from   text;
  v_note   text := nullif(btrim(p_note), '');
  v_active_children integer;
begin
  if not can_manage_insurance() then
    raise exception 'INS_FORBIDDEN: only insurance admins and managers change intermediary status' using errcode = '42501';
  end if;
  select * into v_row from ins_intermediaries where id = p_id and tenant_id = v_tenant for update;
  if not found then
    raise exception 'INS_NOT_FOUND: intermediary';
  end if;
  v_from := v_row.status;
  -- Moves allowed here. Pending to active is verification; expired to active needs re-verification.
  if not (
       (v_from = 'active' and p_to in ('suspended', 'expired', 'terminated'))
    or (v_from = 'suspended' and p_to in ('active', 'terminated'))
    or (v_from = 'expired' and p_to = 'terminated')
    or (v_from = 'pending_verification' and p_to = 'terminated')
  ) then
    raise exception 'INS_STATE: an intermediary cannot move from % to %', v_from, p_to;
  end if;
  if p_to in ('suspended', 'terminated') and v_note is null then
    raise exception 'INS_NOTE_REQUIRED: give the reason for suspending or terminating';
  end if;
  if p_to = 'suspended' and v_from = 'suspended' then
    raise exception 'INS_STATE: already suspended';
  end if;
  if p_to = 'expired' and (v_row.licence_valid_to is null or v_row.licence_valid_to >= current_date) then
    raise exception 'INS_NOT_EXPIRED: the licence has not passed its expiry date';
  end if;
  if p_to = 'active' and v_from = 'suspended' and v_row.licence_valid_to is not null and v_row.licence_valid_to < current_date then
    raise exception 'INS_LICENCE_EXPIRED: the licence has expired; renew it before reinstating';
  end if;
  if p_to = 'terminated' then
    select count(*) into v_active_children from ins_intermediaries
     where tenant_id = v_tenant and parent_id = v_row.id and status in ('active', 'suspended', 'pending_verification');
    if v_active_children > 0 then
      raise exception 'INS_HAS_SUBAGENTS: % sub-agent(s) still sit under this intermediary; end or move them first', v_active_children;
    end if;
  end if;
  update ins_intermediaries set status = p_to, updated_at = now() where id = v_row.id returning * into v_row;
  insert into ins_intermediary_events (tenant_id, intermediary_id, action, from_status, to_status, note)
  values (v_tenant, v_row.id, 'status', v_from, p_to, v_note);
  return v_row;
end;
$function$
-- FN ins_set_policy_financial_configuration(uuid,jsonb,jsonb)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.ins_set_policy_financial_configuration(p_policy_id uuid, p_shares jsonb, p_charges jsonb)
 RETURNS void
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
declare p ins_policies%rowtype; x jsonb; lead_id uuid; n integer; total numeric;
begin
 if not can_manage_insurance() or effective_user_id() is distinct from auth.uid() then
   raise exception 'INS_FORBIDDEN: an Insurance manager acting as themselves is required' using errcode='42501';
 end if;
 select * into p from ins_policies where id=p_policy_id and tenant_id=get_my_tenant_id() for update;
 if not found then raise exception 'INS_NOT_FOUND: policy'; end if;
 if p.status<>'draft' then raise exception 'INS_FINANCIAL_IMMUTABLE: only drafts can be configured'; end if;
 if jsonb_typeof(p_shares) is distinct from 'array' or jsonb_typeof(p_charges) is distinct from 'array'
    or jsonb_array_length(p_shares)>30 or jsonb_array_length(p_charges)>100 then raise exception 'INS_CONFIG_INPUT: invalid configuration arrays'; end if;
 -- Existing IDs are draft-only; bound IDs never change and are used by allocations.
 delete from ins_policy_charges where policy_id=p.id;
 delete from ins_policy_insurer_shares where policy_id=p.id;
 for x in select * from jsonb_array_elements(p_shares) loop
   if (x->>'share_pct')::numeric is null or (x->>'share_pct')::numeric<>round((x->>'share_pct')::numeric,4)
      or (x->>'commission_rate_pct')::numeric is null or (x->>'commission_rate_pct')::numeric<>round((x->>'commission_rate_pct')::numeric,4)
      or not exists(select 1 from ins_insurers where id=(x->>'insurer_id')::uuid and tenant_id=p.tenant_id and is_active) then
     raise exception 'INS_CONFIG_INPUT: active insurer, share and commission rate are required (maximum four decimals)';
   end if;
   insert into ins_policy_insurer_shares(tenant_id,policy_id,insurer_id,is_lead,share_pct,commission_rate_pct,insurer_policy_no,reviewed)
   values(p.tenant_id,p.id,(x->>'insurer_id')::uuid,coalesce((x->>'is_lead')::boolean,false),
    (x->>'share_pct')::numeric,(x->>'commission_rate_pct')::numeric,nullif(btrim(x->>'insurer_policy_no'),''),coalesce((x->>'reviewed')::boolean,false));
 end loop;
 select count(*) filter(where is_lead),sum(share_pct) into n,total from ins_policy_insurer_shares where policy_id=p.id;
 if jsonb_array_length(p_shares)>0 and (n<>1 or total<>100) then raise exception 'INS_SHARE_TOTAL: shares must total exactly 100%% with exactly one lead insurer'; end if;
 if jsonb_array_length(p_shares)=0 and jsonb_array_length(p_charges)>0 then raise exception 'INS_SHARE_REQUIRED: charges require explicit insurer allocation'; end if;
 for x in select * from jsonb_array_elements(p_charges) loop
   if (x->>'amount')::numeric is not null and ((x->>'amount')::numeric<>round((x->>'amount')::numeric,2) or (x->>'amount')::numeric>9999999999999999.99) then
     raise exception 'INS_CONFIG_INPUT: charge amounts need at most two decimals';
   end if;
   if (x->>'vat_rate_pct')::numeric is not null and (x->>'vat_rate_pct')::numeric<>round((x->>'vat_rate_pct')::numeric,4) then
     raise exception 'INS_CONFIG_INPUT: VAT rate accepts at most four decimals';
   end if;
   if x->>'recipient_mode'='one_insurer' and not exists(select 1 from ins_policy_insurer_shares where policy_id=p.id and insurer_id=(x->>'recipient_insurer_id')::uuid) then
     raise exception 'INS_CHARGE_RECIPIENT: intended insurer must participate in this policy';
   end if;
   insert into ins_policy_charges(tenant_id,policy_id,kind,description,amount,recipient_mode,recipient_insurer_id,vat_mode,vat_rate_pct,income_gl_account_id,reviewed)
   values(p.tenant_id,p.id,x->>'kind',x->>'description',(x->>'amount')::numeric,x->>'recipient_mode',(x->>'recipient_insurer_id')::uuid,
    coalesce(x->>'vat_mode','not_calculated'),(x->>'vat_rate_pct')::numeric,(x->>'income_gl_account_id')::uuid,coalesce((x->>'reviewed')::boolean,false));
 end loop;
 select insurer_id into lead_id from ins_policy_insurer_shares where policy_id=p.id and is_lead;
 if lead_id is not null then
   perform set_config('ins.via_rpc','on',true);
   update ins_policies set insurer_id=lead_id,
    commission_rate_pct=case when jsonb_array_length(p_shares)=1 then (select commission_rate_pct from ins_policy_insurer_shares where policy_id=p.id) else commission_rate_pct end
    where id=p.id;
 end if;
end $function$
-- FN ins_set_risk_status(uuid,text)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.ins_set_risk_status(p_risk_id uuid, p_status text)
 RETURNS void
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'pg_catalog', 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
begin
  if not can_manage_insurance() then raise exception 'INS_FORBIDDEN: Insurance manager required' using errcode = '42501'; end if;
  if p_status not in ('active', 'inactive') then raise exception 'INS_INPUT: status must be active or inactive'; end if;
  update ins_risks set status = p_status where id = p_risk_id and tenant_id = get_my_tenant_id();
  if not found then raise exception 'INS_NOT_FOUND: risk'; end if;
end $function$
-- FN ins_share_cash_totals(uuid)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.ins_share_cash_totals(p_share_id uuid)
 RETURNS jsonb
 LANGUAGE sql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
 with s as(select * from ins_policy_insurer_shares where id=p_share_id),
 cash as(select coalesce(sum(a.amount),0) as collected from s join fin_open_items i on i.source_id=s.policy_id and i.source_type='ins_policy_premium' and i.tenant_id=s.tenant_id
   join fin_allocations a on a.open_item_id=i.id join fin_settlements f on f.id=a.settlement_id and f.status='posted' and f.direction='in'),
 item as(select i.id,i.amount from s join fin_open_items i on i.source_id=s.id and i.source_type='ins_policy_share_insurer' and i.tenant_id=s.tenant_id),
 paid as(select coalesce(sum(a.amount),0) as amount from item i join fin_allocations a on a.open_item_id=i.id join fin_settlements f on f.id=a.settlement_id and f.status='posted' and f.direction='out'),
 withdrawn as(select coalesce(sum(e.amount),0) as amount from s join ins_commission_entries e on e.insurer_share_id=s.id and e.status='transferred'
   join ins_commission_transfers t on t.id=e.transfer_id and t.status='posted'),
 calc as(select s.*,c.collected,p.amount as remitted,w.amount as withdrawn,
   trunc((s.premium_amount+s.pass_through_amount)*least(c.collected/nullif(sn.invoice_total,0),1),2) as funded,
   coalesce((select outstanding_amount from fin_open_item_balances where id=(select id from item)),0) as outstanding
   from s cross join cash c cross join paid p cross join withdrawn w join ins_policy_financial_snapshots sn on sn.policy_id=s.policy_id)
 select jsonb_build_object('share_id',id,'insurer_id',insurer_id,'share_pct',share_pct,'premium_amount',premium_amount,
  'pass_through_amount',pass_through_amount,'commission_amount',commission_amount,'funded',funded,'remitted',remitted,
  'commission_transferred',withdrawn,'available_allocated_funds',greatest(funded-remitted-withdrawn,0),
  'outstanding',outstanding,'maximum_remittance',least(greatest(funded-remitted-withdrawn,0),greatest(outstanding,0))) from calc;
$function$
-- FN ins_snapshot_bind_config()
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.ins_snapshot_bind_config()
 RETURNS trigger
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
declare
  v_recognition text;
begin
  if new.status = 'active' and old.status is distinct from new.status then
    v_recognition := public.acct_assert_reviewed('ins.commission_recognition', new.inception_date);
    new.accounting_config_snapshot := jsonb_build_object(
      'ins.commission_recognition', v_recognition,
      'resolved_at', now()
    );
  end if;
  return new;
end $function$
-- FN ins_submit_cancellation_cash_void(uuid,text,uuid)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.ins_submit_cancellation_cash_void(p_request_id uuid, p_reason text, p_request_key uuid)
 RETURNS ins_cancellation_cash_requests
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
declare target ins_cancellation_cash_requests%rowtype; q ins_cancellation_cash_requests%rowtype;
begin
 perform ins_policy_cash_require_finance();
 select * into target from ins_cancellation_cash_requests where id=p_request_id and tenant_id=get_my_tenant_id();
 if not found then raise exception 'INS_NOT_FOUND: cash request'; end if;
 perform p.id from ins_policies p join ins_policy_cancellations c on c.policy_id=p.id where c.id=target.cancellation_id for update of p;
 select * into q from ins_cancellation_cash_requests where tenant_id=target.tenant_id and request_key=p_request_key;
 if found then if q.maker_id<>auth.uid() or q.voids_request_id is distinct from target.id or q.notes is distinct from btrim(p_reason) then raise exception 'INS_CXL_CASH_RETRY: different void inputs'; end if; return q; end if;
 if target.kind='void' or target.status<>'posted' or p_request_key is null or p_reason is null or length(btrim(p_reason)) not between 5 and 2000 then raise exception 'INS_CXL_CASH_VOID: posted cash and detailed reason required'; end if;
 insert into ins_cancellation_cash_requests(tenant_id,cancellation_id,kind,adjustment_id,bank_account_id,to_bank_account_id,amount,cash_date,reference,notes,request_key,maker_id,voids_request_id)
 values(target.tenant_id,target.cancellation_id,'void',target.adjustment_id,target.bank_account_id,target.to_bank_account_id,target.amount,current_date,'Void '||target.id,btrim(p_reason),p_request_key,auth.uid(),target.id) returning * into q;
 insert into ins_cancellation_cash_events(tenant_id,request_id,event,actor_id) values(q.tenant_id,q.id,'void_requested',auth.uid()); return q;
end $function$
-- FN ins_submit_cancellation_cash(uuid,text,uuid,uuid,uuid,numeric,date,text,text,uuid)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.ins_submit_cancellation_cash(p_cancellation_id uuid, p_kind text, p_adjustment_id uuid, p_bank_account_id uuid, p_to_bank_account_id uuid, p_amount numeric, p_cash_date date, p_reference text, p_notes text, p_request_key uuid)
 RETURNS ins_cancellation_cash_requests
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
declare q ins_cancellation_cash_requests%rowtype; c ins_policy_cancellations%rowtype;
begin
 perform ins_policy_cash_require_finance();
 select * into c from ins_policy_cancellations where id=p_cancellation_id and tenant_id=get_my_tenant_id();
 if not found then raise exception 'INS_NOT_FOUND: cancellation'; end if;
 perform id from ins_policies where id=c.policy_id for update;
 select * into q from ins_cancellation_cash_requests where tenant_id=c.tenant_id and request_key=p_request_key;
 if found then
 if q.maker_id<>auth.uid() or (q.cancellation_id,q.kind,q.adjustment_id,q.bank_account_id,q.to_bank_account_id,q.amount,q.cash_date,q.reference,q.notes) is distinct from
 (p_cancellation_id,p_kind,p_adjustment_id,p_bank_account_id,p_to_bank_account_id,p_amount,p_cash_date,btrim(p_reference),btrim(p_notes)) then raise exception 'INS_CXL_CASH_RETRY: request key belongs to different inputs'; end if;
 return q; end if;
 if p_request_key is null or p_reference is null or length(btrim(p_reference)) not between 3 and 200 or p_notes is null or length(btrim(p_notes)) not between 5 and 2000 then raise exception 'INS_CXL_CASH_INPUT: request key, reference and detailed purpose required'; end if;
 perform platform_ins_cancellation_cash_validate(c.id,p_kind,p_adjustment_id,p_bank_account_id,p_to_bank_account_id,p_amount,p_cash_date);
 insert into ins_cancellation_cash_requests(tenant_id,cancellation_id,kind,adjustment_id,bank_account_id,to_bank_account_id,amount,cash_date,reference,notes,request_key,maker_id)
 values(c.tenant_id,c.id,p_kind,p_adjustment_id,p_bank_account_id,p_to_bank_account_id,p_amount,p_cash_date,btrim(p_reference),btrim(p_notes),p_request_key,auth.uid()) returning * into q;
 insert into ins_cancellation_cash_events(tenant_id,request_id,event,actor_id) values(c.tenant_id,q.id,'submitted',auth.uid());
 return q;
end $function$
-- FN ins_submit_policy_cancellation(uuid,date,jsonb,jsonb,text,uuid)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.ins_submit_policy_cancellation(p_policy_id uuid, p_effective_date date, p_insurer_terms jsonb, p_charge_refunds jsonb, p_reason text, p_request_key uuid)
 RETURNS ins_policy_cancellations
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
declare c ins_policy_cancellations%rowtype; quote jsonb; v_method text;
begin
 if auth.uid() is null or effective_user_id() is distinct from auth.uid() or not(can_manage_insurance() or is_finance_team_member('finance')) then
  raise exception 'INS_FORBIDDEN: Insurance manager or Finance acting as themselves required' using errcode='42501'; end if;
 -- Company-configured default cancellation treatment. p_insurer_terms is an
 -- array of {insurer_id, terms:{method,...}}; an explicit terms.method (a
 -- per-agreement override) always wins, otherwise the approved
 -- ins.cancellation_treatment setting supplies it. acct_get_setting returns an
 -- object, so its text value is read with ->> 'value'.
 if jsonb_typeof(p_insurer_terms)='array' and exists (
   select 1 from jsonb_array_elements(p_insurer_terms) e
    where jsonb_typeof(e)='object' and jsonb_typeof(e->'terms')='object'
      and nullif(btrim(coalesce(e->'terms'->>'method','')),'') is null) then
   v_method := case (public.acct_get_setting('ins.cancellation_treatment', p_effective_date) ->> 'value')
     when 'pro_rata_refund' then 'pro_rata'
     when 'short_rate_refund' then 'short_period'
     when 'no_refund' then 'non_refundable'
     else 'manual_review' end;
   select coalesce(jsonb_agg(
            case when jsonb_typeof(e)='object' and jsonb_typeof(e->'terms')='object'
                  and nullif(btrim(coalesce(e->'terms'->>'method','')),'') is null
                 then jsonb_set(e,'{terms}',(e->'terms')||jsonb_build_object('method',v_method))
                 else e end
            order by ord),'[]'::jsonb)
     into p_insurer_terms
     from jsonb_array_elements(p_insurer_terms) with ordinality t(e,ord);
 end if;
 if p_request_key is null or p_reason is null or length(btrim(p_reason)) not between 5 and 2000 then raise exception 'INS_CANCELLATION_INPUT: request key and reason required'; end if;
 perform id from ins_policies where id=p_policy_id and tenant_id=get_my_tenant_id() for update;
 if not found then raise exception 'INS_NOT_FOUND: policy'; end if;
 select * into c from ins_policy_cancellations where tenant_id=get_my_tenant_id() and request_key=p_request_key;
 if found then
  if c.proposed_by<>auth.uid() or c.policy_id is distinct from p_policy_id or c.effective_date is distinct from p_effective_date or c.insurer_terms is distinct from p_insurer_terms
   or c.charge_refunds is distinct from p_charge_refunds or c.reason is distinct from btrim(p_reason) then raise exception 'INS_CANCELLATION_RETRY_CONFLICT: different payload'; end if;
  return c;
 end if;
 quote:=ins_preview_policy_cancellation(p_policy_id,p_effective_date,p_insurer_terms,p_charge_refunds);
 if exists(select 1 from ins_policy_cancellations where policy_id=p_policy_id and status in('proposed','prepared','posting','posted')) then raise exception 'INS_CANCELLATION_EXISTS: a live proposal or posted cancellation already exists'; end if;
 insert into ins_policy_cancellations(tenant_id,policy_id,request_key,reason,effective_date,insurer_terms,charge_refunds,proposed_by,calculation)
 values(get_my_tenant_id(),p_policy_id,p_request_key,btrim(p_reason),p_effective_date,p_insurer_terms,p_charge_refunds,auth.uid(),quote) returning * into c;
 insert into ins_cancellation_events(tenant_id,cancellation_id,event,actor_id) values(c.tenant_id,c.id,'proposed',auth.uid());
 return c;
end $function$
-- FN ins_submit_policy_cash_request(uuid,text,uuid,numeric,date,uuid,text,text)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.ins_submit_policy_cash_request(p_policy_id uuid, p_kind text, p_bank_account_id uuid, p_amount numeric, p_cash_date date, p_request_key uuid, p_reference text DEFAULT NULL::text, p_notes text DEFAULT NULL::text)
 RETURNS ins_policy_cash_requests
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
declare q ins_policy_cash_requests%rowtype;
begin
  perform ins_policy_cash_require_finance();
  if p_request_key is null then raise exception 'INS_CASH_INPUT: request key required'; end if;
  -- The policy lock also serializes equal retry keys for this policy.
  perform id from ins_policies where id=p_policy_id and tenant_id=get_my_tenant_id() for update;
  if not found then raise exception 'INS_NOT_FOUND: policy'; end if;
  select * into q from ins_policy_cash_requests where tenant_id=get_my_tenant_id() and request_key=p_request_key;
  if found then
    if q.maker_id<>auth.uid() or q.policy_id<>p_policy_id or q.kind is distinct from p_kind
       or q.bank_account_id is distinct from p_bank_account_id or q.amount is distinct from p_amount
       or q.cash_date is distinct from p_cash_date or q.reference is distinct from nullif(btrim(p_reference),'')
       or q.notes is distinct from nullif(btrim(p_notes),'') then
      raise exception 'INS_CASH_RETRY_CONFLICT: request key already used with a different payload';
    end if;
    return q;
  end if;
  perform ins_policy_cash_validate(p_policy_id,p_kind,p_bank_account_id,p_amount,p_cash_date);
  insert into ins_policy_cash_requests(tenant_id,policy_id,kind,bank_account_id,amount,cash_date,request_key,reference,notes,maker_id)
  values(get_my_tenant_id(),p_policy_id,p_kind,p_bank_account_id,p_amount,p_cash_date,p_request_key,
    nullif(btrim(p_reference),''),nullif(btrim(p_notes),''),auth.uid()) returning * into q;
  return q;
end $function$
-- FN ins_submit_policy_for_approval(uuid)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.ins_submit_policy_for_approval(p_policy_id uuid)
 RETURNS ins_policies
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
declare
  v_tenant uuid := public.get_my_tenant_id();
  v_pol public.ins_policies;
begin
  if v_tenant is null or not public.can_access_insurance() then
    raise exception 'INS_ACCESS_DENIED' using errcode = '42501';
  end if;
  select * into v_pol from public.ins_policies
  where tenant_id = v_tenant and id = p_policy_id for update;
  if not found then raise exception 'INS_NOT_FOUND: policy'; end if;
  if v_pol.status <> 'draft' then
    raise exception 'INS_NOT_DRAFT: policy % is %', v_pol.policy_no, v_pol.status;
  end if;
  if v_pol.gross_premium <= 0 then
    raise exception 'INS_PREMIUM_REQUIRED: enter the gross premium before submitting';
  end if;
  set local ins.via_rpc = 'on';
  update public.ins_policies
  set status = 'pending_approval', updated_at = now()
  where tenant_id = v_tenant and id = p_policy_id
  returning * into v_pol;
  insert into public.ins_policy_approvals (tenant_id, policy_id, action, actor_id, note)
  values (v_tenant, p_policy_id, 'submitted', auth.uid(), null);
  reset ins.via_rpc;
  return v_pol;
end;
$function$
-- FN ins_submit_policy_share_cash_request(uuid,uuid,uuid,numeric,date,uuid,text,text)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.ins_submit_policy_share_cash_request(p_policy_id uuid, p_insurer_share_id uuid, p_bank_account_id uuid, p_amount numeric, p_cash_date date, p_request_key uuid, p_reference text DEFAULT NULL::text, p_notes text DEFAULT NULL::text)
 RETURNS ins_policy_cash_requests
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
declare q ins_policy_cash_requests%rowtype;
begin
 perform ins_policy_cash_require_finance();
 if p_request_key is null then raise exception 'INS_CASH_INPUT: request key required'; end if;
 perform id from ins_policies where id=p_policy_id and tenant_id=get_my_tenant_id() for update;
 if not found then raise exception 'INS_NOT_FOUND: policy'; end if;
 select * into q from ins_policy_cash_requests where tenant_id=get_my_tenant_id() and request_key=p_request_key;
 if found then
  if q.maker_id<>auth.uid() or q.policy_id<>p_policy_id or q.kind<>'remittance' or q.insurer_share_id is distinct from p_insurer_share_id
   or q.bank_account_id is distinct from p_bank_account_id or q.amount is distinct from p_amount or q.cash_date is distinct from p_cash_date
   or q.reference is distinct from nullif(btrim(p_reference),'') or q.notes is distinct from nullif(btrim(p_notes),'') then raise exception 'INS_CASH_RETRY_CONFLICT: request key already used'; end if;
  return q;
 end if;
 perform ins_policy_cash_validate(p_policy_id,'remittance',p_bank_account_id,p_amount,p_cash_date,p_insurer_share_id);
 insert into ins_policy_cash_requests(tenant_id,policy_id,kind,insurer_share_id,bank_account_id,amount,cash_date,request_key,reference,notes,maker_id)
 values(get_my_tenant_id(),p_policy_id,'remittance',p_insurer_share_id,p_bank_account_id,p_amount,p_cash_date,p_request_key,nullif(btrim(p_reference),''),nullif(btrim(p_notes),''),auth.uid()) returning * into q;
 return q;
end $function$
-- FN ins_submit_quote(uuid)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.ins_submit_quote(p_quote_id uuid)
 RETURNS ins_quotes
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
declare
  v_tenant uuid := public.get_my_tenant_id();
  v_quote public.ins_quotes;
begin
  if v_tenant is null or not public.can_access_insurance() then
    raise exception 'INS_ACCESS_DENIED' using errcode = '42501';
  end if;
  select * into v_quote from public.ins_quotes
  where tenant_id = v_tenant and id = p_quote_id;
  if v_quote.id is null then
    raise exception 'INS_QUOTE_NOT_FOUND';
  end if;
  if v_quote.status <> 'draft' then
    raise exception 'INS_QUOTE_NOT_DRAFT';
  end if;
  if v_quote.valid_until is not null and v_quote.valid_until < current_date then
    raise exception 'INS_QUOTE_EXPIRED';
  end if;
  set local ins.via_rpc = 'on';
  update public.ins_quotes
  set status = 'submitted', submitted_at = now(), submitted_by = auth.uid(), updated_at = now()
  where tenant_id = v_tenant and id = p_quote_id
  returning * into v_quote;
  reset ins.via_rpc;
  return v_quote;
end;
$function$

 RETURNS trigger
 LANGUAGE plpgsql

AS $function$
begin
  new.updated_at := now();
  return new;
end;

-- FN ins_transfer_earned_commission(uuid,uuid,date,uuid[],text)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.ins_transfer_earned_commission(p_from_bank_account_id uuid, p_to_bank_account_id uuid, p_transfer_date date, p_entry_ids uuid[] DEFAULT NULL::uuid[], p_notes text DEFAULT NULL::text)
 RETURNS ins_commission_transfers
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
begin
 if not can_manage_insurance() then raise exception 'INS_FORBIDDEN: insurance manager required' using errcode='42501'; end if;
 perform p.id from ins_policies p where p.tenant_id=get_my_tenant_id() and exists(select 1 from ins_commission_entries e where e.policy_id=p.id and e.status='earned' and (p_entry_ids is null or e.id=any(p_entry_ids))) order by p.id for update;
 return platform_ins_transfer_before_cancellation_impl(p_from_bank_account_id,p_to_bank_account_id,p_transfer_date,p_entry_ids,p_notes);
end $function$
-- FN ins_transition_claim(uuid,text,text,numeric)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.ins_transition_claim(p_claim_id uuid, p_to text, p_note text DEFAULT NULL::text, p_amount numeric DEFAULT NULL::numeric)
 RETURNS ins_claims
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
declare
  v_tenant     uuid := get_my_tenant_id();
  v_claim      ins_claims%rowtype;
  v_from       text;
  v_sum        numeric;
  v_insurer    uuid;
  v_client     uuid;
  v_mode       text;
  v_legal      boolean;
  v_live_pays  integer;
begin
  if not can_access_insurance() then
    raise exception 'INS_FORBIDDEN: insurance access required' using errcode = '42501';
  end if;
  select * into v_claim from ins_claims where id = p_claim_id and tenant_id = v_tenant for update;
  if not found then
    raise exception 'INS_NOT_FOUND: claim';
  end if;
  v_from := v_claim.status;
  if p_to in ('approved', 'settled', 'repudiated', 'closed') and not can_manage_insurance() then
    raise exception 'INS_FORBIDDEN: only insurance admins and managers decide claims' using errcode = '42501';
  end if;
  v_legal := (v_from, p_to) in (
    ('notified', 'assessing'), ('notified', 'repudiated'),
    ('assessing', 'approved'), ('assessing', 'repudiated'),
    ('approved', 'settled'), ('approved', 'assessing'),
    ('settled', 'closed'), ('repudiated', 'closed')
  );
  if not v_legal then
    raise exception 'INS_CLAIM_TRANSITION: a claim cannot move from % to %', v_from, p_to;
  end if;
  if p_to = 'repudiated' and nullif(btrim(p_note), '') is null then
    raise exception 'INS_NOTE_REQUIRED: give the reason for repudiating the claim';
  end if;
  -- Un-approval: manager only, reason required, and only before anything was recognised or paid.
  if v_from = 'approved' and p_to = 'assessing' then
    if not can_manage_insurance() then
      raise exception 'INS_FORBIDDEN: only insurance admins and managers un-approve claims' using errcode = '42501';
    end if;
    if nullif(btrim(p_note), '') is null then
      raise exception 'INS_NOTE_REQUIRED: give the reason for un-approving the claim';
    end if;
    if v_claim.recognised_at is not null then
      raise exception 'INS_CLAIM_RECOGNISED: this claim''s receivable is recognised, so it cannot be un-approved';
    end if;
    select count(*) into v_live_pays from ins_claim_payments
     where claim_id = v_claim.id and tenant_id = v_tenant and voided_at is null;
    if v_claim.paid_amount <> 0 or v_live_pays > 0 then
      raise exception 'INS_CLAIM_PAID: this claim has payments recorded, so it cannot be un-approved';
    end if;
  end if;
  -- Segregation of duties: the registrant cannot approve their own claim.
  if p_to = 'approved' and v_claim.created_by is not null and v_claim.created_by = auth.uid() then
    raise exception 'INS_SEGREGATION: the person who registered this claim cannot approve it; another manager must';
  end if;
  perform set_config('ins.via_rpc', 'on', true);
  if p_to = 'approved' then
    if p_amount is null or p_amount <= 0 then
      raise exception 'INS_AMOUNT: approved amount must be greater than zero';
    end if;
    select p.sum_insured, p.insurer_id, p.client_id into v_sum, v_insurer, v_client
      from ins_policies p where p.id = v_claim.policy_id;
    if v_sum > 0 and p_amount > v_sum then
      raise exception 'INS_AMOUNT: approved amount exceeds the policy sum insured';
    end if;
    select i.claim_payment_mode into v_mode from ins_insurers i where i.id = v_insurer and i.tenant_id = v_tenant;
    v_mode := coalesce(v_mode, 'insurer_direct');
    -- Approval does not create a receivable or payable. For a brokerage-managed claim
    -- the claim is 'approved, pending settlement' until ins_recognise_claim_receivable.
    update ins_claims set status = 'approved', approved_amount = p_amount, payment_mode = v_mode
     where id = v_claim.id returning * into v_claim;
  elsif p_to = 'assessing' and v_from = 'approved' then
    -- Un-approval clears the decision. Nothing was posted, so nothing needs reversing.
    update ins_claims set status = 'assessing', approved_amount = null, payment_mode = null
     where id = v_claim.id returning * into v_claim;
  elsif p_to = 'settled' then
    if v_claim.paid_amount < coalesce(v_claim.approved_amount, 0) then
      raise exception 'INS_CLAIM_PAYMENT_REQUIRED: a claim settles when its payments cover the approved amount; record the payments instead';
    end if;
    update ins_claims set status = 'settled', settled_at = coalesce(settled_at, now())
     where id = v_claim.id returning * into v_claim;
  else
    update ins_claims set status = p_to
     where id = v_claim.id returning * into v_claim;
  end if;
  insert into ins_claim_events (tenant_id, claim_id, from_status, to_status, note)
  values (v_tenant, v_claim.id, v_from, p_to, nullif(btrim(p_note), ''));
  return v_claim;
end;
$function$
-- FN ins_unlink_policy_risk(uuid,uuid)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.ins_unlink_policy_risk(p_policy_id uuid, p_risk_id uuid)
 RETURNS void
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'pg_catalog', 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
declare t uuid := get_my_tenant_id(); st text;
begin
  if not can_manage_insurance() then raise exception 'INS_FORBIDDEN: Insurance manager required' using errcode = '42501'; end if;
  select status into st from ins_policies where id = p_policy_id and tenant_id = t for update;
  if not found then raise exception 'INS_NOT_FOUND: policy'; end if;
  if st <> 'draft' then raise exception 'INS_NOT_DRAFT: risks can only be unlinked from a draft policy'; end if;
  update ins_certificate_stock set policy_id = null
   where tenant_id = t and policy_id = p_policy_id and risk_id = p_risk_id and status = 'issued';
  delete from ins_policy_risks where tenant_id = t and policy_id = p_policy_id and risk_id = p_risk_id;
end $function$
-- FN ins_unremitted_premium_report(date)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.ins_unremitted_premium_report(p_as_of date DEFAULT CURRENT_DATE)
 RETURNS TABLE(policy_id uuid, policy_no text, insurer_id uuid, unremitted_amount numeric, receipt_date date, deadline_date date, status text)
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 STABLE SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
declare
  v_tenant  uuid := get_my_tenant_id();
  p         record;
  v_avail   numeric;
  v_receipt date;
  v_due     date;
  v_status  text;
begin
  if not has_module_role('insurance', array['admin', 'manager', 'member']) then
    raise exception 'INS_FORBIDDEN: insurance access is required' using errcode = '42501';
  end if;
  for p in
    select ip.id, ip.policy_no, ip.insurer_id
      from public.ins_policies ip
     where ip.tenant_id = v_tenant
       and ip.status in ('active', 'renewed')
  loop
    v_avail := coalesce((public.ins_policy_cash_totals(p.id) ->> 'available_allocated_funds')::numeric, 0);
    continue when v_avail <= 0;
    select min(s.settlement_date) into v_receipt
      from public.fin_open_items i
      join public.fin_allocations a on a.open_item_id = i.id
      join public.fin_settlements s on s.id = a.settlement_id
       and s.status = 'posted' and s.direction = 'in'
     where i.tenant_id = v_tenant
       and i.source_type = 'ins_policy_premium'
       and i.source_id = p.id;
    v_due := public.ins_remittance_due_date(v_tenant, v_receipt);
    v_status := case
      when v_receipt is null then 'receipt_date_missing'
      when v_due is null then 'deadline_not_set'
      when v_due < p_as_of then 'overdue'
      when v_due = p_as_of then 'due_today'
      else 'within_deadline'
    end;
    policy_id := p.id;
    policy_no := p.policy_no;
    insurer_id := p.insurer_id;
    unremitted_amount := v_avail;
    receipt_date := v_receipt;
    deadline_date := v_due;
    status := v_status;
    return next;
  end loop;
end;
$function$
-- FN ins_verify_claim_document(uuid,text)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.ins_verify_claim_document(p_claim_id uuid, p_doc_type text)
 RETURNS ins_claim_documents
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
declare
  v_tenant uuid := public.get_my_tenant_id();
  v_doc public.ins_claim_documents;
begin
  if v_tenant is null or not public.can_manage_insurance() then
    raise exception 'INS_ACCESS_DENIED' using errcode = '42501';
  end if;
  select * into v_doc from public.ins_claim_documents
  where tenant_id = v_tenant and claim_id = p_claim_id and doc_type = p_doc_type;
  if v_doc.id is null then raise exception 'INS_CLAIM_DOC_NOT_FOUND'; end if;
  if v_doc.status <> 'provided' then
    raise exception 'INS_CLAIM_DOC_NOT_PROVIDED';
  end if;
  set local ins.via_rpc = 'on';
  update public.ins_claim_documents
  set status = 'verified', updated_by = auth.uid(), updated_at = now()
  where tenant_id = v_tenant and id = v_doc.id
  returning * into v_doc;
  reset ins.via_rpc;
  return v_doc;
end;
$function$
-- FN ins_verify_intermediary(uuid,text)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.ins_verify_intermediary(p_id uuid, p_note text DEFAULT NULL::text)
 RETURNS ins_intermediaries
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
declare
  v_tenant uuid := get_my_tenant_id();
  v_row    ins_intermediaries%rowtype;
  v_parent_status text;
begin
  if not can_manage_insurance() then
    raise exception 'INS_FORBIDDEN: only insurance admins and managers verify intermediaries' using errcode = '42501';
  end if;
  select * into v_row from ins_intermediaries where id = p_id and tenant_id = v_tenant for update;
  if not found then
    raise exception 'INS_NOT_FOUND: intermediary';
  end if;
  if v_row.status <> 'pending_verification' then
    raise exception 'INS_STATE: only a pending intermediary can be verified (this one is %)', v_row.status;
  end if;
  if v_row.last_changed_by is not null and v_row.last_changed_by = auth.uid() then
    raise exception 'INS_SEGREGATION: the person who last changed this record cannot verify it; another manager must';
  end if;
  if v_row.licence_valid_to is not null and v_row.licence_valid_to < current_date then
    raise exception 'INS_LICENCE_EXPIRED: the licence has expired, so the intermediary cannot be verified';
  end if;
  if v_row.parent_id is not null then
    select status into v_parent_status from ins_intermediaries where id = v_row.parent_id and tenant_id = v_tenant;
    if v_parent_status is distinct from 'active' then
      raise exception 'INS_PARENT_NOT_ACTIVE: the parent must be active before this sub-agent can be verified';
    end if;
  end if;
  update ins_intermediaries set status = 'active', verified_by = auth.uid(), verified_at = now(), updated_at = now()
   where id = v_row.id returning * into v_row;
  insert into ins_intermediary_events (tenant_id, intermediary_id, action, from_status, to_status, note)
  values (v_tenant, v_row.id, 'verified', 'pending_verification', 'active', nullif(btrim(p_note), ''));
  return v_row;
end;
$function$
-- FN ins_via_rpc()
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.ins_via_rpc()
 RETURNS boolean
 LANGUAGE sql
 STABLE
 SET search_path TO 'pg_catalog', 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
  select coalesce(current_setting('ins.via_rpc', true), '') = 'on';
$function$
-- FN ins_void_certificate(text,text,text)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.ins_void_certificate(p_serial_no text, p_status text, p_reason text)
 RETURNS void
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'pg_catalog', 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
declare t uuid := get_my_tenant_id(); c ins_certificate_stock%rowtype;
begin
  if not can_manage_insurance() then raise exception 'INS_FORBIDDEN: Insurance manager required' using errcode = '42501'; end if;
  if p_status not in ('spoiled', 'voided') then raise exception 'INS_INPUT: status must be spoiled or voided'; end if;
  if nullif(btrim(coalesce(p_reason, '')), '') is null then raise exception 'INS_INPUT: a reason is required'; end if;
  select * into c from ins_certificate_stock where tenant_id = t and serial_no = btrim(p_serial_no) for update;
  if not found then raise exception 'INS_NOT_FOUND: certificate'; end if;
  if c.status not in ('in_stock', 'issued') or c.policy_id is not null then
    raise exception 'INS_CERTIFICATE_STATE: only a certificate in stock or issued and not attached to a policy can be closed';
  end if;
  update ins_certificate_stock set status = p_status, closed_at = now(), close_reason = btrim(p_reason) where id = c.id;
end $function$
-- FN ins_void_claim_payment(uuid,text)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.ins_void_claim_payment(p_payment_id uuid, p_reason text)
 RETURNS ins_claim_payments
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
declare
  v_tenant uuid := get_my_tenant_id();
  v_pay    ins_claim_payments%rowtype;
  v_status text;
begin
  if not can_manage_insurance() then
    raise exception 'INS_FORBIDDEN: only insurance admins and managers void claim payments' using errcode = '42501';
  end if;
  if p_reason is null or length(btrim(p_reason)) < 5 then
    raise exception 'INS_REASON_REQUIRED: a reason of at least 5 characters is required';
  end if;
  select * into v_pay from ins_claim_payments where id = p_payment_id and tenant_id = v_tenant for update;
  if not found then raise exception 'INS_NOT_FOUND: payment'; end if;
  if v_pay.status <> 'posted' then raise exception 'INS_ALREADY_VOID: this payment is already void'; end if;
  select status into v_status from ins_claims where id = v_pay.claim_id;
  if v_status = 'closed' then
    raise exception 'INS_CLAIM_CLOSED: payments on a closed claim cannot be voided';
  end if;
  perform set_config('ins.via_rpc', 'on', true);
  if v_pay.kind = 'insurer_direct' then
    update ins_claim_payments
       set status = 'void', voided_at = now(), voided_by = auth.uid(), void_reason = btrim(p_reason)
     where id = v_pay.id returning * into v_pay;
    perform ins_claim_apply_payment_totals(v_pay.claim_id, 'Payment voided: ' || btrim(p_reason));
  else
    -- The settlement void trigger marks this row void and recomputes the claim.
    perform platform_fin_void_settlement_impl(v_tenant, v_pay.settlement_id, btrim(p_reason), current_date);
    select * into v_pay from ins_claim_payments where id = p_payment_id;
  end if;
  return v_pay;
end;
$function$
-- FN ins_void_commission_transfer(uuid,text,date)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.ins_void_commission_transfer(p_transfer_id uuid, p_reason text, p_void_date date DEFAULT CURRENT_DATE)
 RETURNS ins_commission_transfers
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
declare
  v_tenant uuid := get_my_tenant_id();
  v_tr     ins_commission_transfers%rowtype;
  v_lines  jsonb;
  v_je     journal_entries;
begin
  if not can_manage_insurance() then
    raise exception 'INS_FORBIDDEN: only insurance admins and managers can void a commission transfer' using errcode = '42501';
  end if;
  if p_reason is null or length(btrim(p_reason)) < 5 then
    raise exception 'INS_TRANSFER_VOID: a reason of at least 5 characters is required';
  end if;
  select * into v_tr from ins_commission_transfers where id = p_transfer_id and tenant_id = v_tenant for update;
  if not found then raise exception 'INS_TRANSFER_VOID: transfer not found in this company'; end if;
  if v_tr.status <> 'posted' then raise exception 'INS_TRANSFER_VOID: transfer % is already void', v_tr.transfer_no; end if;
  if exists (select 1 from bank_reconciliations r where r.commission_transfer_id = v_tr.id) then
    raise exception 'INS_TRANSFER_RECONCILED: transfer % is matched to a bank statement line; unmatch it in bank reconciliation first', v_tr.transfer_no;
  end if;
  if p_void_date is null or p_void_date < v_tr.transfer_date or p_void_date > current_date + 1 then
    raise exception 'INS_TRANSFER_VOID: the void date must be between the transfer date and today';
  end if;
  -- Swap debit and credit, keeping each line's currency, original rate and rounding flag.
  select jsonb_agg(jsonb_build_object(
           'gl_account_id', l.gl_account_id, 'debit', l.credit, 'credit', l.debit,
           'currency', l.currency, 'fx_rate', l.fx_rate, 'is_rounding', l.is_rounding,
           'description', 'Reversal of ' || v_tr.transfer_no))
    into v_lines
    from journal_entry_lines l where l.journal_entry_id = v_tr.journal_entry_id;
  -- Reversal mode: the line guard keeps the original rates, but only rates that the entry
  -- being reversed already used.
  perform set_config('fin.reversal_of', v_tr.journal_entry_id::text, true);
  v_je := post_journal_entry(v_tenant, 'ins_commission_transfer_void', v_tr.id, p_void_date,
    'Void of commission transfer ' || v_tr.transfer_no || ': ' || btrim(p_reason), v_lines);
  perform set_config('fin.reversal_of', '', true);
  insert into ins_commission_events (tenant_id, entry_id, event, amount, journal_entry_id, transfer_id)
  select v_tenant, e.id, 'transfer_voided', e.amount, v_je.id, v_tr.id
    from ins_commission_entries e where e.transfer_id = v_tr.id and e.tenant_id = v_tenant;
  update ins_commission_entries
     set status = 'earned', transfer_id = null, updated_at = now()
   where transfer_id = v_tr.id and tenant_id = v_tenant;
  update ins_commission_transfers
     set status = 'void', void_journal_entry_id = v_je.id, voided_at = now(),
         voided_by = auth.uid(), void_reason = btrim(p_reason)
   where id = v_tr.id
  returning * into v_tr;
  return v_tr;
end;
$function$
-- FN ins_withdraw_insurer_product_rate(uuid,text)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.ins_withdraw_insurer_product_rate(p_id uuid, p_reason text)
 RETURNS ins_insurer_product_rates
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
declare
  v_row    ins_insurer_product_rates%rowtype;
  v_uid    uuid := auth.uid();
  v_reason text := btrim(coalesce(p_reason, ''));
begin
  select * into v_row from ins_insurer_product_rates
  where id = p_id and tenant_id = get_my_tenant_id()
  for update;
  if not found then
    raise exception 'INS_RATE_NOT_FOUND: rate not found';
  end if;
  if length(v_reason) < 5 then
    raise exception 'INS_RATE_REASON: give a reason of at least five characters';
  end if;
  if v_row.status <> 'draft' then
    raise exception 'INS_RATE_NOT_DRAFT: only a draft can be withdrawn (this one is %)', v_row.status;
  end if;
  if not (can_manage_insurance() or v_row.entered_by is not distinct from v_uid) then
    raise exception 'INS_FORBIDDEN: only the person who entered a draft or an insurance admin or manager can withdraw it' using errcode = '42501';
  end if;
  perform set_config('ins.via_rpc', 'on', true);
  update ins_insurer_product_rates
     set status = 'withdrawn', withdrawn_reason = v_reason
   where id = p_id
   returning * into v_row;
  perform set_config('ins.via_rpc', 'off', true);
  return v_row;
end;
$function$
-- FN ins_withdraw_policy_cancellation(uuid,text)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.ins_withdraw_policy_cancellation(p_cancellation_id uuid, p_reason text)
 RETURNS void
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
declare c ins_policy_cancellations%rowtype; pid uuid;
begin
 if auth.uid() is null or effective_user_id() is distinct from auth.uid() or not(can_manage_insurance() or is_finance_team_member('finance')) then raise exception 'INS_FORBIDDEN: acting identity and current role required' using errcode='42501'; end if;
 select policy_id into pid from ins_policy_cancellations where id=p_cancellation_id and tenant_id=get_my_tenant_id();
 if not found then raise exception 'INS_NOT_FOUND: cancellation'; end if;
 perform id from ins_policies where id=pid for update;
 select * into c from ins_policy_cancellations where id=p_cancellation_id for update;
 if c.proposed_by<>auth.uid() or c.status not in('proposed','prepared') or length(btrim(coalesce(p_reason,'')))<5 then raise exception 'INS_CANCELLATION_WITHDRAW: proposer, open case and reason required'; end if;
 update ins_policy_cancellations set status='withdrawn',review_note=btrim(p_reason) where id=c.id;
 insert into ins_cancellation_events(tenant_id,cancellation_id,event,actor_id,detail) values(c.tenant_id,c.id,'withdrawn',auth.uid(),jsonb_build_object('reason',p_reason));
end $function$
-- FN ins_withdraw_policy_endorsement(uuid,text)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.ins_withdraw_policy_endorsement(p_endorsement_id uuid, p_reason text)
 RETURNS void
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
declare
  e ins_policy_endorsements%rowtype;
  pid uuid;
begin
  if auth.uid() is null or effective_user_id() is distinct from auth.uid()
     or not (can_manage_insurance() or is_finance_team_member('finance')) then
    raise exception 'INS_FORBIDDEN: acting identity and current role required' using errcode = '42501';
  end if;
  select policy_id into pid from ins_policy_endorsements
   where id = p_endorsement_id and tenant_id = get_my_tenant_id();
  if not found then
    raise exception 'INS_NOT_FOUND: endorsement';
  end if;
  perform id from ins_policies where id = pid for update;
  select * into e from ins_policy_endorsements where id = p_endorsement_id for update;
  if e.proposed_by <> auth.uid() or e.status not in ('proposed','prepared')
     or length(btrim(coalesce(p_reason, ''))) < 5 then
    raise exception 'INS_ENDORSEMENT_WITHDRAW: proposer, open case and reason required';
  end if;
  update ins_policy_endorsements set status = 'withdrawn', review_note = btrim(p_reason) where id = e.id;
  insert into ins_policy_endorsement_events(tenant_id, endorsement_id, event, actor_id, detail)
    values (e.tenant_id, e.id, 'withdrawn', auth.uid(), jsonb_build_object('reason', p_reason));
end $function$
-- FN invitations_seat_limit_guard()
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.invitations_seat_limit_guard()
 RETURNS trigger
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
declare
  v_limit   integer;
  v_used    integer;
begin
  select seat_limit into v_limit from tenants where id = new.tenant_id;
  if v_limit is null then
    return new;
  end if;
  select
    (select count(*) from app_users   u where u.tenant_id = new.tenant_id)
  + (select count(*) from invitations i where i.tenant_id = new.tenant_id and i.status = 'pending')
  into v_used;
  if v_used >= v_limit then
    raise exception 'SEAT_LIMIT_REACHED: this company has used all % of its seats (members + pending invitations). Raise the seat limit in the platform console before inviting more people.',
      v_limit
      using errcode = 'check_violation';
  end if;
  return new;
end;
$function$
