    raise exception 'SETTLEMENT_VOID: settlement % is reconciled to a bank statement line; unmatch it first', v_set.settlement_no;
  end if;
  if v_set.status <> 'posted' then raise exception 'SETTLEMENT_VOID: settlement % is already void', v_set.settlement_no; end if;
  if v_set.journal_entry_id is null then raise exception 'SETTLEMENT_VOID: settlement % has no posted journal', v_set.settlement_no; end if;
  if p_void_date is null or p_void_date < v_set.settlement_date or p_void_date > current_date + 1 then
    raise exception 'SETTLEMENT_VOID: the void date must be between the settlement date and today';
  end if;
  -- Swap debit and credit, keeping each line's currency, original rate and rounding flag.
  select jsonb_agg(jsonb_build_object(
           'gl_account_id', l.gl_account_id,
           'debit', l.credit,
           'credit', l.debit,
           'currency', l.currency,
           'fx_rate', l.fx_rate,
           'is_rounding', l.is_rounding,
           'description', 'Reversal of ' || v_set.settlement_no))
    into v_lines
    from journal_entry_lines l
   where l.journal_entry_id = v_set.journal_entry_id;
  -- Reversal mode: the line guard keeps the original rates, but only rates that the entry
  -- being reversed already used.
  perform set_config('fin.reversal_of', v_set.journal_entry_id::text, true);
  v_je := post_journal_entry(p_tenant_id, 'fin_settlement_void', v_set.id, p_void_date,
                             'Void of settlement ' || v_set.settlement_no || ': ' || btrim(p_reason), v_lines);
  perform set_config('fin.reversal_of', '', true);
  update fin_settlements
     set status = 'void', void_journal_entry_id = v_je.id, voided_at = now(),
         voided_by = auth.uid(), void_reason = btrim(p_reason)
   where id = v_set.id
   returning * into v_set;
  return v_set;
end;
$function$
-- FN platform_fx_rate(uuid,text,date)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.platform_fx_rate(p_tenant_id uuid, p_currency text, p_on date)
 RETURNS fx_rates
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 STABLE SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
declare
  v       fx_rates%rowtype;
  v_max   numeric;
begin
  if p_tenant_id is null or p_currency is null or p_on is null then
    raise exception 'FX_ARGUMENT: company, currency and date are required';
  end if;
  if p_currency = fin_ledger_currency(p_tenant_id) then
    v.tenant_id := p_tenant_id;
    v.currency  := p_currency;
    v.rate_date := p_on;
    v.rate      := 1;
    v.source    := 'functional currency';
    v.status    := 'approved';
    return v;
  end if;
  select * into v
  from fx_rates
  where tenant_id = p_tenant_id
    and currency = p_currency
    and status = 'approved'
    and rate_date <= p_on
  order by rate_date desc
  limit 1;
  if not found then
    raise exception 'FX_RATE_MISSING: no approved % rate on or before %', p_currency, p_on;
  end if;
  v_max := platform_reg_number(p_tenant_id, 'fx.rate_max_age_days', p_on);
  if (p_on - v.rate_date) > v_max then
    raise exception 'FX_RATE_STALE: the latest approved % rate is dated %, more than % days before %', p_currency, v.rate_date, v_max, p_on;
  end if;
  return v;
end;
$function$
-- FN platform_fx_realised_line(uuid,numeric,text)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.platform_fx_realised_line(p_tenant_id uuid, p_result numeric, p_description text DEFAULT NULL::text)
 RETURNS jsonb
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 STABLE SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
declare
  v_account uuid;
  v_role    text;
begin
  if p_result is null or p_result = 0 then
    return null;
  end if;
  v_role := case when p_result > 0 then 'fx_gain_realised' else 'fx_loss_realised' end;
  select gl_account_id into v_account
    from gl_posting_rules
   where tenant_id = p_tenant_id and account_role = v_role;
  if v_account is null then
    raise exception 'FX_REALISED_ACCOUNT_MISSING: this company has no % account under posting rules', v_role;
  end if;
  return jsonb_build_object(
    'gl_account_id', v_account,
    'debit',  case when p_result < 0 then -p_result else 0 end,
    'credit', case when p_result > 0 then  p_result else 0 end,
    'currency', fin_ledger_currency(p_tenant_id),
    'description', coalesce(p_description,
                            case when p_result > 0 then 'Realised exchange gain' else 'Realised exchange loss' end)
  );
end;
$function$
-- FN platform_fx_revalue_impl(uuid,date)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.platform_fx_revalue_impl(p_tenant_id uuid, p_as_of date)
 RETURNS fx_revaluation_runs
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
declare
  v_functional text;
  v_run        fx_revaluation_runs%rowtype;
  v_prior      fx_revaluation_runs%rowtype;
  v_je         journal_entries%rowtype;
  v_gain       uuid := null;
  v_loss       uuid := null;
  v_rate       numeric;
  v_target     numeric;
  v_unrealised numeric;
  v_lines      jsonb := '[]'::jsonb;
  v_detail     jsonb := '[]'::jsonb;
  r            record;
begin
  if p_tenant_id is null then
    raise exception 'tenant id is required';
  end if;
  if p_as_of is null or p_as_of > current_date then
    raise exception 'FX_REVAL_INPUT: a revaluation needs a run date that is not in the future';
  end if;
  if exists (
    select 1 from fx_revaluation_runs
     where tenant_id = p_tenant_id and as_of = p_as_of and status = 'posted'
  ) then
    raise exception 'FX_REVAL_EXISTS: a revaluation for % is already live; void it first', p_as_of;
  end if;
  v_functional := fin_ledger_currency(p_tenant_id);
  -- The previous live run's journal is reversed first, so exactly one revaluation
  -- journal is ever live.
  select * into v_prior
    from fx_revaluation_runs
   where tenant_id = p_tenant_id and status = 'posted' and journal_entry_id is not null
   order by posted_at desc
   limit 1
   for update;
  drop table if exists pg_temp.reval_rows;
  drop table if exists pg_temp.reval_final;
  create temp table reval_rows on commit drop as
    -- Control accounts with outstanding foreign open items (at their booked base).
    select get_posting_account(p_tenant_id, i.control_role) as gl_account_id,
           i.currency, true as from_items,
           sum(case when i.control_role in ('ap_control', 'insurer_payable', 'client_claim_payable')
                    then -b.outstanding_amount else b.outstanding_amount end) as ccy_balance,
           sum(case when i.control_role in ('ap_control', 'insurer_payable', 'client_claim_payable')
                    then -b.base_outstanding_amount else b.base_outstanding_amount end) as base_balance
      from fin_open_items i
      join fin_open_item_balances b on b.id = i.id
     where i.tenant_id = p_tenant_id
       and i.currency is distinct from v_functional
       and b.outstanding_amount > 0
       and i.control_role in ('ar_control', 'ap_control', 'insurer_payable', 'commission_receivable',
                              'insurer_claim_receivable', 'client_claim_payable')
     group by 1, 2
    union all
    -- Foreign bank account GLs.
    select l.gl_account_id, l.currency, false as from_items,
           sum(l.debit - l.credit),
           sum(l.base_debit - l.base_credit)
      from journal_entry_lines l
     where l.tenant_id = p_tenant_id
       and l.currency is distinct from v_functional
       and l.gl_account_id in (
         select b.gl_account_id from fin_bank_accounts b
          where b.tenant_id = p_tenant_id and b.gl_account_id is not null
            and b.currency is distinct from v_functional)
     group by 1, 2
    union all
    -- The unearned-commission account (per currency): the base residues that 3d left
    -- behind are what this run clears.
    select l.gl_account_id, l.currency, false as from_items,
           sum(l.debit - l.credit),
           sum(l.base_debit - l.base_credit)
      from journal_entry_lines l
     where l.tenant_id = p_tenant_id
       and l.currency is distinct from v_functional
       and l.gl_account_id = get_posting_account(p_tenant_id, 'unearned_commission')
     group by 1, 2;
  delete from reval_rows where gl_account_id is null;
  -- A bank GL can also appear through the control set in theory; merge duplicates.
  create temp table reval_final on commit drop as
    select gl_account_id, currency, bool_or(from_items) as from_items,
           sum(ccy_balance) as ccy_balance, sum(base_balance) as base_balance
      from reval_rows
     group by 1, 2;
  -- All-or-nothing: every involved currency must have an approved rate for the run
  -- date before anything is written.
  for r in select distinct currency from reval_final loop
    perform platform_fx_rate(p_tenant_id, r.currency, p_as_of);
  end loop;
  -- The previous live run's journal is reversed BEFORE the movements are measured:
  -- its lines are undone first, so the new journal books exactly what still differs
  -- from the closing rate instead of moving the same balance twice.
  if v_prior.id is not null then
    declare
      v_rev_lines jsonb;
    begin
      select jsonb_agg(jsonb_build_object(
               'gl_account_id', l.gl_account_id, 'debit', l.credit, 'credit', l.debit,
               'currency', l.currency, 'fx_rate', l.fx_rate, 'is_rounding', l.is_rounding,
               'description', 'Reversal of the revaluation at ' || v_prior.as_of))
        into v_rev_lines
        from journal_entry_lines l
       where l.journal_entry_id = v_prior.journal_entry_id;
      perform set_config('fin.reversal_of', v_prior.journal_entry_id::text, true);
      v_je := post_journal_entry(p_tenant_id, 'fx_revaluation_void', v_prior.id, p_as_of,
                                 'Reversal of the FX revaluation at ' || v_prior.as_of, v_rev_lines);
      perform set_config('fin.reversal_of', '', true);
      update fx_revaluation_runs
         set status = 'void', void_journal_entry_id = v_je.id,
             void_reason = 'superseded by the revaluation at ' || p_as_of, voided_at = now()
       where id = v_prior.id;
    end;
  end if;
  -- Measure again on the post-reversal balances.
  drop table if exists pg_temp.reval_rows;
  drop table if exists pg_temp.reval_final;
  create temp table reval_rows on commit drop as
    -- Control accounts with outstanding foreign open items (at their booked base).
    select get_posting_account(p_tenant_id, i.control_role) as gl_account_id,
           i.currency, true as from_items,
           sum(case when i.control_role in ('ap_control', 'insurer_payable', 'client_claim_payable')
                    then -b.outstanding_amount else b.outstanding_amount end) as ccy_balance,
           sum(case when i.control_role in ('ap_control', 'insurer_payable', 'client_claim_payable')
                    then -b.base_outstanding_amount else b.base_outstanding_amount end) as base_balance
      from fin_open_items i
      join fin_open_item_balances b on b.id = i.id
     where i.tenant_id = p_tenant_id
       and i.currency is distinct from v_functional
       and b.outstanding_amount > 0
       and i.control_role in ('ar_control', 'ap_control', 'insurer_payable', 'commission_receivable',
                              'insurer_claim_receivable', 'client_claim_payable')
     group by 1, 2
    union all
    -- Foreign bank account GLs.
    select l.gl_account_id, l.currency, false as from_items,
           sum(l.debit - l.credit),
           sum(l.base_debit - l.base_credit)
      from journal_entry_lines l
     where l.tenant_id = p_tenant_id
       and l.currency is distinct from v_functional
       and l.gl_account_id in (
         select b.gl_account_id from fin_bank_accounts b
          where b.tenant_id = p_tenant_id and b.gl_account_id is not null
            and b.currency is distinct from v_functional)
     group by 1, 2
    union all
    -- The unearned-commission account (per currency): the base residues that 3d left
    -- behind are what this run clears.
    select l.gl_account_id, l.currency, false as from_items,
           sum(l.debit - l.credit),
           sum(l.base_debit - l.base_credit)
      from journal_entry_lines l
     where l.tenant_id = p_tenant_id
       and l.currency is distinct from v_functional
       and l.gl_account_id = get_posting_account(p_tenant_id, 'unearned_commission')
     group by 1, 2;
  delete from reval_rows where gl_account_id is null;
  -- A bank GL can also appear through the control set in theory; merge duplicates.
  create temp table reval_final on commit drop as
    select gl_account_id, currency, bool_or(from_items) as from_items,
           sum(ccy_balance) as ccy_balance, sum(base_balance) as base_balance
      from reval_rows
     group by 1, 2;
  for r in
    select * from reval_final
     where round(ccy_balance * round((platform_fx_rate(p_tenant_id, currency, p_as_of)).rate, 8), 2)
           <> round(base_balance, 2)
     order by gl_account_id, currency
  loop
    v_rate := round((platform_fx_rate(p_tenant_id, r.currency, p_as_of)).rate, 8);
    v_target := round(r.ccy_balance * v_rate, 2);
    v_unrealised := v_target - round(r.base_balance, 2);
    if v_gain is null or v_loss is null then
      v_gain := get_posting_account(p_tenant_id, 'fx_gain_unrealised');
      v_loss := get_posting_account(p_tenant_id, 'fx_loss_unrealised');
      if v_gain is null or v_loss is null then
        raise exception 'FX_UNREALISED_ACCOUNT_MISSING: this company has no fx_gain_unrealised/fx_loss_unrealised accounts under posting rules';
      end if;
    end if;
    v_detail := v_detail || jsonb_build_object(
      'gl_account_id', r.gl_account_id, 'currency', r.currency,
      'currency_balance', r.ccy_balance, 'base_balance', round(r.base_balance, 2),
      'closing_rate', v_rate, 'unrealised_result', v_unrealised);
    -- The account moves towards its closing-rate value; the P&L side books the move.
    v_lines := v_lines || jsonb_build_object(
      'gl_account_id', r.gl_account_id,
      'debit',  greatest(v_unrealised, 0), 'credit', greatest(-v_unrealised, 0),
      'description', format('FX revaluation %s: %s at %s', r.currency, to_char(v_unrealised, 'FM9999999999999990.00'), v_rate));
    v_lines := v_lines || (case when v_unrealised > 0
      then jsonb_build_object('gl_account_id', v_gain, 'credit', v_unrealised,
                              'description', 'Unrealised exchange gain at ' || p_as_of)
      else jsonb_build_object('gl_account_id', v_loss, 'debit', -v_unrealised,
                              'description', 'Unrealised exchange loss at ' || p_as_of) end);
  end loop;
  insert into fx_revaluation_runs (tenant_id, as_of, status, detail, posted_by)
  values (p_tenant_id, p_as_of, 'posted', v_detail, auth.uid())
  returning * into v_run;
  if jsonb_array_length(v_lines) > 0 then
    v_je := post_journal_entry(p_tenant_id, 'fx_revaluation', v_run.id, p_as_of,
                               'FX revaluation at ' || p_as_of, v_lines);
    update fx_revaluation_runs set journal_entry_id = v_je.id where id = v_run.id returning * into v_run;
  end if;
  return v_run;
end;
$function$
-- FN platform_fx_settlement(uuid,text,numeric,numeric,date,boolean)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.platform_fx_settlement(p_tenant_id uuid, p_currency text, p_amount numeric, p_original_rate numeric, p_on date, p_settles_debit boolean)
 RETURNS TABLE(settlement_rate numeric, base_original numeric, base_settlement numeric, result numeric)
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 STABLE SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
declare
  v_rate numeric;
  v_orig numeric;
  v_sett numeric;
begin
  if p_amount is null or p_amount < 0 or p_original_rate is null or p_original_rate <= 0 then
    raise exception 'FX_ARGUMENT: a settlement needs a non-negative amount and a positive original rate';
  end if;
  -- A line stores its rate to 8 decimals, so the settlement is computed at that precision.
  v_rate := round((platform_fx_rate(p_tenant_id, p_currency, p_on)).rate, 8);
  v_orig := round(p_amount * p_original_rate, 2);
  v_sett := round(p_amount * v_rate, 2);
  return query select v_rate, v_orig, v_sett,
                      case when p_settles_debit then v_orig - v_sett else v_sett - v_orig end;
end;
$function$
-- FN platform_gl_posting_roles()
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.platform_gl_posting_roles()
 RETURNS text[]
 LANGUAGE sql
 IMMUTABLE
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
  select array[
    'ap_control', 'ar_control', 'bank', 'cash', 'vat_input', 'vat_output', 'wht_payable',
    'default_expense', 'default_revenue', 'salaries_payable', 'paye_payable', 'nssf_payable',
    'salaries_expense',
    'client_money_bank', 'insurer_payable', 'commission_receivable', 'commission_income',
    'wht_receivable',
    'unearned_commission',
    'insurer_claim_receivable', 'client_claim_payable',
    'fx_rounding',
    'fx_gain_realised', 'fx_loss_realised',
    'fx_gain_unrealised', 'fx_loss_unrealised',
    'opening_balance_clearing'
  ]::text[];
$function$
-- FN platform_has_admin()
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.platform_has_admin()
 RETURNS boolean
 LANGUAGE sql
 STABLE SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
  select exists (select 1 from app_users where is_platform_admin = true);
$function$
-- FN platform_ins_allocate_cancellation_refund(jsonb,text)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.platform_ins_allocate_cancellation_refund(p_original jsonb, p_refund text)
 RETURNS jsonb
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 IMMUTABLE
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
declare amount numeric:=platform_ins_cancellation_money(p_refund); total numeric; result jsonb;
begin
 if jsonb_typeof(p_original) is distinct from 'array' then raise exception 'INS_CANCELLATION_INPUT: original charge recipients required'; end if;
 if jsonb_array_length(p_original)=0 or exists(select 1 from jsonb_array_elements(p_original) x where nullif(x->>'insurer_id','') is null)
  or (select count(*)<>count(distinct x->>'insurer_id') from jsonb_array_elements(p_original) x) then
  raise exception 'INS_CANCELLATION_INPUT: unique original charge recipients required'; end if;
 select sum(platform_ins_cancellation_money(x->>'amount')) into total from jsonb_array_elements(p_original) x;
 if amount>total then raise exception 'INS_CANCELLATION_INPUT: charge refund exceeds original charge'; end if;
 with bases as (
  select x->>'insurer_id' as insurer_id,
   case when total=0 then 0 else div(amount*100*platform_ins_cancellation_money(x->>'amount')*100,total*100)*.01 end as base,
   case when total=0 then 0 else mod(amount*100*platform_ins_cancellation_money(x->>'amount')*100,total*100) end as fraction,
   ord from jsonb_array_elements(p_original) with ordinality t(x,ord)
 ), ranked as(select *,row_number() over(order by fraction desc,insurer_id collate "C") as rank,
   round((amount-sum(base) over())*100) as cents from bases)
 select jsonb_agg(jsonb_build_object('insurer_id',insurer_id,'amount',(base+case when rank<=cents then .01 else 0 end)::text) order by ord) into result from ranked;
 return result;
end $function$
-- FN platform_ins_bind_policy_legacy_impl(uuid)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.platform_ins_bind_policy_legacy_impl(p_policy_id uuid)
 RETURNS ins_policies
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
declare
  v_pol        ins_policies%rowtype;
  v_client     ins_clients%rowtype;
  v_client_nm  text;
  v_comm       numeric(18,2);
  v_net        numeric(18,2);
  v_ar_acc     uuid;
  v_ins_acc    uuid;
  v_ar_gl      uuid;
  v_payable_gl uuid;
  v_unearned_gl uuid;
  v_income_gl  uuid;
  v_lines      jsonb;
  v_je         journal_entries;
  v_desc       text;
begin
  if not can_manage_insurance() then
    raise exception 'INS_FORBIDDEN: only insurance admins and managers can bind policies' using errcode = '42501';
  end if;
  select * into v_pol from ins_policies where id = p_policy_id for update;
  if not found then
    raise exception 'INS_NOT_FOUND: policy';
  end if;
  perform assert_tenant_access(v_pol.tenant_id);
  if v_pol.status <> 'approved' then
    raise exception 'INS_NOT_APPROVED: policy % is % (submit for approval, then have a manager approve before binding)', v_pol.policy_no, v_pol.status;
  end if;
  if v_pol.gross_premium <= 0 then
    raise exception 'INS_PREMIUM_REQUIRED: enter the gross premium before binding';
  end if;
  v_comm := round(v_pol.gross_premium * v_pol.commission_rate_pct / 100, 2);
  v_net  := v_pol.gross_premium - v_comm;
  if v_net <= 0 then
    raise exception 'INS_COMMISSION_TOO_HIGH: commission would leave nothing to remit to the insurer';
  end if;
  -- Posting roles from the tenant's chart. Missing roles stop the bind.
  v_ar_gl       := get_posting_account(v_pol.tenant_id, 'ar_control');
  v_payable_gl  := get_posting_account(v_pol.tenant_id, 'insurer_payable');
  v_unearned_gl := get_posting_account(v_pol.tenant_id, 'unearned_commission');
  v_income_gl   := get_posting_account(v_pol.tenant_id, 'commission_income');
  if v_ar_gl is null or v_payable_gl is null
     or (v_comm > 0 and (v_unearned_gl is null or v_income_gl is null)) then
    raise exception 'INS_POSTING_RULE_MISSING: the chart of accounts needs ar_control, insurer_payable, unearned_commission and commission_income';
  end if;
  select * into v_client from ins_clients where id = v_pol.client_id;
  select name into v_client_nm from bd_clients where id = v_client.client_id;
  v_ar_acc  := ins_ensure_client_account(v_pol.client_id);
  v_ins_acc := ins_ensure_insurer_account(v_pol.insurer_id);
  v_desc := 'Bind ' || v_pol.policy_no || ' (' || coalesce(v_client_nm, 'client') || ')';
  v_lines := jsonb_build_array(
    jsonb_build_object('gl_account_id', v_ar_gl, 'debit', v_pol.gross_premium, 'currency', v_pol.currency, 'description', v_desc),
    jsonb_build_object('gl_account_id', v_payable_gl, 'credit', v_net, 'currency', v_pol.currency, 'description', v_desc)
  );
  if v_comm > 0 then
    v_lines := v_lines || jsonb_build_array(
      jsonb_build_object('gl_account_id', v_unearned_gl, 'credit', v_comm, 'currency', v_pol.currency,
                         'description', v_desc || ' - unearned commission')
    );
  end if;
  -- Booked on the inception date (at the inception-date rate), so the period-close
  -- check applies to it.
  v_je := post_journal_entry(v_pol.tenant_id, 'ins_policy_bind', v_pol.id, v_pol.inception_date, v_desc, v_lines);
  -- The items carry the policy's currency and the bind entry, so they hold the rate the
  -- policy was bound at (fin_create_open_item_fx takes the rate from that entry's
  -- control line; for the functional currency this is the old behaviour plus the entry).
  perform fin_create_open_item_fx(
    v_pol.tenant_id, v_ar_acc, 'ar_control', 'ins_policy_premium', v_pol.id,
    v_pol.policy_no, v_pol.inception_date, v_pol.inception_date, v_pol.gross_premium,
    'Premium due: ' || v_pol.policy_no, v_pol.currency, v_je.id
  );
  perform fin_create_open_item_fx(
    v_pol.tenant_id, v_ins_acc, 'insurer_payable', 'ins_policy_insurer', v_pol.id,
    v_pol.policy_no, v_pol.inception_date, v_pol.inception_date, v_net,
    'Net premium payable to insurer: ' || v_pol.policy_no, v_pol.currency, v_je.id
  );
  if v_comm > 0 then
    insert into ins_commission_entries (tenant_id, policy_id, amount)
    values (v_pol.tenant_id, v_pol.id, v_comm);
  end if;
  perform set_config('ins.via_rpc', 'on', true);
  update ins_policies
     set status = 'active',
         commission_amount = v_comm,
         net_premium_to_insurer = v_net,
         bound_at = now(),
         bound_by = auth.uid(),
         journal_entry_id = v_je.id
   where id = v_pol.id
  returning * into v_pol;
  if v_pol.renewal_of_id is not null then
    update ins_policies set status = 'renewed'
     where id = v_pol.renewal_of_id and tenant_id = v_pol.tenant_id and status = 'active';
  end if;
  return v_pol;
end;
$function$
-- FN platform_ins_bind_policy_risk_legacy_impl(uuid)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.platform_ins_bind_policy_risk_legacy_impl(p_policy_id uuid)
 RETURNS ins_policies
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
declare p ins_policies%rowtype; s ins_policy_insurer_shares%rowtype; c ins_policy_charges%rowtype;
 n integer; leads integer; pct numeric; extended boolean; premium_used numeric:=0; part numeric;
 comm numeric:=0; payable numeric:=0; fees numeric:=0; vat numeric:=0; total numeric; ar uuid; insurer_account uuid;
 ar_gl uuid; pay_gl uuid; unearned_gl uuid; income_gl uuid; vat_gl uuid; lines jsonb; je journal_entries%rowtype; snap jsonb;
begin
 if not can_manage_insurance() then raise exception 'INS_FORBIDDEN: Insurance manager required' using errcode='42501'; end if;
 select * into p from ins_policies where id=p_policy_id for update;
 if not found then raise exception 'INS_NOT_FOUND: policy'; end if;
 perform assert_tenant_access(p.tenant_id);
 if p.status<>'approved' then raise exception 'INS_NOT_APPROVED: policy is % (submit for approval first)', p.status; end if;
 select count(*),count(*) filter(where is_lead),sum(share_pct) into n,leads,pct from ins_policy_insurer_shares where policy_id=p.id;
 extended:=n>1 or exists(select 1 from ins_policy_charges where policy_id=p.id);
 if n>0 then
  if pct<>100 or leads<>1 then raise exception 'INS_SHARE_TOTAL: exactly 100%% and one lead required'; end if;
  if exists(select 1 from ins_policy_insurer_shares sh join ins_insurers i on i.id=sh.insurer_id where sh.policy_id=p.id and (not sh.reviewed or not i.is_active)) then
    raise exception 'INS_CONFIG_REVIEW: review every insurer share before binding';
  end if;
  if p.insurer_id is distinct from (select insurer_id from ins_policy_insurer_shares where policy_id=p.id and is_lead) then
    raise exception 'INS_SHARE_LEAD: policy insurer must match the configured lead';
  end if;
 end if;
 if not extended then
   if n=1 then
    if (select commission_rate_pct from ins_policy_insurer_shares where policy_id=p.id) is distinct from p.commission_rate_pct then
     raise exception 'INS_LEGACY_RATE: the single-insurer path uses the policy header rate (maximum two decimals)'; end if;
    update ins_policy_insurer_shares set premium_amount=p.gross_premium,pass_through_amount=0,commission_amount=round(p.gross_premium*p.commission_rate_pct/100,2),
     payable_amount=p.gross_premium-round(p.gross_premium*p.commission_rate_pct/100,2) where policy_id=p.id;
   end if;
   -- This function body, its journal and its TWO open items are unchanged.
   p:=platform_ins_bind_policy_legacy_impl(p.id);
   insert into ins_policy_financial_snapshots(policy_id,tenant_id,extended,invoice_total,broker_fee_total,vat_total,snapshot,journal_entry_id,bound_by)
   values(p.id,p.tenant_id,false,p.gross_premium,0,0,jsonb_build_object('policy',to_jsonb(p),'shares',coalesce((select jsonb_agg(to_jsonb(x)) from ins_policy_insurer_shares x where policy_id=p.id),'[]'::jsonb),'charges','[]'::jsonb,'open_items',(select jsonb_agg(to_jsonb(i)) from fin_open_items i where i.source_id=p.id and i.source_type in('ins_policy_premium','ins_policy_insurer'))),p.journal_entry_id,auth.uid());
   return p;
 end if;
 if p.gross_premium<=0 then raise exception 'INS_CONFIG_INPUT: positive premium required'; end if;
 if exists(select 1 from ins_policy_charges where policy_id=p.id and (not reviewed or amount is null)) then
   raise exception 'INS_CONFIG_REVIEW: review charges and enter current amounts, including an explicit zero where intended';
 end if;
 if n=0 then raise exception 'INS_SHARE_REQUIRED: charges require insurer configuration'; end if;
 -- Largest-remainder allocation: exact cents, closest proportional amounts,
 -- deterministic insurer-ID tie-breaks; the lead has no rounding privilege.
 with amounts as (
  select id,insurer_id,trunc(p.gross_premium*share_pct/100,2) as base,
   p.gross_premium*share_pct/100-trunc(p.gross_premium*share_pct/100,2) as fraction
  from ins_policy_insurer_shares where policy_id=p.id
 ), ranked as(select *,row_number() over(order by fraction desc,insurer_id) as rank,
  round((p.gross_premium-sum(base) over())*100) as cents from amounts)
 update ins_policy_insurer_shares sh set premium_amount=r.base+case when r.rank<=r.cents then .01 else 0 end,
  pass_through_amount=0 from ranked r where sh.id=r.id;
 if exists(select 1 from ins_policy_insurer_shares where policy_id=p.id and premium_amount<=0) then
  raise exception 'INS_SHARE_TOO_SMALL: every insurer needs at least one cent of premium'; end if;
 update ins_policy_insurer_shares set commission_amount=round(premium_amount*commission_rate_pct/100,2) where policy_id=p.id;
 select sum(premium_amount) into premium_used from ins_policy_insurer_shares where policy_id=p.id;
 for c in select * from ins_policy_charges where policy_id=p.id order by id loop
   if c.kind='broker_fee' then
     fees:=fees+c.amount;
     part:=case c.vat_mode when 'exclusive' then round(c.amount*c.vat_rate_pct/100,2) else 0 end;
     vat:=vat+part;
     update ins_policy_charges set vat_amount=part where id=c.id;
   elsif c.recipient_mode='one_insurer' then
     update ins_policy_insurer_shares set pass_through_amount=pass_through_amount+c.amount where policy_id=p.id and insurer_id=c.recipient_insurer_id;
     if not found then raise exception 'INS_CHARGE_RECIPIENT: recipient does not participate'; end if;
   else
     with amounts as (
      select id,insurer_id,trunc(c.amount*share_pct/100,2) as base,
       c.amount*share_pct/100-trunc(c.amount*share_pct/100,2) as fraction
      from ins_policy_insurer_shares where policy_id=p.id
     ), ranked as(select *,row_number() over(order by fraction desc,insurer_id) as rank,
      round((c.amount-sum(base) over())*100) as cents from amounts)
     update ins_policy_insurer_shares sh set pass_through_amount=sh.pass_through_amount+r.base+case when r.rank<=r.cents then .01 else 0 end
      from ranked r where sh.id=r.id;
   end if;
 end loop;
 update ins_policy_insurer_shares set payable_amount=premium_amount-commission_amount+pass_through_amount where policy_id=p.id;
 select sum(commission_amount),sum(payable_amount) into comm,payable from ins_policy_insurer_shares where policy_id=p.id;
 total:=p.gross_premium+coalesce((select sum(amount) from ins_policy_charges where policy_id=p.id),0)+vat;
 if premium_used<>p.gross_premium or total<>payable+comm+fees+vat then raise exception 'INS_FINANCIAL_TOTAL: allocation does not reconcile'; end if;
 ar_gl:=get_posting_account(p.tenant_id,'ar_control'); pay_gl:=get_posting_account(p.tenant_id,'insurer_payable');
 unearned_gl:=get_posting_account(p.tenant_id,'unearned_commission'); income_gl:=get_posting_account(p.tenant_id,'commission_income');
 vat_gl:=get_posting_account(p.tenant_id,'vat_output');
 if ar_gl is null or pay_gl is null or (comm>0 and (unearned_gl is null or income_gl is null))
    or (vat>0 and vat_gl is null) then raise exception 'INS_POSTING_RULE_MISSING: configure receivables, insurer payable, commission and (when charged) vat_output'; end if;
 if exists(select 1 from ins_policy_charges chg where chg.policy_id=p.id and chg.kind='broker_fee' and chg.amount>0
   and not exists(select 1 from gl_accounts g where g.id=chg.income_gl_account_id and g.tenant_id=p.tenant_id and g.account_type='revenue' and g.is_active)) then
   raise exception 'INS_POSTING_RULE_MISSING: each broker fee needs an explicit active revenue account'; end if;
 if vat>0 and not exists(select 1 from gl_accounts where id=vat_gl and tenant_id=p.tenant_id and account_type='liability' and is_active) then raise exception 'INS_POSTING_RULE_MISSING: vat_output must map to active tax liability'; end if;
 ar:=ins_ensure_client_account(p.client_id);
 lines:=jsonb_build_array(jsonb_build_object('gl_account_id',ar_gl,'debit',total,'currency',p.currency),jsonb_build_object('gl_account_id',pay_gl,'credit',payable,'currency',p.currency));
 if comm>0 then lines:=lines||jsonb_build_object('gl_account_id',unearned_gl,'credit',comm,'currency',p.currency); end if;
 for c in select * from ins_policy_charges where policy_id=p.id and kind='broker_fee' and amount>0 order by id loop
  lines:=lines||jsonb_build_object('gl_account_id',c.income_gl_account_id,'credit',c.amount,'currency',p.currency);
 end loop;
 if vat>0 then lines:=lines||jsonb_build_object('gl_account_id',vat_gl,'credit',vat,'currency',p.currency); end if;
 -- Booked on the inception date (at the inception-date rate).
 je:=post_journal_entry(p.tenant_id,'ins_policy_bind',p.id,p.inception_date,'Bind '||p.policy_no||' (insurer shares and charges)',lines);
 perform fin_create_open_item_fx(p.tenant_id,ar,'ar_control','ins_policy_premium',p.id,p.policy_no,p.inception_date,p.inception_date,total,'Premium and charges: '||p.policy_no,p.currency,je.id);
 for s in select * from ins_policy_insurer_shares where policy_id=p.id order by insurer_id loop
   insurer_account:=ins_ensure_insurer_account(s.insurer_id);
   if s.payable_amount<=0 then raise exception 'INS_FINANCIAL_TOTAL: each insurer payable must be positive'; end if;
   perform fin_create_open_item_fx(p.tenant_id,insurer_account,'insurer_payable','ins_policy_share_insurer',s.id,p.policy_no,p.inception_date,p.inception_date,s.payable_amount,'Insurer share: '||p.policy_no,p.currency,je.id);
   if s.commission_amount>0 then insert into ins_commission_entries(tenant_id,policy_id,insurer_share_id,amount) values(p.tenant_id,p.id,s.id,s.commission_amount); end if;
 end loop;
 perform set_config('ins.via_rpc','on',true);
 update ins_policies set status='active',commission_amount=comm,net_premium_to_insurer=p.gross_premium-comm,bound_at=now(),bound_by=auth.uid(),journal_entry_id=je.id where id=p.id returning * into p;
 if p.renewal_of_id is not null then update ins_policies set status='renewed' where id=p.renewal_of_id and tenant_id=p.tenant_id and status='active'; end if;
 snap:=jsonb_build_object('policy',to_jsonb(p),'shares',(select jsonb_agg(to_jsonb(x) order by insurer_id) from ins_policy_insurer_shares x where policy_id=p.id),
  'charges',coalesce((select jsonb_agg(to_jsonb(x) order by id) from ins_policy_charges x where policy_id=p.id),'[]'::jsonb),
  'open_items',(select jsonb_agg(to_jsonb(i)) from fin_open_items i where tenant_id=p.tenant_id and ((source_type='ins_policy_premium' and source_id=p.id)
    or(source_type='ins_policy_share_insurer' and source_id in(select id from ins_policy_insurer_shares where policy_id=p.id)))));
 insert into ins_policy_financial_snapshots(policy_id,tenant_id,extended,invoice_total,broker_fee_total,vat_total,snapshot,journal_entry_id,bound_by)
 values(p.id,p.tenant_id,true,total,fees,vat,snap,je.id,auth.uid());
 return p;
end $function$
-- FN platform_ins_calculate_insurer_cancellation(jsonb,jsonb,date,date,date)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.platform_ins_calculate_insurer_cancellation(p_allocation jsonb, p_terms jsonb, p_inception date, p_expiry date, p_effective date)
 RETURNS jsonb
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 IMMUTABLE
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
declare premium numeric; original_comm numeric; rate numeric; minimum numeric;
 retained numeric; returned numeric; clawback numeric; total_days integer; used_days integer;
 band jsonb; band_day integer; band_rate numeric; selected_rate numeric; last_day integer:=-1; last_rate numeric:=-1;
 method text:=p_terms->>'method'; ref text:=btrim(p_terms->>'contract_reference');
begin
 if jsonb_typeof(p_allocation) is distinct from 'object' or jsonb_typeof(p_terms) is distinct from 'object' then
  raise exception 'INS_CANCELLATION_INPUT: insurer allocation and contractual terms required'; end if;
 premium:=platform_ins_cancellation_money(p_allocation->>'premium_amount');
 original_comm:=platform_ins_cancellation_money(p_allocation->>'commission_amount');
 rate:=platform_ins_cancellation_percentage(p_allocation->>'commission_rate_pct',false);
 if nullif(p_allocation->>'insurer_id','') is null or premium<=0 or original_comm<>platform_ins_cancellation_round_ratio(premium*rate,100) then
  raise exception 'INS_CANCELLATION_INPUT: original bound premium and commission do not reconcile'; end if;
 if ref is null or length(ref)<5 then raise exception 'INS_CANCELLATION_REVIEW_REQUIRED: record insurer-specific contract evidence'; end if;
 if p_inception is null or p_expiry is null or p_effective is null or p_expiry<p_inception or p_effective<p_inception or p_effective>p_expiry+1 then
  raise exception 'INS_CANCELLATION_INPUT: effective date is outside the inclusive cover period'; end if;
 total_days:=p_expiry-p_inception+1; used_days:=p_effective-p_inception;
 minimum:=platform_ins_cancellation_money(coalesce(p_terms->>'minimum_retained_premium','0'));
 if minimum>premium then raise exception 'INS_CANCELLATION_INPUT: minimum retained exceeds original premium'; end if;
 case method
 when 'pro_rata' then
  if jsonb_typeof(p_terms->'short_period_bands')='array' and p_terms->'short_period_bands'<>'[]'::jsonb then
   raise exception 'INS_CANCELLATION_REVIEW_REQUIRED: specific short-period terms take priority over pro-rata'; end if;
  if p_terms->'pro_rata_permitted' is distinct from 'true'::jsonb then
   raise exception 'INS_CANCELLATION_REVIEW_REQUIRED: contract must permit pro-rata'; end if;
  retained:=premium-platform_ins_cancellation_round_ratio(premium*(total_days-used_days),total_days);
 when 'short_period' then
  if jsonb_typeof(p_terms->'short_period_bands') is distinct from 'array' then
   raise exception 'INS_CANCELLATION_REVIEW_REQUIRED: complete short-period schedule required'; end if;
  if jsonb_array_length(p_terms->'short_period_bands') not between 1 and 100 then
   raise exception 'INS_CANCELLATION_REVIEW_REQUIRED: complete short-period schedule required'; end if;
  for band in select value from jsonb_array_elements(p_terms->'short_period_bands') loop
   if coalesce(band->>'through_day','') !~ '^\d+$' then raise exception 'INS_CANCELLATION_INPUT: short-period days must be non-negative integers'; end if;
   band_day:=(band->>'through_day')::integer;
   band_rate:=platform_ins_cancellation_percentage(band->>'retained_pct');
   if band_day<=last_day or band_rate<last_rate then raise exception 'INS_CANCELLATION_INPUT: short-period days must increase and rates cannot decrease'; end if;
   if selected_rate is null and used_days<=band_day then selected_rate:=band_rate; end if;
   last_day:=band_day; last_rate:=band_rate;
  end loop;
  if last_day<total_days or last_rate<>100 then raise exception 'INS_CANCELLATION_REVIEW_REQUIRED: final band must cover full term at 100 percent retention'; end if;
  retained:=platform_ins_cancellation_round_ratio(premium*selected_rate,100);
 when 'non_refundable' then retained:=premium;
 when 'manual_review' then
  if p_terms->>'manual_return_premium' is null then raise exception 'INS_CANCELLATION_REVIEW_REQUIRED: Finance must determine the actual return premium'; end if;
  returned:=platform_ins_cancellation_money(p_terms->>'manual_return_premium');
  if returned>premium then raise exception 'INS_CANCELLATION_INPUT: return exceeds original premium'; end if;
  retained:=premium-returned;
 else raise exception 'INS_CANCELLATION_INPUT: unknown cancellation method';
 end case;
 retained:=greatest(retained,minimum);
 if used_days=total_days then retained:=premium; end if;
 returned:=premium-retained;
 clawback:=case when returned=premium then original_comm else platform_ins_cancellation_round_ratio(returned*rate,100) end;
 if clawback>original_comm or clawback>returned then raise exception 'INS_CANCELLATION_INPUT: commission clawback does not reconcile'; end if;
 return jsonb_build_object('insurer_id',p_allocation->>'insurer_id','method',method,'contract_reference',ref,
  'original_premium',premium::text,'retained_premium',retained::text,'return_premium',returned::text,
  'original_commission',original_comm::text,'commission_clawback',clawback::text,'net_insurer_credit',(returned-clawback)::text,
  'requires_finance_exception',method='manual_review');
end $function$
-- FN platform_ins_cancellation_cash_authorized(uuid,uuid,numeric)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.platform_ins_cancellation_cash_authorized(p_item uuid, p_settlement uuid, p_amount numeric)
 RETURNS boolean
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
declare q ins_cancellation_cash_requests%rowtype; c ins_policy_cancellations%rowtype; target uuid;
begin
 select * into q from ins_cancellation_cash_requests where settlement_id=p_settlement and status='posting' and operation_txid=txid_current() and checker_id=auth.uid() and maker_id<>auth.uid() and tenant_id=get_my_tenant_id();
 if not found then return false; end if;
 perform ins_policy_cash_require_finance();
 select * into c from ins_policy_cancellations where id=q.cancellation_id and status='posted';
 target:=case q.kind when 'refund' then c.refund_item_id when 'premium_receipt' then(select id from fin_open_items where source_type='ins_policy_premium' and source_id=c.policy_id)
 when 'recovery' then(select recovery_item_id from ins_cancellation_insurer_adjustments where id=q.adjustment_id and cancellation_id=c.id)
 when 'remittance' then(select original_item_id from ins_cancellation_insurer_adjustments where id=q.adjustment_id and cancellation_id=c.id) end;
 return c.id is not null and target=p_item and q.amount=p_amount and exists(select 1 from fin_settlements s join fin_open_items i on i.id=p_item where s.id=p_settlement and s.amount=q.amount and s.bank_account_id=q.bank_account_id and s.settlement_date=q.cash_date and s.party_account_id=i.party_account_id and s.direction=case q.kind when 'refund' then 'out' when 'remittance' then 'out' else 'in' end) and not exists(select 1 from fin_allocations where settlement_id=p_settlement);
end $function$
-- FN platform_ins_cancellation_cash_bank(uuid,uuid,text)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.platform_ins_cancellation_cash_bank(p_id uuid, p_tenant uuid, p_kind text)
 RETURNS fin_bank_accounts
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
declare b fin_bank_accounts%rowtype;
begin
 select * into b from fin_bank_accounts where id=p_id and tenant_id=p_tenant;
 if not found or not b.is_active or b.kind<>p_kind or b.currency<>fin_ledger_currency(p_tenant)
 or not exists(select 1 from gl_accounts g where g.id=b.gl_account_id and g.tenant_id=b.tenant_id and g.is_active and g.account_type='asset')
 or exists(select 1 from fin_bank_accounts other where other.id<>b.id and other.gl_account_id=b.gl_account_id)
 or exists(select 1 from gl_posting_rules r where r.tenant_id=b.tenant_id and r.gl_account_id=b.gl_account_id and
  ((p_kind='client_money' and r.account_role in('bank','cash')) or(p_kind='operating' and r.account_role='client_money_bank'))) then
 raise exception 'INS_CXL_CASH_BANK: active ledger-currency % bank with a unique, segregated asset GL required',p_kind; end if;
 return b;
end $function$
-- FN platform_ins_cancellation_cash_banks_sound(uuid)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.platform_ins_cancellation_cash_banks_sound(p_case uuid)
 RETURNS boolean
 LANGUAGE sql
 STABLE SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
 select not exists(select 1 from fin_bank_accounts b
 where b.kind='client_money' and b.id in(select bank_id from platform_ins_cancellation_cash_movements(p_case))
 and (select coalesce(sum(l.debit-l.credit),0) from journal_entry_lines l join journal_entries j on j.id=l.journal_entry_id and j.status='posted' where l.gl_account_id=b.gl_account_id and j.tenant_id=b.tenant_id)
 < greatest((select coalesce(sum(case f.direction when 'in' then f.amount else -f.amount end),0) from fin_settlements f where f.bank_account_id=b.id and f.tenant_id=b.tenant_id and f.status='posted')+
 (select coalesce(sum(case when t.to_bank_account_id=b.id then t.amount else -t.amount end),0) from ins_commission_transfers t where b.id in(t.from_bank_account_id,t.to_bank_account_id) and t.status='posted'),0))
$function$
-- FN platform_ins_cancellation_cash_emit(uuid,uuid,boolean)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.platform_ins_cancellation_cash_emit(p_request uuid, p_item uuid, p_second boolean DEFAULT false)
 RETURNS uuid
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
declare q ins_cancellation_cash_requests%rowtype; c ins_policy_cancellations%rowtype; i fin_open_items%rowtype; s fin_settlements%rowtype; b fin_bank_accounts%rowtype;
 sid uuid:=gen_random_uuid(); party uuid; direction text; control uuid; je journal_entries%rowtype; lines jsonb;
begin
 select * into q from ins_cancellation_cash_requests where id=p_request and tenant_id=get_my_tenant_id() and status='posting' and operation_txid=txid_current() and checker_id=auth.uid() and maker_id<>auth.uid();
 if not found then raise exception 'INS_CXL_CASH_CONTEXT: exact checker transaction required'; end if;
 perform ins_policy_cash_require_finance(); select * into c from ins_policy_cancellations where id=q.cancellation_id;
 select * into b from fin_bank_accounts where id=case when p_second then q.to_bank_account_id else q.bank_account_id end;
 direction:=case when p_second then 'in' when q.kind in('refund','remittance','commission_return','commission_withdrawal') then 'out' else 'in' end;
 if p_item is not null then select * into i from fin_open_items where id=p_item; party:=i.party_account_id;
 control:=(c.accounting_mappings->>case q.kind when 'refund' then 'refund_payable' when 'remittance' then 'payable' else 'ar' end)::uuid;
 else select party_account_id into party from fin_open_items where source_type='ins_policy_premium' and source_id=c.policy_id; control:=c.clearing_gl_account_id; end if;
 if p_second then update ins_cancellation_cash_requests set to_settlement_id=sid where id=q.id; else update ins_cancellation_cash_requests set settlement_id=sid where id=q.id; end if;
 insert into fin_settlements(id,tenant_id,settlement_no,direction,bank_account_id,party_account_id,amount,currency,settlement_date,reference,notes)
 values(sid,q.tenant_id,next_doc_number(q.tenant_id,'fin_settlement','STL'),direction,b.id,party,q.amount,b.currency,q.cash_date,q.reference,q.notes);
 if p_item is not null then insert into fin_allocations(tenant_id,settlement_id,open_item_id,amount) values(q.tenant_id,sid,p_item,q.amount); end if;
 lines:=jsonb_build_array(jsonb_build_object('gl_account_id',b.gl_account_id,case direction when 'in' then 'debit' else 'credit' end,q.amount),jsonb_build_object('gl_account_id',control,case direction when 'in' then 'credit' else 'debit' end,q.amount));
 je:=post_journal_entry(q.tenant_id,'ins_cancellation_cash',sid,q.cash_date,'Cancellation cash '||q.kind||': '||q.reference,lines);
 insert into ins_cancellation_cash_journals values(q.tenant_id,q.id,je.id,case when p_second then 'destination_cash' else 'cash' end);
 update fin_settlements set journal_entry_id=je.id where id=sid;
 return sid;
end $function$
-- FN platform_ins_cancellation_cash_movements(uuid)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.platform_ins_cancellation_cash_movements(p_case uuid)
 RETURNS TABLE(bank_id uuid, amount numeric, proven_amount numeric)
 LANGUAGE sql
 STABLE SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
 with c as(select * from ins_policy_cancellations where id=p_case),
 original as(select s.bank_account_id as bank_id,case s.direction when 'in' then a.amount else -a.amount end as amount,
 case when s.direction='out' or platform_ins_cancellation_cash_proven(s.id) then case s.direction when 'in' then a.amount else -a.amount end else 0 end as proven_amount
 from c join ins_policy_cash_items k on k.source_id=c.policy_id join fin_allocations a on a.open_item_id=k.id join fin_settlements s on s.id=a.settlement_id and s.status='posted'
 where not exists(select 1 from ins_cancellation_cash_requests q where s.id in(q.settlement_id,q.to_settlement_id))),
 withdrawn as(select t.from_bank_account_id as bank_id,-e.amount as amount,-e.amount as proven_amount
 from c join ins_commission_entries e on e.policy_id=c.policy_id and e.status='transferred' join ins_commission_transfers t on t.id=e.transfer_id and t.status='posted'),
 new_cash as(select s.bank_account_id as bank_id,case s.direction when 'in' then s.amount else -s.amount end as amount,
 case when s.direction='out' or(platform_ins_cancellation_cash_proven(s.id) and(q.kind<>'commission_return' or platform_ins_cancellation_cash_proven(q.settlement_id))) then case s.direction when 'in' then s.amount else -s.amount end else 0 end as proven_amount
 from ins_cancellation_cash_requests q join fin_settlements s on s.id in(q.settlement_id,q.to_settlement_id) and s.status='posted'
 join fin_bank_accounts b on b.id=s.bank_account_id and b.kind='client_money'
 where q.cancellation_id=p_case and q.kind<>'void' and q.status in('posted','posting'))
 select * from original union all select * from withdrawn union all select * from new_cash
$function$
-- FN platform_ins_cancellation_cash_position(uuid,uuid)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.platform_ins_cancellation_cash_position(p_case uuid, p_bank uuid DEFAULT NULL::uuid)
 RETURNS jsonb
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
declare c ins_policy_cancellations%rowtype; a ins_cancellation_insurer_adjustments%rowtype; b fin_bank_accounts%rowtype; sn ins_policy_financial_snapshots%rowtype;
 original_gross numeric; retained_total numeric; collected numeric; ratio numeric; refund_due numeric; fees numeric; reserve_comm numeric:=0; earned_hold numeric:=0;
 cash numeric; bank_cash numeric; raw_bank_cash numeric; book numeric; due numeric; recovered numeric; returned numeric; withdrawn numeric; spent numeric; funded numeric; hold numeric; paid numeric;
 parts jsonb:='[]'; part jsonb; original_premium numeric; original_pass numeric; original_commission numeric; sound boolean;
begin
 select * into c from ins_policy_cancellations where id=p_case;
 if not found or c.status<>'posted' then raise exception 'INS_CXL_CASH_STATE: posted cancellation required'; end if;
 select * into sn from ins_policy_financial_snapshots where policy_id=c.policy_id;
 select coalesce(sn.invoice_total,p.gross_premium),p.gross_premium into original_gross,original_premium from ins_policies p where p.id=c.policy_id;
 retained_total:=original_gross-(c.calculation->>'invoice_credit')::numeric;
 select coalesce(sum(al.amount),0) into collected from ins_policy_cash_items k join fin_allocations al on al.open_item_id=k.id
 join fin_settlements s on s.id=al.settlement_id and s.status='posted' and s.direction='in'
 where k.source_id=c.policy_id and k.source_type='ins_policy_premium' and platform_ins_cancellation_cash_proven(s.id);
 ratio:=case when retained_total=0 then 0 else least(greatest(collected-(c.calculation->>'client_refund_due')::numeric,0)/retained_total,1) end;
 select coalesce(outstanding_amount,0) into refund_due from fin_open_item_balances where id=c.refund_item_id;
 refund_due:=coalesce(refund_due,0);
 fees:=coalesce(sn.broker_fee_total,0)+coalesce(sn.vat_total,0)-coalesce((select sum((x->>'refund_base')::numeric+(x->>'refund_vat')::numeric) from jsonb_array_elements(c.calculation->'charges') x where x->>'kind'='broker_fee'),0);
 select coalesce(sum(proven_amount),0),coalesce(sum(proven_amount) filter(where bank_id=p_bank),0),coalesce(sum(amount) filter(where bank_id=p_bank),0)
 into cash,bank_cash,raw_bank_cash from platform_ins_cancellation_cash_movements(c.id);
 select * into b from fin_bank_accounts where id=p_bank and tenant_id=c.tenant_id;
 select coalesce(sum(l.debit-l.credit),0) into book from journal_entry_lines l join journal_entries j on j.id=l.journal_entry_id and j.status='posted'
 where j.tenant_id=c.tenant_id and l.gl_account_id=b.gl_account_id;
 sound:=platform_ins_cancellation_cash_banks_sound(c.id);
 for a in select * from ins_cancellation_insurer_adjustments where cancellation_id=c.id order by insurer_id loop
  select coalesce(outstanding_amount,0) into due from fin_open_item_balances where id=a.original_item_id;
  select coalesce(sum(s.amount),0) into recovered from ins_cancellation_cash_requests q join fin_settlements s on s.id=q.settlement_id and s.status='posted'
   where q.adjustment_id=a.id and q.kind='recovery' and platform_ins_cancellation_cash_proven(s.id);
  select coalesce(sum(q.amount),0) into returned from ins_cancellation_cash_requests q where q.adjustment_id=a.id and q.kind='commission_return' and q.status='posted'
   and platform_ins_cancellation_cash_proven(q.settlement_id) and platform_ins_cancellation_cash_proven(q.to_settlement_id);
  select coalesce(sum(q.amount),0) into withdrawn from ins_cancellation_cash_requests q where q.adjustment_id=a.id and q.kind='commission_withdrawal' and q.status='posted';
  select coalesce(sum(al.amount),0) into paid from fin_allocations al join fin_settlements s on s.id=al.settlement_id and s.status='posted' where al.open_item_id=a.original_item_id;
  select coalesce(s.premium_amount,p.gross_premium),coalesce(s.pass_through_amount,0),coalesce(s.commission_amount,p.commission_amount)
    into original_premium,original_pass,original_commission from ins_policies p left join ins_policy_insurer_shares s on s.id=a.insurer_share_id where p.id=c.policy_id;
  funded:=trunc((original_premium+original_pass-a.return_premium-a.pass_through_refund)*ratio,2);
  spent:=paid-recovered+case when a.retained_commission_status='transferred' then original_commission else 0 end-returned+withdrawn;
  hold:=greatest(a.retained_commission-case when a.retained_commission_status='transferred' then a.retained_commission else withdrawn end,0);
  reserve_comm:=reserve_comm+hold;
  if due=0 then earned_hold:=earned_hold+hold; end if;
  parts:=parts||jsonb_build_array(jsonb_build_object('adjustment_id',a.id,'insurer_id',a.insurer_id,'insurer_name',(select name from ins_insurers where id=a.insurer_id),
   'outstanding',due::text,'recovery_due',coalesce((select outstanding_amount from fin_open_item_balances where id=a.recovery_item_id),0)::text,
   'commission_return_due',(a.transferred_clawback_due-coalesce((select sum(amount) from ins_cancellation_cash_requests where adjustment_id=a.id and kind='commission_return' and status='posted'),0))::text,
   'retained_commission',a.retained_commission::text,'commission_withdrawal_due',case when due=0 then hold::text else '0.00' end,
   'funded_gross',funded::text,'spent',spent::text,'funding_cap',greatest(funded-spent,0)::text,'commission_status',case when a.retained_commission_status='transferred' or(withdrawn>=a.retained_commission and a.retained_commission>0) then 'transferred' when due=0 then 'earned' else 'deferred' end));
 end loop;
 return jsonb_build_object('cancellation_id',c.id,'policy_id',c.policy_id,'currency',(select currency from ins_policies where id=c.policy_id),
  'retained_invoice',retained_total::text,'proven_client_collections',collected::text,'funding_ratio',ratio::text,'proven_cash',cash::text,
  'bank_proven_cash',bank_cash::text,'bank_raw_cash',raw_bank_cash::text,'bank_book_cash',book::text,'client_refund_outstanding',refund_due::text,
  'retained_fee_vat_reserve',fees::text,'commission_reserve',reserve_comm::text,'earned_commission_reserve',earned_hold::text,
  'insurer_payable_reserve',coalesce((select sum((x->>'outstanding')::numeric) from jsonb_array_elements(parts) x),0)::text,
  'bank_funding_sound',sound,
 'maximum_refund',case when sound then greatest(least(refund_due,cash-coalesce((select sum((x->>'outstanding')::numeric) from jsonb_array_elements(parts) x),0)-reserve_comm-fees,bank_cash,book),0)::text else '0.00' end,
  'client_premium_outstanding',coalesce((select outstanding_amount from fin_open_item_balances where source_type='ins_policy_premium' and source_id=c.policy_id),0)::text,'insurers',(select jsonb_agg(x||jsonb_build_object(
 'maximum_remittance',case when sound then greatest(least((x->>'outstanding')::numeric,(x->>'funding_cap')::numeric,cash-refund_due-trunc(fees*ratio,2)-earned_hold,bank_cash,book),0)::text else '0.00' end,
 'maximum_commission_withdrawal',case when sound then greatest(least((x->>'commission_withdrawal_due')::numeric,(x->>'funding_cap')::numeric,cash-refund_due-coalesce((select sum((y->>'outstanding')::numeric) from jsonb_array_elements(parts) y),0)-fees-(reserve_comm-(x->>'commission_withdrawal_due')::numeric),bank_cash,book),0)::text else '0.00' end,
 'maximum_commission_return',greatest(least((x->>'commission_return_due')::numeric,book),0)::text)) from jsonb_array_elements(parts) x));
end $function$
-- FN platform_ins_cancellation_cash_proven(uuid)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.platform_ins_cancellation_cash_proven(p_settlement_id uuid)
 RETURNS boolean
 LANGUAGE sql
 STABLE SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
 select exists(select 1 from fin_settlements s join bank_reconciliations r on r.settlement_id=s.id
 join bank_statement_lines l on l.id=r.bank_statement_line_id and l.tenant_id=s.tenant_id
 join fin_bank_accounts b on b.id=s.bank_account_id
 where s.id=p_settlement_id and s.status='posted' and r.variance=0 and r.matched_by is not null
 and l.bank_account=b.name and l.currency=s.currency and l.amount=case s.direction when 'in' then s.amount else -s.amount end)
$function$
-- FN platform_ins_cancellation_cash_recognize(uuid,uuid)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.platform_ins_cancellation_cash_recognize(p_request uuid, p_adjustment uuid)
 RETURNS void
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
declare q ins_cancellation_cash_requests%rowtype; c ins_policy_cancellations%rowtype; a ins_cancellation_insurer_adjustments%rowtype; outstanding numeric; recognized numeric; delta numeric; je journal_entries%rowtype;
begin
 select * into q from ins_cancellation_cash_requests where id=p_request and status='posting' and operation_txid=txid_current() and checker_id=auth.uid() and tenant_id=get_my_tenant_id();
 if not found then raise exception 'INS_CXL_CASH_CONTEXT: recognition requires current checker'; end if;
 select * into a from ins_cancellation_insurer_adjustments where id=p_adjustment and cancellation_id=q.cancellation_id;
 if not found or a.retained_commission_status<>'deferred' or a.retained_commission=0 then return; end if;
 select * into c from ins_policy_cancellations where id=q.cancellation_id;
 select outstanding_amount into outstanding from fin_open_item_balances where id=a.original_item_id;
 select coalesce(sum(amount),0) into recognized from ins_cancellation_commission_events where adjustment_id=a.id;
 delta:=case when outstanding=0 then a.retained_commission else 0 end-recognized;
 if delta=0 then return; end if;
 je:=post_journal_entry(q.tenant_id,'ins_cancellation_cash',q.id,current_date,'Retained commission recognition: '||q.reference,
 jsonb_build_array(jsonb_build_object('gl_account_id',(c.accounting_mappings->>'unearned')::uuid,'debit',greatest(delta,0),'credit',greatest(-delta,0)),jsonb_build_object('gl_account_id',(c.accounting_mappings->>'income')::uuid,'credit',greatest(delta,0),'debit',greatest(-delta,0))));
 insert into ins_cancellation_cash_journals values(q.tenant_id,q.id,je.id,'retained_commission_recognition');
 insert into ins_cancellation_commission_events(tenant_id,request_id,adjustment_id,amount,journal_entry_id) values(q.tenant_id,q.id,a.id,delta,je.id);
end $function$
-- FN platform_ins_cancellation_cash_validate(uuid,text,uuid,uuid,uuid,numeric,date)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.platform_ins_cancellation_cash_validate(p_case uuid, p_kind text, p_adjustment uuid, p_bank uuid, p_to_bank uuid, p_amount numeric, p_date date)
 RETURNS uuid
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
declare c ins_policy_cancellations%rowtype; a ins_cancellation_insurer_adjustments%rowtype; v jsonb; sh jsonb; b fin_bank_accounts%rowtype; dest fin_bank_accounts%rowtype;
 cap numeric:=0; item uuid; original_transfer ins_commission_transfers%rowtype;
begin
 perform ins_policy_cash_require_finance();
 select * into c from ins_policy_cancellations where id=p_case and tenant_id=get_my_tenant_id();
 if not found or c.status<>'posted' or not exists(select 1 from ins_policies where id=c.policy_id and status='cancelled') then raise exception 'INS_CXL_CASH_STATE: posted cancellation and cancelled policy required'; end if;
 if p_amount is null or p_amount<=0 or p_amount<>round(p_amount,2) or p_amount>9999999999999999.99 or p_date is distinct from current_date then raise exception 'INS_CXL_CASH_INPUT: positive exact-cent amount and current accounting date required'; end if;
 if p_kind is null or p_kind not in('recovery','premium_receipt','refund','remittance','commission_return','commission_withdrawal') then raise exception 'INS_CXL_CASH_INPUT: unsupported cash kind'; end if;
 if exists(select 1 from accounting_periods where tenant_id=c.tenant_id and status='closed' and current_date between period_start and period_end) then raise exception 'INS_CXL_CASH_PERIOD: current period closed'; end if;
 if p_kind in('recovery','remittance','commission_return','commission_withdrawal') then
 select * into a from ins_cancellation_insurer_adjustments where id=p_adjustment and cancellation_id=c.id;
 if not found then raise exception 'INS_CXL_CASH_INSURER: select an adjustment from this cancellation'; end if;
 elsif p_adjustment is not null then raise exception 'INS_CXL_CASH_INPUT: this action has no insurer adjustment'; end if;
 b:=platform_ins_cancellation_cash_bank(p_bank,c.tenant_id,case when p_kind='commission_return' then 'operating' else 'client_money' end);
 if p_kind in('commission_return','commission_withdrawal') then
 dest:=platform_ins_cancellation_cash_bank(p_to_bank,c.tenant_id,case p_kind when 'commission_return' then 'client_money' else 'operating' end);
 if b.id=dest.id or b.gl_account_id=c.clearing_gl_account_id or dest.gl_account_id=c.clearing_gl_account_id then raise exception 'INS_CXL_CASH_BANK: distinct banks and clearing GL required'; end if;
 elsif p_to_bank is not null then raise exception 'INS_CXL_CASH_INPUT: no destination bank for an external settlement'; end if;
 v:=platform_ins_cancellation_cash_position(c.id,b.id);
 if a.id is not null then select x into sh from jsonb_array_elements(v->'insurers') x where x->>'adjustment_id'=a.id::text; end if;
 case p_kind
 when 'recovery' then item:=a.recovery_item_id; cap:=(sh->>'recovery_due')::numeric;
 when 'premium_receipt' then select id into item from fin_open_items where source_type='ins_policy_premium' and source_id=c.policy_id; cap:=(v->>'client_premium_outstanding')::numeric;
 when 'refund' then
 item:=c.refund_item_id;
 cap:=least((v->>'client_refund_outstanding')::numeric,greatest((v->>'proven_cash')::numeric-(v->>'insurer_payable_reserve')::numeric-(v->>'commission_reserve')::numeric-(v->>'retained_fee_vat_reserve')::numeric,0));
 when 'remittance' then
 item:=a.original_item_id;
 cap:=least((sh->>'outstanding')::numeric,(sh->>'funding_cap')::numeric,greatest((v->>'proven_cash')::numeric-(v->>'client_refund_outstanding')::numeric-trunc((v->>'retained_fee_vat_reserve')::numeric*(v->>'funding_ratio')::numeric,2)-(v->>'earned_commission_reserve')::numeric,0));
 when 'commission_return' then
 select * into original_transfer from ins_commission_transfers where id=(a.original_commission_state->>'transfer_id')::uuid and status='posted';
 if original_transfer.id is null or original_transfer.to_bank_account_id<>b.id then raise exception 'INS_CXL_CASH_BANK: return commission from its original operating bank'; end if;
 cap:=least((sh->>'commission_return_due')::numeric,greatest((v->>'bank_book_cash')::numeric,0));
 when 'commission_withdrawal' then
 cap:=least((sh->>'commission_withdrawal_due')::numeric,(sh->>'funding_cap')::numeric,
 greatest((v->>'proven_cash')::numeric-(v->>'client_refund_outstanding')::numeric-(v->>'insurer_payable_reserve')::numeric-(v->>'retained_fee_vat_reserve')::numeric-((v->>'commission_reserve')::numeric-(sh->>'commission_withdrawal_due')::numeric),0));
 end case;
 if p_kind in('refund','remittance','commission_withdrawal') then
 if not(v->>'bank_funding_sound')::boolean then raise exception 'INS_CXL_CASH_FUNDS: unattributed client-bank shortfall; protect other policies and claims, Finance review required'; end if;
 cap:=least(cap,greatest((v->>'bank_proven_cash')::numeric,0),greatest((v->>'bank_book_cash')::numeric,0)); end if;
 if cap is null or p_amount>cap then raise exception 'INS_CXL_CASH_FUNDS: maximum % is %, requested %; refresh balances and reconcile actual funds first',p_kind,coalesce(cap,0),p_amount; end if;
 if item is not null then
  perform id from fin_open_items where id=item for update;
  if get_posting_account(c.tenant_id,(select control_role from fin_open_items where id=item)) is distinct from
   (c.accounting_mappings->>case p_kind when 'refund' then 'refund_payable' when 'remittance' then 'payable' else 'ar' end)::uuid then raise exception 'INS_CXL_CASH_MAPPING: original control mapping changed'; end if;
 end if;
 return item;
end $function$
-- FN platform_ins_cancellation_cash_void_authorized(uuid)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.platform_ins_cancellation_cash_void_authorized(p_settlement uuid)
 RETURNS boolean
 LANGUAGE sql
 STABLE SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
 select exists(select 1 from ins_cancellation_cash_requests target join ins_cancellation_cash_requests v on v.voids_request_id=target.id and v.cancellation_id=target.cancellation_id
 where p_settlement in(target.settlement_id,target.to_settlement_id) and target.status='posted' and v.status='posting' and v.operation_txid=txid_current() and v.checker_id=auth.uid() and v.maker_id<>auth.uid() and v.tenant_id=get_my_tenant_id())
$function$
-- FN platform_ins_cancellation_context(uuid)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.platform_ins_cancellation_context(p_id uuid)
 RETURNS ins_policy_cancellations
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
declare c ins_policy_cancellations%rowtype;
begin
 perform ins_policy_cash_require_finance();
 select * into c from ins_policy_cancellations where id=p_id and tenant_id=get_my_tenant_id();
 if not found or c.status<>'posting' or c.operation_txid<>txid_current() or c.checked_by is distinct from auth.uid()
  or c.prepared_by=auth.uid() or c.proposed_by=auth.uid() then
  raise exception 'INS_CANCELLATION_CONTEXT: independent current-transaction approval required' using errcode='42501'; end if;
 return c;
end $function$
-- FN platform_ins_cancellation_credit(uuid,uuid,numeric)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.platform_ins_cancellation_credit(p_cancellation_id uuid, p_item_id uuid, p_amount numeric)
 RETURNS uuid
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
declare c ins_policy_cancellations%rowtype; i fin_open_items%rowtype; nid uuid:=gen_random_uuid(); no text; je journal_entries%rowtype; lines jsonb; control uuid;
 v_functional text; v_ccy text; v_foreign boolean; v_nr numeric:=1; v_rate numeric:=1; v_booked numeric; v_base numeric; v_result numeric:=0; v_fx jsonb;
begin
 c:=platform_ins_cancellation_context(p_cancellation_id);
 select * into i from fin_open_items where id=p_item_id and tenant_id=c.tenant_id for update;
 if not found or i.control_role not in('ar_control','insurer_payable') or p_amount<=0 or p_amount<>round(p_amount,2) then raise exception 'INS_CANCELLATION_CONTEXT: invalid original credit target'; end if;
 if not exists(select 1 from ins_policy_cash_items m where m.id=i.id and m.source_id=c.policy_id and m.source_type in('ins_policy_premium','ins_policy_insurer')) then raise exception 'INS_CANCELLATION_CONTEXT: target outside original policy'; end if;
 no:=next_doc_number(c.tenant_id,'fin_credit_note','CRN'); control:=(c.accounting_mappings->>case i.control_role when 'ar_control' then 'ar' else 'payable' end)::uuid;
 v_functional:=fin_ledger_currency(c.tenant_id);
 v_ccy:=i.currency; v_foreign:=v_ccy is distinct from v_functional;
 if v_foreign then
  -- The original item must carry the rate it was booked at; the note-date rate prices
  -- the clearing side, and the difference is realised.
  if i.journal_entry_id is null or i.fx_rate is null or i.fx_rate<=0 then
   raise exception 'INS_CANCELLATION_RATE: the original item has no booked rate, so it cannot be credited in %', v_ccy;
  end if;
  v_rate:=i.fx_rate;
  v_nr:=round((platform_fx_rate(c.tenant_id,v_ccy,current_date)).rate,8);
  v_booked:=round(p_amount*v_rate,2);
  v_base:=round(p_amount*v_nr,2);
  v_result:=case i.side when 'receivable' then v_base-v_booked else v_booked-v_base end;
 end if;
 insert into ins_cancellation_credit_links(tenant_id,cancellation_id,credit_note_id,open_item_id,amount) values(c.tenant_id,c.id,nid,i.id,p_amount);
 insert into fin_credit_notes(id,tenant_id,credit_note_no,side,control_role,party_account_id,offset_gl_account_id,amount,currency,fx_rate,realised_result,credit_date,reason,reference)
 values(nid,c.tenant_id,no,i.side,i.control_role,i.party_account_id,c.clearing_gl_account_id,p_amount,v_ccy,v_nr,v_result,current_date,c.reason,c.cancellation_no);
 insert into fin_credit_applications(tenant_id,credit_note_id,open_item_id,amount) values(c.tenant_id,nid,i.id,p_amount);
 lines:=case i.side when 'receivable' then jsonb_build_array(jsonb_build_object('gl_account_id',c.clearing_gl_account_id,'debit',p_amount),jsonb_build_object('gl_account_id',control,'credit',p_amount))
 else jsonb_build_array(jsonb_build_object('gl_account_id',control,'debit',p_amount),jsonb_build_object('gl_account_id',c.clearing_gl_account_id,'credit',p_amount)) end;
 if v_foreign then
  -- Control side at the booked rate (naming the booking entry); clearing side at today's rate.
  lines:=(select jsonb_agg(case when (x->>'gl_account_id')::uuid=control
           then x||jsonb_build_object('currency',v_ccy,'rate_from_entry',i.journal_entry_id,'fx_rate',v_rate)
           else x||jsonb_build_object('currency',v_ccy) end)
          from jsonb_array_elements(lines) x);
  v_fx:=platform_fx_realised_line(c.tenant_id,v_result,'Realised exchange result on '||no);
  if v_fx is not null then lines:=lines||v_fx; end if;
 end if;
 je:=post_journal_entry(c.tenant_id,'fin_credit_note',nid,current_date,'Cancellation credit '||c.cancellation_no,lines);
 update fin_credit_notes set journal_entry_id=je.id where id=nid;
 insert into ins_cancellation_journals values(c.tenant_id,c.id,je.id,'original_item_credit');
 return nid;
end $function$
-- FN platform_ins_cancellation_mappings(uuid,uuid)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.platform_ins_cancellation_mappings(p_policy_id uuid, p_clearing uuid)
 RETURNS jsonb
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
declare p ins_policies%rowtype; sn ins_policy_financial_snapshots%rowtype; ar uuid; pay uuid; ue uuid; income uuid; vat uuid; ap uuid;
 total numeric; payable numeric; treatment text; ch record;
begin
 perform ins_policy_cash_require_finance();
 select * into p from ins_policies where id=p_policy_id and tenant_id=get_my_tenant_id();
 if not found then raise exception 'INS_NOT_FOUND: policy'; end if;
 if not exists(select 1 from gl_accounts g where g.id=p_clearing and g.tenant_id=p.tenant_id and g.is_active and g.account_type='asset' and not g.is_control_account
   and not exists(select 1 from gl_posting_rules r where r.tenant_id=p.tenant_id and r.gl_account_id=g.id)
   and not exists(select 1 from fin_bank_accounts b where b.tenant_id=p.tenant_id and b.gl_account_id=g.id)) then
  raise exception 'INS_CANCELLATION_MAPPING: choose an active, non-bank, unmapped non-control asset clearing account'; end if;
 select * into sn from ins_policy_financial_snapshots where policy_id=p.id;
 total:=coalesce(sn.invoice_total,p.gross_premium);
 payable:=case when sn.extended then (select sum(payable_amount) from ins_policy_insurer_shares where policy_id=p.id) else p.net_premium_to_insurer end;
 ar:=get_posting_account(p.tenant_id,'ar_control'); pay:=get_posting_account(p.tenant_id,'insurer_payable');
 ue:=get_posting_account(p.tenant_id,'unearned_commission'); income:=get_posting_account(p.tenant_id,'commission_income');
 vat:=get_posting_account(p.tenant_id,'vat_output'); ap:=get_posting_account(p.tenant_id,'ap_control');
 if not exists(select 1 from journal_entries where id=p.journal_entry_id and tenant_id=p.tenant_id and source_type='ins_policy_bind' and status='posted')
  or coalesce((select sum(debit-credit) from journal_entry_lines where journal_entry_id=p.journal_entry_id and gl_account_id=ar),0)<>total
  or coalesce((select sum(credit-debit) from journal_entry_lines where journal_entry_id=p.journal_entry_id and gl_account_id=pay),0)<>payable then
  raise exception 'INS_CANCELLATION_MAPPING: original receivable/payable accounts or journal no longer reconcile'; end if;
 treatment:='none';
 if p.commission_amount>0 then
  if coalesce((select sum(credit-debit) from journal_entry_lines where journal_entry_id=p.journal_entry_id and gl_account_id=ue),0)=p.commission_amount then treatment:='deferred';
  elsif coalesce((select sum(credit-debit) from journal_entry_lines where journal_entry_id=p.journal_entry_id and gl_account_id=income),0)=p.commission_amount then treatment:='legacy';
  else raise exception 'INS_CANCELLATION_MAPPING: original commission treatment is unclear'; end if;
 end if;
 if not exists(select 1 from gl_accounts where id=ap and tenant_id=p.tenant_id and is_active and account_type='liability')
  or exists(select 1 from gl_accounts where id=any(array[ar,pay,ue,income]) and not is_active) then
  raise exception 'INS_CANCELLATION_MAPPING: active AP refund liability and original posting mappings required'; end if;
 if coalesce(sn.vat_total,0)>0 and (not exists(select 1 from gl_accounts where id=vat and tenant_id=p.tenant_id and is_active and account_type='liability')
  or coalesce((select sum(credit-debit) from journal_entry_lines where journal_entry_id=p.journal_entry_id and gl_account_id=vat),0)<>sn.vat_total) then
  raise exception 'INS_CANCELLATION_MAPPING: original VAT liability mapping changed'; end if;
 for ch in select * from ins_policy_charges where policy_id=p.id and kind='broker_fee' and amount>0 loop
  if not exists(select 1 from gl_accounts where id=ch.income_gl_account_id and tenant_id=p.tenant_id and is_active and account_type='revenue') then
   raise exception 'INS_CANCELLATION_MAPPING: original fee income account inactive'; end if;
 end loop;
 return jsonb_build_object('ar',ar,'payable',pay,'unearned',ue,'income',income,'vat',vat,'refund_payable',ap,'clearing',p_clearing,'commission_treatment',treatment);
end $function$
-- FN platform_ins_cancellation_money(text)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.platform_ins_cancellation_money(p_value text)
 RETURNS numeric
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 IMMUTABLE
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
begin
 if p_value is null or p_value !~ '^\d{1,16}(\.\d{1,2})?$' then
  raise exception 'INS_CANCELLATION_INPUT: non-negative decimal money with at most two decimals required';
 end if;
 return p_value::numeric;
end $function$
-- FN platform_ins_cancellation_percentage(text,boolean)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.platform_ins_cancellation_percentage(p_value text, p_allow_100 boolean DEFAULT true)
 RETURNS numeric
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 IMMUTABLE
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
declare n numeric;
begin
 if p_value is null or p_value !~ '^\d{1,3}(\.\d{1,4})?$' then raise exception 'INS_CANCELLATION_INPUT: percentage requires at most four decimals'; end if;
 n:=p_value::numeric;
 if n>100 or (not p_allow_100 and n=100) then raise exception 'INS_CANCELLATION_INPUT: percentage out of range'; end if;
 return n;
end $function$
-- FN platform_ins_cancellation_review_position(uuid)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.platform_ins_cancellation_review_position(p_policy_id uuid)
 RETURNS jsonb
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
begin
 perform ins_policy_cash_require_finance();
 if not exists(select 1 from ins_policies where id=p_policy_id and tenant_id=get_my_tenant_id()) then raise exception 'INS_NOT_FOUND: policy'; end if;
 if exists(select 1 from fin_settlements s join fin_allocations a on a.settlement_id=s.id join ins_policy_cash_items k on k.id=a.open_item_id
  left join journal_entries j on j.id=s.journal_entry_id where k.source_id=p_policy_id and s.status='posted' and (j.id is null or j.status<>'posted'))
 or exists(select 1 from ins_commission_events e join ins_commission_entries ce on ce.id=e.entry_id left join journal_entries j on j.id=e.journal_entry_id
  where ce.policy_id=p_policy_id and e.event in('recognised','transferred') and e.journal_entry_id is not null and j.status<>'posted') then
  raise exception 'INS_CANCELLATION_HISTORY: cash or commission journal was independently voided; Finance review required'; end if;
 return jsonb_build_object('cash_position',(select jsonb_object_agg(key,value#>>'{}') from jsonb_each(ins_policy_cash_totals(p_policy_id))),
  'insurer_balances',coalesce((select jsonb_agg(jsonb_build_object('item_id',b.id,'insurer_share_id',k.insurer_share_id,'insurer_id',coalesce((select sh.insurer_id from ins_policy_insurer_shares sh where sh.id=k.insurer_share_id),(select pp.insurer_id from ins_policies pp where pp.id=p_policy_id)),'outstanding',b.outstanding_amount::text) order by b.id)
    from fin_open_item_balances b join ins_policy_cash_items k on k.id=b.id where k.source_id=p_policy_id and k.source_type='ins_policy_insurer'),'[]'::jsonb),
  'commission_entries',coalesce((select jsonb_agg(to_jsonb(e) order by e.id) from ins_commission_entries e where e.policy_id=p_policy_id),'[]'::jsonb));
end $function$
-- FN platform_ins_cancellation_round_ratio(numeric,numeric)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.platform_ins_cancellation_round_ratio(p_numerator numeric, p_denominator numeric)
 RETURNS numeric
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 IMMUTABLE
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
begin
 if p_numerator is null or p_denominator is null or p_numerator<0 or p_denominator<=0 then
  raise exception 'INS_CANCELLATION_INPUT: invalid non-negative ratio'; end if;
 return div(p_numerator*200+p_denominator,p_denominator*2)*.01;
end $function$
-- FN platform_ins_cash_position_before_cancellation(uuid)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.platform_ins_cash_position_before_cancellation(p_policy_id uuid)
 RETURNS jsonb
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
declare p ins_policies%rowtype; requests jsonb; result jsonb;
begin
  if auth.uid() is null or not (can_access_insurance() or is_finance_team_member('finance')) then
    raise exception 'INS_CASH_FORBIDDEN: Insurance or Finance access required' using errcode='42501';
  end if;
  select * into p from ins_policies where id=p_policy_id and tenant_id=get_my_tenant_id();
  if not found then raise exception 'INS_NOT_FOUND: policy'; end if;
  result := ins_policy_cash_totals(p.id);
  select coalesce(jsonb_agg(jsonb_build_object(
    'id',q.id,'kind',q.kind,'amount',q.amount,'cash_date',q.cash_date,'reference',q.reference,'notes',q.notes,
    'status',case when s.status='void' then 'void' else q.status end,
    'maker_id',q.maker_id,'checker_id',q.checker_id,'created_at',q.created_at,'checked_at',q.checked_at,
    'decision_reason',q.decision_reason,'bank_account_id',q.bank_account_id,'bank_name',b.name,
    'insurer_share_id',q.insurer_share_id,'insurer_name',(select i.name from ins_policy_insurer_shares sh join ins_insurers i on i.id=sh.insurer_id where sh.id=q.insurer_share_id),'settlement_id',q.settlement_id,'settlement_no',s.settlement_no,
    'reconciled',r.id is not null,'reconciled_by',r.matched_by,'reconciled_at',r.matched_at
  ) order by q.created_at desc,q.id), '[]'::jsonb) into requests
  from ins_policy_cash_requests q join fin_bank_accounts b on b.id=q.bank_account_id and b.tenant_id=q.tenant_id
