  left join fin_settlements s on s.id=q.settlement_id and s.tenant_id=q.tenant_id
  left join bank_reconciliations r on r.settlement_id=s.id and r.tenant_id=q.tenant_id
  where q.policy_id=p.id and q.tenant_id=p.tenant_id;
  return result || jsonb_build_object('policy_id',p.id,'policy_status',p.status,'currency',p.currency,'requests',requests,
    'commission_amount',p.commission_amount,
    'commission_status',(select case when count(*)=1 then min(e.status) when count(*)>1 then 'per insurer share' else null end from ins_commission_entries e where e.policy_id=p.id and e.tenant_id=p.tenant_id),
    'can_prepare',is_finance_team_member('finance') and effective_user_id()=auth.uid(), 'actor_id',auth.uid());
end $function$
-- FN platform_ins_create_renewal_legacy_impl(uuid)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.platform_ins_create_renewal_legacy_impl(p_policy_id uuid)
 RETURNS ins_policies
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
declare
  v_tenant uuid := get_my_tenant_id();
  v_old    ins_policies%rowtype;
  v_row    ins_policies%rowtype;
  v_term   integer;
begin
  if not can_access_insurance() then
    raise exception 'INS_FORBIDDEN: insurance access required' using errcode = '42501';
  end if;
  select * into v_old from ins_policies where id = p_policy_id and tenant_id = v_tenant for update;
  if not found then
    raise exception 'INS_NOT_FOUND: policy';
  end if;
  if v_old.status <> 'active' then
    raise exception 'INS_RENEWAL_STATE: only an active policy can be renewed (this one is %)', v_old.status;
  end if;
  if exists (select 1 from ins_policies where renewal_of_id = v_old.id and status = 'draft') then
    raise exception 'INS_RENEWAL_EXISTS: a renewal draft already exists for %', v_old.policy_no;
  end if;
  v_term := v_old.expiry_date - v_old.inception_date;
  perform set_config('ins.via_rpc', 'on', true);
  insert into ins_policies (
    tenant_id, policy_no, client_id, insurer_id, product_line_id, status,
    inception_date, expiry_date, sum_insured, currency, gross_premium,
    commission_rate_pct, risk_description, renewal_of_id, branch_id, agent_id, transaction_type
  ) values (
    v_tenant, next_doc_number(v_tenant, 'INS_POLICY', 'POL', 5), v_old.client_id, v_old.insurer_id, v_old.product_line_id, 'draft',
    v_old.expiry_date, v_old.expiry_date + v_term, v_old.sum_insured, v_old.currency, v_old.gross_premium,
    v_old.commission_rate_pct, v_old.risk_description, v_old.id, v_old.branch_id, v_old.agent_id, 'renewal'
  )
  returning * into v_row;
  return v_row;
end;
$function$
-- FN platform_ins_create_renewal_pre_outcome_impl(uuid)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.platform_ins_create_renewal_pre_outcome_impl(p_policy_id uuid)
 RETURNS ins_policies
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
declare p ins_policies%rowtype;
begin
  if exists (
    select 1 from ins_policy_endorsements
     where policy_id = p_policy_id and status in ('proposed','prepared','posting')
  ) then
    raise exception 'INS_ENDORSEMENT_LIVE: finish or withdraw the live endorsement before renewing this policy';
  end if;
  p := platform_ins_create_renewal_legacy_impl(p_policy_id);
  insert into ins_policy_insurer_shares(tenant_id,policy_id,insurer_id,is_lead,share_pct,commission_rate_pct,insurer_policy_no,reviewed)
  select tenant_id,p.id,insurer_id,is_lead,share_pct,commission_rate_pct,insurer_policy_no,false from ins_policy_insurer_shares where policy_id=p_policy_id;
  insert into ins_policy_charges(tenant_id,policy_id,kind,description,amount,recipient_mode,recipient_insurer_id,vat_mode,vat_rate_pct,income_gl_account_id,reviewed)
  select tenant_id,p.id,kind,description,null,recipient_mode,recipient_insurer_id,vat_mode,vat_rate_pct,income_gl_account_id,false from ins_policy_charges where policy_id=p_policy_id;
  return p;
end $function$
-- FN platform_ins_cxl_before_match_settlement(uuid,uuid)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.platform_ins_cxl_before_match_settlement(p_statement_line_id uuid, p_settlement_id uuid)
 RETURNS bank_reconciliations
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
declare
  v_tenant_id uuid := get_my_tenant_id();
  v_line bank_statement_lines%rowtype;
  v_set fin_settlements%rowtype;
  v_bank_name text;
  v_signed numeric;
  v_row bank_reconciliations%rowtype;
begin
  if not is_finance_team_member('finance') then
    raise exception 'not authorized to reconcile bank transactions';
  end if;
  select * into v_line from bank_statement_lines
   where id = p_statement_line_id and tenant_id = v_tenant_id;
  if not found then
    raise exception 'statement line not found';
  end if;
  -- Lock the settlement so a concurrent void cannot slip past the reconciled check.
  select * into v_set from fin_settlements
   where id = p_settlement_id and tenant_id = v_tenant_id
   for update;
  if not found then
    raise exception 'settlement not found';
  end if;
  if v_set.status <> 'posted' then
    raise exception 'only posted settlements can be reconciled (% is %)', v_set.settlement_no, v_set.status;
  end if;
  select b.name into v_bank_name from fin_bank_accounts b
   where b.id = v_set.bank_account_id and b.tenant_id = v_tenant_id;
  if v_bank_name is distinct from v_line.bank_account then
    raise exception 'statement line and settlement are on different bank accounts (% vs %)',
      v_line.bank_account, v_bank_name;
  end if;
  if v_set.currency is distinct from v_line.currency then
    raise exception 'statement line and settlement are in different currencies (% vs %)',
      v_line.currency, v_set.currency;
  end if;
  v_signed := case when v_set.direction = 'in' then v_set.amount else -v_set.amount end;
  begin
    insert into bank_reconciliations (
      tenant_id, bank_statement_line_id, settlement_id, match_type, variance, matched_by
    ) values (
      v_tenant_id, p_statement_line_id, p_settlement_id, 'manual', v_line.amount - v_signed, auth.uid()
    )
    returning * into v_row;
  exception when unique_violation then
    raise exception 'statement line or settlement is already reconciled';
  end;
  return v_row;
end;
$function$
-- FN platform_ins_cxl_before_unmatch(uuid)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.platform_ins_cxl_before_unmatch(p_reconciliation_id uuid)
 RETURNS void
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
begin
  if not is_finance_team_member('finance') then
    raise exception 'not authorized to undo a bank reconciliation match';
  end if;
  delete from bank_reconciliations
  where id = p_reconciliation_id and tenant_id = get_my_tenant_id();
  if not found then
    raise exception 'reconciliation match not found';
  end if;
end;
$function$
-- FN platform_ins_endorsement_money(text)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.platform_ins_endorsement_money(p_value text)
 RETURNS numeric
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 IMMUTABLE
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
begin
  if p_value is null or p_value !~ '^[-+]?\d{1,16}(\.\d{1,2})?$' then
    raise exception 'INS_ENDORSEMENT_INPUT: signed decimal money with at most two decimals required';
  end if;
  return p_value::numeric;
end $function$
-- FN platform_ins_import_allocate(numeric,jsonb)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.platform_ins_import_allocate(p_total numeric, p_shares jsonb)
 RETURNS TABLE(insurer_id uuid, amount numeric)
 LANGUAGE sql
 IMMUTABLE
 SET search_path TO 'pg_catalog', 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
  with s as (
    select (e ->> 'insurer_id')::uuid as insurer_id, (e ->> 'share_pct')::numeric as pct
      from jsonb_array_elements(p_shares) e
  ), a as (
    select insurer_id,
           trunc(p_total * pct / 100, 2) as base,
           p_total * pct / 100 - trunc(p_total * pct / 100, 2) as fraction
      from s
  ), r as (
    select *, row_number() over (order by fraction desc, insurer_id) as rk,
           round((p_total - sum(base) over ()) * 100) as cents
      from a
  )
  select insurer_id, base + case when rk <= cents then 0.01 else 0 end from r;
$function$
-- FN platform_ins_import_date(text)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.platform_ins_import_date(p_text text)
 RETURNS date
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 IMMUTABLE
 SET search_path TO 'pg_catalog', 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
begin
  if p_text is null or btrim(p_text) !~ '^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}' then return null; end if;
  return left(btrim(p_text), 10)::date;
exception when others then
  return null;
end $function$
-- FN platform_ins_import_err(text,text)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.platform_ins_import_err(p_code text, p_message text)
 RETURNS jsonb
 LANGUAGE sql
 IMMUTABLE
 SET search_path TO 'pg_catalog', 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
  select jsonb_build_object('code', p_code, 'message', p_message);
$function$
-- FN platform_ins_import_num(text)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.platform_ins_import_num(p_text text)
 RETURNS numeric
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 IMMUTABLE
 SET search_path TO 'pg_catalog', 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
begin
  if p_text is null or btrim(p_text) = '' then return null; end if;
  return replace(btrim(p_text), ',', '')::numeric;
exception when others then
  return null;
end $function$
-- FN platform_ins_line_needs_certificate(uuid,uuid)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.platform_ins_line_needs_certificate(p_tenant_id uuid, p_line_id uuid)
 RETURNS boolean
 LANGUAGE sql
 STABLE SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'pg_catalog', 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
  select coalesce((select l.requires_certificate or l.code = 'MOTOR'
                     from ins_product_lines l
                    where l.id = p_line_id and l.tenant_id = p_tenant_id), false);
$function$
-- FN platform_ins_policy_cash_legacy_totals(uuid)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.platform_ins_policy_cash_legacy_totals(p_policy_id uuid)
 RETURNS jsonb
 LANGUAGE sql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
  with money as (
    select coalesce(sum(a.amount) filter (where i.source_type='ins_policy_premium' and s.direction='in'),0) as collected,
           coalesce(sum(a.amount) filter (where i.source_type='ins_policy_insurer' and s.direction='out'),0) as remitted
    from fin_open_items i join fin_allocations a on a.open_item_id=i.id and a.tenant_id=i.tenant_id
    join fin_settlements s on s.id=a.settlement_id and s.tenant_id=a.tenant_id and s.status='posted'
    where i.source_id=p_policy_id and i.source_type in ('ins_policy_premium','ins_policy_insurer')
  ), transferred as (
    select coalesce(sum(e.amount),0) as amount from ins_commission_entries e
    join ins_commission_transfers t on t.id=e.transfer_id and t.tenant_id=e.tenant_id and t.status='posted'
    where e.policy_id=p_policy_id and e.status='transferred'
  ), outstanding as (
    select coalesce(sum(b.outstanding_amount) filter (where b.source_type='ins_policy_premium'),0) as client_due,
           coalesce(sum(b.outstanding_amount) filter (where b.source_type='ins_policy_insurer'),0) as insurer_due
    from fin_open_item_balances b where b.source_id=p_policy_id
      and b.source_type in ('ins_policy_premium','ins_policy_insurer')
  )
  select jsonb_build_object('collected',m.collected,'remitted',m.remitted,'commission_transferred',t.amount,
    'available_allocated_funds',greatest(m.collected-m.remitted-t.amount,0),
    'client_outstanding',greatest(o.client_due,0),'insurer_outstanding',greatest(o.insurer_due,0),
    'maximum_remittance',least(greatest(m.collected-m.remitted-t.amount,0),greatest(o.insurer_due,0)))
  from money m cross join transferred t cross join outstanding o;
$function$
-- FN platform_ins_policy_cash_legacy_validate(uuid,text,uuid,numeric,date)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.platform_ins_policy_cash_legacy_validate(p_policy_id uuid, p_kind text, p_bank_id uuid, p_amount numeric, p_date date)
 RETURNS fin_open_items
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
declare p ins_policies%rowtype; b fin_bank_accounts%rowtype; i fin_open_items%rowtype; v jsonb; cap numeric;
begin
  perform ins_policy_cash_require_finance();
  if p_kind is null or p_kind not in ('receipt','remittance') or p_amount is null or p_amount<=0
     or p_amount<>round(p_amount,2) or p_amount>9999999999999999.99 then
    raise exception 'INS_CASH_INPUT: choose receipt/remittance and a positive amount with at most two decimals';
  end if;
  if p_date is null or p_date>current_date then raise exception 'INS_CASH_DATE: cash date must not be in the future'; end if;
  select * into p from ins_policies where id=p_policy_id and tenant_id=get_my_tenant_id() for update;
  if not found then raise exception 'INS_NOT_FOUND: policy'; end if;
  if p.status not in ('active','renewed') then raise exception 'INS_CASH_STATE: only bound, non-reversed policies accept cash'; end if;
  -- Same policy/item lock order as reversal. Serializes receipts, remittances,
  -- voids and commission withdrawals for this policy across different banks.
  perform id from fin_open_items where tenant_id=p.tenant_id and source_id=p.id
    and source_type in ('ins_policy_premium','ins_policy_insurer') order by id for update;
  select * into b from fin_bank_accounts where id=p_bank_id and tenant_id=p.tenant_id for share;
  if not found or b.kind<>'client_money' or not b.is_active or b.gl_account_id is null or b.currency<>p.currency then
    raise exception 'INS_CASH_BANK: an active mapped client-money bank in the policy currency is required';
  end if;
  select * into i from fin_open_items where tenant_id=p.tenant_id and source_id=p.id
    and source_type=case p_kind when 'receipt' then 'ins_policy_premium' else 'ins_policy_insurer' end;
  if not found then raise exception 'INS_CASH_ITEM: policy open item missing'; end if;
  v := ins_policy_cash_totals(p.id);
  cap := case p_kind when 'receipt' then (v->>'client_outstanding')::numeric else (v->>'maximum_remittance')::numeric end;
  if p_amount>cap then
    raise exception 'INS_CASH_CAP: requested % exceeds available %; remittances are capped by allocated collections and outstanding insurer liability',p_amount,cap;
  end if;
  return i;
end $function$
-- FN platform_ins_policy_reversal_legacy_context(uuid,boolean)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.platform_ins_policy_reversal_legacy_context(p_policy_id uuid, p_in_progress boolean DEFAULT false)
 RETURNS jsonb
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
declare
  p ins_policies%rowtype; r ins_policy_reversals%rowtype;
  a fin_open_items%rowtype; b fin_open_items%rowtype;
  ar uuid; payable uuid; offset_id uuid; treatment text := 'none';
  je journal_entries%rowtype; n integer; ce ins_commission_entries%rowtype;
begin
  if not can_manage_insurance() or effective_user_id() is distinct from auth.uid() then
    raise exception 'INS_FORBIDDEN: insurance manager required; user-level impersonation cannot reverse policies' using errcode='42501';
  end if;
  select * into p from ins_policies where id=p_policy_id and tenant_id=get_my_tenant_id() for update;
  if not found then raise exception 'INS_NOT_FOUND: policy'; end if;
  if p.status <> 'active' then raise exception 'INS_REVERSAL_STATE: only active policies can be reversed'; end if;
  if p.renewal_of_id is not null or exists(select 1 from ins_policies where renewal_of_id=p.id) then
    raise exception 'INS_REVERSAL_RENEWAL: renewal chains require finance review';
  end if;
  if exists(select 1 from ins_claims where policy_id=p.id) then
    raise exception 'INS_REVERSAL_CLAIM: this policy has a claim; use the future cancellation/refunds workflow';
  end if;
  if exists(select 1 from accounting_periods where tenant_id=p.tenant_id and status='closed'
            and current_date between period_start and period_end) then
    raise exception 'INS_REVERSAL_PERIOD: the current accounting period is closed';
  end if;
  -- Same deterministic item order as the shared settlement/credit machinery.
  perform id from fin_open_items where tenant_id=p.tenant_id and source_id=p.id
    and source_type in ('ins_policy_premium','ins_policy_insurer') order by id for update;
  select * into a from fin_open_items where tenant_id=p.tenant_id and source_id=p.id and source_type='ins_policy_premium';
  select * into b from fin_open_items where tenant_id=p.tenant_id and source_id=p.id and source_type='ins_policy_insurer';
  select count(*) into n from fin_open_items where tenant_id=p.tenant_id and source_id=p.id
    and source_type in ('ins_policy_premium','ins_policy_insurer');
  if n<>2 or a.control_role<>'ar_control' or b.control_role<>'insurer_payable'
     or a.amount is distinct from p.gross_premium or b.amount is distinct from p.net_premium_to_insurer
     or a.currency is distinct from p.currency or b.currency is distinct from p.currency then
    raise exception 'INS_REVERSAL_HISTORY: original open items do not reconcile; finance review required';
  end if;
  select * into r from ins_policy_reversals where original_policy_id=p.id;
  if p_in_progress and (r.id is null or r.outcome<>'building' or r.operation_txid<>txid_current()
     or r.reversed_by is distinct from auth.uid()) then
    raise exception 'INS_REVERSAL_INTERNAL: no authorized reversal transaction';
  end if;
  if exists(select 1 from fin_allocations x join fin_settlements s on s.id=x.settlement_id
       where x.open_item_id in (a.id,b.id) and s.status='posted')
     or exists(select 1 from fin_credit_applications x join fin_credit_notes c on c.id=x.credit_note_id
       where x.open_item_id in (a.id,b.id) and c.status='posted'
       and (not p_in_progress or c.id is distinct from r.premium_credit_note_id
            and c.id is distinct from r.insurer_credit_note_id)) then
    raise exception 'INS_REVERSAL_MOVEMENT: effective payment or credit remains; use cancellation/refunds';
  end if;
  select * into ce from ins_commission_entries where policy_id=p.id for update;
  if ce.id is not null and (ce.status<>'deferred' or ce.earned_on is not null or ce.transfer_id is not null) then
    raise exception 'INS_REVERSAL_COMMISSION: effective recognition or commission transfer remains';
  end if;
  select * into je from journal_entries where id=p.journal_entry_id and tenant_id=p.tenant_id for update;
  if not found or je.status<>'posted' or je.source_type<>'ins_policy_bind' or je.source_id is distinct from p.id then
    raise exception 'INS_REVERSAL_HISTORY: original bind journal is missing or void; finance review required';
  end if;
  ar:=get_posting_account(p.tenant_id,'ar_control');
  payable:=get_posting_account(p.tenant_id,'insurer_payable');
  select count(*) into n from journal_entry_lines where journal_entry_id=je.id;
  if n <> (case when p.commission_amount>0 then 3 else 2 end)
    or not exists(select 1 from journal_entry_lines where journal_entry_id=je.id and gl_account_id=ar and debit=p.gross_premium and credit=0)
    or not exists(select 1 from journal_entry_lines where journal_entry_id=je.id and gl_account_id=payable and credit=p.net_premium_to_insurer and debit=0) then
    raise exception 'INS_REVERSAL_HISTORY: bind amounts or historical control mappings do not reconcile';
  end if;
  if p.commission_amount>0 then
    select gl_account_id into offset_id from journal_entry_lines where journal_entry_id=je.id
      and credit=p.commission_amount and debit=0 and gl_account_id not in (ar,payable);
    if offset_id=get_posting_account(p.tenant_id,'unearned_commission') then treatment:='deferred';
    elsif offset_id=get_posting_account(p.tenant_id,'commission_income') then treatment:='legacy';
    else raise exception 'INS_REVERSAL_HISTORY: historical commission mapping cannot be identified'; end if;
    if treatment='legacy' and ce.id is not null then
      raise exception 'INS_REVERSAL_HISTORY: legacy journal has an inconsistent deferred commission record';
    end if;
    if treatment='deferred' and (ce.id is null or ce.amount<>p.commission_amount) then
      raise exception 'INS_REVERSAL_HISTORY: deferred commission record does not reconcile';
    end if;
  else
    -- Zero commission: equal opposite gross offset legs cancel. Explicitly use
    -- the mapped commission-income account; missing mapping needs finance review.
    offset_id:=get_posting_account(p.tenant_id,'commission_income');
  end if;
  if offset_id is null or offset_id in (ar,payable) then
    raise exception 'INS_REVERSAL_HISTORY: a distinct commission offset mapping is required';
  end if;
  return jsonb_build_object('premium_item_id',a.id,'insurer_item_id',b.id,
    'client_party_id',a.party_account_id,'insurer_party_id',b.party_account_id,
    'offset_id',offset_id,'gross',p.gross_premium,'net',p.net_premium_to_insurer,'treatment',treatment);
end $function$
-- FN platform_ins_transfer_before_cancellation_impl(uuid,uuid,date,uuid[],text)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.platform_ins_transfer_before_cancellation_impl(p_from_bank_account_id uuid, p_to_bank_account_id uuid, p_transfer_date date, p_entry_ids uuid[] DEFAULT NULL::uuid[], p_notes text DEFAULT NULL::text)
 RETURNS ins_commission_transfers
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
declare
  v_tenant   uuid := get_my_tenant_id();
  v_from     fin_bank_accounts%rowtype;
  v_to       fin_bank_accounts%rowtype;
  v_ids      uuid[];
  v_total    numeric(18,2);
  v_max_date date;
  v_trust    numeric;
  v_id       uuid := gen_random_uuid();
  v_no       text;
  v_je       journal_entries;
  v_tr       ins_commission_transfers%rowtype;
  v_desc     text;
  v_ccy      text;
  v_foreign  boolean;
  v_grp      record;
  v_rate     numeric;
  v_nr       numeric;
  v_lines    jsonb;
  v_booked   numeric := 0;
  v_result   numeric;
  v_fx_line  jsonb;
  v_unearned_gl uuid;
  v_other       text;
begin
  if not can_manage_insurance() then
    raise exception 'INS_FORBIDDEN: only insurance admins and managers can transfer commission' using errcode = '42501';
  end if;
  if v_tenant is null then
    raise exception 'INS_FORBIDDEN: no company context' using errcode = '42501';
  end if;
  if p_transfer_date is null or p_transfer_date > current_date + 1 then
    raise exception 'INS_TRANSFER_INPUT: the transfer date is required and cannot be in the future';
  end if;
  if p_from_bank_account_id is null or p_to_bank_account_id is null
     or p_from_bank_account_id = p_to_bank_account_id then
    raise exception 'INS_TRANSFER_INPUT: choose a client-money bank account and a different operating bank account';
  end if;
  select * into v_from from fin_bank_accounts where id = p_from_bank_account_id and tenant_id = v_tenant for share;
  select * into v_to   from fin_bank_accounts where id = p_to_bank_account_id   and tenant_id = v_tenant for share;
  if v_from.id is null or v_to.id is null then
    raise exception 'INS_TRANSFER_BANK: bank account not found in this company';
  end if;
  if v_from.kind <> 'client_money' or v_to.kind <> 'operating' then
    raise exception 'INS_TRANSFER_BANK: commission moves from a client-money account to an operating account';
  end if;
  if not v_from.is_active or not v_to.is_active then
    raise exception 'INS_TRANSFER_BANK: both bank accounts must be active';
  end if;
  if v_from.gl_account_id is null or v_to.gl_account_id is null or v_from.gl_account_id = v_to.gl_account_id then
    raise exception 'INS_TRANSFER_BANK: each bank account needs its own mapped GL account';
  end if;
  -- A transfer moves money between accounts that hold the same currency; converting is a
  -- bank payment and a realised result, not a transfer.
  if v_from.currency <> v_to.currency then
    raise exception 'INS_TRANSFER_CURRENCY: a transfer moves money in one currency (% vs %); convert through a bank payment instead',
      v_from.currency, v_to.currency;
  end if;
  v_ccy     := v_from.currency;
  v_foreign := v_ccy <> fin_ledger_currency(v_tenant);
  select array_agg(e.id order by e.id), sum(e.amount), max(e.earned_on)
    into v_ids, v_total, v_max_date
    from ins_commission_entries e
   where e.tenant_id = v_tenant and e.status = 'earned'
     and not exists(select 1 from ins_policy_cancellations c where c.policy_id=e.policy_id and c.status='posted')
     and (p_entry_ids is null or e.id = any (p_entry_ids));
  if v_ids is null then
    raise exception 'INS_TRANSFER_NOTHING: there is no earned commission to transfer';
  end if;
  if p_entry_ids is not null and cardinality(v_ids) <> cardinality(
       (select array_agg(distinct x) from unnest(p_entry_ids) x)) then
    raise exception 'INS_TRANSFER_ENTRIES: every selected entry must be earned and not yet transferred';
  end if;
  -- The trust account holds the commission in the policies' currency; transferring it
  -- through accounts of another currency would not net it.
  select min(p.currency) into v_other
    from ins_commission_entries e join ins_policies p on p.id = e.policy_id
   where e.tenant_id = v_tenant and e.id = any (v_ids) and p.currency is distinct from v_ccy;
  if v_other is not null then
    raise exception 'INS_TRANSFER_CURRENCY: selected commission was earned on % policies; transfer it through % accounts of its own', v_other, v_ccy;
  end if;
  perform 1 from ins_commission_entries where id = any (v_ids) order by id for update;
  if p_transfer_date < v_max_date then
    raise exception 'INS_TRANSFER_DATE: the transfer date cannot be before commission was earned (%)', v_max_date;
  end if;
  -- Client money actually held, in the accounts' currency.
  select coalesce(sum(l.debit - l.credit), 0) into v_trust
    from journal_entry_lines l
   where l.tenant_id = v_tenant and l.gl_account_id = v_from.gl_account_id and l.currency = v_ccy;
  if v_trust < v_total then
    raise exception 'INS_TRANSFER_TRUST_SHORT: client-money account % holds % in the ledger, less than the % to transfer',
      v_from.name, v_trust, v_total;
  end if;
  v_no   := next_doc_number(v_tenant, 'ins_commission_transfer', 'CTR');
  v_desc := 'Commission transfer ' || v_no;
  if not v_foreign then
    v_je := post_journal_entry(v_tenant, 'ins_commission_transfer', v_id, p_transfer_date, v_desc,
      jsonb_build_array(
        jsonb_build_object('gl_account_id', v_to.gl_account_id,   'debit',  v_total, 'description', v_desc),
        jsonb_build_object('gl_account_id', v_from.gl_account_id, 'credit', v_total, 'description', v_desc)));
  else
    -- The trust side is credited at the rate each policy was bound at (naming the bind
    -- entry as the rate source), the operating side debited at the transfer-date rate,
    -- and the difference is the realised result. The trust nets to zero in the currency
    -- and in base.
    v_unearned_gl := get_posting_account(v_tenant, 'unearned_commission');
    v_nr     := round((platform_fx_rate(v_tenant, v_ccy, p_transfer_date)).rate, 8);
    v_lines  := jsonb_build_array();
    for v_grp in
      select p.journal_entry_id as bind_entry, sum(e.amount) as amt
        from ins_commission_entries e join ins_policies p on p.id = e.policy_id
       where e.tenant_id = v_tenant and e.id = any (v_ids)
       group by p.journal_entry_id
       order by p.journal_entry_id
    loop
      select l.fx_rate into v_rate
        from journal_entry_lines l
       where l.journal_entry_id = v_grp.bind_entry and l.tenant_id = v_tenant and l.currency = v_ccy
         and (v_unearned_gl is null or l.gl_account_id = v_unearned_gl)
       order by l.id
       limit 1;
      if v_rate is null then
        select l.fx_rate into v_rate
          from journal_entry_lines l
         where l.journal_entry_id = v_grp.bind_entry and l.tenant_id = v_tenant and l.currency = v_ccy
         order by l.id
         limit 1;
      end if;
      if v_rate is null then
        raise exception 'INS_TRANSFER_HISTORY: the bind journal of entry % has no % line, so the booked rate is unknown', v_grp.bind_entry, v_ccy;
      end if;
      v_lines := v_lines || jsonb_build_object('gl_account_id', v_from.gl_account_id, 'credit', v_grp.amt,
        'currency', v_ccy, 'rate_from_entry', v_grp.bind_entry, 'fx_rate', v_rate, 'description', v_desc);
      v_booked := v_booked + round(v_grp.amt * v_rate, 2);
    end loop;
    v_lines := v_lines || jsonb_build_object('gl_account_id', v_to.gl_account_id, 'debit', v_total,
      'currency', v_ccy, 'description', v_desc);
    v_result  := round(v_total * v_nr, 2) - v_booked;
    v_fx_line := platform_fx_realised_line(v_tenant, v_result, 'Realised exchange result on ' || v_no);
    if v_fx_line is not null then
      v_lines := v_lines || v_fx_line;
    end if;
    v_je := post_journal_entry(v_tenant, 'ins_commission_transfer', v_id, p_transfer_date, v_desc, v_lines);
  end if;
  insert into ins_commission_transfers (
    id, tenant_id, transfer_no, from_bank_account_id, to_bank_account_id,
    transfer_date, amount, currency, notes, journal_entry_id
  ) values (
    v_id, v_tenant, v_no, v_from.id, v_to.id,
    p_transfer_date, v_total, v_from.currency, nullif(btrim(p_notes), ''), v_je.id
  ) returning * into v_tr;
  update ins_commission_entries
     set status = 'transferred', transfer_id = v_id, updated_at = now()
   where id = any (v_ids);
  insert into ins_commission_events (tenant_id, entry_id, event, amount, journal_entry_id, transfer_id)
  select v_tenant, e.id, 'transferred', e.amount, v_je.id, v_id
    from ins_commission_entries e where e.id = any (v_ids);
  return v_tr;
end;
$function$
-- FN platform_job_runs_prune()
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.platform_job_runs_prune()
 RETURNS integer
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
declare v_n integer;
begin
  delete from platform_job_runs where started_at < now() - interval '90 days';
  get diagnostics v_n = row_count;
  return v_n;
end;
$function$
-- FN platform_module_activity_raw(integer)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.platform_module_activity_raw(p_days integer DEFAULT 30)
 RETURNS TABLE(tenant_id uuid, module text, table_name text, events bigint, events_prev bigint, first_event_at timestamp with time zone, last_event_at timestamp with time zone)
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 STABLE SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
begin
  if not is_platform_admin() then
    raise exception 'PLATFORM_ADMIN_REQUIRED: module activity is restricted to platform admins'
      using errcode = '42501';
  end if;
  return query select * from platform_module_activity_scan(p_days);
end;
$function$
-- FN platform_module_activity_scan(integer)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.platform_module_activity_scan(p_days integer DEFAULT 30)
 RETURNS TABLE(tenant_id uuid, module text, table_name text, events bigint, events_prev bigint, first_event_at timestamp with time zone, last_event_at timestamp with time zone)
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 STABLE SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
declare
  r        record;
  v_days   integer := greatest(1, least(coalesce(p_days, 30), 365));
  v_since  timestamptz := now() - make_interval(days => v_days);
  v_prev   timestamptz := now() - make_interval(days => 2 * v_days);
begin
  for r in
    select s.module, s.table_name
    from platform_module_activity_sources s
    where to_regclass('public.' || quote_ident(s.table_name)) is not null
      and exists (select 1 from pg_attribute a
                  where a.attrelid = to_regclass('public.' || quote_ident(s.table_name))
                    and a.attname = 'tenant_id' and not a.attisdropped)
      and exists (select 1 from pg_attribute a
                  where a.attrelid = to_regclass('public.' || quote_ident(s.table_name))
                    and a.attname = 'created_at' and not a.attisdropped)
    order by s.module, s.table_name
  loop
    return query execute format(
      'select x.tenant_id, %L::text, %L::text,
              count(*) filter (where x.created_at >= $1),
              count(*) filter (where x.created_at >= $2 and x.created_at < $1),
              min(x.created_at), max(x.created_at)
       from public.%I x
       where x.tenant_id is not null
       group by x.tenant_id',
      r.module, r.table_name, r.table_name
    ) using v_since, v_prev;
  end loop;
end;
$function$
-- FN platform_module_activity(integer)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.platform_module_activity(p_days integer DEFAULT 30)
 RETURNS TABLE(tenant_id uuid, module text, events bigint, events_prev bigint, first_event_at timestamp with time zone, last_event_at timestamp with time zone)
 LANGUAGE sql
 STABLE SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
  select r.tenant_id, r.module,
         sum(r.events)::bigint, sum(r.events_prev)::bigint,
         min(r.first_event_at), max(r.last_event_at)
  from platform_module_activity_raw(p_days) r
  group by r.tenant_id, r.module;
$function$
-- FN platform_onboarding_status_all()
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.platform_onboarding_status_all()
 RETURNS TABLE(tenant_id uuid, name text, status text, created_at timestamp with time zone, plan text, subscription_status text, trial_ends_at timestamp with time zone, contact_email text, is_internal boolean, member_count bigint, stage integer, stage_key text, next_step text, stage_reached_at timestamp with time zone, days_in_stage integer, stalled boolean, admin_invited_at timestamp with time zone, admin_joined_at timestamp with time zone, modules_enabled_at timestamp with time zone, team_invited_at timestamp with time zone, first_activity_at timestamp with time zone, last_activity_at timestamp with time zone)
 LANGUAGE sql
 STABLE SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
  with act as (
    select a.tenant_id, min(a.first_event_at) as first_at, max(a.last_event_at) as last_at
    from platform_module_activity_scan(30) a
    group by a.tenant_id
  ),
  base as (
    select
      t.id, t.name, t.status, t.created_at, t.plan, t.subscription_status, t.trial_ends_at, t.contact_email,
      (t.plan = 'internal' or t.id = '00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000099') as is_internal,
      (select count(*) from app_users u where u.tenant_id = t.id) as member_count,
      least(
        (select min(i.created_at) from invitations i where i.tenant_id = t.id and i.role_bundle = 'company_admin'),
        (select min(u.created_at) from app_users u where u.tenant_id = t.id)
      ) as admin_invited_at,
      least(
        (select min(u.created_at) from app_users u where u.tenant_id = t.id and u.is_company_admin),
        (select min(u.created_at) from app_users u join staff_roles sr on sr.user_id = u.id and sr.tenant_id = u.tenant_id
          where u.tenant_id = t.id and sr.role = 'admin')
      ) as admin_joined_at,
      (select min(tm.enabled_at) from tenant_modules tm where tm.tenant_id = t.id) as modules_enabled_at,
      least(
        (select min(i.created_at) from invitations i where i.tenant_id = t.id and i.role_bundle = 'member'),
        (select u.created_at from app_users u where u.tenant_id = t.id order by u.created_at offset 1 limit 1)
      ) as team_invited_at,
      a.first_at as first_activity_at,
      greatest(
        a.last_at,
        (select max(au.last_sign_in_at) from app_users u join auth.users au on au.id = u.id where u.tenant_id = t.id)
      ) as last_activity_at
    from tenants t
    left join act a on a.tenant_id = t.id
  ),
  staged as (
    select b.*,
      case
        when b.admin_invited_at   is null then 0
        when b.admin_joined_at    is null then 1
        when b.modules_enabled_at is null then 2
        when b.team_invited_at    is null then 3
        when b.first_activity_at  is null then 4
        when b.status = 'active' and b.last_activity_at >= now() - interval '30 days' then 6
        else 5
      end as stage
    from base b
  ),
  keyed as (
    select s.*,
      case s.stage
        when 0 then 'created'
        when 1 then 'admin_invited'
        when 2 then 'admin_joined'
        when 3 then 'modules_enabled'
        when 4 then 'team_invited'
        when 5 then 'first_activity'
        else 'live'
      end as stage_key,
      case s.stage
        when 0 then 'Invite the company admin'
        when 1 then 'Waiting for the admin to accept the invitation'
        when 2 then 'Enable the modules they bought'
        when 3 then 'Invite the rest of the team'
        when 4 then 'Waiting for the first real activity'
        when 5 then case
          when s.status = 'pending' then 'Activate the company - it is in use but still marked pending'
          when s.status = 'suspended' then 'Suspended'
          else 'No activity in the last 30 days - check in'
        end
        else null
      end as next_step,
      case s.stage
        when 0 then s.created_at
        when 1 then s.admin_invited_at
        when 2 then s.admin_joined_at
        when 3 then s.modules_enabled_at
        when 4 then s.team_invited_at
        when 5 then coalesce(s.last_activity_at, s.first_activity_at)
        else s.last_activity_at
      end as stage_reached_at
    from staged s
  )
  select
    k.id, k.name, k.status, k.created_at, k.plan, k.subscription_status, k.trial_ends_at, k.contact_email,
    k.is_internal, k.member_count,
    k.stage, k.stage_key, k.next_step, k.stage_reached_at,
    greatest(0, extract(epoch from (now() - k.stage_reached_at)) / 86400)::integer as days_in_stage,
    (k.stage < 5 and k.status <> 'suspended' and not k.is_internal
       and k.stage_reached_at < now() - interval '7 days') as stalled,
    k.admin_invited_at, k.admin_joined_at, k.modules_enabled_at, k.team_invited_at,
    k.first_activity_at, k.last_activity_at
  from keyed k
  order by k.created_at desc;
$function$
-- FN platform_operator_digest_payload(timestamp with time zone,timestamp with time zone)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.platform_operator_digest_payload(p_period_start timestamp with time zone, p_period_end timestamp with time zone)
 RETURNS jsonb
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 STABLE SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
declare
  v_threshold integer := coalesce(
    (select (notifications ->> 'pending_company_threshold_days')::integer from platform_settings where id = true), 2);
  v_payload jsonb;
begin
  with onb as materialized (
    select * from platform_onboarding_status_all() where not is_internal
  )
  select jsonb_build_object(
    'period', jsonb_build_object('from', p_period_start, 'to', p_period_end),
    'settings', jsonb_build_object('pending_company_threshold_days', v_threshold),
    'totals', jsonb_build_object(
      'companies', (select count(*) from onb),
      'active',    (select count(*) from onb where status = 'active'),
      'pending',   (select count(*) from onb where status = 'pending'),
      'suspended', (select count(*) from onb where status = 'suspended'),
      'live',      (select count(*) from onb where stage = 6),
      'users',     (select count(*) from app_users u join onb o on o.tenant_id = u.tenant_id)
    ),
    'new_companies', (
      select coalesce(jsonb_agg(jsonb_build_object('id', tenant_id, 'name', name, 'plan', plan, 'status', status, 'created_at', created_at) order by created_at desc), '[]'::jsonb)
      from onb where created_at >= p_period_start and created_at < p_period_end
    ),
    'new_users', (
      select count(*) from app_users u join onb o on o.tenant_id = u.tenant_id
      where u.created_at >= p_period_start and u.created_at < p_period_end
    ),
    'stalled', (
      select coalesce(jsonb_agg(jsonb_build_object('id', tenant_id, 'name', name, 'stage_key', stage_key, 'next_step', next_step, 'days_in_stage', days_in_stage, 'contact_email', contact_email) order by days_in_stage desc), '[]'::jsonb)
      from (select * from onb where stalled order by days_in_stage desc limit 25) s
    ),
    'quiet', (
      select coalesce(jsonb_agg(jsonb_build_object('id', tenant_id, 'name', name, 'plan', plan,
        'days_quiet', greatest(0, extract(epoch from (now() - coalesce(last_activity_at, created_at))) / 86400)::integer,
        'contact_email', contact_email) order by coalesce(last_activity_at, created_at)), '[]'::jsonb)
      from (select * from onb where status = 'active' and stage = 5 and created_at < now() - interval '30 days'
            order by coalesce(last_activity_at, created_at) limit 25) q
    ),
    'trials_ending', (
      select coalesce(jsonb_agg(jsonb_build_object('id', tenant_id, 'name', name, 'trial_ends_at', trial_ends_at,
        'days_left', greatest(0, ceil(extract(epoch from (trial_ends_at - now())) / 86400))::integer,
        'stage_key', stage_key, 'contact_email', contact_email) order by trial_ends_at), '[]'::jsonb)
      from (select * from onb where subscription_status = 'trialing' and trial_ends_at is not null
              and trial_ends_at <= now() + interval '7 days' and status <> 'suspended'
            order by trial_ends_at limit 25) t
    ),
    'pending_over_threshold', (
      select coalesce(jsonb_agg(jsonb_build_object('id', tenant_id, 'name', name, 'stage_key', stage_key,
        'days_pending', greatest(0, extract(epoch from (now() - created_at)) / 86400)::integer,
        'contact_email', contact_email) order by created_at), '[]'::jsonb)
      from (select * from onb where status = 'pending' and created_at < now() - make_interval(days => v_threshold)
            order by created_at limit 25) p
    ),
    'open_impersonations', (
      select coalesce(jsonb_agg(jsonb_build_object('admin_email', au.email, 'tenant_name', t.name,
        'started_at', s.started_at, 'expires_at', s.expires_at, 'reason', s.reason) order by s.started_at), '[]'::jsonb)
      from impersonation_sessions s
      join tenants t on t.id = s.tenant_id
      left join auth.users au on au.id = s.platform_admin_id
      where s.ended_at is null and (s.expires_at is null or s.expires_at > now())
    ),
    'admins_without_mfa', (
      select coalesce(jsonb_agg(u.email order by u.email), '[]'::jsonb)
      from app_users u
      where u.is_platform_admin
        and not exists (select 1 from auth.mfa_factors f where f.user_id = u.id and f.status = 'verified')
    )
  ) into v_payload;
  -- Items that need a human today: everything except totals/new.
  v_payload := v_payload || jsonb_build_object('attention_count',
    jsonb_array_length(v_payload -> 'stalled')
    + jsonb_array_length(v_payload -> 'quiet')
    + jsonb_array_length(v_payload -> 'trials_ending')
    + jsonb_array_length(v_payload -> 'pending_over_threshold')
    + jsonb_array_length(v_payload -> 'admins_without_mfa'));
  return v_payload;
end;
$function$
-- FN platform_policy_version_immutable()
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.platform_policy_version_immutable()
 RETURNS trigger
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
begin
  if new.accounting_policy_version is distinct from old.accounting_policy_version then
    raise exception 'ACCT_POLICY_VERSION_IMMUTABLE: the accounting-policy version of a posted record cannot change (on %)', tg_table_name;
  end if;
  return new;
end;
$function$
-- FN platform_record_job_run(text,uuid,text,integer,text)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.platform_record_job_run(p_job text, p_tenant_id uuid, p_status text, p_affected integer, p_detail text DEFAULT NULL::text)
 RETURNS void
 LANGUAGE sql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
  insert into platform_job_runs (job, tenant_id, started_at, finished_at, status, affected, detail)
  values (p_job, p_tenant_id, now(), now(), coalesce(p_status, 'ok'), p_affected, left(p_detail, 500));
$function$
-- FN platform_reg_number(uuid,text,date,text)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.platform_reg_number(p_tenant_id uuid, p_key text, p_on date, p_class text DEFAULT NULL::text)
 RETURNS numeric
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 STABLE SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
declare
  v regulatory_parameters%rowtype;
begin
  v := platform_reg_resolve(p_tenant_id, p_key, p_on, p_class);
  if v.value_num is null then
    raise exception 'REG_PARAM_NOT_NUMERIC: % holds a text value', p_key;
  end if;
  return v.value_num;
end;
$function$
-- FN platform_reg_resolve(uuid,text,date,text)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.platform_reg_resolve(p_tenant_id uuid, p_key text, p_on date, p_class text DEFAULT NULL::text)
 RETURNS regulatory_parameters
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 STABLE SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
declare
  v regulatory_parameters%rowtype;
begin
  if p_tenant_id is null or p_on is null then
    raise exception 'REG_ARGUMENT: tenant and date are required';
  end if;
  if not exists (select 1 from regulatory_parameter_definitions where key = p_key) then
    raise exception 'REG_UNKNOWN_KEY: % is not a known parameter key', p_key;
  end if;
  select * into v
  from regulatory_parameters
  where tenant_id = p_tenant_id
    and parameter_key = p_key
    and status = 'approved'
    and effective_from <= p_on
    and (effective_to is null or p_on < effective_to)
    and (applies_to is null or applies_to = p_class)
  order by (applies_to is not null) desc
  limit 1;
  if not found then
    raise exception 'REG_PARAM_MISSING: no approved value for % on % (class %) for this company; add one under regulatory parameters and have a company admin approve it',
      p_key, p_on, coalesce(p_class, 'none');
  end if;
  return v;
end;
$function$
-- FN platform_request_role()
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.platform_request_role()
 RETURNS text
 LANGUAGE sql
 STABLE
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
  select nullif(current_setting('request.jwt.claims', true), '')::jsonb ->> 'role';
$function$
-- FN platform_run_operator_digest_logged()
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.platform_run_operator_digest_logged()
 RETURNS uuid
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
declare v_id uuid;
begin
  begin
    v_id := platform_run_operator_digest('scheduled');
    perform platform_record_job_run('operator_digest', null, 'ok', case when v_id is null then 0 else 1 end,
      case when v_id is null then 'skipped: digest_enabled = false' else v_id::text end);
    perform platform_job_runs_prune();
  exception when others then
    perform platform_record_job_run('operator_digest', null, 'error', 0, sqlerrm);
    raise;
  end;
  return v_id;
end;
$function$
-- FN platform_run_operator_digest(text)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.platform_run_operator_digest(p_trigger text DEFAULT 'scheduled'::text)
 RETURNS uuid
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
declare
  v_enabled    boolean := coalesce((select (notifications ->> 'digest_enabled')::boolean from platform_settings where id = true), true);
  v_recipients text[]  := coalesce((select array(select jsonb_array_elements_text(notifications -> 'alert_recipients')) from platform_settings where id = true), '{}');
  v_end        timestamptz := now();
  v_start      timestamptz;
  v_payload    jsonb;
  v_id         uuid;
  v_summary    text;
  v_admin      record;
begin
  if p_trigger not in ('scheduled', 'manual') then
    raise exception 'trigger must be scheduled or manual';
  end if;
  -- Scheduled runs respect the switch; a manual "Generate now" always runs.
  if p_trigger = 'scheduled' and not v_enabled then
    return null;
  end if;
  -- Period = since the previous run (any trigger), capped at 7 days so a
  -- long gap does not produce a "new companies" list going back months.
  v_start := greatest(
    coalesce((select max(period_end) from platform_digests), v_end - interval '1 day'),
    v_end - interval '7 days');
  v_payload := platform_operator_digest_payload(v_start, v_end);
  v_summary := platform_digest_summary(v_payload);
  insert into platform_digests (period_start, period_end, trigger, generated_by, attention_count, payload, recipients, delivery_status)
  values (v_start, v_end, p_trigger, auth.uid(), coalesce((v_payload ->> 'attention_count')::integer, 0), v_payload, v_recipients,
          case when cardinality(v_recipients) = 0 then 'skipped' else 'pending' end)
  returning id into v_id;
  -- In-app: one notification per platform admin. notifications.tenant_id
  -- is NOT NULL, so use the admin's own tenant (the home/internal one).
  for v_admin in select id, tenant_id from app_users where is_platform_admin loop
    insert into notifications (tenant_id, recipient_id, type, title, body)
    values (
      v_admin.tenant_id, v_admin.id, 'operator_digest',
      case when coalesce((v_payload ->> 'attention_count')::integer, 0) = 0
        then 'Operator digest: all clear'
        else format('Operator digest: %s item(s) need attention', v_payload ->> 'attention_count') end,
      v_summary
    );
  end loop;
  perform log_platform_event(
    'platform.digest.run', null, 'platform_digest', v_id::text, null, null,
    jsonb_build_object('trigger', p_trigger, 'attention_count', v_payload -> 'attention_count',
                       'recipients', cardinality(v_recipients), 'period_start', v_start, 'period_end', v_end)
  );
  return v_id;
end;
$function$
-- FN platform_seed_workflow_from_template(uuid,text,text)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.platform_seed_workflow_from_template(p_tenant_id uuid, p_template_key text, p_applies_to text DEFAULT 'requests'::text)
 RETURNS integer
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
declare
  v_item record;
  v_ids  jsonb := '{}'::jsonb;   -- sort_order -> stage id
  v_id   uuid;
  v_n    integer := 0;
begin
  for v_item in
    select i.sort_order, i.name, i.payload
    from industry_template_items i
    where i.template_key = p_template_key and i.kind = 'workflow_stage'
    order by i.sort_order
  loop
    insert into workflow_stages (
      tenant_id, name, sequence_order, approver_role, threshold_amount, applies_to,
      requires_offer_entry, blocks_offer_submitter_approval, is_finance_terminal_stage, requires_offer_selection
    )
    values (
      p_tenant_id, v_item.name, v_item.sort_order, v_item.payload ->> 'approver_role',
      nullif(v_item.payload ->> 'threshold_amount', '')::numeric, p_applies_to,
      coalesce((v_item.payload ->> 'requires_offer_entry')::boolean, false),
      coalesce((v_item.payload ->> 'blocks_offer_submitter_approval')::boolean, false),
      coalesce((v_item.payload ->> 'is_finance_terminal_stage')::boolean, false),
      coalesce((v_item.payload ->> 'requires_offer_selection')::boolean, false)
    )
    returning id into v_id;
    v_ids := v_ids || jsonb_build_object(v_item.sort_order::text, v_id);
    v_n := v_n + 1;
  end loop;
  -- Second pass: routing.
  for v_item in
    select i.sort_order, i.payload
    from industry_template_items i
    where i.template_key = p_template_key and i.kind = 'workflow_stage'
  loop
    update workflow_stages
       set next_stage_low_id  = (v_ids ->> (v_item.payload ->> 'next_low'))::uuid,
           next_stage_high_id = (v_ids ->> (v_item.payload ->> 'next_high'))::uuid
     where id = (v_ids ->> v_item.sort_order::text)::uuid;
  end loop;
  return v_n;
end;
$function$
-- FN platform_session_is_mfa()
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.platform_session_is_mfa()
 RETURNS boolean
 LANGUAGE sql
 STABLE SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
  select coalesce(
    (current_setting('request.jwt.claims', true)::jsonb ->> 'aal') = 'aal2',
    false
  );
$function$
-- FN platform_stamp_policy_version()
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.platform_stamp_policy_version()
 RETURNS trigger
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
declare
  v_raw    text := to_jsonb(new) ->> tg_argv[0];
  v_date   date := coalesce(left(v_raw, 10)::date, current_date);
  v_batch  uuid;
begin
  begin
    v_batch := nullif(current_setting('acct.import_batch', true), '')::uuid;
  exception when others then
    v_batch := null;
  end;
  if v_batch is not null and exists (
       select 1 from ins_import_batches b
        where b.id = v_batch and b.tenant_id = new.tenant_id and b.status in ('validated', 'committed')) then
    new.accounting_policy_version := 1;
  else
    new.accounting_policy_version := platform_accounting_policy_version(new.tenant_id, v_date);
  end if;
  return new;
end;
$function$
-- FN platform_users_directory(text,uuid,text,text,integer,integer,integer)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.platform_users_directory(p_search text DEFAULT NULL::text, p_tenant_id uuid DEFAULT NULL::uuid, p_kind text DEFAULT NULL::text, p_module text DEFAULT NULL::text, p_quiet_days integer DEFAULT NULL::integer, p_limit integer DEFAULT 50, p_offset integer DEFAULT 0)
 RETURNS TABLE(user_id uuid, name text, email text, role_title text, tenant_id uuid, tenant_name text, tenant_status text, is_platform_admin boolean, is_company_admin boolean, modules jsonb, finance_role text, mfa_enrolled boolean, last_sign_in_at timestamp with time zone, created_at timestamp with time zone, total_count bigint)
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 STABLE SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
declare
  v_search text := nullif(btrim(p_search), '');
begin
  perform require_platform_admin('The user directory');
  return query
  with base as (
    select
      u.id                as b_user_id,
      u.name              as b_name,
      u.email             as b_email,
      u.role_title        as b_role_title,
      u.tenant_id         as b_tenant_id,
      t.name              as b_tenant_name,
      t.status            as b_tenant_status,
      u.is_platform_admin as b_is_platform_admin,
      u.is_company_admin  as b_is_company_admin,
      coalesce(
        (select jsonb_agg(jsonb_build_object('module', sr.module, 'role', sr.role) order by sr.module)
         from staff_roles sr
         where sr.user_id = u.id and sr.tenant_id = u.tenant_id),
        '[]'::jsonb
      )                   as b_modules,
      (select ftm.role
       from finance_team_members ftm
       where ftm.user_id = u.id and ftm.tenant_id = u.tenant_id
       order by case ftm.role when 'finance' then 0 else 1 end
       limit 1)           as b_finance_role,
      exists (select 1 from auth.mfa_factors f where f.user_id = u.id and f.status = 'verified')
                          as b_mfa_enrolled,
      au.last_sign_in_at  as b_last_sign_in_at,
      u.created_at        as b_created_at
    from app_users u
    join tenants t on t.id = u.tenant_id
    left join auth.users au on au.id = u.id
  )
  select
    b.b_user_id, b.b_name, b.b_email, b.b_role_title, b.b_tenant_id, b.b_tenant_name, b.b_tenant_status,
    b.b_is_platform_admin, b.b_is_company_admin, b.b_modules, b.b_finance_role, b.b_mfa_enrolled,
    b.b_last_sign_in_at, b.b_created_at,
    count(*) over () as total_count
  from base b
  where (v_search is null
         or b.b_name ilike '%' || v_search || '%'
         or b.b_email ilike '%' || v_search || '%'
         or b.b_tenant_name ilike '%' || v_search || '%')
    and (p_tenant_id is null or b.b_tenant_id = p_tenant_id)
    and (p_kind is null or case p_kind
           when 'platform_admin' then b.b_is_platform_admin
           when 'company_admin'  then b.b_is_company_admin
           when 'finance'        then b.b_finance_role is not null
           when 'member'         then not b.b_is_platform_admin and not b.b_is_company_admin
           else true end)
    and (p_module is null or b.b_modules @> jsonb_build_array(jsonb_build_object('module', p_module)))
    and (p_quiet_days is null
         or b.b_last_sign_in_at is null
         or b.b_last_sign_in_at < now() - make_interval(days => p_quiet_days))
  order by b.b_tenant_name, b.b_email
  limit greatest(1, least(coalesce(p_limit, 50), 500))
  offset greatest(0, coalesce(p_offset, 0));
end;
$function$
-- FN pmo_check_task_dependency_validity()
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.pmo_check_task_dependency_validity()
 RETURNS trigger
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
DECLARE
  v_pred_project uuid;
  v_succ_project uuid;
  v_cycle_found boolean;
BEGIN
  SELECT project_id INTO v_pred_project FROM public.pmo_tasks WHERE id = NEW.predecessor_task_id;
  SELECT project_id INTO v_succ_project FROM public.pmo_tasks WHERE id = NEW.successor_task_id;
  IF v_pred_project IS DISTINCT FROM v_succ_project THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'pmo_task_dependencies: predecessor and successor must belong to the same project';
  END IF;
  -- Would this edge close a loop? True iff the successor can already
  -- reach the predecessor through existing edges (excluding the row
  -- being updated, if this is an UPDATE) -- adding predecessor->successor
  -- on top of that path would complete a cycle.
  WITH RECURSIVE reachable AS (
    SELECT successor_task_id AS task_id
    FROM public.pmo_task_dependencies
    WHERE predecessor_task_id = NEW.successor_task_id
      AND (TG_OP = 'INSERT' OR id <> NEW.id)
    UNION
    SELECT d.successor_task_id
    FROM public.pmo_task_dependencies d
    JOIN reachable r ON d.predecessor_task_id = r.task_id
    WHERE (TG_OP = 'INSERT' OR d.id <> NEW.id)
  )
  SELECT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM reachable WHERE task_id = NEW.predecessor_task_id) INTO v_cycle_found;
  IF v_cycle_found THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'pmo_task_dependencies: this dependency would create a cycle';
  END IF;
  RETURN NEW;
END;
$function$
-- FN pmo_time_entry_apply_rate()
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.pmo_time_entry_apply_rate()
 RETURNS trigger
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
BEGIN
  IF NEW.hourly_rate IS NULL THEN
    SELECT a.hourly_rate INTO NEW.hourly_rate
    FROM hr_employees e
    JOIN pmo_resource_allocations a
      ON a.employee_id = e.id
     AND a.project_id = NEW.project_id
     AND a.status = 'active'
    WHERE e.user_id = NEW.user_id
    ORDER BY a.created_at DESC
    LIMIT 1;
  END IF;
  RETURN NEW;
END;
$function$

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.post_issue_items_to_stock()
 RETURNS trigger
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
DECLARE
  v_issue goods_issues%ROWTYPE;
BEGIN
